### [1] [Subliminal Learning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08538)
*Arman Hatami,Romina Aalishah,Ilya E. Monosov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统验证了"潜隐学习"（subliminal learning）现象在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的存在：教师VLM的隐藏偏好（动物偏好、面部属性偏好）可经由完全无关的几何形状标注任务（仅传递数字）转移给学生VLM，偏好选择率差异最高约10个百分点，且学生的解释文本也更接近有偏教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在纯文本LLM中发现潜隐学习——教师通过与其偏好无关的训练数据把隐藏偏好传给学生。但该现象是否存在于多模态的视觉-语言模型中尚不清楚；若存在，则意味着用"看似无关"的数据微调VLM也可能无意中注入偏好，对VLM训练与数据治理具有直接风险。

Method: 构造教师-学生迁移实验范式：① 以同一基座模型微调出偏好特定动物或面部属性的"有偏教师"，并保留同基座的"对照教师"；② 让两位教师对涉及几何形状的无关视觉任务生成数字；③ 两个由同一基座初始化的学生VLM在同一批图像上微调，分别使用有偏教师与对照教师生成的数字作为标签；④ 在2B–7B参数范围的多个VLM上比较学生对目标偏好的选择率差异，并进一步比较学生解释文本与教师解释文本的用词重合度。

Result: 在2B至7B的多个VLM上，动物偏好与面部偏好均可通过该无关任务实现迁移：用有偏教师输出训练的学生选择目标的比例高于对照学生，动物偏好任务中差距最高约10个百分点。迁移强度随训练数据量增加而增强，并受目标偏好类型、具体模型以及数字生成方式的影响。此外，即便三个模型选择同一张图像，有偏学生的解释也比对照学生的解释使用更多与有偏教师相同的词语和短语。

Conclusion: 潜隐学习在视觉-语言模型中同样成立，且不仅改变模型对图像的选择偏好，还改变其解释选择的方式（解释风格/措辞）。这表明在无关数据上微调VLM可能悄然引入非预期偏好，增加数据量甚至会放大迁移效应，因此VLM训练与数据审查需将"语义无关"数据视为潜在偏好载体。

Abstract: Subliminal learning is a phenomenon in large language models (LLMs) where a teacher passes a hidden preference to a student through training data unrelated to that preference. We study whether subliminal learning also occurs in vision-language models (VLMs). We finetune a teacher VLM to prefer a specific animal or facial attribute and use a control teacher from the same base model. Both teachers generate numbers for an unrelated visual task involving geometric shapes. Two students initialized from the same base VLM are finetuned on the same images, using numbers generated by either the biased or control teacher. Across VLMs ranging from 2B to 7B parameters, we find that both animal and facial preferences can transfer through this unrelated task. Students trained on biased-teacher outputs select the target more often than control students, with a difference of up to $\sim$10 percentage points in the animal preference task. Transfer can become stronger with more training data and varies with the target preference, model, and how the numbers are generated. Beyond preferences, we show that the biased student's explanations use more of the same words and phrases as the biased teacher's explanations than those of the control student, even when all three models select the same image. These results matter for training VLMs and show that training on unrelated data may change both a VLM's image preferences and how it explains its choices, and that transfer can become stronger with more training samples.

</details>


### [2] [Towards Label-Efficient Geospatial Foundation Model Adaptation via Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Building Damage Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08561)
*Hengwei Zhao,Qirui Wang,Zhuo Zheng,Xiaoyan Lu,Rusty Feagin,Wenzhe Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PUMA框架，仅需少量受损建筑标注即可将地理空间基础模型（GFM）适配到建筑损毁制图，通过非对比弱监督表征精炼与潜在状态标签消歧实现标签高效的参数高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFM结合PEFT可提升灾害响应能力，但参数高效不等于标签高效；灾害损毁制图面临标注数据稀缺、标注时间有限的瓶颈。引入弱监督后，关键挑战在于难以学到足够判别性的表征。

Method: PUMA框架：（1）非对比式弱监督表征精炼，从梯度角度缓解噪声监督对表征学习的影响；（2）基于潜在状态的标签消歧，实现可逆的监督精炼，且无需估计类别先验。仅需少量受损建筑正样本标注（正-未标注学习范式）。

Result: 在全球多个灾害基准数据集上验证了有效性与鲁棒性；在2025年洛杉矶山火的真实应用中，在极有限监督下仍能准确制图。

Conclusion: 弱监督PEFT为GFM走向真实应用提供了标签高效路径。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) are reshaping remote sensing by shifting the field from task-specific modeling toward general-purpose geospatial intelligence. Mapping the damage to buildings stands to benefit substantially from this shift, as GFMs pretrained on large-scale Earth observation data provide generalizable geospatial representations that can facilitate effective responses to sudden-onset disasters. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) facilitates GFM adaptation, parameter efficiency does not necessarily translate into label efficiency. Building damage mapping remains challenging due to scarce pre-existing labeled data or limited time for annotation. When weak supervision is introduced to alleviate the labeling bottleneck, we identify the difficulty of learning sufficiently discriminative representations as a key challenge for label-efficient GFM adaptation. To address this challenge, we propose a Positive-Unlabeled GFM Adaptation (PUMA) framework that requires annotations for only a small number of damaged buildings. More specifically, PUMA integrates non-contrastive weakly supervised representation refinement to mitigate the impact of noisy supervision on representation learning from a gradient perspective, and latent-state-based label disambiguation to enable reversible supervision refinement without requiring class prior estimation. Extensive experiments on disaster benchmark datasets from around the world demonstrated the effectiveness and robustness of PUMA. A real-world application to the 2025 Los Angeles wildfires further highlighted its potential to accurately map damage under highly limited supervision. More broadly, these findings suggest that weakly supervised PEFT offers a label-efficient pathway from GFMs to real-world applications.

</details>


### [3] [HaNDF: Object-Conditioned Geometric Neural Hand Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08578)
*Zuhao Liu,Melih Darcan,Zhengdi Yu,Riza Alp Guler,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HaNDF，一种物体条件神经距离场，作为手部关节流形上的数据驱动先验，用于建模合理的手-物交互构型，并支持姿态精化、遮挡下重建与物理合理的操作合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无条件生成先验在人体姿态建模上有效，但手是与物体频繁交互的铰接体，因此需要能以待交互物体为条件的生成先验来刻画合理手部姿态，而非忽略物体影响。

Method: 构建几何感知的物体条件神经距离场：以条件场的零水平集表示合理的手部关节构型；借助黎曼几何工具将流形上的先验零水平集参数化，并把给定姿态投影到该场上；同时可反向优化条件（即交互物体）以确定所交互对象。

Result: 大量实验表明 HaNDF 可作为灵活且强力的手-物交互先验，在姿态精化、遮挡下重建、物理合理操作合成等任务上均取得良好效果。

Conclusion: HaNDF 将神经距离场与手部关节流形几何结合，实现了物体条件化的合理手姿建模，为手-物交互相关下游任务提供了通用先验框架。

Abstract: We propose, HaNDF, object-conditional neural distance fields as data-driven priors for modeling the plausible hand-object configurations, in the product manifold of hand articulations. While recent unconditional generative priors showed success in modeling human pose, hands are versatile articulated bodies often found in interaction with other objects. This makes it crucial to develop a generative prior which can model the plausible hand poses conditioned on the object under interaction. To this end, our HaNDF proposes a geometry-aware, object-conditioned neural distance field, representing the plausible articulations in the zero-level set of a conditional field. We leverage tools from Riemannian geometry to (i) represent plausible hands in the zero level-set of an object-induced neural field, and (ii) project a given pose onto this field. Using HaNDF, we can also optimize for a plausible condition, determining the object under interaction. Our extensive evaluations demonstrate that our HaNDF provides a flexible and powerful prior for hand-object interaction, suitable for applications such as pose refinement, reconstruction under occlusion, and physically plausible manipulation synthesis.

</details>


### [4] [InstaGen: Group-Shared Importance Sampling for Fast Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08610)
*Kislay Aditya Oj,Kumar Krishna Agrawal,Sri Surya Varma Datla,Aditya Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InstaGen，将置换块稀疏注意力(PBSA)的块选择重构为重要性采样：保留精确top块集合，其余块按估计注意力概率采样并以逆概率重加权，在视频生成中显著提升近似质量与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT是视频生成主流架构，但二次注意力复杂度带来巨大计算开销与延迟。PBSA通过语义置换后只计算部分块来降本，然而现有方法普遍采用top-p范式，仅选少数最高分键块。作者发现在视频生成常见的高熵注意力分布下，top-p采样效率极低，同等算力下采样能给出更好的近似。

Method: 将块选择建模为重要性采样：top打分键构成精确集合被完整计算，其余键按其估计注意力概率抽样，并以逆采样概率重加权。基于此提出InstaGen，可参数化任意PBSA方法——用"精确集+加权样本"替换原块选择；每个query聚类抽取一个样本供簇内所有query共享，使被采样键构成适配GPU高效执行的块。实验以SVG族（SVG2、SVG-EAR）为基线。

Result: 在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B与Wan2.1-T2V-14B上，同等注意力密度下InstaGen(基于SVG2)相对SVG2的PSNR分别提升3.4 dB和2 dB；同等墙钟时间下提升1.6 dB和2.2 dB。在14B模型上，InstaGen以几乎相同密度超过SVG-EAR 1.1 dB，且更快（1.65x vs 1.48x 相对稠密的加速）。

Conclusion: 重要性采样视角优于top-p式块选择，尤其适用于高熵注意力；InstaGen作为通用插件可无缝增强现有PBSA方法，在质量与效率上同时获益，为视频DiT的高效注意力提供实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) are the dominant architecture for video generation, but their quadratic attention complexity results in substantial computational cost and latency. Permuted block-sparse attention (PBSA) offers a promising approach to reducing this cost by selectively computing blocks of a semantically permuted attention matrix. However, existing approaches typically select only the top few key blocks for each query block, following a top-$p$ paradigm. We find that top-$p$ selection can be highly sample inefficient in high-entropy attention regimes, which are common in video generation. In these regimes, we show that sampling can provide substantially better approximations under the same computational budget. More generally, we cast block selection as importance sampling, where the top-scoring keys form an exact set that is computed in full and the remaining keys are represented by a sample drawn in proportion to their estimated attention and reweighted by inverse sampling probability. Building on this formulation, we present InstaGen, which can be parameterized by any PBSA method: it replaces the method's block selection with an exact set plus a weighted sample. Each query cluster draws one sample shared by all its queries, so the sampled keys form blocks amenable to efficient GPU execution. In this paper, we take the SVG family of PBSA methods and show that, on Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B and Wan2.1-T2V-14B, InstaGen parameterized by SVG2 improves PSNR over SVG2 by 3.4 dB and 2 dB, respectively, at matched attention density, and by 1.6 dB and 2.2 dB at matched wall-clock time. On Wan2.1-T2V-14B, InstaGen also beats SVG-EAR by 1.1 dB PSNR at nearly the same density while running faster (1.65x against 1.48x over dense).

</details>


### [5] [BEVCast: Path-Adaptive BEV for Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08631)
*Qiren Wang,Mingyu Guo,Zixuan Wang,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEVCast 提出一种分辨率随预测行驶路径自适应的两阶段 BEV：先以 4.096 m 粗网格编码全场景，再由热力图头筛选高热单元格以 1.024 m 精化（固定预算 10%），其余保持粗分辨率，轨迹头读取混合分辨率 BEV。在 nuScenes 与 NAVSIM 上精度优于同架构的全细网格基线，同时显著降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机的驾驶模型构建稠密 BEV 网格，并对每个单元格投入相同的计算量；但车辆在规划时域内实际只经过其中极少数单元格，这种"均匀分配算力"的方式存在明显的计算冗余。作者希望让 BEV 的分辨率分配跟随任务需求（即预测行驶路径），而非跟随网格几何。

Method: 两阶段自适应分辨率 BEV：第一阶段用 4.096 m 单元格的粗网格编码整个场景；一个在真实行驶路径上训练的热力图头（heat head）为每个粗单元格打分，按固定预算将热值最高的单元格精化为 1.024 m；其余单元格保持粗分辨率。轨迹头读取这种混合分辨率 BEV，仍覆盖全场景，且不需要 HD 地图或导航指令。对照基线为相同架构与训练配方、全程 1.024 m 的全细网格模型。

Result: 10% 精化预算下，BEVCast 仅编码 28% 的 BEV 查询，batch size 1 时 BEV 编码器耗时降至 66%。nuScenes 上平均位移误差 1.078 m（基线 1.149 m），可行驶区域违规率 1.46%（基线 2.29%）；NAVSIM navtest 上平均位移误差 0.577 m（基线 0.598 m），扩展预测驾驶员模型得分 82.51（基线 81.28）。与已发表规划器相比，在不使用导航指令的条件下达到可比误差。

Conclusion: 驾驶 BEV 的分辨率可以遵循任务需求而非固定网格：通过在预测路径附近集中细粒度算力、在其余区域保留粗粒度，可在降低编码计算量的同时提升轨迹精度与安全性指标。

Abstract: Camera-based driving models build a dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) grid and spend the same computation on every cell of it, although the vehicle crosses only a few of those cells within the planning horizon. We present BEVCast, a two-stage BEV whose resolution adapts to the predicted driving path. A coarse pass encodes the whole scene on a grid of 4.096 m cells. A heat head trained on the recorded path assigns a heat to every coarse cell and the cells with the highest heat are refined into 1.024 m cells under a fixed budget. The remaining cells keep their coarse resolution, so the trajectory head reads a mixed-resolution BEV that still covers the entire scene and needs neither an HD map nor a navigation command. Against a full grid of 1.024 m cells with the same architecture and training recipe, BEVCast at a 10% refinement budget encodes 28% of the BEV queries and needs 66% of the BEV encoder time at batch size 1. On nuScenes its mean displacement error is 1.078 m against 1.149 m and its drivable area violation rate is 1.46% against 2.29%. On NAVSIM navtest its mean displacement error is 0.577 m against 0.598 m and its extended predictive driver model score is 82.51 against 81.28. Next to published planners BEVCast reaches a comparable error on both benchmarks without a navigation command. The resolution of a driving BEV can therefore follow the task instead of the grid.

</details>


### [6] [From Geometry to Function: A Multi-Task LiDAR Benchmark Dataset for Semantic and Functional Parsing of Urban Facades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08645)
*Abdiaziz Muse,Tooba Javed,Tao Pu,Jiju Poovvancheri,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HFX3D，一个约4.92亿点、覆盖加拿大哈利法克斯26栋建筑、跨四季昼夜与多天气的大规模城市立面 LiDAR 基准，含语义、实例、实例级功能属性三层标注，并给出多类主流架构的基准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR 基准仅停留在几何与语义分割层面，缺乏对每个建筑元素功能角色的推理支持，而建筑立面理解对城市数字孪生、遗产建模与可持续城市分析至关重要。

Method: 构建 HFX3D 数据集：多季节、昼夜、多天气采集点云；采用“几何引导启发式 + 专家校验”的半自动流水线完成三层级标注（语义、实例、实例级功能属性）；并评测点基、图基、卷积与 Transformer 四类代表性架构。

Result: 即使在严重类别不平衡下，语义分割已具挑战性；重复结构的实例分离与基于几何的功能推理难度显著更高。

Conclusion: HFX3D 是首个将几何、语义与功能标签相结合的建筑立面数据集，为功能感知的三维城市理解建立新基础。

Abstract: Understanding building facades is central to urban digital twins, heritage modelling, and sustainable city analysis, yet existing LiDAR benchmarks stop at geometry and semantic segmentation and offer little support for reasoning about the functional role of each architectural element. We introduce HFX3D, a large-scale urban facade benchmark of approximately 492 million LiDAR points from 26 buildings in Halifax, Canada, captured across four seasons, day and night, and varied weather. Each facade is annotated at three tiers, semantic, instance, and per-instance functional attributes, making it, to our knowledge, the first facade dataset to pair geometry and semantics with functional labels. Annotations are produced by a semi-automatic pipeline that combines geometry-guided heuristics with expert validation. We benchmark representative point-based, graph-based, convolutional, and transformer architectures and find that semantic segmentation is already challenging under severe class imbalance, while instance separation of repetitive structures and functional reasoning from geometry are substantially harder. The benchmark establishes a new foundation for function-aware 3D urban understanding. Project page: https://jijup.github.io/hfx3d-benchmark/ ; code: https://github.com/jijup/hfx3d-benchmark

</details>


### [7] [VoluCompose: Constructing Compact Volumetric Visual Representations for Medical Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08649)
*Zanting Ye,Yunrui Lin,Shengyuan Liu,Jing Hao,Chen Hu,Yefeng Zheng,Lijun Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、模型无关的框架 VoluCompose，将"保留什么内容"与"分配多少 token"解耦，用双曲空间 token 组合与基于注意力熵的容量控制压缩医学体积图像的视觉 token，平均仅保留 22.78% token 仍达 121.68% 相对质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型处理体数据（volumetric）时，数百张切片会产生长且冗余的视觉 token 序列，计算成本高。现有压缩方法通常将内容选择与预算确定耦合在一起；同时作者观察到相邻切片共享视觉特征、且不同视觉编码器的注意力集中程度差异明显，因此需要一个可解耦、可适配不同模型的压缩方案。

Method: 1) 双曲 token 组合模块：将连续切片分组成有序支撑单元（ordered support units），在洛伦兹（Lorentz）距离度量下同时考虑特征幅值与方向，用于 token 分配、锚点选择与细化；2) 容量控制模块：利用视觉编码器的注意力熵，在注意力更分散时分配更多 token。整体免训练、模型无关，不改变原模型参数。

Result: 在 2 个任务、3 个数据集、4 个骨干网络上评估；9 组标定设置中 VoluCompose 取得最高平均质量-保留率超体积（quality-retention hypervolume）。平均相对质量为未压缩参考的 121.68%，平均仅保留 22.78% 视觉 token；10% token 预算下仍鲁棒。各设置下相对未压缩参考的最低 ROUGE-L 比率为 96.4%，而压缩基线的最低值仅为 15.0%–70.5%。

Conclusion: 将内容选择与 token 预算分配解耦，并结合双曲几何度量与注意力熵驱动的容量控制，可在免训练条件下显著压缩医学体积视觉 token，同时保持甚至提升下游质量，且在不同任务、数据集与骨干网络上稳定有效。

Abstract: Medical vision--language models can interpret volumetric images, but processing full volumes is costly because hundreds of slices produce long, redundant visual-token sequences. Existing compression methods often couple content selection with budget determination. We observe that neighboring slices share visual features and that attention concentration varies across visual encoders. We propose VoluCompose, a training-free, model-agnostic framework that separates what visual content to retain from how many tokens to allocate. Its hyperbolic token composition module groups contiguous slices into ordered support units and uses the Lorentz distance to account for feature magnitude and direction during token allocation, anchor selection, and refinement. Its capacity control module uses visual-encoder attention entropy to allocate more tokens when attention is more dispersed. We evaluate VoluCompose on two tasks across three datasets and four backbones. Across nine calibrated settings, VoluCompose attains the highest mean quality--retention hypervolume among the compared methods. It achieves a mean relative quality of 121.68\% against the uncompressed references while retaining 22.78% of visual tokens on average. Compression remains robust even at a 10% token budget. Across settings, the lowest ROUGE-L ratio to the uncompressed reference is 96.4\% for VoluCompose. The corresponding minima for the compression baselines range from 15.0% to 70.5%. Code is available at https://github.com/yezanting/VoluCompose.

</details>


### [8] [Turkish Multimodal Video Fact-Checking: A New Dataset and a Comparison of Zero-Shot Agentic and Fine-Tuned Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08651)
*Azra Açıl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个土耳其语多模态视频事实核查数据集TeViD，并基于Qwen2.5-7B/Qwen2-VL-7B构建两条共享同一开源基座的核查流水线（3MFact-TR多模块流水线与LoRA微调闭卷流水线），报告准确率、宏F1、True类F1与True类召回率及消融结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频类虚假信息在社交媒体快速传播，但现有事实核查系统几乎只针对英文文本；土耳其语等低资源语言的核查工具严重匮乏。

Method: 1) 构建TeViD数据集：从IFCN认证机构Teyit.org的文章中整理，并补充翻译的Snopes文章与合成样本以缓解类别不平衡。2) 流水线一3MFact-TR：将多角色3MFact框架适配土耳其语，使用Qwen2.5-7B与Qwen2-VL-7B，新增语义不一致检测器、5W1H问题管理器、排除事实核查网站以防泄漏的双语证据检索器。3) 流水线二微调模型：用LoRA微调单个Qwen2.5-7B，依据声明与视频文本描述输出闭卷判定。4) 评估指标为准确率、宏F1、True类F1、True类召回率，并开展消融实验。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅说明对两条系统均报告准确率、宏F1、True类F1与True类召回率，并提供消融研究（具体数值需见正文）。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于填补土耳其语多模态视频事实核查的空白：发布TeViD数据集，并以同一开源基座实现"多模块流水线"与"LoRA闭卷微调"两种范式，为低资源语言的多模态核查提供可复现基线。

Abstract: Video-based misinformation spreads rapidly on social media, but almost all existing fact-checking systems focus exclusively on English text. Tools for low-resource languages such as Turkish are severely limited. In this work, we present TeViD (Turkish Video Fact-checking Dataset), to the best of our knowledge the first multimodal video fact-checking dataset for Turkish. We compiled the dataset from articles published by Teyit.org, an IFCN-certified organisation, and enriched it with translated Snopes articles and synthetic examples to reduce class imbalance. Building on TeViD, we develop two verification pipelines that share the same open-source base model. First, in 3MFact-TR (Pipeline 1), we adapt the multi-role 3MFact framework to Turkish with the Qwen2.5-7B large language model and the Qwen2-VL-7B vision-language model, and add three modules of our own: a semantic inconsistency checker, a 5W1H question manager, and a bilingual evidence retriever that excludes fact-checking sites to prevent leakage. Second, as our Fine-Tuned Model (Pipeline 2), we fine-tune a single Qwen2.5-7B with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to produce closed-book verdicts from the claim and the video's textual description. We report accuracy, macro F1, True class F1, and True class recall for both systems, together with ablation studies.

</details>


### [9] [An Anatomy-guided 3D Segmentation Pipeline for Prostate Surgical Phantoms from CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08652)
*Iven Chen,Kuan-I Chung,Ayberk Acar,Susheela Sharma Stern,Alejandro O. Chara,Jie Ying Wu,Daniel Moyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于经典医学图像处理技术的自动化3D分割流程，将前列腺体模的已知解剖结构编码为几何与强度先验，无需训练语料且可跨体模版本迁移，在57例扫描中实现0.83中位Dice的内腔分割并检出全部32个病灶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人前列腺手术自动化在体模上原型测试时需对体模进行 volumetric 成像以测量感知与运动误差，但体模迭代快、数据量小且不一致，人工标注解剖不可靠且耗时1–2小时，导致深度学习难以在合理规模下使用。

Method: 构建自动化3D分割流水线，采用经典医学图像处理技术，将体模已知解剖结构显式编码为几何先验与强度先验；方法无需训练语料，可直接迁移至新版本体模而无需重新标注。

Result: 在涵盖多个体模版本的57例扫描上，于11例对比度抑制扫描中以内腔恢复达到0.83中位Dice；检出全部32个标注局灶病灶且无误报；普通CPU硬件上2–8分钟完成全扫描，人工需1–2小时。

Conclusion: 基于解剖先验的经典图像处理分割可替代人工标注与数据饥渴型深度学习，为手术机器人自动化体模测试提供高效、可复用且可靠的分割方案。

Abstract: Full autonomy has been a long-term goal in robotic surgery and a near-to-medium-term goal for prostatectomy. Due to the relatively closed structure of the procedure, its standard-of-care robotic/laparoscopic solutions, and procedure economics, prostate procedures are an optimal early target for surgical automation. Prototyping and testing of possible solutions is done in constructed laboratory phantoms, either synthetic tissues or animal phantoms. To enable measurement of perceptual and robotic motion error, phantoms undergo volumetric imaging; these images are critical to both a potential deployed automatic system, used in planning motion and treatment (cutting, ablation, suturing, etc.), as well as post-hoc assessment of system performance. Rapid iteration on these phantom models yields small, inconsistent datasets, while the current workflow for human annotation is often anatomically unreliable and prohibitively time-consuming, rendering deep learning unsuitable at reasonable scales. To address these limitations, we present an automated 3D segmentation pipeline that uses classical medical image-processing techniques to encode the known anatomy of the phantoms as explicit geometric and intensity priors, which requires no training corpus and is transferred to each new phantom revision without re-annotation. Across 57 scans spanning multiple phantom revisions, our pipeline recovers the capsule interior at 0.83 median Dice on 11 contrast-suppressed scans and detects all 32 annotated focal lesions without false positives, completing a full scan in 2-8 minutes on commodity CPU hardware versus 1-2 hours by hand.

</details>


### [10] [Scoring, Not Learning: Decomposing Catalog-Consolidation Gains in Web-Scraped Retail Product Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08655)
*Kaden Leis,Xiang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以16.5万张奶酪图像目录为对象，证明"整理噪声目录"带来的精度提升主要来自评估标签空间的改变（尤其是类别粗化），而非识别器本身变强，且其收益与模型容量无交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络图像搜索构建的商品目录廉价但噪声严重（包装图混入、同名条目视觉不可分）。此前人们常将"清洗/合并目录"视作扩充模型的替代方案，作者要检验这一比较是否成立，即提升到底来自识别器改进，还是来自"正确答案"定义的改变。

Method: 1,824个查询命名条目、165,769张奶酪图像；经包装过滤与近重复去除得到998类识别集，固定group-aware划分。将条目规范化到基础类型（844类），比较：模型不变但按组内概率求和映射到合并标签空间，与在合并目录上重训（28/50/88M参数，6个随机种子）；并做随机合并对照与三种骨干对比，分解增益来源（类型粗化 vs 别名归并），检验合并与模型规模的交互。

Result: 规范化标签使top-1提升4.0点；不重训直接对合并标签空间概率求和即得+4.27点（6种子+4.25）。重训仅带来-0.10±0.53、+0.08±0.65、-0.10±0.65点，各容量下与零无差异。随机同规模合并仅+0.68点，三骨干差异<0.1点，说明该效应具特异性。37个合并组中，类型粗化贡献94%增益，别名归并仅7%。骨干参数三倍化在原始目录+1.50±0.57、规范化目录+1.42±0.44，组内交互-0.08 [-0.79,+0.63]，无交互。

Conclusion: 所谓"目录整合增益"主要反映评估标签空间的变化，而非模型改进；分离这一因素仅需一次评估。整合不能替代模型扩容，二者不构成可比方案。

Abstract: Retail product catalogs assembled from web image search are cheap to build and pervasively noisy: queries return packaging shots instead of products, and entries proliferate under names that cannot be distinguished visually. Consolidating such a catalog can look like an alternative to scaling the model. We show this is not a comparison between two ways of improving a recognizer, but between improving a recognizer and changing what counts as correct. We study a public archive of 165,769 cheese images across 1,824 query-named entries. A packaging filter and near-duplicate removal reduce it to a 998-class recognition set on one fixed, group-aware split. Canonicalizing entries to their base type (844 classes) raises top-1 accuracy by 4.0 points. Scoring the unchanged model against the merged label space, by summing its predicted probability within each canonical group, recovers +4.27 points with no retraining (+4.25 over six seeds). Retraining on the consolidated catalog then adds -0.10$\pm$0.53, +0.08$\pm$0.65 and -0.10$\pm$0.65 points at 28, 50 and 88 M parameters, indistinguishable from zero at every capacity ($\pm$ is the across-seed standard deviation). That component is specific, not mechanical: random merges with the identical size profile yield only +0.68 points, and the three backbones agree to within 0.1 points. But it is overwhelmingly category coarsening, not alias correction: across the 37 merged groups, type coarsenings supply 94% of the gain and alias collapses only 7%. We also detect no interaction between consolidation and backbone size: tripling backbone parameters is worth +1.50$\pm$0.57 points on the raw catalog and +1.42$\pm$00.44 on the canonical one, a within-seed interaction of -0.08 [-0.79,+0.63]. On this archive, the reported consolidation gain primarily reflects the evaluation label space, and separating that out costs one evaluation

</details>


### [11] [JEPAhead: From Inpainting to Causal Forecasting for Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08657)
*Daniel G. P. Petrini,Davi Pincinato,Fabio Grassiotto,Pedro L. F. Prado,Alexandre F. S. Osorio,Raphael V. Ramos,Felipe Pamboukian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将冻结编码器的V-JEPA预测器由双向潜空间修补微调为"无动作因果预测"，单次前向预测未来16帧（8组token）；CARLA Town12检索准确率69.0%（持续基线55.3%），语义与检索增益显著，但像素级LPIPS改善有限、下游实用性未验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索潜空间（latent）预测在驾驶感知中的可行性：避免像素级重建的高成本与低语义相关性，检验预训练V-JEPA预测器能否经微调承担纯观测驱动的因果式未来预测任务。

Method: 保持编码器冻结，将V-JEPA1预测器从双向潜变量inpainting任务微调为无动作（action-free）因果预测：采用连续历史观测、与预测视界对齐的目标、组级因果注意力（group-level causal attention），一次前向输出8个未来token组（对应16帧）。评估包括CARLA Town12上的未来检索（591个候选）、车辆条件检索、固定线性探针语义评估（mIoU）、像素L1与LPIPS，并设oracle重建参照；与持续基线（persistence）及参数量少6倍的从零训练紧凑预测器对比。

Result: 591候选检索准确率69.0% vs 持续基线55.3%，长视界与变化更大的token上增益更大；优于参数量仅1/6的从零训练预测器。真实编码器token上训练的固定线性探针在语义类别变化patch上达0.16 mIoU（持续基线0.13）；车辆条件检索在最长远视界下较持续基线提升15个百分点。像素空间仅见小幅L1改善，LPIPS几乎无提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明冻结编码器下的潜空间因果预测能携带驾驶相关语义信息，支持继续研究潜空间预测用于驾驶感知；但像素级收益有限、下游任务效用尚未验证，需以潜空间与语义指标结合oracle重建参考进行评估。

Abstract: We fine-tune a pre-trained V-JEPA1 predictor from bidirectional latent inpainting to action-free causal forecasting while keeping the encoder frozen. Using contiguous past observations, horizon-aligned targets, and group-level causal attention, the model forecasts eight future token groups, corresponding to sixteen frames, in one forward pass. On CARLA Town12, it retrieves the correct future among 591 candidates in 69.0% of cases, compared with 55.3% for persistence, with greater gains at longer horizons and on tokens exhibiting greater change. It also outperforms a compact predictor trained from scratch with six times fewer parameters. Semantic analysis indicates driving-relevant information in predicted features: a fixed linear probe trained on real encoder tokens achieves 0.16 mIoU on patches undergoing semantic-class changes, versus 0.13 for persistence. Vehicle-conditioned retrieval improves over persistence by 15 percentage points at the longest horizon. Pixel-space evaluation shows modest L1 gains but little improvement under LPIPS, motivating direct evaluation through latent and semantic measures alongside an oracle reconstruction reference. These findings support further investigation of latent forecasting for driving perception, while downstream utility remains unverified.

</details>


### [12] [eSharp: Diffusion-based Sharp Monocular Metric Event-to-Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08731)
*Qiushi Dai,Aniket Dashpute,Yuhao Liu,Rishubh Parihar,Guha Balakrishnan,Ashok Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出eSharp：首个复用预训练扩散先验的事件相机单目深度估计（MDE）框架，通过跨模态蒸馏用帧相机稠密伪标签训练，在真实数据上取得高精度、高锐度的度量深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计在功耗与体积受限场景中被广泛使用，但传统基于帧的MDE在快速运动和高动态范围场景下失效；事件相机可缓解此类问题，然而事件数据集仅有稀疏LiDAR深度监督，难以训练稠密预测模型。

Method: 1）将预训练扩散模型的图像先验迁移到纯事件输入的MDE任务，输出度量深度；2）采用跨模态蒸馏：利用与事件相机空间配准的帧相机，生成经LiDAR标定的稠密伪标签作为监督信号，弥补稀疏LiDAR真值不足。

Result: 在真实数据实验中，eSharp在度量深度精度与空间保真度（锐度）两方面均优于既有事件到深度基线方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可作为事件驱动3D感知的有力基础模型，事件相机结合扩散先验是应对高速运动与高动态范围MDE的有效路线。

Abstract: Depth perception is fundamental for autonomous navigation, industrial inspection, and mixed-reality systems. It often operates under strict constraints on power and form factor. In such cases, monocular depth estimation (MDE) is often used. Even as recent advances continue to improve monocular depth quality, conventional frame-based MDE systems remain challenged by fast motion and high-dynamic-range scenes. This work introduces eSharp, an MDE framework that addresses these problems using an event camera. eSharp repurposes pretrained diffusion priors for event-only MDE and produces accurate metric depth with unparalleled sharpness on real data. Real-world event datasets provide only sparse LiDAR depth maps for ground-truth supervision, making dense prediction challenging. We address this limitation through cross-modal distillation, using LiDAR-calibrated dense pseudo-labels generated from a spatially co-registered frame camera to train eSharp. Experiments show that eSharp outperforms prior event-to-depth baselines in both metric-depth accuracy and spatial fidelity, establishing diffusion models as a promising foundation for event-driven 3D perception.

</details>


### [13] [FishSense Lite: A Camera-Based, Single Laser Hardware Framework with Software System for In Situ Fish Length Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08732)
*Christopher L. Crutchfield,Kyle Hu,Nathan Hui,Omair Qazi,Jennifer Loch,Alli Candelmo,Brice X. Semmens,Curt Schurgers,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FishSense Lite 利用休闲潜水相机、潜用激光笔和3D打印支架，从单张水下照片中恢复鱼体长度，并通过公民科学计划在野外验证了其精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼类资源评估需要大量地点上的鱼体长度与物种配对数据，远超受训调查员所能覆盖的范围。休闲潜水员已遍及这些地点，但水下测鱼此前依赖受训观察员或专业设备。

Method: 系统由一个休闲潜水相机、一个准直激光笔和一个3D打印支架组成。潜水员将激光点对准鱼并拍照；图像中激光点的位置给出相机到鱼的距离，结合该距离将鱼在图像中的像素尺寸换算为实际长度。系统开发者培训了一个公民科学项目，由其自行培训潜水员并采集数据。

Result: 在七次开放水域潜水中，采集了73条野生鱼的物种-长度配对数据。对同一批七条野生鱼，与水下立体成像系统的测量结果无显著偏差（平均差+0.8%）。对已知长度的刚性目标，全部目标恢复误差在1.3%以内，1,055帧中99%落在15%误差预算内。

Conclusion: 基于所测量的误差来源，作者提出野外操作规范：每次潜水都用潜水记录板做标定；在与鱼体垂直方向15度以内且距离超过1米的条件下拍摄；每条鱼至少拍摄十帧。

Abstract: Fish stock assessments need fish lengths paired with species from far more places than trained surveyors can reach. Recreational divers already visit many of those places, but measuring a fish underwater has required trained observers or specialist equipment. We present FishSense Lite, which recovers fish length from a single photograph taken with a recreational dive camera, a dive laser pointer, and a 3D-printed mount. The diver aims the laser at a fish and takes a picture; afterward, the dot's position in the image gives the range to the fish, and the range turns the fish's size in the image into a length. A citizen-science program, trained by the system's builders, taught its own divers and collected lengths paired with species for 73 wild fish across seven open-water dives. On seven wild fish also measured by an underwater stereo-imaging system, the two instruments agreed without detectable bias (mean difference +0.8%). On rigid targets of known length, FishSense Lite recovered every target to within 1.3%, and 99% of 1,055 frames fell within a 15% error budget. From the error sources we measure, we derive a field protocol: calibrate against a dive slate on every dive, photograph fish within 15 degrees of perpendicular and beyond 1 m, and take at least ten frames of each.

</details>


### [14] [AtariBench: Evaluating Spatiotemporal Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in Atari Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08755)
*Mingjia Huo,Yao Fu,Bo Chang,Yaqing Wang,Dawei Zhu,Dylan Zhang,Xinyang Yi,Lichan Hong,Ed H. Chi,Hao Zhang,Jiaxi Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AtariBench基准，用20款Atari 2600游戏评估VLM在动态环境中的序列决策能力，揭示其被动感知与主动行动之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在静态视觉理解基准上表现优异，但其在动态环境中的行动能力尚不清楚；已有评测难以区分模型感知速度与实时推理速度的限制，缺乏对快速视觉状态变化感知、时空推理与动作落地的严格检验。

Method: 构建受控基准AtariBench：覆盖20款Atari 2600游戏，要求模型基于高时间分辨率游戏画面流进行序列决策；关键设计是将游戏内快速动态与wall-clock推理速度解耦，从而专注于模型能否感知快速视觉状态变化、进行时空动态推理并将动作固着于当前观测。另引入可分性分析（separability analysis），识别哪些游戏内得分可作为模型能力的可靠信号，并用该基准研究记忆设计与提示策略对性能的影响。

Result: 实验显示当前最优VLM存在显著局限：常无法推断物体运动与方向，序列决策脆弱，且过度依赖文本交互历史，即使新追加了视觉反馈；可分性分析给出了不同游戏得分的可靠性区分，记忆设计与提示策略对性能有可测影响。

Conclusion: AtariBench暴露了被动视觉感知与主动决策之间持续存在的差距，为开发能在动态环境中推理并行动的VLM提供了轻量且严谨的测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on static visual understanding benchmarks, yet their ability to act in dynamic environments remains less well understood. We introduce AtariBench, a controlled benchmark that evaluates VLMs across 20 Atari 2600 games, requiring models to make sequential decisions from high-temporal-resolution gameplay streams. By decoupling fast in-game dynamics from wall-clock inference speed, AtariBench focuses on whether models can perceive rapid visual state changes, reason over spatiotemporal dynamics, and ground actions in the current observation. Our experiments reveal substantial limitations in state-of-the-art VLMs: models often fail to infer object motion and direction, exhibit brittle sequential decision-making, and over-rely on textual interaction history despite newly appended visual feedback. We further introduce a separability analysis to identify which in-game scores provide reliable signals of model competence, and use the benchmark to study how memory design and prompting strategies affect performance. Overall, AtariBench exposes a persistent gap between passive visual perception and active decision-making, providing a lightweight and rigorous testbed for developing VLMs that can reason and act in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [15] [Federated training performs as well as centralized for 3D Latent Diffusion for Diagnosis-Conditioned Brain MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08771)
*Abhijith Shaji,Paul Kurian,Greg Ver Steeg,Paul M. Thompson,Jose-Luis Ambite*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文验证了3D条件潜变量生成模型在联邦学习（数据不出站点）下训练，其条件保真度、多样性与真实感与集中式训练相当，证明联邦训练可作为构建多站点脑部MRI生成模型的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑部MRI生成模型有助于理解脑部病变并缓解神经影像数据的稀缺与不平衡，但训练此类模型需要大规模、多站点数据集，而数据共享受隐私与合规限制。作者因此希望检验联邦学习能否在数据保留于各站点的前提下达到集中式训练的效果。

Method: 采用3D条件潜变量生成模型，分别在联邦学习设置（数据本地私有）与集中式设置（数据汇集至单一站点）下训练，并从三个维度进行评估：条件保真度（生成MRI在分类任务中的表现）、多样性（用MultiScale-SSIM衡量生成图像与真实数据分布的匹配程度）、真实感（用Fréchet Inception Distance衡量生成图像与真实图像的接近程度）。

Result: 联邦训练的3D条件潜变量模型在三项指标上的表现与集中式训练相似，即在条件保真度、多样性和真实感上均未出现明显退化。

Conclusion: 联邦训练是开发多站点脑部MRI生成模型的可行途径，可在不共享原始数据的情况下获得与集中式训练相近的生成质量，为隐私受限场景下的多中心神经影像生成建模提供了支持。

Abstract: Generative models of brain MRI are useful to understand brain conditions, and to enrich scarce and imbalanced neuroimaging data. However, training these models requires large, multi-site training sets, which is challenging. Federated learning (FL) allows for training deep learning models without sharing data. We show that training a 3D conditional latent model federatedly (where data is kept private at each site) performs similarly to centralized training (where all the data have been copied to a single site). We evaluate three metrics: conditional fidelity (how well generated MRIs perform in classification), diversity (how the generated images match the real data distribution, using MultiScale-SSIM), and realism (how close generated images are to real images, using Fréchet Inception Distance). Our results show that federated training is a viable route to develop multi-site generative models of brain MRI.

</details>


### [16] [Extrapolation Matters: Sparse-Only Depth Completion Beyond LiDAR Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08782)
*Maximilian Jansen,Gasser Elazab,Praveen Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究仅用单帧稀疏LiDAR监督的"超出覆盖范围"深度补全，基于冻结的单目深度基础模型+轻量逐帧对齐+细化模块，并提出高频法向一致性损失与分离覆盖/超覆盖区域的评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实相机-LiDAR系统中传感器部分重叠、遮挡与有限视场导致大量图像区域无或弱LiDAR观测；现有深度补全工作对"超出LiDAR覆盖"的外推场景评估不足，且通常依赖稠密深度标签、LiDAR累积或伪LiDAR。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的单目深度基础模型提供先验；2) 轻量级逐帧对齐将先验与稀疏LiDAR几何对齐；3) 细化模块融合对齐先验与稀疏LiDAR锚点，输出稠密米制深度；4) 在原始单帧稀疏监督下引入高频法向一致性损失——定义为逐像素法向余弦差异在图像空间的梯度，惩罚标量角度误差的空间变化，而非直接匹配相邻法向向量变化。

Result: 在LiDAR覆盖范围之外取得较强的深度精度，同时在LiDAR覆盖区域内保持有竞争力的性能；所提评测协议揭示了传统深度补全指标所掩盖的外推行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为在多传感器系统中部署单目基础先验提供了可行途径，并强调了超覆盖外推这一此前被低估的深度补全问题及其评估方式的重要性。

Abstract: Extrapolation beyond LiDAR coverage is an important yet under-evaluated challenge of depth completion. In real camera--LiDAR systems, partial sensor overlap, occlusions, and limited field coverage often leave large image regions weakly observed or completely unobserved by LiDAR. Thus, we investigate depth completion in this beyond-coverage regime using only sparse supervision from raw single-sweep LiDAR, without dense depth labels, LiDAR accumulation, or pseudo-LiDAR generation. Our approach leverages a frozen monocular depth foundation model, lightweight per-frame alignment to sparse LiDAR geometry, and a refinement module that fuses the aligned prior with sparse LiDAR anchors to predict dense metric depth. To regularize learning under raw sparse supervision, we introduce a high-frequency normal-consistency loss defined as the image-space gradient of the per-pixel normal cosine discrepancy. This term penalizes spatial variation in scalar angular error; it does not directly match changes in neighboring normal vectors. We further introduce an evaluation protocol that explicitly separates LiDAR-supported and beyond-coverage regions, revealing extrapolative behavior that conventional depth-completion metrics can obscure. Our method achieves strong depth accuracy beyond LiDAR coverage while remaining competitive in LiDAR-covered regions, providing a practical way to deploy monocular foundation priors in multi-sensor systems.

</details>


### [17] [Scale as a Modality: Multi-Scale Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08802)
*Ruibo Chen,Kaishen Wang,Tianyi Xiong,Chenxi Liu,Yanshuo Chen,Heng Huang,Jian Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多尺度像素空间扩散Transformer（MS-pDiT），将空间尺度视为一种模态，通过多尺度 tokenization 与双向跨尺度联合注意力实现新的架构缩放维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在扩散模型依赖预训练自编码器，带来有损压缩并限制空间粒度；像素空间扩散虽可灵活多尺度 tokenization，但现有方法多为两级粗到细结构，跨尺度交互有限。

Method: 对同一噪声图像以多种 patch size 分别 tokenize，每个尺度由专用 transformer 流处理，并通过压缩式 MM-DiT 风格联合注意力实现双向信息交换，且注意力开销与最细 patch size 无关。

Result: ImageNet 256×256 上尺度数由 2 增至 5，FID 从 4.39 依次降至 2.31、2.26、2.19；同等参数预算下增加中间尺度优于增加深度。最终在 256×256 与 512×512 上 FID 分别为 1.43 和 1.44，文生图 GenEval 0.86、DPG-Bench 83.9。

Conclusion: 尺度数量可作为新的架构缩放维度；双向跨尺度交互与细粒度流的延迟引入均具关键作用，且该方法可自然扩展至文生图任务。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models rely on pretrained autoencoders that introduce lossy compression and constrain spatial granularity. Pixel-space diffusion avoids this bottleneck and enables flexible tokenization at multiple spatial scales, yet existing approaches largely adopt two-level coarse-to-fine architectures with limited cross-scale interaction. We introduce MS-pDiT, a multi-scale pixel-space diffusion transformer that treats spatial scale as a modality. MS-pDiT tokenizes the same noisy image at multiple patch sizes, processes each scale in a dedicated transformer stream, and enables bidirectional information exchange through compacted MM-DiT-style joint attention, with attention cost independent of the finest patch size. This design establishes the number of scales as a new architectural scaling dimension. On ImageNet $256\times256$, increasing the number of scales from two to five consistently improves FID from 4.39 to 2.31, 2.26, and 2.19, while adding an intermediate scale substantially outperforms increasing depth under comparable parameter budgets. Further analysis highlights the importance of bidirectional cross-scale interaction and late introduction of fine-grained streams. MS-pDiT achieves FID scores of 1.43 and 1.44 on ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$, respectively, and naturally extends to text-to-image generation, achieving 0.86 on GenEval and 83.9 on DPG-Bench.

</details>


### [18] [Impact of color in computer and human-based object recognition under occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08808)
*Courtney M. King,Elissa Aminoff,Daniel D. Leeds*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究"能产生伪装效果的部分遮挡"对人类与计算机视觉模型识别的影响，并提出NOP、NOR、AIO三项新指标解释模型在遮挡下的行为与表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分遮挡对识别的挑战取决于遮挡属性与模型设计；颜色属性在生物识别中极为关键且常被用于伪装，但其有效性来源及其在CV中的迁移尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对"伪装型遮挡"的系统比较与可解释性度量。

Method: 生成两类伪装遮挡（与目标物体混同的object-based、与场景混同的scene-based），在人类被试与从CNN到Transformer的多类模型上评测识别性能；并设计三项指标：non-occlusion precision (NOP)与non-occlusion recall (NOR)衡量有无遮挡时激活的对齐程度，activation in occlusion (AIO)衡量落在遮挡区域内的激活比例；另用生成伪装遮挡做训练增强。

Result: 人类识别与多数Transformer受生成伪装遮挡负面影响，scene-based伪装比object-based更严重；经典CNN呈相反趋势，在中性色遮挡下表现最差，说明与上下文相关的颜色（尤其场景相关）反而有利于识别而非破坏形状/边界线索；对选定CNN而言，用生成伪装遮挡做训练增强可提升对未见伪装的鲁棒性，遮挡指标揭示了学习机制的趋势。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人机视觉在伪装遮挡下的表征差异与模型脆弱性，对人与计算机视觉的对齐、模型鲁棒性及可解释性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Partial occlusions challenge object recognition in both human and computer vision (CV), but the impact is largely dependent on the occlusion and model design. Properties relating to color are particularly important in biological recognition and frequently leveraged for camouflage, however there are still open questions about what makes them effective and how they translate to CV. This work studies partial occlusions that create camouflage, including those that blend in with target objects or with scenes, which alter object representations in distinct ways. Experiments evaluate recognition performance on both human participants and models ranging from convolutional neural networks (CNNs) to transformers, and additional analysis is performed on models to uncover trends in processing and object representations under multiple occlusion types. Novel metrics offer explainability of model behavior under occlusion: non-occlusion precision (NOP) and non-occlusion recall (NOR) measure alignment of model activations with and without occlusion, and activation in occlusion (AIO) measures the percentage of activations that occur in occluded regions. Findings show that human recognition, and most transformers, are negatively impacted by generated camouflaging occluders, with scene-based camouflage performing worse than object-based. Classical CNNs show a strong opposite trend, performing the worst on neutral-colored occlusions, suggesting that contextually-related colors, particularly scene-based, benefit object recognition rather than disrupting shape or boundary cues. Additionally, generated camouflaging occluders for training augmentation improves robustness to unseen camouflage for selected CNN model, and occlusion metrics suggest trends about learned mechanisms. Findings have interesting implications for alignment in human and computer vision, model vulnerabilities, and explainability.

</details>


### [19] [The APL Doesn't Fall Far From The Tree: Unsupervised Artefact Prototype Learning in Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08838)
*Emilia Agasi,Charlie Gourley,C. Simon Herrington,Robert L. Hollis,T. Ian Simpson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督的伪影原型学习（APL）方法，利用预训练嵌入空间的几何结构检测全切片图像中的技术伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型能表征组织形态，但全切片图像中的技术伪影引入噪声，可能影响诊断准确性；现有检测方法依赖人工标注与微调，成本高。

Method: APL 不将伪影视为孤立离群点，而是在冻结的预训练嵌入空间中计算分布外（OOD）图块的原型，用这些原型区分伪影相关模式与组织，全程无需人工标签、无需微调。

Result: 单中心数据集评估显示，APL 在模糊、暗斑、切片边缘、笔迹等伪影检测上优于 GrandQC 与 HistoART，但在组织褶皱与血液的检测上表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 冻结的预训练嵌入已蕴含可用于伪影检测的信息，无需昂贵标注或大规模算力即可实现有效检测。

Abstract: Histopathology foundation models provide rich representations of tissue morphology, but technical artefacts in whole slide images introduce noise that may impact diagnostic accuracy. We introduce Artefact Prototype Learning (APL), a novel approach to detecting these artefacts that exploits the geometry of pre-trained embedding spaces without manual labels or fine-tuning. Rather than treating artefacts as isolated outliers, APL computes prototypes of out-of-distribution patches and uses them to distinguish artefact-associated patterns from tissue. Evaluation on a single-centre dataset shows that APL outperforms prior methods GrandQC and HistoART in detecting artefacts such as blur, dark spots, slide edges and pen marks, but struggles with tissue folds and blood. These results show that frozen pre-trained embeddings already carry information that allows artefact detection without expensive labels or vast computational resources.

</details>


### [20] [Align3D: Image-Structure Co-Aligned 3D Scene Generation with Observability-Aware Fidelity-Preserving Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08861)
*Chenliang Zhou,Yifan Wang,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align3D：以单张照片为条件的扩散模型，生成既忠于输入图像又在视野外物理合理的3D室内布局；核心是置信度校准的场景图与图像-结构协同对齐的去噪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图3D室内布局重建面临双重矛盾：一方面生成结果需忠实匹配输入照片，另一方面视野外区域缺乏观测，需依靠物理先验补全，而现有方法难以在两者间取得平衡，容易为满足图像约束而牺牲场景质量（如物体穿插、悬浮），或因过度依赖先验而偏离图像。

Method: 1）从单张照片推断置信度校准的场景图（confidence-calibrated scene graph）；2）训练集合扩散（set-diffusion）布局去噪器，引入图像-结构协同对齐（image-structure co-alignment）：a）置信度加权模态融合，利用预测置信度平衡图像对齐与场景图对齐；b）可观测性感知的保真引导（observability-aware fidelity-preserving guidance），将冲突的场景质量梯度投影，并用物体级置信度掩码重加权，使欠观测自由度吸收物理先验而不破坏图像-图匹配。

Result: 在3D-FRONT数据集上，Align3D在图像对齐度与场景质量指标上均优于近期基线；训练完成后可单次前馈将未见过的照片映射为布局。

Conclusion: 通过置信度加权融合与可观测性感知引导，Align3D有效协调了图像保真与物理合理性，实现高效的单视图3D室内布局生成。

Abstract: We present Align3D, an image-conditioned diffusion model that generates 3D indoor layouts that are both faithful to a single input photograph and physically plausible beyond the observed view. From the photograph we infer a confidence-calibrated scene graph and train a set-diffusion layout denoiser with image-structure co-alignment where confidence-weighted modality fusion balances image and scene-graph alignment using predicted confidences, while observability-aware fidelity-preserving guidance projects conflicting scene-quality gradients and reweighs them with an object-level confidence mask so under-observed degrees of freedom absorb physical priors without undoing the image-graph match. On 3D-FRONT, Align3D improves image-alignment and scene-quality metrics over recent baselines; once trained, it maps previously unseen photographs to layouts in a single feed-forward pass.

</details>


### [21] [GeoJeV: Unified Spatial Probability for Training-Free Efficient Evidence Acquisition in Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08879)
*Xinyi Niu,Lingfeng Chen,Jiehua Zhang,Panhe Hu,Li Liu,Zhen Liu,Yongxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法GeoJeV，将UHR遥感影像的证据获取建模为空间概率驱动的决策过程，在三个基准上提升Qwen3-VL-8B平均精度+5.32pp，且每题仅增加1.12秒并实现7.8倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高分辨率遥感影像细节丰富但多模态理解困难；现有证据获取方法依赖生成式MLLM进行中间搜索控制，而RLHF只对齐人类偏好响应、未显式优化区域级相关性判断，且重复自回归解码带来额外计算开销。

Method: GeoJeV为免训练方法：在每个被评估区域，由冻结的JeV模型输出候选区域上的概率分布；通过Region Rank对候选区域排序、Sparse Branch依稀疏概率支持自适应确定分支宽度、Depth Gate依分支稀疏度与分布集中度控制细化深度，三者由同一分布协调搜索优先级、分支宽度与细化深度。

Result: 在LRS-GRO、MME-RealWorld-RS、LRS-VQA上分别达到60.59%、52.79%、34.32%准确率；三基准平均较Qwen3-VL-8B提升+5.32pp，每题仅多耗1.12秒，相比近期方法提速7.8倍。

Conclusion: 以概率驱动的统一决策替代RLHF对齐的生成式搜索控制，可在免训练条件下同时获得精度提升与显著推理效率优势，验证了空间概率信号用于UHR遥感证据获取的有效性。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing imagery provides rich spatial details but poses challenges for accurate and efficient multimodal understanding. Existing evidence acquisition methods selectively explore relevant image regions, yet largely rely on generative multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for intermediate search control. However, reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) primarily aligns these models with human-preferred responses rather than explicitly optimizing region-level relevance judgments, while repeated autoregressive decoding introduces additional computational overhead. To address these limitations, we propose GeoJeV, a training-free method that formulates evidence acquisition as spatial probability-driven decision making. At each evaluated region, a frozen JeV model produces a probability distribution over candidate regions. Specifically, Region Rank prioritizes candidate regions, Sparse Branch adaptively determines branching width through sparse probability support, and Depth Gate controls refinement depth based on branch sparsity and distribution concentration. By coordinating search priority, branching width, and refinement depth through a shared distribution, GeoJeV reduces reliance on RLHF-aligned generation and eliminates intermediate decoding. Experiments on LRS-GRO, MME-RealWorld-RS, and LRS-VQA achieve accuracies of 60.59%, 52.79%, and 34.32%, respectively. Across the three benchmarks, GeoJeV improves the average accuracy of Qwen3-VL-8B by +5.32pp with only 1.12 additional seconds per question, while achieving a 7.8-fold inference speedup over the recent method.

</details>


### [22] [Computer Vision by YOLOs for Detection of Major Diseases in Vietnamese Custard Apple Using Fruit Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08886)
*Tri Nhut Do,Quoc Tuan Vo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅提供了一句背景性陈述，缺少研究目标、方法、结果与结论，无法构成可分析的完整摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出越南农业数字化转型加速，将人工智能用于作物病虫害的识别、监测与预测已成为必然趋势，这一现实需求构成了研究的动机背景。

Method: 摘要中未提及任何方法信息，包括数据来源、模型架构、实验设置或评估指标均缺失。

Result: 摘要中未报告任何结果、定量指标或对比实验数据。

Conclusion: 摘要中未给出结论。现有文本仅完成"问题背景—趋势判断"的铺垫，属于引言性表述而非研究结论。

Abstract: Amid Vietnam rapid digital transformation in agriculture, the integration of artificial intelligence technologies for identifying, monitoring, and predicting crop pests and diseases has emerged as an inevitable trend.

</details>


### [23] [Decoding spatial and semantic information from large-scale visual cortical activity for natural-movie reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08887)
*Yukai Huang,Haoyu Albert Wang,Jiawei Zhang,Jialun Ma,Jiayao Ding,Yiran Lin,Xingyu Chen,Ziqi Gao,Yuguo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ca2REAL框架，用钙成像群体活动通过布局与语义双通路条件化预训练视频扩散模型，实现自然动态视觉场景的高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从皮层群体活动重建自然视觉体验是神经解码的核心难题，钙成像信号噪声大、时间滤波强，且配对的神经-视频数据稀少，现有确定性解码方法难以生成高保真动态场景。

Method: 构建神经到视频的生成式框架Ca2REAL：从钙活动中解耦出布局控制与语义控制，分别条件化预训练视频扩散模型；在单只小鼠7,863个V1神经元的实测响应上评估，并构建由11个独立数据集编码模型预测响应组成的116,705神经元刺激对齐虚拟群体进行验证；辅以扰动与消融分析及时间动态分析。

Result: 在留出自然电影片段上达到81.4%全配对刺激识别准确率，在感知与语义指标上相较确定性基线提升最明显；重建依赖分布式的、刺激特异的群体结构，布局与语义通路提供互补的空间与身份信息；重建片段对语义动态的时间保持性亦有改善。

Conclusion: 大规模皮层群体活动约束了自然视觉体验的空间、语义与时间结构，Ca2REAL为动态视觉场景的高保真神经重建提供了生成式框架。

Abstract: Reconstructing natural visual experience from cortical population activity is a central challenge in neural decoding, particularly for calcium imaging, where signals are noisy, temporally filtered, and paired neural-video data are limited. Here we present Ca2REAL (Calcium Activity to Reconstruct Events, Appearance, and Layout), a neural-to-video framework that conditions a pretrained video diffusion model through separate layout and semantic controls derived from calcium activity. Using measured responses from 7,863 V1 neurons in one mouse, Ca2REAL reconstructed held-out natural-movie clips with 81.4% all-pairs stimulus-identification accuracy, with its clearest gains over deterministic baselines in perceptual and semantic measures. We further evaluated the framework in a stimulus-aligned virtual population of 116,705 neurons assembled from encoding-model-predicted responses across 11 independently recorded datasets. Perturbation and ablation analyses showed that reconstruction depends on distributed, stimulus-specific population structure, with layout and semantic pathways providing complementary spatial and identity information. Temporal analyses further showed improved preservation of semantic dynamics across reconstructed clips. These results show that large-scale cortical population activity constrains the spatial, semantic, and temporal structure of natural visual experience, providing a generative framework for high-fidelity neural reconstruction of dynamic visual scenes.

</details>


### [24] [MotionCore: A Latent Neural Simulator for Video Generation with Rigid-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08904)
*Zheng Wang,Zhengwei Gong,Yuanzhi Liang,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionCore 通过学习式 PDE 求解器显式建模刚体物理动力学，并将其预测转化为扩散生成模型的空间对齐隐式控制，从而提升视频生成的物理一致性与轨迹遵循度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能产出高度逼真画面，但物体运动常违背物理规律，说明仅靠视觉建模难以获得可迁移的物理动力学表征。论文因此主张将物理动力学从外观渲染中解耦，显式学习动力学并对生成过程施加控制。

Method: 提出 MotionCore 框架：1）引入可学习偏微分方程（PDE）求解器，聚焦刚体运动；2）构建隐空间神经模拟器，从观测运动中预测物体级物理状态的演化；3）将预测结果转换为空间对齐的隐式控制，注入可控视频扩散模型；4）生成器仅作视觉接口负责外观与场景细节，动力学模型负责物理轨迹；5）训练上结合 diffusion-matching 目标与对物体身份、位置的低容量可解码性约束，以保证轨迹在接口中不被破坏。

Result: 在公开物理一致性基准及已知轨迹的配对数据上评估：MotionCore 在受测刚体场景中提升了物理一致性与轨迹遵循度，同时保持可比的视觉质量；并通过受控消融协议将学习动力学与解析先验进行对比，分析了隐式演化处理器（latent evolution processor）的选择。

Conclusion: 将显式学习的物理动力学与扩散生成器解耦并通过隐式控制耦合，是提升视频生成物理合理性的可行路径；学习式动力学在刚体设定下优于纯视觉建模及解析先验。

Abstract: Video generation models can produce highly realistic videos while generating physically inconsistent object motion. This limitation suggests that visual modeling alone may not provide a transferable representation of physical dynamics. We introduce MotionCore, a framework for learning physical dynamics via a learnable partial differential equation solver and focuses on rigid-body motion. We introduce a latent neural simulator learning to predict the evolution of object-level physical states from observed motion. Its predictions are then converted into spatially aligned latent controls for a controllable video diffusion model. The generator serves as a visual interface: it provides appearance and scene detail, while the learned dynamics model specifies the physical trajectory. To preserve this trajectory through the interface, MotionCore combines a diffusion-matching objective with a low-capacity decodability constraint on object identity and position. We evaluate MotionCore on public physical-consistency benchmarks and on paired data with known trajectories. Results indicate that MotionCore improves physical consistency and trajectory obedience in the evaluated rigid-body settings, while maintaining comparable visual quality. A controlled ablation protocol compares learned dynamics with an analytic prior and studies the choice of latent evolution processor.

</details>


### [25] [FANO: Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization for Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08923)
*Shilong Han,Hongxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FANO方法：在退火式解耦求解中固定上一步去噪估计为锚点、只优化已知分布的前向噪声e而非x_t，并用极坐标半径软势约束，抑制径向漂移，非线性逆问题上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型已成为图像逆问题的零样本先验，退火解耦类方法在非线性任务上重建性能领先。但作者发现这类方法直接优化去噪器输入x_t时，在后期层出现"径向漂移"：层内优化使x_t的噪声分量范数偏离其典型值，把去噪器推出高性能区间，导致重建质量下降。

Method: FANO（Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization）：每个退火层将上一步去噪估计固定为锚点，仅优化前向噪声e而非x_t，从而保持去噪器输入的前向扩散结构；由于e服从标准高斯先验、其范数聚集在典型值附近，进一步将e在极坐标下分解为方向与半径幅值，对半径施加软势（soft potential）进行正则约束，使x_t保持在标准正态前向分布的高密度区域。

Result: 在多个线性与非线性逆问题上验证：线性任务上FANO与当前最强基线持平，非线性任务上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: x_t直接优化的径向漂移是退火式解耦方法的关键瓶颈；改以锚点+前向噪声参数化并在半径上加软势约束可有效保持去噪器处于高密度工作区，是一种即插即用、兼容现有退火框架的改进方案。

Abstract: Pre-trained diffusion models have become a widely adopted zero-shot prior for image inverse problems, among which annealed decoupling methods achieve leading reconstruction performance on nonlinear tasks. We find, however, that annealed methods that directly optimize the denoiser input $x_t$ exhibit radial drift in later layers: within-layer optimization drives the noise-component norm of $x_t$ away from its typical value, pushing the denoiser out of its high-performance regime and degrading reconstruction quality. To address this, we propose FANO (Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization). At each annealing layer, FANO fixes the previous denoiser estimate as an anchor and optimizes only the forward noise $e$, whose distribution is known, instead of $x_t$, thereby preserving the forward-diffusion structure of the denoiser input. The standard Gaussian prior on $e$ concentrates its norm near the typical value, and deviations degrade denoiser performance. We accordingly decompose $e$ in polar coordinates into a direction and a radial magnitude, and apply a soft potential to regulate the radius, keeping $x_t$ in the high-density region of the forward distribution. On multiple linear and nonlinear inverse problems, FANO matches the strongest current baselines on linear tasks and achieves consistent improvements on nonlinear tasks.

</details>


### [26] [UAV-Based Computer Vision for Cattle Detection and Feed Bunk Spatial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08928)
*Pedro Oliveira Torrente,Diego Giaretta de Paulo,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Jianglong Yan,Everton Castelão Tetila,Jayme Garcia Arnal Barbedo,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于无人机与计算机视觉的框架，量化饲养场牛只采食栏处对齐与空间分布，为精准畜牧提供可扩展指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西肉牛产业面临效率、可持续与动物福利压力；采食管理对生产性能关键，但评估仍依赖主观、劳动密集、难扩展且易有偏差的人工目视检查，亟需客观可扩展的量化手段。

Method: 在巴西南马托格罗索州商业饲养场用无人机采集图像，人工标注牛头与牛背两类；比较YOLOv8与YOLOv11（下游选用YOLOv8s）；用SAM分割采食栏区域；基于检测到的头部位置构建几何指标，刻画对齐、间距规律性与聚类。

Result: 检测器达84.30% mAP50、35.50% mAP50-95，RTX 5060 Ti上约2.7 ms/图；指标可区分规则与不规则分布：RMSD 1.06 m vs 4.26 m，CV 42.70% vs 96.31%，聚类数0 vs 2。

Conclusion: 该无人机视觉框架能为PLF中采食栏空间模式评估提供客观、可扩展的量化指标，替代主观目视检查。

Abstract: Beef cattle production is central to Brazilian agribusiness and faces growing demands for efficiency, sustainability, and animal welfare. Precision Livestock Farming (PLF) offers tools to address these challenges, particularly through monitoring feeding-related spatial patterns in confined systems. Feeding management plays a decisive role in animal performance, yet its evaluation still relies largely on subjective, labor-intensive visual inspections that are hard to scale and prone to bias. This work proposes a computer vision framework based on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) to quantify cattle alignment and spatial dispersion at the feed bunk under real-world feedlot conditions. A UAV dataset was collected during flight campaigns at a commercial feedlot in Mato Grosso do Sul, Brazil. Images were manually annotated into cattle head and cattle back classes to support object detection and image-level spatial analysis of cattle distribution. You Only Look Once (YOLO) models, namely YOLOv8 and YOLOv11, were evaluated, with YOLOv8 small used for the downstream spatial analysis. The feed bunk region was segmented using the Segment Anything Model (SAM), and geometric metrics based on detected head positions were developed to characterize alignment, spacing regularity, and clustering. The selected detector achieved 84.30% mAP50 and 35.50% mAP50-95, with an inference time of approximately 2.7 ms per image on an NVIDIA RTX 5060 Ti GPU. The resulting metrics distinguished representative regular and irregular spatial distributions by Root Mean Square Deviation - RMSD (1.06 m and 4.26 m), Coefficient of Variation - CV (42.70% and 96.31%), and clustering (0 and 2 clusters), respectively. These findings indicate that the proposed approach can provide indicators for assessing feed bunk spatial patterns in PLF.

</details>


### [27] [MESAM: Enhancing SAM2 for Few-Shot Segmentation through Semantic-Visual Memory Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08943)
*Tianyu Zou,Shengwu Xiong,Rui Wu,Ruilin Yao,Yi Rong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出记忆增强的 SAM2 方法 MESAM，通过构建语义记忆与视觉记忆并加以精化，解决少样本分割中支持-查询外观差异导致记忆匹配失效的问题，在三个数据集上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 SAM2 用于少样本分割（FSS）时，支持图像与查询图像存在显著外观差异，会削弱 SAM2 记忆匹配过程的有效性。作者通过系统分析发现：基于类别语义信息生成的记忆对外观差异具有鲁棒性，且与 SAM2 更兼容，因此应以此为核心改造记忆构建方式。

Method: 1）语义-视觉记忆生成模块（SVMG）：分别构建查询语义记忆、查询前景视觉记忆与支持自适应视觉记忆；2）交互式语义记忆精化模块（ISMR）：用细粒度视觉细节丰富语义记忆，并抑制其误激活的背景响应。

Result: 在 PASCAL-5^i、COCO-20^i 和 LVIS-92^i 三个数据集上，MESAM 一致优于现有 FSS 方法，刷新了分割性能的 SOTA；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以语义记忆为主、视觉记忆为辅并与 SAM2 适配的记忆增强范式，能有效缓解外观差异带来的匹配退化，为 SAM2 在少样本分割中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to leverage the powerful capabilities of the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) to tackle few-shot segmentation (FSS) tasks. One major challenge lies in the substantial appearance differences between the support and query images, which may diminish the effectiveness of the memory-matching process in SAM2. Through systematic analysis, we find that the memory generated based on the categorical semantic information is robust to this appearance divergence and is more compatible with SAM2. Based on these observations, we propose a new Memory-Enhanced SAM2 (MESAM) method, which first introduces a Semantic-Visual Memory Generation (SVMG) module to separately construct a query semantic memory, a query foreground visual memory, and a support adaptive visual memory. Then, in our designed Interactive Semantic Memory Refinement (ISMR) module, we further enrich the obtained semantic memory with fine-grained visual details, and suppress its misatcivated background responses. Extensive experiments on the PASCAL-$5^i$, COCO-$20^i$, and LVIS-$92^i$ datasets demonstrate that MESAM consistently outperforms existing FSS methods, achieving new state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Our source code is available at: \href{https://github.com/tianyu-zou/MESAM}{https://github.com/tianyu-zou/MESAM}.

</details>


### [28] [MetricPose: Flexible 3D Human Pose Estimation with Depth and Geometric Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08954)
*Shifan Zhu,Sabad Modi,Hang Yang,Arushi Agrawal,Steven Gao,Meghan Huber,Nathan Wycoff,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种预测相机坐标系下公制精确人体关节位置的三维人体姿态估计器，通过融合RGB先验、深度测量与几何约束（如地面接触）缓解单目尺度-距离耦合歧义，统一模型支持RGB、RGB-Ground、RGB-D三种模式，相机局部关节误差较SAM 3D Body分别降低12.16%、31.00%、54.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目图像中人体尺寸与相机距离经透视投影耦合，仅凭图像无法唯一确定，导致相机坐标系下的公制三维定位存在本质歧义；同时传统骨盆相对误差无法衡量相对相机的绝对放置精度。

Method: 在预训练全身姿态估计器基础上，扩展可选的公制深度模块与几何处理模块，以改进相机距离估计并实现深度引导的人体细化；保持仅RGB可运行，三种模式共享学习参数，输出相机坐标系下带尺度的关节位置。

Result: 与基础模型SAM 3D Body相比，RGB、RGB-Ground、RGB-D三种模式的相机局部关节位置误差分别降低12.16%、31.00%和54.61%。

Conclusion: 融合学习型RGB先验、直接深度测量与环境几何约束可有效消解尺度-距离歧义；统一参数的多模态架构在保持RGB-only可用性的同时，显著提升相机局部公制精度，且相机局部误差是比骨盆相对误差更严格的评价指标。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a 3D human pose estimator that predicts metrically accurate human joint positions in the camera coordinate frame. This task requires recovering the configuration of the body joints, scale, and metric camera-local placement. In monocular images, body size and camera distance are coupled through perspective projection and therefore cannot be determined uniquely from image alone. This ambiguity can be reduced by combining three complementary sources of information: learned RGB priors, direct depth measurements, and geometric constraints (e.g., ground contact) from the environment. We extend a pretrained full-body estimator with optional metric depth and geometry-processing modules that improve camera-distance estimation and depth-guided body refinement while preserving RGB-only operation. The resulting unified model supports RGB, RGB-Ground, and RGB-D using shared learned parameters. Unlike pelvis-relative error, camera-local error also measures placement relative to the camera. Compared with the existing foundation model SAM 3D Body, our methods with RGB, RGB-Ground, and RGB-D modes reduce camera-local joint-position error by 12.16%, 31.00% and 54.61% respectively.

</details>


### [29] [Region-aware error triage for medical image segmentation with groupwise conformal risk control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08962)
*Nishi Kanta Paul,Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Hoileong Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RACE-Triage：一种不改变分割掩码的事后误差分诊框架，用预测轮廓把图像划分为边界带与非边界区域，在各区域内分别做保形风险控制，从而降低非边界误差的漏警比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割掩码更多是被"审阅"而非重绘，因此需要知道掩码何处可能出错。学习式误差图虽能按风险排序像素，但告警像素数量需人为设定（ad hoc），且对全部误差像素统一校准一个告警阈值，会使得远离预测轮廓的误差（非边界误差）告警不足。

Method: 风险网络在 backbone 无关输入上、基于折外（out-of-fold）预测训练；预测轮廓将图像划分为边界带与非边界区域，在每个区域内分别应用保形风险控制，以约束"该区域存在误差时漏检误差的期望比例"上界；掩码本身保持不变。

Result: 在ISIC 2018皮肤镜与BUSI乳腺超声上（缺患者标识，假设图像间独立），全局池化控制在总体上达标，但分别平均漏掉31.8%与36.4%的非边界误差；区域控制将其降至19.3%与20.9%，尺寸过滤后假阴性连通域召回率由0.722升至0.818、由0.611升至0.763。平均告警面积增加1.6与4.9个百分点，95分位达23.5%与38.5%。结论在三个随机种子与第二个backbone上保持一致，面积匹配的启发式方法无法复现。外部PH²集上冻结阈值超出两个目标，用100张本地图像重校准后多次划分的均值低于目标。

Conclusion: 区域化校准在付出可测量的告警面积增加代价下，改善了非边界误差的覆盖；该框架可作为与backbone无关的事后分诊模块，但跨域应用需要少量本地数据重校准。

Abstract: Segmentation masks are increasingly reviewed rather than redrawn, which requires knowing where a mask is likely wrong. Learned error maps rank pixels by risk, but the number of pixels to warn is chosen ad hoc, and calibrating one warning threshold over all error pixels may leave errors far from the predicted contour under-flagged. We propose RACE-Triage, a post-hoc error triage framework that leaves the segmentation mask unchanged. A risk network is trained on out-of-fold predictions from backbone-independent inputs. The predicted contour partitions each image into a boundary band and a non-boundary region, and conformal risk control is applied separately in each region to bound the expected fraction of missed errors among cases with errors there. On ISIC 2018 dermoscopy and BUSI breast ultrasound (where patient identifiers are unavailable, so independence across images is assumed), pooled control met its overall target but missed on average 31.8% and 36.4% of non-boundary errors. Regional control reduced these fractions to 19.3% and 20.9% and raised the recall of size-filtered false-negative components from 0.722 to 0.818 and from 0.611 to 0.763. The mean warned area grew by 1.6 and 4.9 percentage points, and its 95th percentile reached 23.5% and 38.5%. The effect held across three seeds and a second backbone, and area-matched heuristics did not reproduce it. On the external PH$^2$ set, frozen thresholds exceeded both targets, whereas recalibration with 100 local images brought the mean over repeated splits below them. Regional calibration improves non-boundary error coverage at a measured increase in warned area.

</details>


### [30] [Refer-CityFlow and M3Track: A Benchmark Dataset and Reference Framework for Language-Driven Multi-Target Multi-Camera Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08976)
*Phuong T. Nguyen,Hao Vo,Tam Bang,Hoang K. Tran,Hoang-Minh Dau,Mahshid Malazizi,Tuan-Anh Hoang,Mina Sartipi,Thanh-Nam Doan,Ngan Le,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Refer-MTMCT 新任务与 Refer-CityFlow 基准，并给出 M3Track 参考框架，实现跨相机语言引导的多目标追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Refer-MOT 基准主要面向单相机设定，无法覆盖自由语言查询在多相机网络下恢复身份一致轨迹的需求，缺少同时要求语言 grounding、跨相机关联与时空推理的评测平台。

Method: 基于 CityFlow 的全局跨相机身份标注构建 Refer-CityFlow：96 段视频、40 台路侧相机、660+ 身份一致跨相机轨迹、4,362 条轨迹级描述、7,699 条查询（含 50 名参与者采集的 1,699 条真实用户查询）。同时提出 M3Track 模块化多模态参考框架，由 LLM 智能体编排可替换的检测、单相机跟踪与跨相机关联模块。

Result: 四相机 Scenario 02 参考实验中，单相机 referring tracker 在真实用户查询上仅达 14.95–18.28 HOTA；所评测的跨视角方法直接迁移约 0.05 HOTA；M3Track 达到 44.10 HOTA 与 65.40 IDF1。

Conclusion: Refer-MTMCT 揭示了单相机方法与跨视角方法在跨相机语言引导追踪上的显著差距，M3Track 为后续研究建立了较强的参考基线，并公开了代码与数据仓库。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking enables users to specify targets through natural language, but existing Refer-MOT benchmarks primarily focus on single-camera settings. We formalize Referring Multi-Target Multi-Camera Tracking (Refer-MTMCT), where free-form queries specify identity-consistent trajectories to be recovered across a network of sequential, sparse-overlap cameras. We introduce Refer-CityFlow, a benchmark built on CityFlow's global cross-camera identity annotations and augmented with language queries requiring joint language grounding, cross-camera association, and spatio-temporal reasoning. Refer-CityFlow covers 96 videos from 40 roadside cameras with 660+ identity-consistent cross-camera trajectories, 4,362 trajectory-level descriptions, and 7,699 queries, including 1,699 real-user queries collected from 50 participants. Reference experiments on the four-camera Scenario 02 show that single-camera referring trackers reach 14.95-18.28 HOTA on real-user queries, while the evaluated cross-view method achieves about 0.05 HOTA in direct transfer. To establish reference performance, we provide M3Track, a modular multimodal reference framework in which a large language model (LLM)-based agent orchestrates interchangeable detection, tracking, and cross-camera association modules. M3Track reaches 44.10 HOTA and 65.40 IDF1 on the same real-user query set. The project repository is available at https://github.com/phuongnguyen194/Refer-CityFlow-and--M3Track.

</details>


### [31] [LEF-JEPA: Denoising-Trained Latent Energy Fields for Spatial Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08989)
*Zhiyuan Liu,Zhihao Xu,Sicheng Liu,Huanhuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEF-JEPA通过目标引导的隐空间恢复学习空间结构，并借助相对能量约束将该监督传递到视觉编码器；能量模块仅用于预训练，不增加推理开销，并构造条件Ising哈密顿量以便硬件求解器搜索低能态。统一CIFAR-10协议下，分类、前景分割与视觉规划均优于V-JEPA2.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅在被掩码位置施加预测监督的JEPA难以在隐表示中清晰刻画物体形状与边界；而保留更多低级纹理/颜色信息并不必然提升表示对预测与动作任务的适配性。核心问题是：如何在不��度编码这些细节的前提下增强支撑预测与动作的空间结构。

Method: 提出LEF-JEPA：(1) 以目标引导的隐空间恢复学习空间结构；(2) 通过相对能量约束把该监督迁移至视觉编码器，能量模块仅用于预训练、推理零开销；(3) 构造条件Ising哈密顿量，使低能态搜索可由专用Ising求解器完成，降低能量优化成本。

Result: 在物理硬件上验证了以更少局部更新达到更低前向时延的可行性；在统一的CIFAR-10预训练+冻结编码器评测协议下，两种实现均在图像分类、前景分割与视觉规划任务上超过V-JEPA2.1。

Conclusion: 空间结构监督与能量约束结合可在不增加推理成本的前提下改善JEPA表示质量，且与Ising硬件加速兼容。

Abstract: A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that applies prediction supervision only at masked positions struggles to clearly capture object shapes and boundaries in its latent representations. We further observe that some existing methods improve spatial structure while also retaining rich variations in texture and color. However, preserving more low-level visual information does not necessarily make representations better suited to prediction and action. This raises a key question: how can we strengthen the spatial structure in latent representations that supports prediction and action without over-encoding these details? We introduce LEF-JEPA, which learns spatial structure through target-guided latent recovery and transfers this supervision to the visual encoder through a relative-energy constraint; the energy module is used only during pretraining and adds no inference overhead. To reduce the cost of energy optimization during pretraining, we further construct a conditional Ising Hamiltonian that allows low-energy-state search to be performed by specialized Ising solvers. On physical hardware, we validate the feasibility of achieving lower forward latency with fewer local updates. Under a unified CIFAR-10 pretraining and frozen-encoder evaluation protocol, both realizations outperform V-JEPA2.1 in image classification, foreground segmentation, and visual planning.

</details>


### [32] [Seeing vs. Counting: Complexity Twins Show Where Multimodal LLMs Fail on Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08998)
*Yin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 解耦视觉读取与计数计算，发现Gemma类多模态LLM在CoT下的计数失败主要源于答案规模而非复杂度类，提供代码与精确转录后可在大网格上正确计数，瓶颈转为读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉图与谜题基准同时改变视觉密度与算法难度，混淆了"看不清"与"算不对"两种失败来源，无法定位多模态LLM计数的真正瓶颈。

Method: 对同一图形配对设计不同计数复杂度类的问题（生成树=行列式、简单路径=#P完全），并引入转录探针与纯文本对照以分离读取与计算；测试Gemini、Gemma、Qwen3-VL；进一步给出代码与精确转录，并比较"重复正向读数"与"旋转副本+裁剪校验"两种读取策略。

Result: Gemini模型结果依赖计算方式：CoT下多数能正确读取小图却在计数环节失败，失败率主要由答案数值规模决定而非复杂度类（主模型答案每增加一个数量级，精确作答的几率下降2.38倍）。给定代码与精确转录后，主模型在10×10网格上仍能正确计数；此时读取成为瓶颈——单次观察会漏掉水平边，旋转副本加裁剪校验的二次读取在96个大网格中读对86个，而重复正向读取仅57个。

Conclusion: 多模态LLM的计数错误更可能是"算"（受答案规模制约）而非"看"，但当计算被外包给代码后，视觉读取成为主要瓶颈，且旋转与裁剪校验的多视角读取显著优于重复单视角。

Abstract: When a multimodal LLM miscounts in a diagram, does it fail at seeing or at counting? Existing visual graph and puzzle benchmarks vary visual density and algorithmic hardness together, confounding the two. We pair counting questions about the same drawing that sit in different counting-complexity classes, such as its spanning trees (a determinant) and its simple paths (#P-complete), and use a transcription probe and a text-only control to separate reading from computing. We test Gemini, Gemma and Qwen3-VL models; for the Gemini models, the answer depends on how the model computes. In chain of thought, most of them read small drawings correctly and then fail at counting, at a rate set far more by the size of the answer than by its complexity class (for the primary model, the odds of an exact answer fall 2.38-fold per decade of the answer). Given code and an exact transcription, the primary model counts correctly even on 10x10 grids, and reading becomes the bottleneck: a single view misses horizontal edges, and a second reading of a rotated copy with crop checks reads 86/96 large grids exactly, against 57/96 when the upright reading is simply repeated.

</details>


### [33] [Did I See It or Think It? Probing Reality Monitoring in Vision-Language Models with Concept Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09014)
*Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh,Seyedarmin Azizi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个扰动图像与注入概念相互独立的实验范式，考察视觉语言模型能否区分"内部注入的想法"与"真正看到的图像内容"（即现实监测），并同时评估报告内容与来源归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的语言模型内省研究均为纯文本实验，只关注模型能否识别注入的概念方向。而视觉语言模型面临认知心理学中的"现实监测"难题：必须将内部生成内容与真实视觉输入区分开来。现有工作未检验模型是否能正确判定注入概念的来源。

Method: 设计一个 2×2 式独立操纵范式：每轮试验中独立改变图像内容与注入的概念方向（activation steering），要求模型报告（1）看到了什么、（2）是否有想法被注入、（3）该想法是什么。评分既衡量概念识别是否正确，也衡量来源归属是否正确。在六个视觉语言模型上进行测试，并进一步检验注入强度、注入路径（视觉通路 vs. 其他）、可见物体干扰的影响，以及普通图像描述任务中的同类失败。

Result: 仅 Gemma-3-27B 能较可靠地识别注入概念（35%，而单纯 steering 基线为 3%），且误报率极低。但其来源报告十分脆弱：强注入或视觉通路注入会被报告为"看到的"；图像中存在可见物体几乎完全消除正确的"想法"报告。在普通图像描述中，注入物体出现在高达 87% 的真实照片描述里。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型难以完成现实监测，来源归属尤其不可靠。评估多模态自我报告必须同时核查其内容与所声明的来源，否则会高估模型的内省能力。

Abstract: Recent work tests whether language models can introspect by injecting a concept direction into their activations and asking whether they notice it and can name it. These studies are text-only. Vision-language models (VLMs) face an additional problem, known in cognitive psychology as reality monitoring: they must keep internally generated content separate from what they actually see. We therefore ask: can VLMs distinguish injected concepts from image content? We introduce a paradigm that varies the image and the injected concept independently. On each trial, the model reports what it sees, whether a thought was injected, and what that thought is. This design scores both whether the model identifies the concept and which source it assigns to it. Across six VLMs, only Gemma-3-27B reliably identifies injected concepts (35% vs. 3% for steering alone), with almost no false alarms. Even then, the source it reports is fragile. Strong or visual-pathway injections are reported as seen, and a visible object nearly eliminates correct thought reports. The same failure appears in ordinary captioning, where injected objects appear in up to 87% of captions of real photographs. Evaluating multimodal self-reports therefore requires checking both their content and their reported source.

</details>


### [34] [Frontier3DBench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for 3D Creation across Objects, Scenes, and Interactive Open Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09017)
*Xinjie Lin,Qingle Liu,Wenyi Li,John Lam,Xiaoqiu Wang,Deyao Hong,Mingju Gao,Zheng Jiang,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Kaisen Yang,Huan-ang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出统一基准Frontier3DBench，涵盖9类145个任务（物体、场景、交互式开放世界），联合评估外观、结构与行为。11种模型-框架配置中GPT-6.1-sol总分最高（66.02/100）。所有配置在参考重建上均弱于文本驱动生成；生成世界在局部机制上达83.08分，但运动间隙仅20.82、状态化工作流仅30.26，说明参考保真度与协同活动完成是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体通过将图像与指令转换为可编辑程序，把3D重建、生成与编辑整合进统一工作流，但现有基准在任务范围与评测协议上相互割裂，无法刻画其在各构建层级上的能力差异。

Method: 构建Frontier3DBench：145个任务、9个类别，覆盖物体级、场景级与交互式开放世界构建；对外观、结构、行为三个维度进行联合评测；在11种模型-框架（model-harness）配置上开展实验。

Result: 能力随层级分化：GPT-6.1-sol在物体与场景层级领先，总分最高66.02/100；GPT-6-astra在交互式世界构建上领先。物体与场景任务中，参考重建分数普遍低于文本驱动生成。生成世界在局部机制上平均83.08/100，但运动间隙仅20.82、状态化工作流仅30.26。

Conclusion: 单一机制通常可运行，但需要协调动作与持久状态的完整活动仍难以完成；参考保真度与协同活动完成度是编码智能体在3D创作中的两大核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Coding agents combine visual understanding with program execution, offering a new route to automated 3D creation. By translating images and instructions into editable programs, they bring reconstruction, generation, and editing into a shared workflow. Yet existing benchmarks fragment evaluation across task scopes and protocols, leaving their capabilities across construction levels unclear. We propose Frontier3DBench, a unified benchmark of 145 tasks across 9 categories spanning object, scene, and interactive open-world creation, with joint evaluation of appearance, structure, and behavior. Experiments with 11 model-harness configurations reveal strengths that vary by level: GPT-6.1-sol leads the object and scene levels and achieves the highest overall score of 66.02/100, whereas GPT-6-astra leads interactive world construction. However, all configurations score lower on reference reconstruction than on text-driven generation in object and scene tasks. Producing plausible content thus remains easier than reproducing specified geometry, layouts, and visual details. Moreover, generated worlds average 83.08/100 on local mechanisms but only 20.82 on motion clearance and 30.26 on stateful workflows: individual mechanisms often function, whereas complete activities that require coordinated actions and persistent state remain difficult. These results identify reference fidelity and coordinated activity completion as central bottlenecks for coding agents in 3D creation.

</details>


### [35] [Grounding-Aware Judge Reward Reinforcement Learning for Out-of-Domain Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09047)
*Xiaoyong Yu,Xinge Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"基于grounding的裁判奖励"（grounding-aware judge reward），用32B视觉语言模型作为judge对FAS的CoT推理链进行过程监督，在GRPO阶段优化，显著提升域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸反欺骗（FAS）推理缺乏可规则验证的ground truth：仅二分类判决可校验，推理链无法用人工规则验证，因此仅优化判决是稀疏且可被"作弊"的信号——模型可能通过错误拒绝偏置等捷径得到正确答案而不具备泛化性。研究目标是在CoT监督微调后已具备良好域内性能的模型上，提升其域外（OOD）泛化。

Method: 以32B视觉语言模型作为裁判（LLM-as-judge），针对图像对每条推理链在证据锚定、逻辑连贯性、领域知识三个维度打分，把图像本身变为验证信号；该奖励用于CoT监督微调之后的GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习阶段，提供判决级奖励所无法给出的过程监督。

Result: OOD泛化：LCC-FASD的ACER由0.288降至0.273，MSU-MFSD由0.431降至0.409；ID保持：CelebA-Spoof由0.033至0.035。与ResNet-50/101、CLIP-ViT-B/16基线相比，该MLLM在ID上占优，OOD上持平或超过最强基线。

Conclusion: grounding-aware奖励是提升FAS多模态大模型OOD泛化的有效手段；据作者所知，这是首次将LLM-as-judge过程监督用于人脸反欺骗任务。过程监督由裁判而非RL目标本身提供。

Abstract: Face Anti-Spoofing (FAS) must distinguish genuine faces from presentation attacks. A unified Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) is an attractive solution: it detects physical attacks (print, replay, mask) in one model and produces interpretable forensic evidence via Chain-of-Thought (CoT). A central obstacle to post-training such a model with reinforcement learning is that FAS reasoning has no rule-verifiable ground truth: only the binary verdict is checkable, while the reasoning chain cannot be verified by any hand-crafted rule. Optimizing the verdict alone is therefore a sparse, hackable signal. The model can reach the correct answer through a shortcut such as a false-rejection bias, which does not generalize. We address this with a grounding-aware judge reward. A 32B vision-language judge scores each reasoning chain against the image on evidence anchoring, logical coherence, and domain knowledge, turning the image itself into the verification signal. The reward is applied in a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) stage after CoT supervised fine-tuning. It is the judge, rather than the RL objective, that supplies the process supervision a verdict-only reward cannot. Our study starts from a model that already performs well In-Domain (ID) after CoT-based supervised fine-tuning and asks how to improve its Out-of-Domain (OOD) generalization. We find that grounding-aware rewards are an effective means to that end. We validate the method on OOD generalization, where the reward reduces ACER on two physical benchmarks (LCC-FASD 0.288 to 0.273, MSU-MFSD 0.431 to 0.409) while preserving ID accuracy (CelebA-Spoof 0.033 to 0.035). Against ResNet-50/101 and CLIP-ViT-B/16 baselines, the MLLM dominates ID and matches or surpasses the strongest baseline OOD. To our knowledge this is the first use of LLM-as-judge process supervision for FAS.

</details>


### [36] [OmniLatent: Unified Latent for 3D Part Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09055)
*Yunhan Yang,Yunfei Zhao,Zeqiang Lai,Jiachen Xu,Minxiu Wang,Xintong Han,Xiangyu Yue,Xihui Liu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV
