Conclusion: 视觉语义蒸馏结合显式几何监督可有效弥补雷达表示的语义与局部建模不足，为高保真雷达人体网格重建提供可行方案。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers a privacy-preserving and illumination-robust alternative for human mesh reconstruction (HMR). However, high-fidelity radar HMR remains challenging. Sparse point cloud-based methods suffer from information loss and missed detections, while raw radar tensor methods like RT-Mesh lack fine-grained semantics and explicit local body modeling. In this paper, we propose RadarDINO, a radar HMR framework that distills visual semantics from a DINOv3 teacher into 3D radar representations and introduces explicit geometric supervision. DINOv3 descriptors are transferred to radar voxels via calibrated RGB-radar geometry, providing voxel- and body-part-level supervision for part-aware radar representations. We further incorporate vertex- and joint-losses to directly constrain reconstructed geometry. On the M4Human dataset, our method outperforms the SOTA baseline across all three evaluation splits.

</details>


### [71] [ExQuery: Explicit Structured Query Priors for Temporal 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09527)
*Honglin Sun,Yifei Zhang,Junyang Zhang,Kenji Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ExQuery显式结构化查询框架：将解码查询拆分为共享语义上下文切片与中心、尺寸、朝向、速度等物理状态切片，并利用该结构重建时序查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏查询式3D检测器把异构的语义、几何、运动信息编码进统一隐式查询（含显式3D锚框情形），信息混杂、结构不可解释，难以被各分支分别利用，也限制时序查询重建质量。

Method: 将每个解码查询组织为共享语义上下文切片加物理状态专用切片（中心、尺寸、朝向、速度），编码端到分支特定回归全程保持该结构；重建时序查询时，用自车运动变换预测物理状态，以分类分数形成软语义先验，再经门控仿射调制与存储的潜在实例特征融合。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上取得有竞争力的检测性能；消融实验验证了各提出模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式结构化查询设计能有效解耦语义与物理状态信息，并提升时序3D检测性能。

Abstract: Sparse query-based 3D detectors commonly encode heterogeneous semantic, geometric, and motion information into unified latent queries, including when explicit 3D anchors are used. We introduce ExQuery, an explicit structured query framework for temporal 3D object detection. Each decoded query is organized into a shared semantic context slice and physical-state-specific slices for center, dimensions, orientation, and velocity. This structure is preserved from prior encoding to branch-specific regression and is reused to reconstruct temporal queries: predicted physical states are transformed by ego motion, classification scores form soft semantic priors, and the reconstructed query is fused with the stored latent instance feature through gated affine modulation. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that our method achieves competitive detection performance, and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our proposed modules.

</details>


### [72] [Multimodal Training with Knowledge Traction for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09536)
*Xiao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种引入外部知识（文本提示+LLM先验、字典学习归一化压缩、LLM保证相似帧文本描述一致性）与跨模态注意力特征处理的弱监督视频异常检测方法，实验优于现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督视频异常检测具有挑战性；现有方法主要对视频帧特征建模并依据其幅值判断异常，缺少外部知识与语义先验，性能、鲁棒性与泛化性受限。

Method: 1) 利用文本提示与大语言模型建立任务相关先验信息，并用字典学习对先验进行归一化与压缩；2) 针对图像描述模型对相似帧生成的文本描述不一致问题，设计借助LLM与辅助信息的方法，保证同一视频内相似帧文本描述的一致性；3) 提出融合先验知识与跨模态注意力的特征处理方式，学习不同模态间的关系与特性，构建更准确可靠的特征表示。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在性能上优于其他state-of-the-art方法，验证了引入外部知识对提升精度与稳定性的有效性。

Conclusion: 将LLM驱动的文本先验、字典学习归一化与跨模态注意力融合进弱监督视频异常检测框架，可同时改善性能、鲁棒性与泛化能力；文本描述一致性问题值得作为独立环节处理。

Abstract: The task of weakly supervised video anomaly detection is challenging. Existing approaches mainly model video frame features and determine whether anomalies are present based on their magnitudes. In this work, we propose a novel method that incorporates external knowledge into training to improve model performance, robustness, and generalization. We leverage textual prompts and a large language model (LLM) to establish prior information for the specific task, and employ dictionary learning to normalize and compress this information. Moreover, during experiments, we observed inconsistencies in the textual descriptions extracted from similar video frames by image caption models. To address this, we devise a method that leverages an LLM and auxiliary information to ensure consistency in the textual descriptions of similar frames within a video. Finally, we introduce a feature processing approach that incorporates prior knowledge and cross-modal attention mechanisms. This approach learns the relationships and characteristics among different modalities to construct more accurate and reliable feature representations, thereby improving the accuracy and stability of downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses other state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [73] [Video Stitching from Multiple Moving Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09570)
*Shira Ifergane,Roy Amoyal,Shahar Benishay,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MViS：面向多自由运动相机的联合多视角视频对齐测试时优化框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何视频对齐方法基于图像拼接或视频稳像，通常限于两相机，或必须假设固定的线性相机排序，难以处理多自由运动相机场景。

Method: 提出MViS轻量级测试时优化框架：用类空间变换网络联合处理短时窗内所有视角的帧，预测逐视角、逐时刻的单应矩阵；优化分两阶段——几何引导对齐与基于外观的细化，两阶段均施加多视角与时序一致性约束；无需场景特定超参调优。

Result: 在标准双视角视频基准上与SOTA持平或更优；引入含两个以上自由运动相机的标注数据集，并建立该设定下的首个基准。

Conclusion: MViS可处理任意且时变的视角相交图，在快速相机运动下保持鲁棒，为多视角视频对齐提供了新框架与新基准。

Abstract: We address geometric video alignment for the same scene captured by multiple freely moving cameras. Existing methods, which are based on image stitching or video stabilization, are typically limited to two cameras or must assume the restrictive setting of a fixed linear ordering of the cameras. We propose MViS, a lightweight test-time optimization framework for joint multi-view alignment with temporal consistency. MViS uses a spatial-transformer-inspired net to jointly process frames from all views within a short temporal window and predict per-view, per-time homographies. Optimization proceeds in two stages: geometry-guided alignment followed by appearance-based refinement, with both stages enforcing multi-view and temporal consistency across the sequence. The method requires no scene-specific hyperparameter tuning, handles arbitrary and time-varying view-intersection graphs, and remains robust under rapid camera motion. On a standard two-view video benchmark, MViS matches or surpasses the state of the art. To facilitate evaluation in more complex scenarios, we introduce an annotated dataset with more than two freely moving cameras and establish the first benchmark for this setting. Project page: https://bgu-cs-vil.github.io/MViS-project/ .

</details>


### [74] [MSR-ABC: Complementary Feature Fusion for Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09583)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MSR-ABC全局–局部框架，融合MedVAE、MedSigLIP与影像组学，用于CT上透明细胞肾细胞癌(ccRCC)分类；内部AUROC 0.806、外部0.879，优于简单拼接与双源融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从CT对ccRCC进行分型有赖于捕捉肿瘤多层面表型的表征；但将重建特征、图文嵌入与影像组学描述符直接拼接，难以充分挖掘其互补信息，需要结构化的融合机制。

Method: MSR-ABC全局–局部框架：空间匹配的局部图像描述符经跨表征注意力交互；影像组学辅助监督约束池化后的局部表征保留定量肿瘤属性；门控残差机制将局部信息与全局汇总融合；辅以区域感知池化(area-aware pooling)以及视觉提示与软文本提示(soft-text prompting)。

Result: 在396例KiTS23患者级划分与111例TCGA/TCIA外部队列上，报告3个训练种子的平均AUROC及95%患者bootstrap置信区间：内部0.806、外部0.879；内部较MedVAE–影像组学全局模型和三源拼接分别高3.35与3.61个百分点。消融显示：去掉任一来源在两个队列均降低AUROC（影像组学移除降幅最大）；单独移除跨表征注意力、辅助监督或门控融合亦降低内部平均AUROC。

Conclusion: 结果与集成架构中各组成部分的互补贡献相一致，支持进一步研究结构化表征融合用于无创肾肿瘤表征。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) classification from computed tomography (CT) may benefit from representations that capture different aspects of tumour phenotype. However, simply concatenating reconstruction-derived features, image--text embeddings, and radiomic descriptors may not fully exploit their complementary information. We propose MSR-ABC, a global--local framework integrating MedVAE, MedSigLIP, and radiomics. Spatially matched local image descriptors interact through cross-representation attention, while radiomic auxiliary supervision encourages the pooled local representation to retain quantitative tumour attributes. A gated residual mechanism integrates local information with global summaries. Area-aware pooling and visual and soft-text prompting complete the framework. We evaluate discrimination using a patient-level split of 396 KiTS23 cases and an external cohort of 111 TCGA/TCIA cases, reporting mean AUROC over three training seeds with 95% patient-bootstrap confidence intervals. MSR-ABC achieves mean AUROCs of 0.806 internally and 0.879 externally. Internally, it exceeds global MedVAE--radiomics and three-source concatenation by 3.35 and 3.61 AUROC percentage points, respectively. Removing any source lowers mean AUROC in both cohorts, with the largest decreases following radiomics removal. Individual removal of cross-representation attention, auxiliary supervision, or gated fusion also lowers internal mean AUROC. These findings are consistent with complementary contributions within the integrated architecture and support further investigation of structured representation fusion for non-invasive renal tumour characterisation.

</details>


### [75] [Dual-Region Slice Aggregation for CT-Based Renal Tumor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09599)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出区域感知架构结合冻结DINOv3特征与影像组学，用于CT图像上透明细胞肾细胞癌（ccRCC）分类，内外验证AUC分别为0.8078和0.9094。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ccRCC是成人最常见的肾癌，需要基于CT的可靠分类方法；现有方法多只用肿瘤区域特征，忽视了瘤周微环境信息，且如何将冻结的视觉基础模型特征适配到肾脏CT尚不清晰。

Method: 采用区域感知架构：从共享的冻结DINOv3特征图中分别池化肿瘤区域与0–10 mm瘤周上下文；在固定的、仅训练集拟合的PCA之前设置共享的位置感知切片聚合器；所得表征通过两个标量特征门与影像组学特征融合。并设置单肿瘤读出、早期PCA、重建等对照实验。

Result: KiTS23内部测试AUC为0.8078±0.0154，111例外部队列AUC为0.9094±0.0015；相比匹配的单肿瘤读出，平均AUC分别提升1.50与2.34个百分点。早期PCA与重建对照的内部平均AUC更低，而外部AUC在早期与晚期PCA间相近。

Conclusion: 区域读出（瘤周上下文）与切片聚合在将冻结视觉特征适配到肾脏CT中共同起关键作用，消融结果支持该架构设计的有效性。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) is the most common kidney cancer in adults. We present a region-aware architecture for CT-based ccRCC classification. Tumor and 0--10 mm peritumoral context are pooled separately from shared frozen DINOv3 feature maps. A shared position-aware slice aggregator precedes fixed training-only principal component analysis (PCA). The resulting representations are fused with radiomics using two scalar feature gates. Evaluation on KiTS23 and 111 external cases yields internal test and external AUCs of 0.8078\pm0.0154 and 0.9094\pm0.0015. Compared with matched single-tumor readout, mean AUC increases by 1.50 and 2.34 percentage points, respectively. Early-PCA and reconstruction controls yield lower internal mean AUCs, while external AUC is similar for early and late PCA. These findings highlight the joint role of regional readout and slice aggregation in adapting frozen visual features to renal CT.

</details>


### [76] [CRTrack-Cap: A New Benchmark for Cross-View Dense Video Object Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09617)
*Xiangqun Zhang,Likai Wang,Zekun Qian,Rui Zhou,Ruize Han,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨视角稠密视频目标描述任务（CVDVOC），构建CRTrack-Cap数据集与CV-CHOTA指标，并给出由VECA与CEG组成的参考框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密视频目标描述（DVOC）逐视角独立处理，对同一人物在各相机产生相互独立的局部轨迹片段与描述，缺乏统一描述，且未利用跨视角互补的外观证据。

Method: 形式化CVDVOC任务：将局部轨迹片段关联为场景级全局轨迹，并基于同步视角为每条轨迹生成单一描述。由CRTrack经文本-身份链接审核、将可用指代表达转为规范描述构建CRTrack-Cap（13个场景、425条全局轨迹、838条局部轨迹片段、335个身份的规范描述）；将CHOTA改造为CV-CHOTA以联合评估跟踪与描述质量。参考框架：VECA做跨相机关联分组，CEG依据各视角证据的可靠性与分歧进行门控融合。

Result: 与三种经MvMHAT改造的单视角方法相比，该框架在场景不相交的域内与跨域测试上分别取得CV-CHOTA 83.43与33.48。

Conclusion: CRTrack-Cap与所提框架为跨视角一致描述同一人物提供了基准与强基线。

Abstract: Dense video object captioning (DVOC) localizes, tracks, and describes object trajectories within a video. Existing methods process each camera view independently, producing separate camera-local tracklets and captions for the same person across cameras. These outputs lack a unified description and leave complementary appearance evidence across views unused. We therefore formulate cross-view DVOC (CVDVOC), which associates these tracklets into scene-level global trajectories and generates one caption for each trajectory from synchronized views. To support this task, we construct CRTrack-Cap from CRTrack by auditing text-identity links and converting usable referring expressions into canonical captions. It retains 425 global trajectories and 838 local tracklets across 13 scenes, with canonical captions for 335 identities. We adapt CHOTA to the cross-view setting as CV-CHOTA to jointly evaluate tracking and caption quality. Our reference framework combines View-Exclusive Cross-Camera Association (VECA) for grouping tracklets with Conflict-Evidence Gating (CEG) for integrating caption evidence using its reliability and disagreement across views. Against three single-view methods adapted with MvMHAT for cross-view association, our framework achieves CV-CHOTA scores of 83.43 and 33.48 on the scene-disjoint in-domain and cross-domain tests, respectively. Together, CRTrack-Cap and our framework provide a benchmark and a strong baseline for describing the same person consistently across camera views.

</details>


### [77] [Breaking the Privacy Promise: Gradient Inversion Attacks on Federated Low-Rank Adapted Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09652)
*Ritik K. Badiya,Siladittya Manna,Ashmit Sinha,M Yashwanth,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究 LoRA 适配的 ViT 在联邦学习中的隐私泄漏风险，提出"空间锚定生成式反演"框架，通过在客户端微调时引入服务器指定的可训练位置嵌入，放大位置梯度信号，从而显著提升服务器端的图像重建质量，说明 LoRA 本身并不能防止数据泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEFT（尤其 LoRA）因低计算与通信开销被广泛用于联邦学习，但其隐私风险鲜有研究。LoRA 的低秩更新会削弱服务器可见的梯度信号；同时 ViT 冻结的位置嵌入缺失空间线索。二者耦合形成"信息不足"与"空间线索缺失"两类歧义，共同限制了梯度反演重建质量，需要针对性破解。

Method: 提出空间锚定生成式反演框架：在客户端微调阶段，将服务器指定的可训练位置嵌入注入 ViT 块中，使位置梯度充当逐层空间锚点，补充位置线索；同时引入生成先验，将重建约束在真实图像流形上，抑制重复数据伪影与 patch 置换伪影（二者会产生与目标客户图像相似的梯度）。将该空间锚定与 GIAS 结合。

Result: FedSGD 设置下，在 CIFAR-10 上 PSNR 提升 10.14 dB、SSIM 提升 0.45、LPIPS 提升 0.098；高分辨率 CelebA 上 PSNR 提升 7.83 dB，CARS 上提升 11.74 dB。在更大 batch size 与 FedAvg 设置下重建质量增益依然保持。

Conclusion: LoRA 类 PEFT 本身并不能阻止联邦学习中的数据泄漏；精心引入的位置嵌入会进一步暴露私有信息。这提示 PEFT 的隐私收益需重新审慎评估，防御设计应考虑位置嵌入等结构性通道。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has been increasingly adopted to fine-tune pretrained models with low computational cost and is therefore also preferred in the federated learning paradigm to reduce computational and communication overhead. In this work, we investigate the sparsely explored problem of the risk of private data leakage in LoRA-adapted Vision Transformers (ViTs), where low-rank updates weaken the gradient signal available to a server with access to individual client updates. We show that quality of reconstruction is limited by two coupled ambiguities: reduced information in LoRA gradients and missing spatial cues from frozen positional embeddings. To address this, we propose a spatially anchored generative inversion framework that incorporates server-prescribed trainable positional embeddings into ViT blocks during fine-tuning at clients. The resulting positional gradients act as layer-wise spatial anchors, providing additional positional cues, while the generative prior keeps reconstructions on a realistic image manifold, reducing duplicate-data and patch-permutation artifacts, {which produce similar gradients as those produced when the target client images are used}. Adding our proposed spatial anchoring to GIAS improves PSNR by 10.14 dB, SSIM by 0.45, and LPIPS by 0.098 on CIFAR-10, as well as PSNR gains of 7.83 and 11.74 dB on high-resolution CelebA and CARS datasets, respectively, in the FedSGD setting. Such gains in reconstructed image quality metrics are also maintained at higher batch sizes and in FedAvg settings. These results indicate that LoRA-based PEFT does not, by itself, prevent data leakage in FL, and carefully incorporated positional embeddings can help in exposing private information.

</details>


### [78] [Decoupled Mode Connectivity for Base-to-Novel Generalization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09655)
*Imad Eddine Marouf,Khalid Oublal,Enzo Tartaglione,Stéphane Lathuilière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦模式连接（DMC），为"基类专门化"与"新类泛化"各配一个提示，在文本特征空间用线性模式连接走廊与视觉锚KL正则，测试时以固定系数合并两个提示的文本特征，实现单提示推理开销下同时提升基类与新类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP提示学习中，交叉熵目标使提示偏向基类专门化，而泛化到未见类则需要贴近CLIP零样本特征空间。两个目标作用于同一个提示，收敛时梯度相互冲突；实验表明单一提示的损失设计难以稳定超越余弦基线，因此需从"解耦"角度重新设计。

Method: DMC从基类训练得到的提示出发，分别训练专门化提示与泛化提示；在文本特征空间构建线性模式连接（LMC）走廊，使两端点间任意插值分类器的分类损失保持较低；专门化阶段加入Visual Anchor KL项，保持每张图像上CLIP的零样本类相似度分布。测试时以固定系数合并两个提示的文本特征，推理成本等同单提示。作者进一步提出正则项应满足类置换不变性（CPI），并借助Fano不等式在理想化模型下证明：违反CPI会使新类精度下降的下界正比于提示与基类标签间的互信息。

Result: 在11个数据集上，DMC对CoOp与KgCoOp均提升了基类/新类精度的调和平均，优于MergeTune；其插值曲线可越过单提示工作点，即移动了基类—新类前沿而非沿此前沿权衡。

Conclusion: 双提示解耦加特征空间模式连接，可在单提示推理成本下缓解基类专门化与零样本泛化的梯度冲突；CPI视角为提示正则设计提供了理论依据，说明应避免引入与基类标签相关的信息。

Abstract: Prompt learning adapts vision-language models such as CLIP by optimizing a short learnable prompt. The cross-entropy objective pulls this prompt toward
  base-class specialization, whereas generalization to unseen classes benefits from staying close to CLIP's zero-shot feature space. Because both objectives
  act on the same prompt, their gradients conflict at convergence, and in our experiments no single-prompt loss design clearly improves on the cosine
  baseline. We propose Decoupled Mode Connectivity (DMC), which gives each objective its own prompt. Starting from a base-trained prompt, DMC trains a
  specialization prompt and a generalization prompt, and merges their text features with a fixed coefficient at test time, at the inference cost of a single
  prompt. A linear mode connectivity (LMC) corridor in text-feature space keeps the classification loss low for every classifier interpolated between the two
  endpoints, and a Visual Anchor KL term preserves CLIP's zero-shot class-similarity distribution of each image during specialization. We further argue that
  such regularizers should satisfy class-permutation invariance (CPI), and show via Fano's inequality, in an idealized model, that violating CPI
  lower-bounds the drop in novel accuracy by a term proportional to the mutual information between the prompt and the base-class labels. Across 11 datasets,
  DMC improves the average harmonic mean of base and novel accuracy over MergeTune for both CoOp and KgCoOp, and its interpolation curves pass beyond the
  single-prompt operating point, shifting the base-novel frontier rather than trading along it. Code is available at https://github.com/IemProg/DMC.

</details>


### [79] [Video2Asset: Online Construction of Persistent 3D Street-Scene Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09661)
*Zihan Zheng,Chunlei Cai,Zhenlong Wu,Linjun Li,Yichen Gong,Xiaoyun Zhang,Qiang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video2Asset是一个在线多视角视频转三维街景资产的框架，以occupancy为中间表示，通过历史载体引导的实例解码与身份编码器构建身份感知观测，并用累积证据关联实现跨时间身份继承与世界坐标状态更新，从而在运动与遮挡下维持资产身份一致，支持网格替换、删除与轨迹编辑等场景操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D街景资产构建需将多视角视频转化为可持续更新、可独立编辑的对象级场景。核心难点在于：运动与遮挡导致观测是部分且间断的，容易造成同一对象身份被割裂（碎片化）或不同对象的证据被错误混合。因此需要一种能跨时间累积证据、保持身份持久性的在线框架。

Method: 以occupancy作为中间表示：(1) 构建身份感知观测——采用Historical Carrier Guidance进行实例解码，并以身份编码器融合实例对齐的3D特征与多视角外观特征；(2) Accumulated-Evidence Association——将当前观测与资产记录中累积的身份、几何、外观证据进行比对，判定身份继承并更新世界坐标下的状态；(3) 基于资产库中的状态实例化网格，并与occupancy导出的静态背景结合，支持场景可视化、网格替换、对象删除与轨迹编辑。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在时间身份一致性与资产质量上均有提升，且可适配多种occupancy模型；在nuScenes上配合SGE实现资产碎片化降低15.4%。

Conclusion: 以occupancy为中间表示、结合身份感知观测与累积证据关联的在线框架，能够有效缓解运动与遮挡下的身份碎片化与证据混淆问题，构建出持久、可编辑的3D街景资产，并具备跨occupancy模型的通用性。

Abstract: Online 3D street-scene asset construction turns multi-view video into a continually updated scene of independently editable objects. The central challenge is to accumulate partial, intermittent observations under motion and occlusion without fragmenting identities or mixing evidence across objects. To address this challenge, we propose Video2Asset, an online framework for constructing persistent 3D assets from multi-view video. Using occupancy as an intermediate representation, we construct identity-aware observations with Historical Carrier Guidance for instance decoding and an identity encoder fusing instance-aligned 3D features with multi-view appearance. Accumulated-Evidence Association compares current observations with identity, geometric, and appearance evidence accumulated within asset records to determine identity inheritance and update world-coordinate states. We instantiate meshes from an asset library using these states and combine them with an occupancy-derived static background to support scene visualization, mesh replacement, object deletion, and trajectory editing. Experiments on several datasets demonstrate improvements in temporal identity consistency and asset quality across multiple occupancy models, including a 15.4% reduction in asset fragmentation on nuScenes with SGE.

</details>


### [80] [Dual Architecture Fusion Vehicle Lidar 3D Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09664)
*Muyun Li,Weizheng Jin,Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合3D稀疏卷积分支与Transformer分支的骨干网络SpconvTransformer，通过局部与全局特征互补解决3D卷积感受野受限问题，在3D检测与BEV检测任务上分别达到81.45%和88.09%的平均精度，平均方向相似度91.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D卷积神经网络受限于有限的感受野，难以建模体素间的远距离依赖关系，导致上下文信息捕获不足。

Method: 构建双分支骨干网络：卷积分支采用3D稀疏卷积与子流形卷积学习局部体素特征及语义信息；Transformer分支利用自注意力机制建模体素间远程关系，以大感受野捕获丰富上下文；最后融合两分支的局部特征与全局特征。

Result: 在3D目标检测任务上平均精度达81.45%，鸟瞰图（BEV）检测任务达88.09%，平均方向相似度达91.10%，显著优于现有其他算法。

Conclusion: 局部卷积特征与全局自注意力特征的融合能有效突破3D卷积感受野限制，验证了卷积-Transformer混合架构在3D点云/体素感知任务中的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, a 3D backbone network (SpconvTransformer) based on the fusion of convolutional architecture branch and Transformer architecture branch is proposed. The convolution branch uses 3D sparse convolution and submanifold convolution to learn local voxel features and semantic information, and the Transformer branch uses the self-attention mechanism to model the remote relationship between voxels, uses the broad receptive field to capture rich context information, and complementing the advantages of the two architectures by fusing the local features extracted from the two branches and the global features solves the problem of limited receptive fields of 3D convolutional neural networks. The experimental results show that the average accuracy of SpconvTransformer in 3D object detection and bird's-eye view detection tasks reaches 81.45% and 88.09%, respectively, and the average directional similarity reaches 91.10%, which is significantly better than other existing algorithms.

</details>


### [81] [PoseRieFlow: Riemannian Flow Matching for Whole-Body Text-to-Pose Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09692)
*Jan Spindler,Bardia Ardakanian,Patrick Stotko,Reinhard Klein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PoseRieFlow：一个紧凑的流匹配框架，在SMPL-H关节旋转的乘积流形上直接进行文本条件的全身3D人体姿态生成，以21.6M可训练参数实现含精细手部关节的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文本到姿态方法要么忽略关节手部，要么在欧氏空间中建模本属非欧几何的旋转，忽略旋转的流形结构。

Method: 将生成问题定义在SMPL-H关节旋转的乘积流形上，学习文本条件的向量场，沿黎曼测地线把各向同性SO(3)高斯先验传输到数据分布；采用部位分词化的Diffusion Transformer，将52个关节聚合为8个身体部位token，为每只手分配专属token与非线性投影器，再通过部位特定解码器恢复逐关节分辨率。

Result: 在PoseScript上，于所有生成手部的方法中取得最低的全身运动学误差；在每个实用推理预算下，黎曼表述相较其欧氏对照均带来一致的运​​动学增益，而分布与检索指标二者相当。

Conclusion: 流形上的流匹配结合部位级token化，可在远小于近期LLM类生成器的规模下实现富有表现力且含手部细节的SMPL-H生成，验证了黎曼几何建模对运动学保真度的价值。

Abstract: We present PoseRieFlow, a compact flow-matching framework for text-conditioned generation of whole-body 3D human poses. Prior text-to-pose methods either neglect articulated hands or model rotations in Euclidean space despite their non-Euclidean geometry. PoseRieFlow instead formulates generation directly on the product manifold of SMPL-H joint rotations, learning a text-conditioned vector field that transports samples from an isotropic $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ Gaussian prior to the data distribution along Riemannian geodesics. A part-tokenized Diffusion Transformer aggregates the 52 joints into eight body-part tokens, allocating a dedicated token and nonlinear projector to each hand, and restores per-joint resolution through part-specific decoders, shortening the sequence while improving kinematic fidelity over per-joint tokenization. With only 21.6M trainable parameters, PoseRieFlow supports expressive SMPL-H generation with detailed hand articulation at a fraction of the scale of recent LLM-based generators. On PoseScript, it attains the lowest whole-body kinematic error among all hand-producing methods, with consistent kinematic gains from the Riemannian formulation over its Euclidean counterpart at every practical inference budget, while distributional and retrieval metrics remain comparable between them.

</details>


### [82] [Direct Regression and Geometric Recovery for Event-based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09717)
*Pruthvi Nandan Janga,Kashyap Hegde Kota,Bharatesh Chakravarthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在统一SPARK 2026隐藏测试协议下系统比较了两种"合成到真实"事件相机6-DoF航天器位姿估计方案，发现总体得分掩盖了分量层面的互补性：直接回归方案姿态（旋转）误差更低，而模块化方案平移误差显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备高时间分辨率与高动态范围，适用于非合作航天器位姿估计；但基于合成事件流训练的模型必须泛化到物理传感器的噪声、背景活动与对比度变化，因此需要厘清不同建模范式在合成到真实迁移中的实际表现与失效模式。

Method: 采用对比性系统研究：系统A为模块化流水线，学习式关键点检测+无标签目标域自适应+几何位姿恢复+时间平滑；系统B为直接回归，基于全局与裁剪事件表示、表观尺度线索、物理启发的合成损坏（corruption）与特征一致性正则，直接回归平移与旋转。二者在同一SPARK 2026隐藏测试协议下、在SPADES真实事件划分上对所有测试帧进行评估。

Result: 聚合挑战分数掩盖了分量反转：直接回归将姿态误差降低22.7%（1.1767→0.9101 rad），综合得分低13.4%；模块化流水线将相对平移误差降低78.7%（0.1335→0.0285）。存档配置与一个定性失败案例提供了对这两种互补误差分布的合理解释，但未分离其成因。

Conclusion: 两方案呈互补优势，单一聚合指标不足以反映系统特性；据此提出可检验的混合设计——直接姿态估计结合几何约束的平移恢复，作为后续工作方向。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and dynamic range for uncooperative spacecraft pose estimation, but models trained on synthetic event streams must generalize to the noise, background activity, and contrast variation of physical sensors. We present a comparative systems study of two independently developed approaches to synthetic-to-real, event-based 6-DoF spacecraft pose estimation. The first is a modular pipeline that combines learned keypoint detection and unlabeled target-domain adaptation with geometry-based pose recovery and temporal smoothing. The second directly regresses translation and rotation from global and cropped event representations using an apparent-scale cue, physics-motivated synthetic corruptions, and feature-consistency regularization. We evaluate both systems under the same SPARK 2026 hidden-test protocol on the real-event split of SPADES, with predictions for all test frames. The results reveal a component-wise reversal concealed by the aggregate challenge score: direct regression reduces orientation error by 22.7% (1.1767 to 0.9101 rad) and obtains a 13.4% lower composite score, whereas the modular pipeline reduces relative translation error by 78.7% (0.1335 to 0.0285). Archived configurations and a qualitative failure case identify plausible explanations for these complementary error profiles, without isolating their causes. These findings motivate a testable hybrid design that combines direct orientation estimation with geometry-constrained translation recovery.

</details>


### [83] [Evaluating Anterior Cruciate Ligament Risk of Injury Using a Graph Convolutional Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09724)
*Athanasios Babouras,Paul A. Martineau,Thomas Fevens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种完全在智能手机上运行的无监督学习模型，基于多层图卷积自编码器，利用姿态估计数据对DVJ和SLH两项测试判定ACL损伤风险状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ACL损伤风险评估依赖昂贵的动作捕捉实验室（资源投入大）或专家对特定运动应激测试的主观评价，缺乏可广泛获取的客观筛查手段。

Method: 构建无监督学习模型：以自编码器为框架，编码器/解码器由多层图卷积网络构成，输入为姿态估计数据（骨骼关键点图结构），端侧部署于智能手机，覆盖Drop Vertical Jump（DVJ）与Single Leg Hop（SLH）两项任务，输出个体损伤风险状态。

Result: DVJ任务平均灵敏度78%、特异度78%；SLH任务平均灵敏度75%、特异度74%，即两项应激测试均能较准确识别高风险运动员。

Conclusion: 结果表明可基于普通智能手机实现ACL损伤风险的实时、可及性筛查，支持该方案的进一步开发与推广。

Abstract: Current solutions for assessing the risk of anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) injury typically rely on expensive motion-capture labs that require substantial resource investment or subjective evaluations of specific athletic stress tests by experts. The Drop Vertical Jump (DVJ) and the Single Leg Hop (SLH) are two examples of such tests typically used to track progress in ACL rehabilitation. In this paper, we develop an unsupervised learning model that runs entirely on a smartphone for two tasks, based on an autoencoder architecture with multiple layers of graph convolutional networks, which utilizes pose estimation data to determine a person's injury risk status. Our model accurately predicts athletes at high risk of injury for both athletic stress tests, with average sensitivities and specificities of 78% and 78% for the DVJ, and 75% and 74% for the SLH. Our findings support the development of a widely accessible smartphone solution for real-time screening of ACL injury risk.

</details>


### [84] [TriNetra: Thermal-Reliability-Guided RGB-Thermal Fusion and Tracking for Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09725)
*Sadique Khatib,Aditya Ahirrao,Vedant Sonawane,Disha Kapse,Y. D. Bhise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TriNetra——可靠性感知RGB-T监控框架，用无标签热成像可靠性系数(TRC)自适应门控热特征，提升热流损坏下的检测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间可见光相机失效，热传感器在饱和、模糊或低对比度下失效；现有RGB-T检测器多以固定权重融合双流，无法判断某一模态何时变得不可靠。故需按帧评估热模态可靠性并动态调节其贡献。

Method: 由五个无标签图像线索计算帧级热可靠性系数TRC；用TRC门控双分支YOLOv8m检测器的热特征（可见分支、neck、head冻结并采用COCO预训练，仅训练部分参数）；同一分数传入基于ByteTrack的跟踪器，驱动规则式行为分析。

Result: M3FD(2940/630/630)测试集：87.6% P、79.4% R、70.0% mAP@50、47.8% mAP@50:95，42.9M参数中仅训练17.0M。TRC在干净帧≈0.78(SD 0.012)，随热饱和与模糊加剧下降至0.53与0.69，对RGB-T错位不敏感。热流损坏时自适应门控保持43.6% mAP@50，优于固定门控38.4%、全量热输入29.2%、关闭门控25.0%；相对固定门控提升5.1点(95% bootstrap CI +3.6~+6.5)。跟踪上TRC门控关联与ByteTrack持平，热饱和下略不稳定。

Conclusion: TRC可作为有效的无标签模态可靠性度量，自适应门控在热流退化时显著优于固定或极端门控策略，收益集中于检测端；跟踪端未见明显增益。作者同时报告了当前评估的局限性。

Abstract: Visible cameras fail at night and thermal sensors fail under saturation, blur or loss of contrast, yet most RGB-thermal (RGB-T) detectors mix the two streams with fixed weights and cannot tell when one modality has become unreliable. TriNetra is a reliability-aware surveillance framework built around this problem. The proposed solution computes a frame-level Thermal Reliability Coefficient (TRC) from five label-free image cues, uses it to gate the thermal features of a dual-branch YOLOv8m detector whose visible branch, neck and head are frozen and COCO-pretrained, and passes the same score to a ByteTrack-based tracker that feeds a rule-based behaviour analyser. On the M3FD benchmark (2,940/630/630 split) the detector reaches 87.6% precision, 79.4% recall, 70.0% mAP@50 and 47.8% mAP@50:95 on the test split, with 17.0 M of its 42.9 M parameters trained. TRC stays near 0.78 on clean frames (standard deviation 0.012), decreases with the severity of thermal saturation and blur, reaching 0.53 and 0.69 at full severity, and is insensitive to RGB-thermal misalignment. With identical weights, the adaptive gate matches a fixed gate on clean data (70.0% versus 69.8% mAP@50) but keeps 43.6% mAP@50 when the thermal stream is corrupted, against 38.4%, 29.2% and 25.0% for a fixed gate, full thermal admission and a closed gate; the 5.1-point gain over the fixed gate has a 95% bootstrap confidence interval of +3.6 to +6.5 points. In tracking, the TRC-gated association performs on par with ByteTrack on a synthetic benchmark with exact ground truth and on 14 M3FD clips, and is slightly less stable under thermal saturation, so its measured benefit is confined to the detector. The limitations of the current evaluation are reported together with the results.

</details>


### [85] [Zero-Shot Benchmarking of Unified Multimodal Models for Shallow-Water UAV Image Distortion Assessment and Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09740)
*Dario Scilla,Omar A. Lopez,Mattia Rosso,Kasper Johansen,Matthew F. McCabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个针对统一多模态大模型（MLLM）的零样本基准，用于在同一框架下评估浅水畸变与浅水图像复原，结果显示识别畸变不等于能可靠恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅水无人机影像可提供厘米级底栖观测，但水体导致的畸变（颜色衰减、散射等）使解译困难；需检验统一MLLM能否在同一模型内既判断畸变又完成物理复原，且缺乏可复现的零样本基准。

Method: 构建1,000对影像基准：600对由真实浅水影像施加合成水体畸变得到的半合成场景，400对全合成穿水场景；三类独立人工标注覆盖5种畸变类型。评估5个开放权重统一MLLM（冻结预训练权重，无配对参考图与真实海底描述）。评估4种评估提示P0–P3（从直接指令到结构化任务分解，全部1,000张）与4种诊断引导复原提示R0–R3（100张子集），并对比两个纯复原基线InstructIR与PromptFix。

Result: 评估性能随模型与提示差异显著，存在系统性预测偏差，畸变存在性判定的最佳合并平衡准确率仅0.654。复原方面最佳合并PSNR增益为+0.255 dB，无统一MLLM改善合并LPIPS，部分输出改变场景结构或产生内容幻觉。InstructIR合并PSNR增益仅+0.040 dB，PromptFix在所有提示下四项合并指标均退化。

Conclusion: 统一MLLM在当前零样本设定下对浅水畸变的识别能力有限，且识别畸变并不意味着具备可靠物理复原能力；该基准为后续研究提供可复现的基线。

Abstract: Shallow-water UAV imagery provides centimeter-scale observations of benthic environments, but water-induced distortions complicate interpretation. To our knowledge, we present the first systematic zero-shot benchmark of unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for both assessing water-induced distortions and restoring shallow-water imagery within the same model framework. We evaluate five open-weight unified MLLMs with frozen pretrained weights and without access to paired reference images or descriptions of the true seabed. The benchmark contains 1,000 image pairs: 600 semi-synthetic UAV scenes created by applying synthetic water distortions to real shallow-water imagery and 400 fully synthetic through-water scenes. Three independent human annotations cover five distortion types. We evaluate four assessment prompts (P0-P3), from direct instruction to structured task decomposition, on all 1,000 images and four diagnosis-guided restoration prompts (R0-R3) on a common 100-image subset. Assessment varies substantially by model and prompt, with systematic prediction biases and a best pooled balanced accuracy of 0.654 for distortion presence. The best pooled PSNR gain is +0.255 dB, no unified MLLM improves pooled LPIPS, and some outputs alter scene structure or hallucinate content. Two restoration-only baselines also show limited transfer: InstructIR achieves a pooled PSNR gain of +0.040 dB, while PromptFix degrades all four pooled metrics under every prompt. These results show that recognizing shallow-water degradation does not imply reliable physical restoration and provide a reproducible baseline for future work.

</details>


### [86] [A lightweight convolutional neural network for target detection in an edge environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09756)
*Hughes Perreault,Ola Ahmad,Daniel Czuboka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向边缘设备部署的轻量级CNN，结合实时热点检测算法，实现热成像场景下目标/威胁的快速识别，推理速度达20 fps、探测距离超过400米，并具备高精度、高灵敏度与低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备算力与功耗受限，难以承载常规深度学习模型；同时安防/监视场景需要在远距离、实时条件下可靠检测潜在目标，因此需要轻量、易部署且低误报的检测方案。

Method: 设计轻量级卷积神经网络用于目标与威胁的分类识别；在模型中集成实时热点检测算法以加速信息处理；基于典型热成像态势感知传感器进行测试与验证。

Result: 推理速度最高达20 fps，探测距离超过400米（配合典型热成像传感器）；实验验证显示高准确率、高灵敏度与低误报率，且模型轻量、部署便捷。

Conclusion: 该轻量级CNN可作为边缘设备扩展安全警戒范围的可靠方案，适用于监视、安防与目标识别等多种应用场景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel, lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for edge device deployment. The CNN is developed to recognize and classify threats or targets of interest. The model is equipped with a real-time hotspot detection algorithm, which enables it to process information quickly and accurately, resulting in improved inference time of up to 20 fps and a detection range of more than 400 meters with typical thermal situational awareness sensors. The proposed CNN has been rigorously tested and validated to demonstrate its high accuracy, sensitivity, and low false positive rate, providing a reliable solution for edge devices to detect potential targets. Additionally, the lightweight design of the CNN allows for easy deployment, making it an ideal solution for extending the security bubble for edge devices. The proposed model can be used for a variety of applications, including surveillance, security, and object recognition.

</details>


### [87] [AR-Edit: Training-Free Autoregressive Video Editing without Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09795)
*Hovhannes Margaryan,Quentin Bammey,Christian Sandor,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AR-Edit 通过分析蒸馏自回归视频扩散模型中自注意力的非对称性，发现仅需一次前向即可在 value 特征中捕获源身份，从而提出无需训练、无需反演的实时流式视频编辑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式应用需要在不访问未来帧的情况下实时编辑视频流。现有免训练视频编辑方法假设可离线访问完整序列；流式方法则依赖额外训练或仅限于特定编辑技术，缺乏通用的免训练流式编辑方案。

Method: 分析 DMD 蒸馏的 AR 视频扩散模型的自注意力动态，揭示关键非对称性：query 与 key 在去噪时间步上稳定且与源对应，而 value 发散并编码演化内容；该非对称性继承自未蒸馏的双向教师模型，在高噪声（少步流式模型工作区）最显著。据此设计 AR-Edit：一次前向缓存源 value，在去噪过程中选择性注入，从纯高斯噪声出发即可重建输入，无需反演。

Result: 从纯高斯噪声出发仅注入源 value 即可重建输入，保真度匹配或超越基于反演的方法；在多个 AR 骨干上，AR-Edit 以极小开销实现实时流式编辑，源保持与色调保真度更优。

Conclusion: AR-Edit 证明免训练、免反演的实时流式视频编辑可行，其核心是从模型内部注意力机制中挖掘可复用的源 value 特征，为流式编辑提供了简单通用的新范式。

Abstract: Editing a video stream in real time, without access to future frames, is essential for interactive applications. Existing training-free video editing methods, however, assume full offline access to the entire sequence, while streaming approaches either rely on additional training or are limited to specific editing techniques. We show that this limitation can be removed by analyzing the self-attention dynamics of DMD-distilled autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. We uncover a key asymmetry: queries and keys remain stable and aligned with their source counterparts across denoising timesteps, while values diverge and encode the evolving content. Inherited from the non-distilled bidirectional teacher, this asymmetry is strongest at high noise, where few-step streaming models operate. The resulting mechanism is simple: a single forward pass suffices to capture source identity in the value features, which can be reused throughout generation. Starting from pure Gaussian noise and injecting only these source values reconstructs the input with fidelity matching or surpassing inversion-based methods, effectively eliminating inversion. Based on this insight, we introduce AR-Edit, a training-free, inversion-free method that caches source values once and selectively injects them during denoising. Across multiple AR backbones, AR-Edit enables real-time streaming editing with superior source preservation and tonal fidelity at minimal overhead.

</details>


### [88] [BEV3SR: Evidence-Aware 3D Spatial Reasoning from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09816)
*Fan Zhu,Rui Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEV3SR：将RGB-D视频转为可查询的度量BEV场景记忆，并按证据类型分流为几何计算或视觉对齐的训练免、骨干无关框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频VLM的3D空间推理受限于：视频仅提供序贯、视角局部的观测而非持久度量场景记录；许多问题需在查询指定视角而非观测相机视角下回答；固定RGB视图提示易遗漏稀疏或被遮挡的视觉线索。

Method: 训练免、骨干无关，输入RGB-D视频+相机参数。构建度量BEV场景记忆；Spatial Task Formalizer显式解析查询的参考系、空间约束、目标与证据需求，并据此选择推理路径：判为几何证据的查询由Geometric Reasoner在查询指定参考系下用BEV度量记录确定性求解；判为视觉证据的查询交由Visual Evidence Aligner，借助BEV索引的物体记录及关联支持视图检索物体一致与场景上下文的RGB帧供VLM接地。

Result: 在标准16帧预算下，于多个3D场景问答基准上对不同VLM骨干均稳定提升；空间密集问题上增益显著，开放式场景问答亦有明显改进。

Conclusion: BEV3SR为从视频进行显式3D空间推理提供了有效、高效且可解释的框架。

Abstract: 3D spatial reasoning from video remains challenging for VLMs because videos provide sequential, view-local observations rather than persistent metric scene records. Many questions must be answered in a query-specified perspective rather than the observed camera viewpoint, and prompting with a fixed set of RGB views may miss sparse or occluded visual cues. To address these gaps, we introduce BEV3SR, a training-free and backbone-agnostic framework for RGB-D videos with camera parameters. BEV3SR turns video observations into queryable metric bird's-eye-view (BEV) scene memory and formalizes each query into an evidence-aware spatial task. The Spatial Task Formalizer specifies the query's reference frame, spatial constraints, objective, and evidence requirement. The evidence requirement determines the appropriate reasoning pathway. A query treated as requiring geometric evidence is handled by the Geometric Reasoner, which deterministically computes the answer using the metric BEV records in the query-specified reference frame. Otherwise, a query treated as requiring visual evidence is passed to the Visual Evidence Aligner, which uses BEV-indexed object records and their associated supporting views to retrieve object-consistent and scene-context RGB frames for VLM grounding. Within a standard 16-frame budget, BEV3SR consistently improves different VLM backbones across multiple 3D scene QA benchmarks, delivering large gains on spatially intensive questions and significant improvements on open-ended scene QA. These results demonstrate that BEV3SR offers an effective, efficient, and interpretable framework for explicit 3D spatial reasoning from video.

</details>


### [89] [Health-System-Wide Mapping of Demographic Information in Routine Clinical Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09851)
*Shengjia Chen,Ruchika Verma,Kevin Clare,Paula Restrepo,Jannes Jegminat,Eugenia Alleva,Kuan-lin Huang,Aryeh Stock,Brandon Veremis,Thomas Fuchs,Gabriele Campanella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基于130万张H&E全切片图像（25个器官、257,322名患者），利用病理基础模型表示与多实例学习，系统绘制了性别、年龄组与自我报告种族等信息在常规组织病理中的可检测性图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口统计学相关信息在常规组织病理学中跨器官、跨疾病状态的可检测程度尚不明确；厘清其来源有助于识别潜在的捷径学习（shortcut learning）并提升计算病理模型的可解释性。

Method: 构建大规模H&E全切片数据集（25器官、257,322患者、约130万WSI）；采用病理基础模型表征结合多实例学习（MIL），在69个器官—人口学组合上预测性别、年龄组与自我报告种族；并进行人口学类别平衡实验、按诊断文本聚类内评估，以及基于诊断分组的训练设定验证；辅以文献引导的组织形态学与上下文解释分析。

Result: 属性整体可恢复：全器官平均AUROC为性别0.828、年龄组0.733、自我报告种族0.662，且器官间差异显著。人口学类别平衡对性能影响较小；诊断文本聚类内评估出现轻度衰减。按诊断分组训练后信号仍可检测，如胃组织性别AUROC 0.865、乳腺年龄组0.816、皮肤自我报告种族0.728。

Conclusion: 研究提供了常规组织病理中人口学相关信息的系统性图谱，为追溯其来源、评估潜在捷径学习风险以及开发更可解释的计算病理模型提供了基础依据。

Abstract: The extent to which demographic-associated information is detectable across organs and disease states in routine clinical histopathology remains unclear. Here, we analyzed 1.3 million hematoxylin and eosin whole-slide images from 257,322 patients across 25 organs, using pathology foundation-model representations and multiple-instance learning to systematically map information associated with sex, age group, and self-reported race. Across 69 organ-demographic combinations, demographic attributes were broadly recoverable, with pan-organ mean AUROCs of 0.828 for sex, 0.733 for age group, and 0.662 for self-reported race, and substantial variation across organs. Demographic-class balancing produced relatively small changes in performance, while evaluation within diagnosis-text clusters showed modest attenuation. Under training with diagnosis-based grouping, demographic-associated signals remained detectable across multiple organs, including sex in gastric tissue (AUROC 0.865), age group in breast (0.816), and self-reported race in skin (0.728). Literature-guided analysis of these settings generated candidate histomorphological and contextual explanations for further investigation. Together, these findings provide a systematic map of demographic-associated information in routine histopathology, informing investigations of its sources, assessments of potential shortcut learning, and the development of more interpretable computational pathology models.

</details>


### [90] [GRAC: Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning for Surgical Scene Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09866)
*Rochak Dhakal,Chen Zhao,Soup Cobb,Xin Qian,Xiaofeng Yang,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRAC框架，将手术场景表示为图并检索相似图-文本对，用器械-组织交互比较特征条件化生成，显著提升手术场景描述质量与事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术记录的信息提取依赖大量人工审阅与临床专业知识，自动化手术场景描述可利用重复出现的手术活动与先验知识来减轻该负担。

Method: 以图结构表示手术场景，刻画器械、动作与解剖结构之间的关系；给定输入场景图，学习从训练数据中检索相关图-文本对，比较检索样例与输入场景的器械-组织交互，并以比较特征条件化检索文本表示用于生成。

Result: EndoVis-18上CIDEr-D为5.88（SGT 4.23，最强传统Transformer基线3.36）；器械-动作-目标三元组集合精确准确率52.6%（SGT 32.7%，最强基线26.2%）。TORS上CIDEr-D为5.06（最强基线3.80）。

Conclusion: 结构化手术场景表示与检索样例相结合可提升手术场景描述质量，生成更具上下文意义且事实更一致的描述。

Abstract: Purpose: Surgical operative reports support procedural documentation, surgical training, performance assessment, and workflow review. However, extracting meaningful information often requires extensive manual review and clinical expertise. Automated surgical scene descriptions could reduce this burden by leveraging recurring surgical activities and prior knowledge.
  Methods: We propose GRAC (Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning), a framework that represents surgical scenes as graphs capturing relationships among instruments, actions, and anatomical structures. Given an input scene graph, GRAC learns to retrieve relevant graph-caption pairs from training data. It compares instrument-tissue interactions between retrieved examples and the input scene, using comparison features to condition retrieved-caption representations during generation.
  Results: On the EndoVis-18 benchmark, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.88, compared with 4.23 for SGT and 3.36 for the strongest conventional Transformer baseline. Exact instrument-action-target triplet-set accuracy reached 52.6%, compared with 32.7% for SGT and 26.2% for the strongest conventional baseline. These results demonstrate improvements in language quality and factual agreement. On TORS, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.06, compared with 3.80 for the strongest baseline.
  Conclusion: GRAC demonstrates that combining structured surgical scene representations with retrieved examples improves surgical scene captioning and produces more contextually meaningful descriptions.

</details>


### [91] [Graded convolutional networks: scale invariance without the scale and robustness to unknown blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09878)
*Tanush Shaska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将图像的高斯射流视为分次向量空间，证明膨胀不变的局部特征恰为经"收缩映射"（retraction）到加权球面分解的特征；据此构造的网络对高斯模糊具有更好的不变性，在MNIST上显著优于尺度归一化导数方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尺度归一化导数（如Lindeberg高斯导数网络）在未知高斯模糊下仍会残留分次因子，径向归一化无法消除；需要从射流的分次结构出发，寻找真正对膨胀不变、且对模糊更稳定的局部特征表示。

Method: 把图像高斯射流建模为分次向量空间（λ膨胀使k阶导数乘λ^{-k}）；指出局部分次卷积即齐次微分算子；证明膨胀不变局部特征当且仅当经尺度归一化射流因子化，或无尺度时经加权球面上的收缩映射因子化；分析模糊下收缩映射仅改变尺度，而尺度归一化射流引入不可去除的分次因子；在零射流处构造可分离性论证；证明基于收缩映射的网络对不变特征具有通用性。

Result: 在MNIST上，基于收缩映射的网络在两像素模糊下保持88.4%准确率，同一网络用尺度归一化导数仅66.2%；在Lindeberg高斯导数网络中引入收缩映射使该模糊下准确率由91.5%提升至95.1%。

Conclusion: 射流的分次几何给出了膨胀不变特征的完整刻画：收缩映射是比尺度归一化导数更合适的归一化方式，能有效提升网络对未知高斯模糊的鲁棒性，并为不变特征网络提供通用性保证。

Abstract: The Gaussian jet of an image is a graded vector space: a dilation by $λ$ multiplies derivatives of order $k$ by $λ^{-k}$. Local graded convolutions are exactly homogeneous differential operators, and a local feature is dilation-invariant if and only if it factors through the scale-normalized jet or, without the scale, through the retraction onto the weighted sphere of the grading. Under unknown Gaussian blur the retraction changes only by a change of scale, whereas the scale-normalized jet acquires a grading factor that no radial normalization removes. At the zero jet the retraction separates image families that radial normalizations and continuous classifiers cannot. Networks built on the retraction are universal for invariant features. On MNIST such a network keeps 88.4% accuracy under a two-pixel blur, against 66.2% for the same network on scale-normalized derivatives; in Lindeberg's Gaussian derivative network the retraction raises accuracy at that blur from 91.5% to 95.1%.

</details>


### [92] [ZeoBench: A Benchmark for Self-Supervised Learning on 3D Zeolite Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09880)
*Aaron Sun,Yachan Liu,Ping Yang,Peng Bai,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ZeoBench 基准，系统比较自监督 3D 表示学习、2D 图像预训练模型迁移、域外 3D 预训练及手工描述符在全硅沸石吸附性质预测中的表现，发现自监督 3D 表示在低数据与跨域（MOF）场景下达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沸石结构数量庞大，但性质标签昂贵且仅覆盖有限的结构-分子对，导致面向目标应用的材料筛选困难；已有工作证明 3D 神经表示可有效预测沸石性质，但在材料发现相关的低数据条件下，何种表示学习策略最有效仍不明确。

Method: 构建 ZeoBench：基于全硅沸石稠密 3D 体数据的自监督预训练与评测框架；在六个吸附性质预测下游任务上、跨多种训练集规模进行评测。对比包括：(1) 体数据自监督 3D 预训练；(2) 通过多视图与通道膨胀将图像预训练 2D CNN/ViT 迁移到 3D；(3) 域外体数据预训练的 3D CNN；(4) 手工描述符与监督基线。

Result: 自监督 3D 表示优于现有方法，在域内标签受限条件及未见过的 MOF 域上均达到 SOTA；学习到的表示稳定优于手工描述符；该设定下卷积架构总体优于视觉 Transformer。

Conclusion: ZeoBench 可作为沸石 3D 表示学习的实用基准，并为数据受限条件下的吸附性质预测提供策略指导。

Abstract: Zeolites are an important class of porous crystalline materials with broad applications in gas storage, separations, and catalysis. Although hundreds of thousands of experimentally realized and hypothetical zeolite structures are known, identifying materials optimized for a target application remains challenging because property labels are expensive to obtain and available only for limited structure-molecule pairs. While prior work has demonstrated that 3D neural representations can predict zeolite properties effectively, it remains unclear which representation-learning strategies are most effective in the low-data regime relevant to materials discovery. We present ZeoBench, a benchmark for self-supervised learning on dense 3D representations of all-silica zeolites, and evaluate the resulting features on six existing downstream adsorption-property prediction tasks, across a range of training set sizes. Our study compares self-supervised 3D pretraining on volumetric zeolite data, adaptations of image-pretrained 2D ConvNets and vision transformers to 3D via multiview and channel-inflation strategies, and 3D ConvNets pretrained on out-of-domain volumetric datasets, alongside hand-crafted descriptors and supervised baselines. Using our pretraining and evaluation framework, we find self-supervised 3D representations outperform existing methods and achieve state-of-the-art performance across both in-domain label regimes and for the unseen domain of metal organic frameworks. Learned representations consistently outperform hand-crafted descriptors, and convolutional architectures are generally more effective than vision transformers in this setting. These results establish ZeoBench as a practical benchmark for 3D representation learning in zeolites and provide guidance for data-limited adsorption property prediction.

</details>


### [93] [FlowMoP: Stochastic Multi-Person Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09882)
*Aadya Agrawal,Ho Kei Cheng,Alexander Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出条件流匹配框架联合生成多人多样化未来3D运动，结合B样条重参数化与双流Transformer社交条件建模，在多个数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人未来运动本质上是随机的：同一观测历史可能对应多种合理延续，取决于意图、协同与社会语境。但主流多人3D运动预测仍是确定性范式，每个agent只输出固定数量结果，无法刻画预测分布。

Method: 条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）联合生成多agent多样未来运动，同时建模全局轨迹与root-relative姿态；用B样条重参数化压缩生成状态空间并强制时间平滑；用双流Transformer以观测到的邻居运动为条件实现交互感知预测。

Result: 在CMU-Mocap (UMPM) 与3DPW上取得SOTA，尽管输出完整预测分布，仍在关节位置误差、姿态误差、末端位移误差上优于确定性基线；并在大规模职业足球数据集WorldPose上建立首个运动预测基线，验证社交条件能将预测不确定性集中于交互约束方向。

Conclusion: 生成式流匹配框架可同时实现多agent多样性、时间平滑与交互感知，在给出完整预测分布的同时超越确定性方法，并揭示社交条件对不确定性结构的塑造作用。

Abstract: Predicting the future motion of multiple people is inherently stochastic: the same observed history admits many plausible continuations depending on intent, coordination, and social context. Yet the dominant paradigm in multi-person 3D motion prediction remains deterministic, with existing methods producing a fixed number of outputs per agent. In contrast, we present a conditional flow matching framework that jointly generates diverse future motions for multiple agents, modeling both global trajectories and root-relative poses. A B-spline reparameterization compresses the generative state space while enforcing temporal smoothness, and a dual-stream transformer conditions each agent's predictions on observed neighbor motion for interaction-aware forecasting. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on CMU-Mocap (UMPM) and 3DPW, outperforming deterministic baselines on joint position error, pose error, and final displacement error despite producing a full predictive distribution. We additionally establish the first motion prediction baseline on WorldPose, a large-scale professional soccer dataset, where social conditioning demonstrably concentrates predictive uncertainty along interaction-constrained directions.

</details>


### [94] [Video-Coder: Reinforcing Agentic Coding for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09894)
*Chaoyang Wang,Sicheng Gao,Junan Hu,Kunyang Li,Dongyang Chen,Yiyang Zhou,Huaxiu Yao,Yong Jae Lee,Radu Timofte,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video-Coder：首个"代码驱动"的视频生成智能体，自主编写并执行代码构建动态三维场景（Draft Video），为下游视频生成器提供结构与运动信号；配套数据集、交互环境与两阶段训练（SFT + 带双奖励的智能体强化学习）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成在视觉质量与时间一致性上进步显著，但仍难以精确控制空间结构、运动动力学与场景构图。作者希望用可执行代码这一显式、可解释的中间表示来桥接"精确结构/运动意图"与"像素级视频生成"。

Method: 1) 数据：设计专用数据流水线，构建 Video-Coder-SFT-6k 与 Video-Coder-RL-2k，含多样提示与真值参考视频。2) 环境：提出 Video-Coder Harness，统一集成代码执行、三维场景渲染与执行反馈，用于训练与推理。3) 训练：先 SFT，再做 harness 原生智能体强化学习，采用执行奖励 + 视频奖励的双重反馈，并设计退火策略逐步降低执行奖励权重，使后期更依赖视频奖励。

Result: 作为下游生成器的结构/运动信号提供者，Video-Coder 使 Minimax-H3 在 PhyGenBench 上提升约 7 分，在 VBench-2.0 上提升约 24 分。

Conclusion: 代码驱动的视频生成智能体是一种有效范式，可作为该方向的开源基础；作者开源数据、模型与代码。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable progress in video generation in terms of visual quality and temporal consistency. However, generating videos with precise spatial structure, coherent motion dynamics, and controllable scene composition remains challenging. In this paper, we introduce Video-Coder, the first attempt to train a code-driven video generation agent, which autonomously writes and executes code to construct a dynamic 3D scene (Draft Video) that provides structure and motion signals to downstream video generators. To support this paradigm, we design a dedicated data pipeline and curate two high-quality datasets, Video-Coder-SFT-6k and Video-Coder-RL-2k, which contain diverse prompts and ground-truth reference videos. We further develop the Video- Coder Harness, a unified interactive environment integrating code execution, 3D scene rendering, and execution feedback for agent training and inference. We then train Video-Coder with SFT fol- lowed by harness-native agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward feedback, which combines execution-based and video-based rewards. Additionally, we design an annealing training strategy that gradually reduces the execution reward over time, allowing the model to rely more on the video reward in the later training stages. Experiments show that Video-Coder achieves significant gains, improving Minimax-H3 by approximately 7 points on PhyGenBench and 24 points on VBench-2.0. We hope this work can serve as an open foundation for coding agents in video generation, and we fully open-source our data, models, and code

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [FasterRW: An Efficient Prior-Assisted Random Walk Solver for Thermal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08490)
*Zixiao Wang,Tianshu Hou,Chenghan Wang,Zhen Zhuang,Leilei Jin,Tsung-Yi Ho,Farzan Farnia,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FasterRW 提出一种"延拓值"框架，通过停止时间残差恒等式与先验校正的单点估计器，加速无 Dirichlet 边界的 Feynman–Kac 热随机游走，实现最高 28 倍的加速且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热仿真在 IC 设计与制造中日益关键。Feynman–Kac 随机游走可在不求解全局场的情况下估计局部温度，但在实际缺乏 Dirichlet 边界的设定中，为控制截断残差需要极长的游走路径，计算代价过高。

Method: 1) 证明停止时间残差恒等式：截断后的期望尾部等于当前衰减权重乘以停止位置处的温度；2) 据此构造先验校正的单点估计器，用廉价、含噪的先验替代未知延拓值，使截断偏差随先验误差而非绝对温度缩放，并给出误差可控的阈值选择规则；3) 面向多点查询，将跨查询的穿透尾部转化为伪样本，并在先验误差坐标下用 kriging 对观测进行精化。

Result: 相较于先前最先进方法，FasterRW 在保持精度的前提下实现最高 28× 的加速；代码已开源（https://github.com/ShiningSord/FastRW）。

Conclusion: FasterRW 通过理论上的残差恒等式将截断偏差重新参数化到先验误差空间，并借助伪样本与 kriging 复用多查询信息，显著缩短所需路径长度，为无边界热随机游走提供了高效、误差可控的加速方案。

Abstract: Thermal simulation is becoming increasingly critical in modern IC design and manufacturing. Feynman--Kac random walk methods can estimate localized temperatures without solving the global field, but in practical settings without Dirichlet boundaries they require very long paths to control the truncation residual. This paper presents FasterRW, a continuation-value framework for accelerating Feynman--Kac thermal random walks. We prove a stopping-time residual identity showing that the expected tail after truncation equals the current attenuation weight multiplied by the temperature at the stopping location. This identity enables a prior-corrected single-point estimator: a cheap, noisy prior replaces the unknown continuation value, making the truncation bias scale with prior error rather than absolute temperature and yielding an error-controlled threshold-selection rule. For multi-point queries, FasterRW further converts cross-query pass-through tails into pseudo-samples and refines the resulting observations in prior-error coordinates through kriging. Compared with prior state-of-the-art methods, FasterRW achieves up to $28\times$ acceleration while maintaining accuracy. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/ShiningSord/FastRW.

</details>


### [96] [Embedding Consistent Recurrent Neural Networks in Finite Element Simulations for Path-Dependent Damage Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08494)
*Muhammed Adil Yatkın,Mihkel Kõrgesaar,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将训练好的RNN损伤准则用Fortran重写并嵌入Abaqus/Explicit的VUMAT中，逐增量推进网络状态；提出ConsRNN使预测随应变路径细化而收敛，解决了SimpleRNN随增量数漂移的问题，并在结构尺度钢板上实现首次非标准转移函数循环单元的嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐式/显式非线性求解器中嵌入代理模型时，预测结果必须与求解器的应变增量数目无关；但显式非线性代码中的增量数难以控制，SimpleRNN类循环代理的预测会随增量数变化而漂移，导致无法作为实时断裂准则使用。

Method: 在Fortran中重新实现训练好的RNN损伤准则，作为逐步增量式断裂准则嵌入Abaqus/Explicit的VUMAT，使网络状态与求解器同步推进。对比两种架构：SimpleRNN与所提出的ConsRNN——后者的转移函数经过专门设计，使应变路径细化时预测收敛而非随增量数漂移。两者在双线性应变路径上训练，并在不同时间离散化和非线性加载历史下评估；随后在结构尺度（夹持钢板）上以ConsRNN控制单元删除。

Result: SimpleRNN的预测随增量数增加而漂移，不具备嵌入资格；ConsRNN在细化下收敛，但在多线性路径上相对SimpleRNN存在明显的固定分辨率精度损失。结构尺度部署中，嵌入的ConsRNN达到与成熟的双参数断裂准则相同的峰值力和峰值位移；作者指出该峰值前一致性是构造上必然的（峰值前无单元删除，本构路径相同），两者仅在后峰值软化段分叉。

Conclusion: 离散化一致性是循环代理模型嵌入显式FEM求解器的决定性属性；本文首次在结构尺度上实现了具有非标准转移函数的定制循环单元的嵌入，为代理型断裂准则的工程部署提供了可行性依据，但精度—一致性的权衡仍需权衡处理。

Abstract: Capturing sheet-metal localisation physics with a trained surrogate inside a finite element (FEM) solver requires the surrogate to deliver consistent predictions irrespective of the solver's strain-increment count, which is difficult to control in non-linear explicit codes. We address this by re-implementing a trained recurrent neural network (RNN) damage criterion in Fortran and embed it as a live, increment-by-increment fracture criterion inside an Abaqus/Explicit user material subroutine (VUMAT), advancing the network state alongside the solver. Two architectures are compared: a SimpleRNN and the proposed Consistent RNN (ConsRNN), whose transition function is designed so that predictions converge as the strain path is refined rather than drifting with the increment count. Both are trained on bilinear strain paths and evaluated under varying temporal discretisations and nonlinear histories. SimpleRNN predictions drift as the increment count increases, which disqualifies it for embedding; ConsRNN converges under refinement, at a substantial fixed-resolution accuracy cost relative to SimpleRNN on multilinear paths. Deployed at structural scale, the embedded ConsRNN surrogate governs element deletion in a clamped steel plate and reaches the same peak force and peak displacement as an established two-parameter fracture criterion. This pre-peak agreement is expected by construction, since no element is deleted before the peak and the two runs therefore share an identical constitutive path; the responses diverge only on the post-peak softening branch. The results establish discretisation consistency as the decisive property for embedding recurrent surrogates in explicit FEM solvers, and report the first embedding of a customised, non-standard-transition recurrent cell at structural scale.

</details>


### [97] [Rethinking Data Selection as Distribution Matching on Semantic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08495)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Mingshuo Chen,Chunxiao Li,Yao Zhu,Huawen Shen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QMAC：将指令微调数据选择建模为分布匹配与质量过滤的联合优化问题，通过多频带经验特征函数匹配实现子集与全量数据在嵌入空间的一致性，并在4种数据类型、8个目标模型、44个基准上优于现有基线，部分设置超过全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型指令微调依赖海量训练数据，带来巨大计算与存储开销，且数据中存在冗余、噪声与低质量样本。现有数据选择方法多基于多样性、重要性或二者结合，未显式约束所选子集与全量数据在嵌入空间中的分布一致性，因而难以同时保证覆盖性与低噪声。

Method: 提出QMAC（质量感知多频带特征函数匹配）。将数据选择形式化为"分布匹配+质量过滤"的联合优化问题：在多个频率尺度上匹配所选子集与全量数据的经验特征函数（empirical characteristic function），低频刻画全局语义覆盖、高频捕捉局部扰动；同时引入任务相关的质量分数，用于剔除噪声与低质量样本。

Result: 在4种数据类型、8个目标模型、44个基准上进行系统评测：在标准图文指令数据上优于现有数据选择基线；在标准文本指令、图文推理与文本推理数据上显著超过随机选择；在多种模型与预算组合下超过全量数据训练。

Conclusion: 显式约束子集与全量数据的分布一致性并联合质量过滤，是高效数据选择的有效范式，可在降低训练开销的同时保持甚至提升微调性能；代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Instruction tuning of large language models and large vision language models relies on massive training data, which incurs substantial computational and storage costs. Meanwhile, training data often contains redundancy, noise, and low quality samples. Existing data selection methods mostly rely on diversity, importance, or a combination of both, without explicitly constraining the distributional consistency between the selected subset and the full data in the embedding space. We propose QMAC, a quality aware multiband characteristic function matching method that formulates data selection as a joint optimization problem of distribution matching and quality filtering. QMAC matches the empirical characteristic functions of the selected subset and the full data across multiple frequency scales, capturing both global semantic coverage and local perturbations, while using task related quality scores to filter noisy and low quality samples. We systematically evaluate QMAC on 4 data types, 8 target models, and 44 benchmarks. Experimental results show that QMAC outperforms existing data selection baselines on standard image-text instruction data, substantially surpasses random selection on standard text instruction, image-text reasoning, and text reasoning data, and exceeds full data training in multiple model and budget combinations. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [98] [Generative Reconstruction of Full-Field Sensor States for Safety-Critical Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08499)
*Umme Mahbuba Nabila,Linyu Lin,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文面向HTTF高温试验设施的稀疏传感器场重建，评估三类条件生成模型（DM、VAE、GAN）、两种确定性RNN及岭回归在实验内与实验间两种协议下的表现；GNN-GRU VAE在PG-29内误差最低（MAE 11.73°C、RMSE 20.54°C、R²=0.98），DM在已知传感器由91降至45时仍保持R²=0.94，分节上下文DM在PG-27→PG-29迁移中RMSE 42.84°C、R²=0.91。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键数字孪生需要完整的状态感知，但高温与辐照环境中的热电偶易漂移、失效或间歇性不可用，造成部分可观测性。作者希望系统比较不同重建方法在该真实设施数据上的精度、鲁棒性与跨实验泛化能力，为缺失传感器重建提供方法论依据。

Method: 以俄勒冈州立大学HTTF为对象构建稀疏传感器重建框架，设实验内与实验间两种协议。对比六类方法：条件扩散模型、VAE、GAN、两种确定性RNN（GNN-GRU与普通GRU）以及岭回归。实验内重建针对PG-29降压导热冷却（DCC）试验，采用图结构空间编码结合门控循环单元捕捉时空依赖；实验间迁移用PG-27加压导热冷却（PCC）数据训练、在PG-29上测试，并引入"分节上下文"条件化。

Result: 实验内PG-29：GNN-GRU VAE误差最低，MAE 11.73°C、RMSE 20.54°C、R²=0.98；确定性GNN-GRU与普通GRU的RMSE分别为25.26°C与35.22°C，显示空间建模收益；三种生成模型RMSE较岭回归降低一半以上。DM在已知传感器从91个减至45个时R²仍为0.94。实验间迁移：分节上下文DM最优，RMSE 42.84°C、R²=0.91。

Conclusion: 时空生成式建模在安全关键数字孪生的缺失传感器重建中具有明确价值：图结构编码提升精度，生成模型显著优于岭回归且对传感器稀疏化更鲁棒，分节上下文条件化可支持跨实验迁移。未来可扩展到更复杂工况与在线部署。

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of incomplete sensor fields is critical for state awareness in safety-critical digital twins (DTs). Thermocouples in high-temperature and radiation environments may drift, fail, or become intermittently unavailable, causing partial observability. This study develops a sparse-sensor reconstruction framework for Oregon State University's High Temperature Test Facility (HTTF) and evaluates six methods under intra- and inter-experiment protocols: three conditional generative models (i.e., a diffusion model [DM], variational autoencoder [VAE], and generative adversarial network [GAN]), two deterministic recurrent neural-network models, and ridge regression. For intra-experiment reconstruction within the PG{-}29 depressurized conduction cooldown (DCC) test, the models combine graph-based spatial encoding with gated recurrent units. The graph neural network (GNN)--gated recurrent unit (GRU) VAE achieves the lowest error, with a mean absolute error (MAE) of $11.73,^\circ$C, a root mean square error (RMSE) of $20.54,^\circ$C, and an $R^2$ of 0.98. The deterministic GNN--GRU and plain GRU obtain RMSE values of $25.26,^\circ$C and $35.22,^\circ$C, respectively, demonstrating the benefit of spatial modeling. All three generative models reduce RMSE by more than half compared with ridge regression. The DM maintains an $R^2$ of 0.94 when known sensors decrease from 91 to 45. For inter-experiment reconstruction, models trained on PG{-}27 pressurized conduction cooldown (PCC) are evaluated on PG{-}29 using section-context conditioning. The section-context DM achieves the best transfer performance, with an RMSE of $42.84,^\circ$C and an $R^2$ of 0.91. These results demonstrate the value of spatiotemporal generative modeling for missing-sensor reconstruction in safety-critical DT applications.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptive Depth Aggregation in Graph Transformers for Long-Range Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08500)
*Yifeng Lu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Graph Transformer 的残差计算形式化为因果的节点级历史，并用 Attention Residuals（含 Block AttnRes）实现节点自适应的深度聚合，在 GPS/GRIT 上于五个 ECHO 任务全部降低 MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Graph Transformer 主要改进跨节点通信（全局或结构感知交互），但跨节点通信的增强并不决定"不同深度产生的计算结果应如何组合"。标准残差流以固定的单位系数、全节点共享的方式累加各层贡献，构成"残差接口约束"：中间贡献一旦产生，接口便无法按节点对其显式重新加权。作者假设：当中间贡献的相对效用因节点和结构上下文而异时，该约束会造成深度维度的聚合失配。

Method: 将 Graph Transformer 的残差计算形式化为因果的、逐节点的历史（graph-propagation 与 FFN 贡献的序列），使深度聚合成为显式的接口选择；在此历史上用 Attention Residuals（AttnRes）实现节点自适应聚合，同时保留骨干网络的图传播与辅助状态机制。为降低长历史的 routed 成本，提出 Block AttnRes，通过池化连续贡献减少路由开销。将该接口实例化到 GPS 与 GRIT 两个骨干上，在 ECHO 的五个任务上评估；并通过仅替换路由模块（routing-only donor-swap）的干预实验与消融验证假设与设计选择。

Result: 两种变体在每个任务上的 MAE 均低于各自独立调优的骨干；每个任务中至少有一个变体取得所有对比方法中的最低平均 MAE。routing-only donor-swap 干预支持了深度聚合失配假设；附加消融考察了关键设计选择的影响。

Conclusion: 结果表明，节点自适应的深度聚合可作为跨节点通信的有益补充，共同服务于长程图推理；残差接口本身是一个值得显式设计的维度。

Abstract: Graph Transformers improve long-range node-to-node communication through global or structure-aware interactions. However, improving cross-node communication does not determine how computations produced at different depths should be combined. Standard residual streams accumulate successive computations with fixed unit coefficients shared across nodes. This fixed aggregation is a residual-interface constraint: once intermediate contributions have been produced, the interface cannot explicitly reweight them by node. We hypothesize that this constraint can create a depth-wise aggregation mismatch when the relative utility of these intermediate contributions varies across nodes and structural contexts. To study this hypothesis, we formalize Graph Transformer residual computation as a causal node-wise history of graph-propagation and feed-forward network (FFN) contributions, making depth aggregation an explicit interface choice. We realize node-adaptive aggregation over this history with Attention Residuals (AttnRes) while preserving the backbone's graph-propagation and auxiliary-state machinery. Block AttnRes reduces the routing cost of long histories relative to Full AttnRes by pooling consecutive contributions. We instantiate the interface in GPS and GRIT and evaluate it on all five ECHO tasks. Both variants achieve lower mean absolute error (MAE) than their independently tuned backbones on every task, and at least one obtains the lowest mean among all compared methods in each task. Routing-only donor-swap interventions support the mismatch hypothesis, while additional ablations examine the key design choices. Together, these results support node-adaptive depth aggregation as a useful complement to cross-node communication in long-range graph reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [Emergent Low-Dimensional Topological Dynamics Dictate Generalization and Escape Mechanics in Billion-Parameter Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08502)
*Dongqing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出优化轨迹被约束在低维执行子流形（有效维数 d_eff ≤ 12），据此基于正则化 Hessian 度量与投影子空间持久同调导出拓扑泛化界，并给出各向异性噪声下从严格鞍点指数逃逸的 Morse 指标—Betti 数湮灭机制；进而提出 Top-SGD / Top-AdamW 封装算法，在 ImageNet 上总墙钟训练时间降低 14.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化网络的泛化能力无法被经典统计学习界解释；作者希望从优化轨迹的几何/拓扑结构出发，给出超越环境维数 R^N 的泛化刻画，并解决严格鞍点逃逸速率的理论缺失。

Method: 1) 假设并实证优化轨迹位于低维执行子流形 M_exec（经验有效维数 d_eff ≤ 12）；2) 在参数空间赋予正则化 Hessian 度量 g_ij = ∇_i∇_j L + εδ_ij；3) 在损失下水平集上分析随机 Langevin 扩散，用投影子空间持久同调推导拓扑泛化界；4) 将 Morse 指标跃迁与 Betti 数湮灭（β_1 → 0）联系，给出各向异性噪声下严格鞍点指数逃逸的精确机制；5) 用受限在活跃 Hessian 子空间内的随机 Lanczos 迭代计算投影持久同调代理量，缓解维数灾难；6) 基于活跃曲率签名动态调节学习率，提出 Top-SGD 与 Top-AdamW。

Result: 在 80+ 个架构（ResNet、ViT、7B 级大语言模型）上验证框架；ImageNet 上总墙钟训练时间减少 14.1%，且计算与内存开销均已完整计入。

Conclusion: 优化轨迹的低维拓扑结构可作为泛化与鞍点动力学的统一数学描述，并可转化为低开销的通用优化器封装；但摘要未给出泛化界的显式形式、代理量近似的误差保证，以及有效维数上界的严格条件。

Abstract: The remarkable generalization of modern over-parameterized neural networks defies classical statistical learning bounds. Here we establish that optimization trajectories in billion-parameter models do not sample the ambient dimension $\mathbb{R}^N$ homogeneously; instead, they are dynamically constrained to a low-dimensional execution submanifold $\mathcal{M}_{\text{exec}}$ (with empirically observed effective dimension $d_{\text{eff}} \le 12$). By equipping the parameter space with a regularized Hessian metric $g_{ij} = \nabla_i \nabla_j L + εδ_{ij}$ and analyzing stochastic Langevin diffusion on loss sublevel sets, we derive a rigorous Topological Generalization Bound grounded in projected subspace persistent homology. Furthermore, by linking Morse index transitions to Betti number annihilation ($β_1 \to 0$), we formulate an exact mathematical mechanism for exponential escape from strict saddles under anisotropic noise. We validate our framework across 80+ architectures (ResNets, Vision Transformers, and 7B LLMs). To bridge theoretical topology with computational feasibility, we resolve the curse of dimensionality by computing projected persistent homology proxies via randomized Lanczos iterations restricted strictly within active Hessian subspaces. Building upon this mathematical foundation, we introduce Topological-Adaptive Optimization (Top-SGD and Top-AdamW)---a family of wrapper algorithms that dynamically modulate learning rates using active curvature signatures. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate a 14.1\% reduction in total wall-clock training time on ImageNet with all computational and memory overheads fully accounted for. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/imslab-research/Topological-SGD.

</details>


### [101] [Does Country Matter? Evaluating Geographic Sensitivity of Large Language Models in Zero-Shot Chronic Kidney Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08507)
*Sirajam Munira,Muhammad Ashad Kabir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在临床输入完全不变的情况下，仅改变提示词中的国家属性（25个国家语境），会使开源LLM的CKD阳性筛查预测发生系统性变化，且变化分组与流行病学相似性无关，而与模型内部的国家token表征有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM零样本推理能力强但对提示语境敏感，其在部署中的可靠性存疑：若仅凭提示中的地理上下文就能改变临床筛查结论，则LLM辅助决策在多元医疗环境中存在隐患。作者据此系统检验地理语境是否独立于患者特征影响CKD筛查判断。

Method: 使用孟加拉国社区CKD数据集（284人，112例CKD、172例非CKD）；在保持全部临床输入不变的前提下，系统替换25个国家语境属性；评估两个开放权重模型（Qwen3 8B、Mistral 7B）；实验限于两模型基线均预测正确的共156名参与者的公共正确子集，以保证可比性。

Result: 非CKD预测在各国家语境下高度稳定，两模型几乎在所有设置下均保持一致；相反，CKD阳性预测敏感性较高：Qwen3的CKD预测数由基线65最低降至50，Mistral变异较小但不可忽略。各国家语境的分组不符合已知流行病学或医疗体系相似性，而似乎反映各模型内部对国家token的表征方式。

Conclusion: 仅地理token即可在患者输入完全相同的情况下系统性改变临床筛查结果，说明LLM临床决策支持存在语境诱导的脆弱性；在不同医疗环境下部署此类模型需对提示中的地理/人口语境进行审计与稳健性评估，并关注模型内部token表征导致的偏倚。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong zero-shot reasoning capabilities, yet their sensitivity to prompt context raises questions about reliability in deployment. This study investigates whether geographic context embedded in prompts influences LLM-based chronic kidney disease (CKD) screening, independent of any change in patient features. Using a community-based CKD dataset from Bangladesh (284 participants with 112 CKD and 172 non-CKD), we systematically varied the country attribute across 25 global contexts while holding all clinical inputs constant, and evaluated two open-weight LLMs (Qwen3 8B and Mistral 7B). Experiments were conducted on a common-correct subset of 156 participants for whom both models produced correct baseline predictions. Results show that non-CKD predictions were highly stable across geographic contexts, with both models maintaining consistent non-CKD predictions in nearly all settings. In contrast, CKD-positive predictions were considerably more sensitive. For Qwen3, CKD prediction dropped from a baseline of 65 to as low as 50 across country contexts, while Mistral showed smaller but non-negligible variation. Country contexts did not group according to known epidemiological or healthcare similarities; instead, groupings appear to reflect how country tokens are internally represented within each model. These findings demonstrate that geographic tokens can systematically shift clinical screening outcomes despite identical patient inputs, with implications for the deployment of LLM-based decision support in diverse healthcare settings.

</details>


### [102] [Interpretable Multifidelity Learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatiotemporal Nuclear Reactor Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08512)
*Meredith Eaheart,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较黑箱多保真神经网络（MFNN）与可解释多保真Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（MFKAN）在PWR堆芯193个燃料组件时空质量流量预测中的表现，并利用TabDDPM合成数据缓解CFD瞬态稀缺问题；结论是KAN精度与MFNN相当，但额外提供符号方程，且跨保真预测精度本质上受保真层间相关性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核反应堆系统高保真CFD模拟计算代价高昂，多保真代理建模可用少量高保真数据配合廉价低保真数据降低成本；同时需探究预测全堆芯193个组件时空质量流量所需的高保真数据能否进一步减少，并在黑箱模型之外寻求可解释的建模方案。

Method: 以PWR堆芯193个燃料组件的时空质量流量为预测目标，对比黑箱多保真前馈神经网络（MFNN）与可解释多保真Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（MFKAN）；分析全堆芯跨保真相关性的强弱；针对每种保真度仅有单个CFD瞬态的数据稀缺问题，采用TabDDPM生成合成数据增强跨保真预测；并开展组件级相关性分析以定位误差来源。

Result: 全堆芯跨保真相关性弱，问题难度大，两种架构的绝对瞬态预测精度均不高；MFKAN在单步前向预测上与MFNN相当，并能给出MFNN不具备的闭式符号方程。合成数据增强下，KAN与FNN自评估精度相当（Pearson相关约0.73–0.81），但真实跨保真预测表现均差（相关约0.03–0.06）；组件级分析显示各组件预测相关性与真实跨保真相关性高度一致（KAN r=0.840–0.896，FNN r=0.804–0.903）。

Conclusion: KAN类多保真建模在所有设置下精度与MFNN持续相当，且额外提供与样条表示精度相当或更优的符号方程；跨保真预测精度从根本上受限于保真层之间既有的相关程度，而非模型架构本身，这为高保真CFD数据的可约简性提供了判据。

Abstract: High-fidelity computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations of nuclear reactor systems are computationally expensive, motivating machine learning methods that reduce cost while preserving accuracy. Multifidelity surrogate modeling addresses this by combining inexpensive low-fidelity data with limited high-fidelity data. This work investigates whether the high-fidelity data required to predict the spatiotemporal mass flow rate across all 193 fuel assemblies of a pressurized water reactor (PWR) core can be reduced, comparing black-box multifidelity feedforward neural networks (MFNNs) with interpretable multifidelity Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (MFKANs). Cross-fidelity correlation across the core is weak, making this a challenging multifidelity problem, and neither architecture predicts the transient particularly well in absolute terms. MFKAN nonetheless performs comparably to MFNN for one-time-step-forward prediction while also providing closed-form symbolic equations unavailable with MFNN. To address the scarcity of CFD transients, with only one available per fidelity level, TabDDPM-generated synthetic data was used to augment cross-fidelity forecasting. KAN and FNN achieve comparable self-evaluation accuracy (Pearson correlation of roughly 0.73--0.81), but both perform poorly at true cross-fidelity forecasting (correlations of 0.03--0.06). Assembly-level analysis shows that each assembly's forecast correlation tracks its real cross-fidelity correlation for both architectures ($r=0.840$--$0.896$ for KAN, $r=0.804$--$0.903$ for FNN), confirming that forecasting accuracy is fundamentally limited by how correlated the fidelity levels already are. Overall, KAN-based multifidelity modeling achieves accuracy consistently comparable to MFNN across all settings, while additionally providing symbolic equations that match or exceed the spline representation's accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [What Are LLM Agents Actually Learning on Low-Budget Molecular Optimization Benchmarks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08522)
*Jiayu Qin,Zhengquan Luo,Changyou Chen,Zhiqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对"LLM agent在低预算分子优化上大幅超越"的结论进行归因分析：性能提升主要来自在线优化、可检索的任务先验信息以及宽松的oracle景观，而非LLM本身；作者据此提出两项廉价稳健性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作报告LLM agent在低预算分子优化（如PMO的50次oracle调用）上取得"巨大提升"，但这些提升的来源未被拆解，可能存在任务信息泄漏（任务名、参考化合物）与评测oracle过于宽松造成的虚高，也缺乏合理的下界基线。作者希望厘清增益究竟来自何处。

Method: (1) 消融实验：在PMO（50次oracle调用）上隐藏任务名与精选参考化合物，对比GPT-4o mini agent的sum AUC；(2) 身份盲化条件：仅告知oracle形式（如"与隐藏参考的相似度"）；(3) 无LLM的确定性检索-only基线；(4) 23任务2×2完整消融与8任务三随机种子复现；(5) 蛋白质靶向基准上使用AutoDock Vina复合oracle，以单编辑随机游走作为下界；(6) 阳性对照：无检索的population agent与REINVENT在匹配的50次调用AUC预算下比较。

Result: PMO 50次调用下，GPT-4o mini agent的sum AUC由14.44降至4.85（隐藏任务名与参考化合物后）；身份盲化条件得分5.39；无LLM的确定性检索基线达12.16，为完整agent的84%。在Vina复合oracle基准上，单编辑随机游走即为强下界，三随机种子复合分0.636±0.008、对接改善−1.11±0.04 kcal/mol。阳性对照中，无检索population agent在50次调用AUC预算下超过REINVENT，100次调用时效果最佳。

Conclusion: LLM agent在低预算分子优化中的多数收益可由可检索的任务信息、在线优化与宽松oracle解释，而非模型推理能力；因此需采用两项廉价 sanity check：检索安全的PMO提示设计（避免任务名/参考化合物泄漏）与基于对接优化的随机游走下界基线。

Abstract: Recent work reports large gains for LLM agents on low-budget molecular optimization. We measure the contributions of online optimization, retrievable task information, and permissive oracle landscapes. On PMO at 50 oracle calls, a GPT-4o mini agent's sum AUC falls from 14.44 to 4.85 after task names and curated reference compounds are hidden. An identity-blinded condition that states only the oracle form (e.g., similarity to a hidden reference) scores 5.39, while a deterministic retrieval-only baseline with no LLM reaches 12.16 (84% of the full agent). A full 23-task 2x2 ablation and a three-seed replication on eight tasks support this interpretation. On a protein-targeted benchmark with an AutoDock Vina composite oracle, a single-edit random walk is a strong lower bound; across three seeds it obtains composite 0.636 +/- 0.008 and docking improvement -1.11 +/- 0.04 kcal/mol. As a positive control, a retrieval-free population agent exceeds REINVENT at a matched 50-call AUC budget, with its strongest result at 100 calls. These results motivate two inexpensive sanity checks: retrieval-safe PMO prompts and a random-walk floor for docking-based optimization.

</details>


### [104] [Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning: A Multi-Objective Framework for Learning Local Distance Functions in Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08549)
*Isaac Touza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EAML框架，用低维参数化学习实例相关的正定度量，并以实数编码精英遗传算法优化；理论性质完备，实验显示仅部分数据集受益，整体未显著优于全局度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻分类器的性能继承其距离函数的几何结构。固定距离与全局学习度量在整个输入空间施加同一几何，但相关特征方向可能随区域变化，因此需要实例相关（局部）度量。

Method: EAML采用低维参数化构造实例相关正定度量：局部度量由输入相关的对角缩放（通过锚点间softmax插值生成）与全局低秩相关分量组合而成；参数由实数编码精英遗传算法优化，标量目标融合留一预测误差、鲁棒性、度量平滑性与正则项。理论上证明正定性、度量等价性、最优解存在性与度量场的Lipschitz连续性，并在显式假设下分析预测稳定性、泛化界与进化收敛性。

Result: 在区域相关特征重要性的合成数据上，EAML能恢复局部度量变化并优于全局度量；13个公开数据集的嵌套5折交叉验证显示更细致的结论：EAML在部分数据集上提升性能，但整体未显著超越全局度量基线。补充实验表明，在冗余/干扰特征与测试时噪声下，全局对角度量可能更可取。

Conclusion: 研究界定了进化式局部度量学习有效的条件，同时指出其实际局限：局部度量并非普遍优于全局度量，需结合数据特性与噪声条件权衡选择。

Abstract: Nearest neighbour classifiers inherit the geometry of their distance function. Fixed distances and globally learned metrics apply the same geometry throughout the input space, although relevant feature directions may vary across regions. We propose EAML (Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning), a framework that learns instance-dependent positive-definite metrics through a low-dimensional parameterization. Each local metric combines an input-dependent diagonal scaling, generated by softmax interpolation between anchor points, with a global low-rank correlation component. A real-coded elitist genetic algorithm optimizes the parameters using a scalar objective combining leave-one-out prediction error, robustness, metric smoothness, and regularization. We establish positive definiteness, metric equivalence, existence of an optimal solution, and Lipschitz continuity of the metric field.
