We further investigate prediction stability, generalization bounds, and evolutionary convergence under explicit assumptions. Experiments on synthetic data with region-dependent feature relevance show that EAML can recover local metric variations and outperform global metrics. Evaluation on thirteen public datasets using nested 5-fold cross-validation reveals a more nuanced picture: EAML improves performance on selected datasets but does not significantly outperform global metric baselines overall. Additional experiments show that global diagonal metrics can be preferable under nuisance features and test-time noise. These findings identify the conditions under which evolutionary local metric learning is beneficial and highlight its practical limitations.

</details>


### [105] [How Small Can We Go? Calibrating Activation-Cache Compression for SAE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08551)
*Abraham Yeung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究激活缓存压缩对稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征的影响，发现"保留特征比例"缺乏清晰标度，遂以"重训练上限（相同字节）"与"重播种下限"校准测量，并据此比较各压缩编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练SAE需要海量模型激活缓存，因此必须对缓存做压缩；但压缩究竟损伤多少SAE特征、以及如何正确度量这种损伤，此前缺乏清晰标准。作者观察到保留特征比例随扰动性质（fp16取整、单值微小扰动、换随机种子）呈现反直觉的结果，说明现有读数不可直接比较。

Method: 设计两种测试：(1) 冻结SAE测试——保持原fp32训练的SAE不变，将压缩缓存输入同一SAE，统计特征激活保持不变的比例，并与L2距离排序对比；(2) 重训练测试——在压缩缓存上重训SAE，统计原特征仍存在的比例。进而构建校准框架：以"相同字节重训"为上限、以"重播种"为下限，将各编解码器效果对照其所在站点的下限衡量。实验对象为GPT-2 small与Pythia-1.4B上的TopK SAE，研究规模预算，比较逐token int4、PCA、随机投影、通道标准化后int4、bf16前向、旋转8位存储等方案。

Result: 重训练测试中保留特征比例无清晰标度：GPT-2缓存上全域转fp16保留60.6%，而按fp32最小步长改动百万分之一的单值（变化小八个数量级）仍保留70.5%，换随机种子则保留23.0%。冻结SAE测试中，冻结SAE与原SAE的相关性随编解码器精度提高而上升，但朴素L2距离给出几乎相同的排序；同一冻结读数在不同站点对应不同保留百分比。校准后：朴素逐token int4在所有测试站点损失特征均超过重播种下限，PCA与随机投影在GPT-2上亦然；量化前逐通道标准化可在相同原始文件大小下把int4拉回下限之上；最小预算下，bf16前向比旋转8位存储损失略多特征。每次站点校准需4次训练与2次评估。

Conclusion: 压缩对SAE的影响不能仅用"保留特征比例"或L2/相关性等单一读数衡量，必须相对站点特定的噪声下限（重播种）来解读；据此，通道标准化后的int4是代价低且有效的方案，而朴素int4、PCA、随机投影及bf16前向在多数设置下劣于基线噪声水平。

Abstract: To train a sparse autoencoder (SAE), you need a massive cache of model activations. We look into the effects that compression has on an SAE in two ways: we keep the original fp32-trained SAE and pass the compressed cache into the same SAE to see how many feature activations stay the same (the frozen-SAE test), and we retrain an SAE on the compressed cache and see how many of the original features still exist (the retraining test). We found that in the retraining test, there is no clear scale for retained features. On a GPT-2 cache, rounding everything to fp16 keeps 60.6% of features, but changing one value in a million by the smallest step fp32 allows (a change eight orders of magnitude smaller) keeps only 70.5%. Meanwhile, changing the random seed keeps 23.0%. For the frozen-SAE test, the frozen SAE's correlation with the original increases in order of how precise the codecs are, but plain $L_2$ distance gives nearly the same ordering. We also see the same frozen reading correspond to different percentages retained at different sites. This leaves the question of the right measurement. We build one by calibrating: retraining on identical bytes sets the ceiling, and a reseed sets the floor. We then measure the effect of each compressed codec against its own site's floor. With TopK SAEs on GPT-2 small and Pythia-1.4B at research-scale budgets, naïve per-token int4 loses more features than a reseed everywhere we tested, as do PCA and random projection on GPT-2. Standardizing each channel before quantizing brings int4 back above the floor in terms of retained features, at the same raw file size. At our smallest budget, a bf16 forward pass, which some pipelines use to save memory, loses slightly more features than rotated 8-bit storage. Calibrating a site costs four training runs and two evaluation passes.

</details>


### [106] [Minimum-Sufficient Knowledge and Computation for Wind Energy Predictive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08558)
*Purbak Sengupta,Aparna S. varde,Emil Jørgensen Njor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出最小充分知识与计算（MSKC）原则，并以自适应知识路由在风电场爬坡事件预测中验证：仅注入35.9%关系知识即可达到与全量注入统计不可区分的性能，同时减少64%的知识调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限网络物理决策系统普遍采用固定的感知、知识与计算流水线，忽视状态依赖的信息需求，导致"始终最大智能"式的过度计算与浪费，与Green-IT/可持续部署目标冲突。

Method: 提出MSKC原则——选择满足决策风险与部署约束的最低代价路由，仅在廉价路由不足时升级。作为概念验证，聚焦KGML中的自适应知识路由，针对风电爬坡事件预测构造局部、关系与物理信息三类路由，并用状态感知门控在两个真实风电场公开数据上评估；同时给出可行性保持、代价支配与严格期望节省的理论条件。

Result: 在35.9%关系知识注入率下，自适应路由在报告指标上与全量注入统计不可区分，而知识调用减少64%；理论条件保证可行性保持与相对始终开启路由的代价支配及严格期望节省。

Conclusion: 结果支持MSKC前提——统一最大化智能并非必要；分析过程本身可按状态决定数据应获得多少知识与计算，为大数据流提供自适应智能分析范式。

Abstract: Resource-constrained cyber-physical decision systems often apply fixed sensing, knowledge, and computational pipelines despite state-dependent information needs. We introduce Minimum-Sufficient Knowledge and Computation (MSKC), a resource-aware computing principle: select the least-costly route meeting decision-risk and deployment constraints, escalating only when cheaper routes are insufficient. As proof of concept, we isolate adaptive knowledge routing, the decision underlying Knowledge-Guided Machine Learning (KGML), which conventionally injects external knowledge unconditionally rather than only where needed. We instantiate this for wind ramp-event predictive analysis, an edge-oriented decision-support task, evaluating local, relational, and physics-informed routes with a state-aware gate on publicly available real data from two wind farms. We establish conditions for feasibility preservation, cost dominance, and strict expected savings over always-on routing. At a 35.9% relational-knowledge injection rate, adaptive routing is statistically indistinguishable from always-on injection on the reported metrics while reducing knowledge calls by 64%, supporting sustainable, Green-IT-aligned deployment. These results support MSKC's premise that uniformly maximal intelligence is unnecessary. This offers intelligent data analysis for big data streams: the analysis itself decides, per state, how much knowledge and computation the data warrant.

</details>


### [107] [PINNeval: A Comprehensive Evaluation Standard for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08559)
*Kevin von Bargen,Hamid Ebrahimy,Tim Römer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向PINN的综合评估协议，将训练流程视为整体系统，并要求未研究的组件采用当前竞争性实现，同时发布JAX工具包PINNpipe（含18种方法与9个基准问题），其结果挑战了若干常见假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN文献方法数量激增，但基准研究往往只孤立研究训练流程的单一组件（域重要性自适应、损失平衡、网络架构、优化方案），并将其余组件设为已不代表当前最佳实践的最小默认设置，导致结论难以迁移到竞争性流水线。

Method: 提出新的评估协议：(1) 将四个组件视为集成系统；(2) 要求未被考察的组件采用竞争性实现；(3) 明确指标与报告格式；(4) 配套结构化文档以保证跨研究评估的可追溯性。并据此发布JAX实现的开源包PINNpipe，集成18种现有文献方法与9个高效实现的基准问题。

Result: 借助PINNpipe演示该评估协议并与既有基准研究对照，所得结果对若干普遍假设提出挑战（摘要未给出具体数值结论）。

Conclusion: PINN方法的评估应基于集成系统视角与竞争性基线组件，并配合标准化指标、报告格式及可追溯文档；PINNpipe为该规范化评估提供了可复用的实现基础。

Abstract: The Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) literature has produced a rapidly expanding catalogue of methods. We divide a PINN training pipeline systematically into four components and observe that many existing benchmarks study these components in isolation. These components are domain-importance adaptation, loss balancing, neural network architecture, and optimization scheme. Moreover, the studies set the surrounding and not considered components of the pipeline to a minimal default that is no longer representative of current best practice approaches. Conclusions drawn under such conditions may not transfer to competitive pipelines. In this work, we propose a new evaluation protocol that (1) treats the four components as an integrated system; (2) crucially requires competitive implementations of the components not under investigation; (3) specifies the metrics and reporting format; and (4) is accompanied by structured documentation that ensures the traceability of evaluations across studies. Based on this comprehensive protocol, we release PINNpipe, an innovative JAX package containing 18 different methods from the current literature as well as nine efficiently implemented benchmark problems. Using PINNpipe, we illustrate our evaluation protocol in relation to established benchmark studies and report results that challenge common assumptions.

</details>


### [108] [Not All Misalignment Is Alike: A Decomposed Benchmark for Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08569)
*Afonso Domingues,Mubashara Akhtar,Bhada Yun,Xin Chen,Lukas Fluri,Anna Hedström*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出分解式基准 EMERGE，从八个维度刻画涌现失配（EM）的行为谱，替代单一失配率评分，并在8个开源模型、13个微调数据集上揭示"总失配率相近但行为剖面差异巨大"的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EM 研究普遍沿用原论文的评测方法，其结果对少量评测问题、judge 选择与阈值高度敏感；单一评分还会混淆"失配"与"能力退化"，无法说明失配的具体表现形式。同时数据集、模型与微调配置的差异使跨研究结论难以比较。因此需要一个更稳健、可分解、可对照的评测标准。

Method: 构建 EMERGE 分解式基准：跟踪基座模型到微调模型在八类不同失配行为上的变化；在域外（out-of-domain）提示上评测，并与匹配的良性对照提示进行对比；同时监测能力、连贯性与拒答率等指标，以控制潜在混淆因素。实验覆盖 8 个开源权重模型与 13 个微调数据集。

Result: 总体失配率相近的模型，其八维行为剖面可能显著不同，单一标量分数无法捕捉这种差异；分解评测结合能力/连贯性/拒答控制，可区分真正的失配变化与能力损失带来的伪信号。

Conclusion: EMERGE 有望成为测量涌现失配的共享且稳健的标准，提升不同研究结论的可比性，并推动该领域从"检测现象"走向"理解机制"。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM), in which fine-tuning on a narrow task induces misaligned behaviour on unrelated prompts, has been demonstrated across a wide range of datasets, model families, and training setups. Yet, most studies continue to rely on the evaluation approach introduced in the original EM paper, producing rates that are sensitive to the small set of evaluation questions used, the choice of judge, and the thresholds applied. These evaluations can also conflate misalignment with capability loss and offer little insight into the specific ways misalignment manifests. Differences in datasets, models, and fine-tuning configurations further make findings difficult to compare across studies. We introduce EMERGE, a decomposed benchmark that tracks changes in eight distinct misaligned behaviours from base to fine-tuned models. It evaluates these behaviours on out-of-domain prompts against matched benign controls and tracks capability, coherence, and refusal to account for potential confounders. Applying EMERGE to 8 open-weight models and 13 fine-tuning datasets, we find that models with similar broad misalignment rates can exhibit very different behavioural profiles that a single score cannot capture. We hope EMERGE can help establish a shared and robust standard for measuring EM, making findings more comparable and helping move research from detecting the phenomenon towards understanding it.

</details>


### [109] [Benchmarking Machine Learning for Chaotic Three-Body Dynamics in Astrophysics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08634)
*Sheng Wan,Pavan Vynatheya,Duo Xu,Li Kevin Wenliang,Ye Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于混沌三体系统的分层预测任务基准，对比通用机器学习模型、基础物理模型与天体物理传统判据，发现短期动力学下稳定性预测可取得强性能，而长时域精确轨迹预测仍然困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习与生成式AI在物理理解方面进展显著，但缺乏针对混沌动力系统（如三体问题）的系统性、分层次的基准评测，难以判断现有方法在何种任务上真正有效、在何处失效。

Method: 首先模拟稳定/不稳定临界边界附近的三体系统轨迹；随后设计分层学习任务，主要分为两类：稳定性预测（判断给定三体系统在特定时域内是否发散）与轨迹预测（预测精确轨迹）；在统一输入特征下，评测从简单回归方法到最先进基础物理模型的一系列ML方法，并与Mardling-Aarseth稳定性判据等天体物理方法进行对比。

Result: 实验揭示混沌三体动力学中任务难度存在清晰层级：早期动力学信息足以支撑较强的稳定性预测；而精确的长时域轨迹预测依然十分困难。基础物理模型相较通用预测算法未表现出决定性优势。

Conclusion: 混沌三体问题可作为检验ML物理理解能力的有效基准；稳定性的短期可预测性与长期轨迹的不可预测性形成鲜明对比，表明未来研究应聚焦于不确定性量化与混合物理-数据驱动方法。代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) and generative AI have recently made impressive progress in understanding physics. We explore a hierarchy of prediction problems stemming from chaotic three-body systems and benchmark existing ML approaches, including general-purpose prediction algorithms and those that build on top of foundational physics models. We first simulate the trajectories of triple systems near the critical boundary of stable/unstable initial conditions. We then design a hierarchy of learning tasks, focusing mainly on two broad problems: stability prediction and trajectory prediction. The former classifies whether a given triple system has diverged or not up to a specific horizon, while the latter predicts the exact trajectory, both given suitable input features. We benchmark a range of ML approaches, from simple regression methods to state-of-the-art foundational physics models, across these tasks, and compare them against astrophysical methods such as the Mardling-Aarseth stability criterion. Our experiments reveal a clear order of task difficulties in chaotic three-body dynamics: early dynamics enable strong stability prediction, while precise long-horizon trajectory forecasting remains difficult. Code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/Simonwan03/ThreeBodyMLBench.

</details>


### [110] [Higher Order Component Attribution via Learned Surrogates in High Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08656)
*Lauri Laatu,Aritra Bal,Benedikt Maier,Markus Klute,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的组件归因方法，将ParT（Particle Transformer）喷注识别模型的判别依据回溯到可解释的喷注物理描述量，以检验多头注意力（MHA）与ParT架构是否真正学到了这些高阶关联特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ParT在喷注标记（jet tagging）任务上精度达到SOTA，但其注意力机制与显式粒子关联信息为何有效仍缺乏可解释性；现有归因手段难以直接映射到物理上有意义的喷注描述量。

Method: 以Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks作为可解释的旁路/代理模型，构建组件归因（component attribution）框架：对ParT内部由MHA及各架构组件产生的表示进行分解与归因，并将归因结果与喷注的物理描述量（如粒子运动学与关联变量）建立直接对应关系，从而定量探测这些机制是否编码了相应的判别信息。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果；论文定位为方法学提案，通过归因分析回答MHA与ParT是否学到可作为判别特征的物理描述量。

Conclusion: KAN可作为连接深度模型内部机制与喷注物理可观测量之间的可解释桥梁，为高能物理中Transformer类模型的有效性提供机制层面的证据，并为后续模型设计与特征工程提供依据。

Abstract: Experimental High Energy Physics (HEP) has adopted the transformer architecture for identification of jets, particularly the state-of-the-art Particle Transformer (ParT) which injects explicit information about particle correlations into the attention mechanism. While able to achieve state-of-the-art results in accuracy, interpretability to its effectiveness has been limited. In this paper we propose component attribution methods using Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) that directly tie back to the physical descriptors of jets, to probe whether mechanisms such as Multi-Head Attention (MHA), or the ParT architecture, are able to learn these as discriminating features.

</details>


### [111] [Does a Trading Policy Need to Know What It Trades? Microstructure Invariance and Zero-Shot Transfer of Execution Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08701)
*Hieu Tran,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过逐笔精确回放Nasdaq公开数据，检验微观结构不变性归一化能否让RL执行策略实现跨资产的零样本泛化。结果表明：仅凭近期交易率、单笔规模、价差与tick成本感知的策略，在954只股票上训练后可无改动迁移至1964只2020-21年未见股票、2629只2025-26年股票、加密交易对及第二家交易所，性能接近逐股定制策略；但不变性单位的优势不足1个基点，且无任何表示能外推到未见价差区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行算法因各资产在排队、成交率与买卖价差上相差数个数量级而必须逐资产校准，这对RL意味着跨尺度语境的零样本泛化问题。微观结构不变性提出"以各资产自身的时间、规模与价格单位计量时市场彼此相似"，本文旨在用严格实证检验该理论归一化是否真能支撑可迁移的执行策略，并质疑"近乎零的迁移差距"作为表示正确性证据的有效性。

Method: 在Nasdaq公开数据的逐笔精确回放环境中，将决策设定为"排队等待 vs 穿越价差"；在2019年954只股票上训练一次策略，再原封不动地评测于2020-21年1964只未见过股票、2025-26年2629只股票、加密交易对与第二家交易所。策略输入仅为近期交易率、交易规模、价差与tick价格增量。比较不变性单位与原始单位、z-score、资产描述符三类表示；并以合成市场与倒立摆任务复核迁移差距指标的诊断力。

Result: (1) 仅凭近期微观结构统计的策略，与训练数据含该标的的策略相比，每股成本差在0.015个价差以内（约为学习本身节省成本的五分之一），且标的自身历史对其无帮助；在等权episode设定下与第二交易所训练的策略差距相近，在加密资产上差距更宽。(2) 在扣除费用前的平均成本上，不变性单位优于原始单位、z-score与资产描述符，但优势不足1个基点；其优势主要来自信息与共同成本尺度，而这些也可作为普通输入直接提供给学习器；所有测试表示均无法外推至未见价差区间。(3) 对合并比较器的近零迁移差距是"表示正确"的弱证据：该比较器只能检出劣质表示所损失的一小部分，作者在合成市场与倒立摆上予以确认。

Conclusion: 理论导出的不变性归一化确实带来可测量但幅度很小的收益，RL执行策略可在一定程度内以零样本方式跨资产、跨交易所迁移，但跨价差区间外推仍失败。更重要的方法论结论是：常用的合并比较器与近乎零的迁移差距不足以验证表示质量，容易造成过度乐观的判断，需改用能更敏感暴露表示失效的评测设计。

Abstract: Execution algorithms are calibrated asset by asset, because the queues, trade rates and bid-ask spreads they face differ by orders of magnitude across assets. For reinforcement learning this is zero-shot generalisation across contexts of very different scales, and microstructure invariance proposes a normalisation fixed by theory: measured in each asset's own units of time, size and price, markets look alike. In an exact order-by-order replay of public Nasdaq data, we train policies once, on 954 stocks in 2019, to choose between waiting in the queue and crossing the spread, and evaluate them unchanged on 1,964 unseen stocks in 2020-21, 2,629 stocks in 2025-26, crypto pairs and a second exchange. First, a policy that sees a stock only through its recent trade rate, trade size, spread and price increment (tick) costs within 0.015 spreads per share (a fifth of what learning saves) of a policy whose training data included the stock, and the stock's own history does not help it; it comes within a similar margin of policies trained on the second exchange when every episode counts equally, and within a wider one on crypto. Second, in mean cost before fees, invariant units beat raw units, z-scores and asset descriptors, but by under a basis point: most of their advantage is information and a common cost scale that a learner can also take as plain inputs, and none of the representations tested extrapolates to unseen spread regimes. Third, a near-zero transfer gap to a pooled comparator is weak evidence that a representation is right: we show that such a comparator detects only a small share of what a poor representation loses, and confirm it in synthetic markets and on pendulums.

</details>


### [112] [Foundation-Model Residuals as Signals of Urban Bus Irregularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08703)
*Csaba I. Sidló,Jamil J. Ghazal,Domonkos Rózsay,Rita Rónai,András J. Molnár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将预训练轨迹模型（UniTraj、TrajFM）跨城迁移时的残差重新解读为反映本地城市运行状况的系统性信号，而非单纯性能缺陷；基于巴西尼泰罗伊的NetMob26公交位置、票务、天气、路网与社会经济数据，利用可解释AI分析空间与旅行时间残差的聚集性、重叠性及其与城市背景变量的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城迁移的轨迹基础模型误差通常被视为性能局限而被忽略；作者提出这些残差可能系统性地编码了目标城市的局部条件（如公交运行不规律），从而具有诊断价值。同时，15分钟城市等新城市范式评估需要更丰富的分析视角，动机在于把残差转化为可解释的城市情境信号。

Method: 以UniTraj与TrajFM等轨迹基础模型为对象，在新城市尼泰罗伊（Niteroi）的NetMob26多源数据（公交定位、票务、天气、路网、社会经济）上计算迁移残差；提出三个研究问题：高残差是否在空间上聚集、空间残差与旅行时间残差是否重叠、哪些城市背景变量可解释残差分布；采用可解释AI（XAI）方法分析残差与情境变量的关联，并将高残差定位为"交通运行不规律"的候选代理信号。

Result: 摘要为研究计划式表述，未给出量化结果；其分析框架旨在检验残差的空间聚集性、两类残差的交叠结构以及城市背景变量对残差分布的解释能力，并以15分钟城市视角组织与扩展分析。

Conclusion: 模型迁移残差可被重新利用为城市情境的探测工具，为评估新城市范式与交通运行状况提供补充于标准出行指标的视角；但高残差仅作候选代理信号，不构成服务中断的直接证据。

Abstract: Pretrained trajectory models encode mobility patterns learned across cities, but their errors when transferred to a new city are usually treated only as performance limitations. We instead ask whether these residuals reveal systematic local conditions. Architectures such as UniTraj and TrajFM learn transferable trajectory representations, although cross-context transfer remains challenging. Using the NetMob26 bus location, ticketing, weather, network, and socioeconomic data for Niteroi, we analyze spatial and travel-time residuals of these recent trajectory foundation models. We test whether high residuals concentrate spatially, whether spatial and travel-time residuals overlap, and which urban-context variables explain their distribution.
  We interpret high residuals as candidate proxy signals of transit irregularity rather than direct evidence of service disruption, and analyze their contextual associations using Explainable AI (XAI). Insights into such varied environments are increasingly valuable for evaluating new urban paradigms; for instance, the 15-minute-city perspective serves as a key motivation for our approach, guiding our methods and expanding the analysis beyond standard mobility metrics.

</details>


### [113] [Dual-Stream LLM-Guided Reward Design for Reject-Inference-Aware Reinforcement Learning in E-Commerce Payment Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08706)
*Bo Qu,Zhurong Wang,Frank Zahradnik,Ming Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 LLM 引导的奖励设计框架扩展到拒绝推断问题，用双流奖励把被遗留系统拦截的无标签交易纳入训练信号，在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升美元加权精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的欺诈检测模型只能学习被截断的流量：被遗留系统拦截的交易永远拿不到欺诈标签，模型因此只在有偏的"仅批准"分布上训练（拒绝推断/选择偏差问题）。

Method: 在 Qu et al. (2025) 的 LLM 引导奖励设计框架（LLM 进化两步 REINFORCE 欺诈拦截策略的奖励）基础上，让单个 LLM 设计双流奖励：流 1 对已标注交易使用标准美元加权精度目标；流 2 对遗留系统拦截的无标签交易打分，通过对比最新的梯度提升风险信号与驱动当初拦截的遗留分数来构造信号。作者不预设哪些信号重要，而是提供证据超集，由 LLM 仅依据评估反馈自行选择；由于奖励以代码形式写出，该选择可直接审计。

Result: 在留出的四个月时间外测试集上，双流智能体在 80%/85%/90% 召回率下，相比仅用标签的先前智能体分别提升 4.7、2.4、3.0 个百分点的美元加权精度，平均比生产基线高 6.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 所得到的策略在架构上与 Qu et al. (2025) 完全一致且不增加推理成本，说明将拒绝推断嵌入 LLM 设计的奖励中可在零额外部署代价下带来稳定收益，且奖励代码化保证了可审计性。

Abstract: Production fraud-detection models learn from a censored slice of their own traffic: transactions a legacy system blocked never receive fraud labels, so models train on a biased, approved-only distribution. We extend LLM-guided reward design, the framework of Qu et al. (2025) in which an LLM evolves the reward of a two-step REINFORCE fraud-blocking policy, to attack this reject-inference problem inside the reward. A single LLM designs a dual-stream reward: stream 1 applies the standard dollar-weighted precision objective to labeled transactions; stream 2 scores legacy-blocked, unlabeled transactions by contrasting the newest gradient-boosted risk signal against the legacy scores that drove the original block. Rather than prescribe which signals matter, we expose a superset of evidence and let the LLM decide for itself which to use, guided by evaluation feedback alone; because it writes the reward as code, that choice is directly auditable. On a held-out four-month out-of-time test, the dual-stream agent beats the prior labeled-only agent at every target recall, by +4.7, +2.4 and +3.0 points of dollar-weighted precision at 80%, 85% and 90% recall, and the production baseline by +6.8 points on average. The resulting policy is architecturally identical to that of Qu et al. (2025) and adds no inference cost.

</details>


### [114] [An Educated Guess: Deriving Statistically Aligned Gradient Estimates for Zeroth-Order Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08712)
*Justus Huebotter,Serge Thill,Marcel van Gerven,Nasir Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱零阶优化方法族 SZO-κ，利用前向激活几何而非盲随机噪声构造零阶梯度估计方向，缓解高维扰动失配问题，并在多层与卷积网络上系统分析其梯度对齐、偏差-方差与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型硬件架构（神经形态芯片、异步系统、ASIC）的探索需要适配的机器学习学习算法，而反向传播依赖全局同步与精确可微性，难以在非传统基底上实现；零阶优化（SPSA、权重扰动）虽仅需前向推理即可估计梯度，但受"维数灾难"困扰——高维空间中随机扰动与真实梯度的对齐度急剧下降。

Method: 摒弃盲随机噪声，从前向传播中可获得的激活几何结构推导零阶搜索方向，提出谱零阶（SZO-κ）方法族；其方向在显式随机假设下求解基于局部响应的"梯度猜测"目标，从而优先探索特征支撑（feature-supported）方向，这类方向更可能产生有信息量的损失响应。

Result: 在多层的与卷积的网络中，相对标准扰动基线与反向传播，分析了这些方法的梯度对齐性、偏差-方差结构以及训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过利用激活几何构造零阶搜索方向，可缓解高维盲扰动导致的梯度失配问题，为在缺乏精确可微性与全局同步的非传统硬件上实现有效学习提供了有前景的替代路径。

Abstract: As novel hardware architectures are explored for the purposes of machine learning, including neuromorphic chips, asynchronous systems, and ASICs, effective learning algorithms are highly desirable. While backpropagation is the standard for differentiation on standard hardware, its requirement for global synchrony and exact differentiability limits the exploration of exotic algorithms on non-traditional substrates and devices. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimisation methods, such as SPSA and weight perturbation, offer a compelling alternative by estimating gradients through inference-only passes; however, these methods have historically suffered due to the `curse of dimensionality', where random perturbations become increasingly misaligned with the true gradient in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we move beyond blind random noise by deriving zeroth-order search directions from activation geometry available during the forward pass. This leads to a family of Spectral Zeroth-Order methods, SZO-$κ$, whose directions solve local response-based gradient-guessing objectives under explicit stochastic assumptions. These methods explore feature-supported directions that are more likely to produce informative loss responses. Using multilayer and convolutional networks, we analyse gradient alignment, bias-variance structure, and training stability of these methods relative to standard perturbation baselines and backpropagation.

</details>


### [115] [Patching the Blind Spots: Reversible LLM Rule Overlay for Frozen Fraud Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08714)
*Bo Qu,Zhurong Wang,Frank Zahradnik,Ming Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种不改动已部署GBDT模型的"补丁"框架：用LLM针对SHAP揭示的欠权重特征提出规则，经经验筛选与校准成可加、可逆的log-lift覆盖层，并以三个LLM骨干的等权logit平均保证稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业表格欺诈分类中，梯度提升树虽为SOTA，但存在盲区，且对部署模型继续训练并不总是安全（在选择性偏差数据上即使精细调参也会退化），因此需要一种不修改原模型即可弥补其缺陷的安全方案。

Method: 以SHAP识别模型欠权重特征；由LLM据此提出规则，形成噪声规则池；对规则池做经验支持度筛选，并校准为在生产线截断点上运行的可加、可逆log-lift覆盖层。鉴于单一LLM收益高方差且跨数据集方向反转，采用三种多样化骨干的等权logit平均进行池化。

Result: 在真实电商欺诈检测器上（30–57%精度、23–63%召回的生产截断区间），覆盖层平均提升ΔR@P +2.26%、ΔP@R +1.60%，而继续训练反而退化；在含七种设计偏差偏移变体的公开基准上，所有变体的ΔP@R均提升，ΔR@P基本持平。

Conclusion: LLM生成的规则覆盖层可作为部署GBDT的安全补充：以牺牲峰值收益换取有界最坏情况，每条修正均为可读谓词并可逆，兼具可解释性与工程可行性。

Abstract: In industrial tabular fraud classification, gradient-boosted trees remain state-of-the-art, but even a well-trained model has blind spots, and retraining the deployed model is not always safe. We propose a framework that patches such a model without modifying it: LLMs propose rules anchored on features the model underweights by SHAP, and the resulting noisy pool is screened for empirical support and calibrated into an additive, reversible log-lift overlay that runs at the production cut-off. Single-LLM benefit is high-variance and inverts between datasets, so we pool three diverse backbones by equal-weight logit averaging, trading peak gain for a bounded worst case. On a real-world e-commerce fraud detector trained on selection-biased data, continued training regresses even when carefully tuned, whereas the overlay gains on average +2.26% recall at equal precision ($Δ$R@P) and +1.60% precision at equal recall ($Δ$P@R) over production cut-offs spanning 30-57% precision and 23-63% recall. On a public benchmark of seven designed bias-shift variants it raises $Δ$P@R on every variant and keeps $Δ$R@P near neutral. Each amendment is a readable predicate naming the fraud pattern it covers.

</details>


### [116] [On Differentially Private Mechanisms for Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08737)
*Bardiya Aryanfard,Monika Henzinger,Farhood Rostamkhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContinualAdaSSP——首个面向无约束线性回归的持续差分隐私（continual DP）机制，其次优间隙为 √d·polylog(n)，空间 O(d²log n)、更新时间 O(d²log n + d^ω)；同时给出 box 约束最小二乘回归的首个下界（min(n, √d/log^1.5 d) 对 (1,O(1/n))-DP，min(n, d/ε) 对 ε-DP），实验优于逐步重跑批处理算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理场景下的差分隐私线性回归已被广泛研究，但持续观测（continual observation）场景研究不足，既有工作仅限约束线性回归且更新时间关于 n 呈多项式；同时该任务的内在困难程度缺乏下界刻画。

Method: 基于 AdaSSP 思路构造 ContinualAdaSSP：在流式数据下维护带噪的充分统计量（如 XᵀX、Xᵀy）并利用矩阵机制/树状机制保证持续 DP，实现无约束回归估计；下界通过从 fingerprinting code 问题的归约得到。

Result: 机制次优间隙为 √d·polylog(n)，空间 O(d²log n)，更新时间 O(d²log n + d^ω)；下界为 min(n, √d/log^1.5 d)（(1,O(1/n))-DP）与 min(n, d/ε)（ε-DP）；真实数据集上精度明显优于逐步重跑批处理算法等已有 DP 机制。

Conclusion: 持续 DP 下的无约束线性回归可高效实现近优精度与低更新时间；同时所给下界揭示了该典型任务的固有难度，为后续算法设计与理论极限研究提供基准。

Abstract: We study the classic least-squares linear regression problem under differential privacy (DP) in the batch and the continual observation setting. In the latter setting, the mechanism receives a stream of $n$ data points $(x_i,y_i)$ ($x_i\in\mathbb{R}^d$ and $y_i\in\mathbb{R}$) and must output an estimate of the optimal parameters at each time step $i$. Although differentially private linear regression has been extensively studied in the batch setting, the problem remains underexplored for the continual setting, with prior work being restricted to constrained linear regression and update time polynomial in $n$.
  In this work, we introduce ContinualAdaSSP, the first mechanism for unconstrained linear regression under continual differential privacy. Our mechanism's suboptimality gap is proportional to $\sqrt{d}\operatorname{polylog}(n)$. It requires $O(d^2\log{n})$ space and $O(d^2\log{n}+d^ω)$ update time, where $d$ is the dimension of the input space.
  We also study fundamental limits of differentially private linear regression by showing the first lower bounds for the box-constrained least-squares linear regression problem. Our lower bounds are proportional to $\min(n,\frac{\sqrt{d}}{\log^{1.5}d})$ for $(1,O(\frac{1}{n}))$-DP, and $\min(n,\frac{d}ε)$ for $ε$-DP, and are derived using a reduction from a fingerprinting code problem from [Peter et al., 2024], providing new insights into the inherent hardness of this canonical task.
  Our experimental evaluation on real-world datasets shows that the accuracy of our continual observation mechanism clearly outperforms any other known differentially private mechanism for this problem such as rerunning the batch algorithm at each time step.

</details>


### [117] [Random Symbolic Boosting: A Geometric Approach to Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08753)
*José R. Ortegaa,José Manuel Muñoz Contreras,Leonardo Trujillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Random Symbolic Boosting（RSB）：在不做代际进化的前提下，用随机生成语法树的集成做boosting，构建显式可加的符号模型，并在回归与分类上实现训练误差单调下降，同时引入与尺度无关的几何早停。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号模型可解释性强，但通过代际进化（遗传编程类）构建代价高昂，需大量计算；作者希望在保留显式符号表达能力的同时大幅降低搜索成本。

Method: RSB基于两个观察：高维空间可由足够多的线性无关向量张成；语法树的语义可直接作为向量操控。方法对随机生成的语法树种群做boosting，线性组合其语义向量构成加性符号模型，无需交叉、变异等代际演化。理论部分证明在OLS目标下boosting过程对回归和分类均保证训练误差单调下降。另提出自适应几何早停：以预测向量与目标向量之间的夹角为依据，与问题尺度无关，跨任务无需调参。

Result: 在标准符号回归基准上，RSB位于预测精度与训练时间的Pareto最优点，在真实数据集上排名第一；在含200个问题的大规模表格分类基准上取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 用随机语法树种群上的boosting替代代际进化，可在获得可解释加性符号模型的同时兼顾精度与效率，并具备误差单调性的理论保证与免调参的早停机制，是一种实用且可扩展的符号建模方案。

Abstract: Symbolic models offer interpretability, but their construction through generational evolution can be computationally expensive. This article presents Random Symbolic Boosting (RSB), a method that constructs explicit additive symbolic models via boosting over populations of randomly generated syntax trees, eliminating the need for generational evolution. The idea combines two observations: high-dimensional spaces can be spanned by enough linearly independent vectors, and the semantics of syntax trees can be directly manipulated as such vectors. It is shown that, for Ordinary Least Squares, the boosting process guarantees a monotonic reduction of the training error for both regression and classification. Furthermore, an adaptive geometric early stopping mechanism is introduced. It is based on the angle between the prediction and target vectors, is independent of the problems scale, and requires no adjustments across tasks. Evaluated on a standard benchmark for symbolic regression, the method is located at the Pareto-optimal point between predictive accuracy and training time, ranking first on the real-world datasets. Additionally, it was tested on a large tabular classification benchmark with 200 problems, achieving competitive results.

</details>


### [118] [MEDALLION: Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08767)
*Vicky Jung,Claire M. He,Irene Chang,Genevera I. Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEDALLION 通过知识蒸馏将任意降维嵌入（如 t-SNE、UMAP）蒸馏为神经编码器，再用神经特征归因方法解释该编码器，从而揭示哪些原始特征贡献了低维表示，并在 MNIST 与单细胞转录组数据上验证其归因信息量优于现有可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维被广泛用于高维数据可视化与科学发现引导，但对于 t-SNE、UMAP 等黑盒方法，难以解释究竟是哪些原始特征促成了低维表示的形成，缺乏有效的归因手段。

Method: 提出 MEDALLION 框架，分两步：第一步以知识蒸馏方式用神经编码器拟合给定的低维嵌入；第二步借助一系列现有的神经特征归因方法（neural feature attribution）对该编码器进行解释，从而将归因结果回溯至原始特征。

Result: 在 MNIST 数据集实验及单细胞转录组应用中，MEDALLION 给出的特征归因具有信息量，能够为底层嵌入提供额外洞见，并与现有可解释性方法进行了性能对比。

Conclusion: 利用蒸馏+归因的解耦思路可有效解释黑盒降维嵌入，为高维数据的特征级解读提供通用框架。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction is widely used to visualize high-dimensional data and guide scientific discovery, yet interpreting which original features contribute to low-dimensional representations remains challenging, particularly for black-box methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. We introduce \textbf{MEDALLION} (Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON), a novel framework for interpreting low-dimensional embeddings through knowledge distillation and neural feature attribution. MEDALLION distills any given embedding with a neural encoder which we explain via a broad range of existing neural feature attribution methods. Through experiments on MNIST and applications in single-cell transcriptomics, we show MEDALLION provides informative feature attributions that cast additional insights into the underlying embedding and compare its performance with existing interpretability approaches.

</details>


### [119] [On Lipschitz Explosion in Deep Neural Networks with Normalization: Consequences for Optimization and Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08775)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Reyhaneh Hosseinpourkhoshkbari,Hadi Daneshmand,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：含归一化层（以批量归一化为代表）的深度网络，即使逐层谱范数受控（如‖W_ℓ‖₂≤1）、且使用线性激活，其输入Lipschitz常数与参数Lipschitz常数仍可随深度（即随参数维度）指数增长，导致基于Lipschitz的优化、泛化与鲁棒性保证失效；并提出“秩创造型扰动”应被更强放大的机制，在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lipschitz常数是优化保证、泛化界与鲁棒性证书中的核心稳定性量。深度网络存在两种Lipschitz性：输入Lipschitz性（对数据扰动的敏感性）与参数Lipschitz性（对权重扰动的敏感性）。实践中常以逐层范数约束来控制稳定性，但作者质疑这种局部控制能否保证全局稳定性——尤其是归一化层（如BN）被广泛用于稳定训练，其归一化操作本身可能引入逐层不可见的worst-case不稳定性。因此需要建立深度归一化网络Lipschitz常数随深度的严格下界，并给出可检验的行为预测。

Method: 理论分析为主：针对含归一化层的深网（以批量归一化为典型实例），在强逐层范数控制‖W_ℓ‖₂≤1、甚至线性激活的设定下，构造并证明输入Lipschitz常数与参数Lipschitz常数随深度指数增长的下界；据此推导对Lipschitz依赖的非光滑优化保证（退化为维度指数级界）、Lipschitz型泛化与鲁棒性证书（变空洞）的后果。进一步从理论上刻画秩分离机制：引入新奇异方向的扰动与停留在原输入奇异子空间内的同等噪声水平扰动在放大效应上的差异。实验端在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上对比“秩创造型扰动”与“同子空间扰动”对准确率、置信度与间隔的影响。

Result: 理论结果：在逐层谱范数≤1的强控制下，归一化深网的输入Lipschitz常数与参数Lipschitz常数均可随深度/参数维度指数增长，且线性激活下即已发生；由此使Lipschitz依赖的非光滑优化保证变为维度指数级界，并使基于Lipschitz的worst-case泛化界与鲁棒性证书变得空洞（vacuous）。理论还预测：引入新奇异方向的扰动应比同噪声水平但限制在原奇异子空间内的扰动被显著更强地放大，并给出对抗脆弱性的秩分离解释。实验支持：在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上，秩创造型扰动导致准确率、置信度与间隔的下降显著强于同子空间扰动。

Conclusion: 稳定性不能仅靠逐层范数界来保证：被广泛用于稳定训练的归一化机制可能在全局层面制造严重的worst-case不稳定性，且这种不稳定性在逐层局部视角下不可见。因此，逐层Lipschitz/范数约束不足以支撑深度归一化网络的优化、泛化与鲁棒性理论保证；秩创造型扰动作为一种可实验验证的脆弱性机制，为理解与设计对抗鲁棒性提供了新的视角与准则。

Abstract: Can a network look stable at every layer and still be exponentially unstable as a whole? The Lipschitz constant of a neural network is a basic stability quantity appearing in optimization guarantees, generalization bounds, and robustness certificates. Deep networks naturally admit two such notions, input Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to data perturbations, and parameter Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to weight perturbations. We prove that, for deep networks with normalization layers, with batch normalization as the canonical example, both Lipschitz constants can grow exponentially with depth and hence with parameter dimension, even under strong per-layer norm control such as $\|W_\ell\|_2\le 1$, and already for linear activations. Thus, a mechanism widely used to stabilize training can create severe worst-case instabilities that are invisible locally, i.e., from layerwise norm bounds alone. Parameter-Lipschitz explosion turns Lipschitz-dependent nonsmooth optimization guarantees into exponential-in-dimension bounds for deep normalized networks, while input-Lipschitz explosion makes worst-case Lipschitz-based generalization and robustness certificates vacuous. The latter also yields a concrete rank-separation mechanism for adversarial vulnerability. The theory predicts that perturbations introducing new singular directions should be amplified much more strongly than perturbations at the same noise level that remain within the input's original singular subspace. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 support this prediction, showing that rank-creating perturbations cause substantially sharper drops in accuracy, confidence, and margins than same-subspace perturbations.

</details>


### [120] [Training Stability under Adaptive Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08787)
*Johan Obando-Ceron,Walter Mayor,Michael Dennis,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究UED（无监督环境设计）中学生学习器在自适应课程分布下是否"可训练"，识别出参数增长、价值-结果排序退化、表征多样性丧失等学习器侧病理，并证明缓解这些病理可在不改课程规则、不增交互的前提下提升长时程性能与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往UED研究主要关注课程如何构建（教师侧），而忽视了学习器（学生侧）在课程产生的分布上能否持续有效学习，即"可训练性"问题。

Method: 在多种UED机制、环境复杂度与延长训练时程下系统观察学习器行为；对比分析停滞与性能退化时出现的病理现象，并与常规深度RL中已知的优化失败相联系；通过受控干预（针对性地缓解所选病理）进行验证。

Result: 识别出反复出现的学习器侧病理：参数增长、价值-结果排序退化、表征多样性丧失，且与训练停滞/性能下降相伴随；受控干预表明缓解这些病理可改善学习，提升长时程性能与零样本泛化，且无需改变课程规则或增加环境交互。

Conclusion: 将"学习器可训练性"确立为UED的互补设计原则：有效课程不仅取决于教师如何生成分布，也要求学生能持续从这些分布中学习；自适应课程会将学习器优化状态与其未来训练分布耦合。

Abstract: Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) approaches train reinforcement learning (RL) agents on diverse curricula that may evolve with their capabilities. While prior work has largely focused on how these curricula are constructed, we study whether their students remain trainable under the resulting distributions. Across multiple UED mechanisms, environment complexities, and extended training horizons, we identify recurring learner-side pathologies, including parameter growth, degraded value--outcome ordering, and loss of representational diversity, that accompany stagnation and performance degradation. These findings connect limitations in UED to optimization failures previously identified in conventional deep RL, while showing how adaptive curricula couple the learner's optimization state to its future training distribution. Controlled interventions show that mitigating selected pathologies improves learning. Stabilizing the learner improves long-horizon performance and zero-shot generalization without changing the curriculum rule or increasing environment interaction. Our results establish learner trainability as a complementary design principle for UED. Effective curricula require students that remain capable of learning from the distributions they produce.

</details>


### [121] [Not All Latents Are Equal: Importance-Weighted Latent Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08788)
*Biraaj Rout,Jay Revolinsky,Han Liu,Harry Shomer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Latent Importance Reweighting (LIR)，通过对潜变量各维度按其对图重构的相对重要性加权，缓解潜空间图生成模型因潜维度重要性分布不均而落后于离散扩散模型的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散图扩散的计算复杂度随节点数呈二次增长，难以扩展到大规模图；潜空间图生成因维度更低、可扩展性更好，但在实际性能上落后于离散方法。作者发现该差距源于不同潜维度对图重构的影响分布极不均衡——绝大多数影响集中在少数潜维度上，而现有损失函数对所有潜维度等权重建，导致模型在无关下游任务的信息上浪费优化能力。

Method: 提出LIR（Latent Importance Reweighting）：以单个潜单元对图重构的相对影响度量其重要性，该重要性通过解码器相对于潜变量的雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）计算；并据此对每个潜维度在损失函数中加权。该方法与模型无关，不引入额外模型参数或超参数。

Result: 在多种基准数据集上，将LIR应用于两种不同的自编码器均可提升生成性能，并带来更快的训练收敛。

Conclusion: 潜图生成性能落后的关键原因在于潜维度重要性的不均衡分布与等权重建损失之间的错配；基于雅可比的重要性重加权可作为一种通用、轻量的改进手段，有效提升潜空间图生成模型的性能与训练效率。

Abstract: Graph generation has shown tremendous effectiveness in modeling complex scientific tasks ranging from molecule generation to optimized circuit design. Most recent advancements have developed models that learn to denoise noisy graphs using either discrete diffusion or flow matching. However, discrete graph diffusion scales quadratically with the number of nodes, making it difficult to use such models on larger graphs. On the other hand, latent graph generation instead seeks to denoise noisy graph latents, which scale better to more nodes due to their lower dimension. However, in practice, latent graph generation models lag behind their discrete counterparts in performance. We study this problem, finding that it is caused by the unequal distribution of that different latent dimensions have on graph reconstruction. the impact. We observe that the vast majority of impact is contained in only a smaller number of latent dimensions. Since equal emphasis is placed on reconstructing the entire latent in the loss function, existing models are optimized to reconstruct information irrelevant to the downstream task. To tackle this problem, we propose Latent Importance Reweighting ({\bf LIR)} which weights each latent dimensions via it's importance to final graph reconstruction. Importance is calculated as the relative influence of a single latent unit on graph reconstruction, which is represented via the Jacobian of the decoder relative to the latent. Our method is model-agnostic and requires no additional model parameters or hyperparameters. Experiments show that LIR can boost performance and lead to faster training convergence when applied to two different autoencoders on various benchmark datasets. Code: https://github.com/biraaj/GraphGenLatentReweight

</details>


### [122] [TopoSHAP: Shapley for Explainable and Efficient Topological Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08789)
*Mathilde Papillon,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TopoSHAP 可解释性框架，含 TopoSHAP-cell（为拓扑数据单元分配 Shapley 值，提取紧凑解释）和 TopoSHAP-neighbor（解释邻域对模型性能的贡献并用于反向消元架构搜索），兼顾拓扑域与架构两个层面的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习（TDL）将图学习扩展到分子中的环、社交网络中的群体等高阶结构，但目前难以解释哪些拓扑结构驱动了模型预测，以及哪些架构组件对性能最关键。

Method: 以 Shapley 值为基础构建通用、高效的拓扑解释框架：TopoSHAP-cell 向拓扑数据单元（cell）分配 Shapley 值并提取紧凑解释；TopoSHAP-neighbor 评估邻域对模型性能的贡献，并将其转化为反向消元搜索，以在训练早期识别更小且具竞争力的架构。

Result: 在 GraphXAI 基准数据上，TopoSHAP-cell 的解释质量匹配或优于基于图的对照方法，同时速度最高快 112×；并揭示有利的拓扑特征设计，带来解释恢复率与预测精度的可测量提升。TopoSHAP-neighbor 的架构搜索比传统 TDL 训练扫描最多便宜 11.3×。

Conclusion: TopoSHAP 提供了跨拓扑域与模型架构的统一解释方案，在效率与解释质量上兼具优势，并可用于指导拓扑特征设计与轻量化架构发现。

Abstract: Topological deep learning (TDL) extends learning on graphs, such as molecules or social networks, to higher-order structures, such as rings in a molecule or groups in a social network. Explaining which of these structures drive a model's predictions, and which architectural components matter most for its performance, remains challenging. We introduce TopoSHAP, an efficient and general explanation framework that addresses both questions across topological domains and model architectures. TopoSHAP-cell assigns Shapley values to topological data cells and extracts compact explanations. These explanations match or outperform graph-based counterparts on GraphXAI benchmark data while running up to 112$\times$ faster. They also reveal advantageous topological feature design, leading to measured improvements in explanation recovery and predictive accuracy. On the architecture side, TopoSHAP-neighbor explains how neighborhoods contribute to model performance, and turns these evaluations into a backward-elimination search that identifies smaller, competitive architectures early in training. This is up to $11.3\times$ cheaper than traditional TDL training sweeps.

</details>


### [123] [MotionBench-XAI: Benchmarking What Shapes Shapley Explanations in Spatiotemporal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08797)
*Mathilde Papillon,Louisa Cornelis,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MotionBench-XAI基准，用带精确Shapley真值的合成时空数据检验归因方法，发现"玩家"定义（时间/空间/时空分组）比填补器选择更关键，生成式填补器仅在其条件模型准确时才有益，且真实数据上标准代理指标相互矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空数据（如用人体运动评估帕金森病严重程度）的预测不仅要求准确，还需要可解释性，即医生需知道预测由哪个身体关节（空间）或哪个时间窗（时间）驱动。Shapley值虽被广泛使用，但其计算依赖三个隐含选择：何为一个"玩家"、缺失玩家如何填补、博弈如何评估。这些选择对解释质量的影响在时空数据上缺乏研究，且真实数据没有真值归因可供验证。

Method: 构建MotionBench-XAI基准，包含四部分：(i) 五个合成时空数据集，具备精确的Shapley真值；(ii) 三个真实数据集，覆盖临床步态、心电图与音频；(iii) 十种归因方法，横跨三类玩家集合（时间、空间、时空）；(iv) 三种已训练的网络架构。通过对比各方法输出与合成真值的偏差，系统分析玩家划分、填补策略与博弈评估方式的影响。

Result: 实验表明：将特征按时间、空间或时空组合成玩家，其影响大于填补器的选择；生成式填补器只有在条件模型准确时才能改善解释质量；在真实数据上，常用的代理指标（如删除曲线、AOPC等）会选出相互矛盾的最优方法，说明其可靠性存疑。

Conclusion: 作者将上述发现提炼为可供实践者参考的指导性结论，并开源完整流水线，为时空数据上的可解释性方法评估提供标准化基准与可信的选型依据。

Abstract: Predicting outcomes from spatiotemporal data, such as assessing Parkinson's severity from human motion, often requires explainability in addition to accuracy. Practitioners need to know what drove the prediction, whether it be spatial (a body joint), temporal (a specific window), or both. Shapley values are attributions computed by treating input features as players in a cooperative game and measuring how the model output changes as coalitions of players are removed. Computing them involves choosing what counts as players, how absent players are imputed, and how the game is evaluated. How these choices affect explanation quality is poorly understood for spatiotemporal data, and real data carries no ground-truth attributions to check against. We present MotionBench-XAI, a benchmark built on i) five synthetic spatiotemporal datasets with exact ground-truth Shapley values, ii) three real datasets across clinical gait, electrocardiogram, and audio, iii) ten attribution methods across three player sets, and iv) three trained architectures. We show that grouping features into players, across time, space, or both, matters more than the choice of imputer. Generative imputers improve explanations only when their conditional models are accurate. On real data, the standard proxy metrics select contradictory best methods. We distill these findings into practical insights and provide an open-source pipeline.

</details>


### [124] [On the Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models: A Few Gradient Signs Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08800)
*Nairouz Mrabah,Ismail Ben Ayed,Mahdi S. Hosseini,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言模型少样本适配中梯度估计噪声大的问题，提出仅用梯度幅值选更新位置、用固定幅值符号决定更新方向的无状态优化器 SignSparseSGD 与 DynSignSparseSGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本适配的监督信号极少，梯度估计噪声大；作者发现梯度幅值严重失准（miscalibrated），而少数大幅值分量的符号仍然具有信息量。

Method: 两种无状态（state-free）优化器：梯度幅值仅用于选择"在哪里"更新（稀疏支撑集），更新方向采用固定幅值的符号。静态版本在第一个 minibatch 后固定支撑集；动态版本每步刷新支撑集。不引入任务特定模块，不维护一阶/二阶矩状态，所有数据集、骨干、shot 设置共用一套配置。

Result: 在 11 个数据集、3 个 CLIP 骨干、5 种 shot 设置上，平均性能与参数高效及基于优化的基线相当或更优，且对长训练计划（schedule）的敏感性显著更低。

Conclusion: 以符号稀疏更新替代幅值加权更新，可在极低监督下实现稳健、低内存的少样本适配优化。

Abstract: The limited supervision available for the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models makes gradient estimation particularly noisy. We find that the gradient magnitudes are severely miscalibrated, while the signs of a small top-magnitude subset remain informative. This motivates SignSparseSGD and DynSignSparseSGD, two state-free optimizers that use gradient magnitudes only to select where to update and fixed-magnitude signs to decide how to update. The static variant fixes the support after the first minibatch. The dynamic variant refreshes it at every step. Across 11 datasets, three CLIP backbones, and five shot levels, the proposed methods are competitive with or outperform parameter-efficient and optimization-based baselines on average, while exhibiting markedly lower sensitivity to long training schedules. They introduce no task-specific modules, maintain no first- or second-moment optimizer states, and use one shared configuration across datasets, backbones, and shot levels. Our code is included in the supplementary materials and will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [125] [Global Solar Siting from Geospatial Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08831)
*Abdullah Rehman,Quentin Paletta,Rui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用地理空间嵌入(dense vector)学习潜太阳能适宜性信号，构建RF+MLP软投票集成模型，在全球13国验证取得中位ROC-AUC 0.90，较MCDA方法提升0.06–0.30，并生成10 m分辨率全球太阳能选址适宜性数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球光伏装机快速增长，但最优选址仍困难，需兼顾地理、城市与基础设施条件。现有多准则决策分析(MCDA)依赖人工挑选的地球物理图层与太阳资源产品，地理可迁移性差，且难以反映真实电站的实践需求。

Method: 以地理空间嵌入（表示地点地球物理属性的稠密向量）为特征，分析38,591个现有光伏电站及其所在土地，学习潜在的太阳能适宜性信号；比较多种线性与非线性模型，最终选用随机森林与多层感知机(RF+MLP)的软投票集成，兼顾精度与大规模可部署性；在13个国家的未来新增电站上验证，并与已发表MCDA策略对比；结合标准选址约束进行全球制图。

Result: 集成模型区分现有电站与随机土地的ROC-AUC中位数为0.90；在各国家较MCDA方法提升ROC-AUC 0.06–0.30；排序具有强聚集性：最适宜20%土地覆盖约84%的估算年发电量与67%的装机容量；施加标准选址约束后，全球5.6%土地（若允许耕地则9.8%）高度适宜太阳能开发。产出全球10 m分辨率适宜性数据集与代码。

Conclusion: 基于地理空间嵌入的机器学习集成方法可地理迁移、精度优于传统MCDA，能有效刻画光伏电站的真实选址偏好，并为全球高分辨率太阳能选址规划提供公开数据与工具支撑。

Abstract: Global solar photovoltaic (PV) capacity is rapidly growing, helping decrease our emissions and dependence on fossil fuels. However, optimally siting a solar plant remains challenging, requiring anticipation of geographical, urban, and infrastructure needs. Current methods rely on multi-criteria decision analysis (MCDA) using hand-picked geophysical layers and solar-resource products, however these strategies are not geographically transferable and do not capture the practical requirements of solar sites. We use geospatial embeddings, dense vectors representing geophysical properties of a location, to analyse 38,591 existing solar sites and the land they were built on to learn the latent solar suitability signal. We assess a set of linear and non-linear models and select a soft-voting ensemble of a random forest and a multilayer perceptron (RF+MLP ensemble), chosen for accuracy and large scale deployability. Validated against future installations in thirteen countries, the ensemble separates existing solar sites from random land with a median ROC-AUC of 0.90. Compared with published MCDA strategies, the ensemble increases ROC-AUC by 0.06-0.30 in every country. The ranking also concentrates real deployment: the most-suitable 20% of land holds 84% of estimated generation (annual energy yield) and 67% of installed capacity (potential power output). After applying standard siting constraints, 5.6% of global land (9.8% if cropland is permitted) remains highly suited to solar. The global 10 m resolution solar suitability dataset and code can be found here: https://github.com/Abdullahr29/Global-Solar-Siting.

</details>


### [126] [What Matters for LLM Persona Simulation: Signal Form in Cold-Start E-Commerce Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08840)
*Jiwoo Choi,In Ik Lee,Woo Sung Seo,Minho Lee,Yun Young Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究冷启动广告效果预估，通过真实Google Ads电商广告数据识别出影响LLM角色模拟质量的三个信号形式维度，MAE 24.9、MAPE 26.1%，显著优于非LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动场景下广告上线前无历史日志，传统效果预估困难；LLM可模拟带角色条件的用户响应，但哪类设计选择真正驱动模拟质量尚不清楚。

Method: 基于真实Google Ads电商广告活动、覆盖五个人口统计分群的日志，构建LLM角色条件模拟方法，并系统消融三类信号形式设计：(i) 仅用人口统计锚点、去除噪声性丰富属性；(ii) 使用相对定性表达（定性的"横幅盲视"信号）而非定量锚点；(iii) 提示词措辞。并与直接先验、规则基线及其他LLM配置对比。

Result: MAE 24.9、MAPE 26.1%；较最佳非LLM基线MAE降低51.3%，较使用显式CTR锚点的LLM配置降低77.3%。消融显示最小化人口统计描述误差低于丰富画像配置，相对定性线索显著优于所测定量锚点。

Conclusion: LLM角色模拟的收益主要来自信号形式而非信息量：简洁人口统计锚点加相对定性线索是有效设计原则，为冷启动广告效果预估提供了可复现的配置范式。

Abstract: Estimating advertising performance in cold-start settings is challenging because historical logs are unavailable before launch. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by simulating persona-conditioned user responses, but it remains unclear which design choices actually drive LLM-based persona simulation. Using real Google Ads e-commerce campaign data across five demographic segments, we identify three signal-form dimensions that affect simulation quality: (i) a demographic anchor without noisy rich attributes, (ii) a relative qualitative banner-blindness signal rather than quantitative anchors, and (iii) prompt phrasing. On this campaign, our method attains an MAE of 24.9 and a MAPE of 26.1%, a 51.3% lower MAE than the best tested non-LLM baseline and 77.3% lower than an LLM configuration using explicit CTR anchors. Systematic ablations show that minimal demographic descriptions achieve lower mean error than richer persona configurations, while relative qualitative cues substantially outperform the tested quantitative anchors. We additionally compare these configurations with direct-prior and rule-based baselines and with alternative LLM configurations.

</details>


### [127] [Reconstructing LLM Lineage Trees via Fine-Tuning Path Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08866)
*Sota Ogawa,Shojiro Yamabe,Daiki Nishiyama,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE-Edge方法，在灰盒访问（仅teacher-forced对数似然）下重建含内部节点的有向微调模型树，以非对称逐任务对数似然偏差构造边权并求解最大生成有向树，父节点匹配准确率0.74。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调衍生模型大量发布，但其来源仅靠自我声明、无法验证；错误归属使验证者无法审计外包微调、确认许可合规或追踪父模型传递给子模型的漏洞，而这些都需要推导的"方向"而非仅存在性。已有仅基于输出的方法局限于成对推导检验或无向谱系估计，恢复有向树需要白盒权重访问。

Method: 将灰盒访问（仅观测teacher-forced对数似然、不检查权重）下重建含内部节点的有向模型树形式化。利用微调提升训练数据对数似然且子模型继承父模型该提升的性质，定义任意两模型间的非对称逐任务对数似然偏差作为微调历史的痕迹，并据此为每对有序模型导出边权；TRACE-Edge在该边权下精确求解最大生成有向树，无需超参数与树结构约束。

Result: 在3个基座模型、7个任务构成的5种配置共25棵模型树上评估：以微调样本为探针时父节点匹配准确率为0.74；即使使用与训练数据同分布的留出探针，平均而言仍优于输出相似度基线。

Conclusion: 非对称对数似然偏差可作为微调历史的可验证痕迹，使灰盒条件下有向谱系树的精确重建成为可能，且无需超参数与结构先验，并在同分布留出探针下保持对相似度基线的优势。

Abstract: Fine-tuned (FT) derivatives of large language models (LLMs) are now released in large numbers, yet their provenance relies on self-reports and cannot be verified. Misattributed lineage leaves a verifier unable to audit outsourced fine-tuning, confirm license compliance of derived models, or trace vulnerabilities that a parent passes to its children. All three require the direction of derivation, not merely its presence. Existing output-only methods are limited to pairwise derivation tests or undirected lineage estimation, while recovering a directed tree requires white-box access to model weights. We formalize the problem of reconstructing a directed model tree, including internal nodes, under gray-box access, where only teacher-forced log-likelihoods are observed and weights are never inspected. Fine-tuning raises the log-likelihood of the training data, and a child inherits this increase from its parent. Building on this property, we define an asymmetric per-task log-likelihood deviation between any two models as a trace of fine-tuning history, and derive from it an edge weight for each ordered pair of models. We propose TRACE-Edge, which exactly solves for the maximum spanning directed tree under these edge weights, with neither hyperparameters nor tree-structure constraints. We evaluate it on five configurations totaling 25 model trees built from three base models and seven tasks. TRACE-Edge achieves a parent-match accuracy of 0.74 when probed with the fine-tuning examples, and on average outperforms an output-similarity baseline even with held-out probes drawn from the same distribution as the training data.

</details>


### [128] [Learning to Restart: Differentiable Restart Policies for Optimization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08883)
*Yiman Fong,Zhonglin Xie,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微的重启策略框架：借助连续时间极限，为离散重启事件构造替代梯度，使重启策略可端到端训练，并在二次规划、线性规划和真实数据上超越手工重启准则且可零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应重启机制（重置动量或辅助状态）对加速一阶方法的经验性能至关重要，但现有重启规则几乎全靠人工设计。其根本障碍在于：与常规 L2O 不同，重启是离散事件，导致轨迹对算法参数不可微，无法用梯度法优化重启策略。

Method: 在连续时间极限下建立重启策略的可微框架：为重启事件与优化轨迹引入高效可计算的替代梯度（surrogate gradients），并证明其收敛到对应的连续时间量，从而将离散重启决策纳入基于梯度的 L2O 训练。

Result: 在 L2O 设置中验证替代梯度的有效性；训练得到的重启策略在二次规划与线性规划上优于标准手工自适应重启准则，并能从合成二次问题零样本迁移到真实数据集上的逻辑回归任务。

Conclusion: 连续时间极限是绕开重启离散性、实现重启策略可微学习的一条可行路径，所提替代梯度兼顾计算效率与理论收敛性，学到的策略具备跨问题族迁移能力。

Abstract: Adaptive restart mechanisms, which reset the momentum or other auxiliary state of an iterative optimizer, play a central role in the empirical performance of accelerated first-order methods, yet existing restart rules remain almost entirely hand-designed. The obstruction to learning restart policies is that, unlike the typical Learning to Optimize (L2O) setting, the discrete restart events make the resulting trajectory non-differentiable in the algorithm parameters and thus block gradient-based optimization of the restart policy. We resolve this obstruction by developing a differentiable framework for restart policies through their continuous-time limit. We introduce surrogate gradients for the restart events and optimization trajectories that are efficient to compute and provably converge to their continuous-time counterparts. We validate the effectiveness of our surrogate gradients in L2O settings, training restart policies that improve upon standard handcrafted adaptive restart criteria on quadratic and linear programs, and that transfer zero-shot from synthetic quadratics to logistic regression on real datasets.

</details>


### [129] [SSF-Net: A Spatial-Spectral Fusion Network for Accurate PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08912)
*Siqi Zhao,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SSF-Net，以空间-谱双分支融合模块同时捕获PDE的局部传播与全局耦合动态，在更低参数与近线性复杂度下取得更低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器（如FEM）效率受限，机器学习方法虽提升效率，但PDE动力学同时包含局部（扰动逐点传播与衰减）与全局（全域同时耦合）两类行为，现有低复杂度模型难以兼顾二者。

Method: 核心为空间-谱融合（SSF）块：空间分支采用多方向Mamba，缓解其因果单向扫描偏置，以线性复杂度捕获局部交互并降低方向偏差；谱分支采用傅里叶变换配合块对角MLP，以较少参数和复杂度高效提取全局谱特征。SSF块嵌入多分辨率卷积编码器-解码器以提取多尺度特征，构成SSF-Net。

Result: 在多任务类型构成的综合PDE基准上评估，并考察长时程稳定性；相较注意力类和卷积类基线，SSF-Net以更少参数量取得更低预测误差，同时在空间token数量上保持近线性渐近复杂度。

Conclusion: 空间分支与谱分支的融合是兼顾PDE局部与全局动态的有效范式，可在低参数、近线性复杂度约束下提升求解精度与长时程稳定性。

Abstract: Machine learning methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) significantly improve computational efficiency over traditional numerical solvers such as finite element method (FEM). However, PDE dynamics inherently span both local and global dynamics: disturbances propagate and decay gradually through the domain, while global coupling effects can influence the whole system simultaneously. Capturing both types of dynamics with a low complexity model remains a key challenge. To address this, we propose the Spatial-Spectral Fusion Network (SSF-Net), built around a dual-branch Spatial-Spectral Fusion (SSF) block comprising a spatial branch and a spectral branch. The spatial branch employs a multi-directional Mamba, which mitigates its inherent causal, single-direction scanning bias and capture local interactions with reduced directional bias at linear complexity. The spectral branch leverages a Fourier transform with a block-diagonal MLP to efficiently capture global spectral features with reduced parameter count and complexity. The SSF block is embedded in a multiresolution convolutional encoder-decoder, which extracts multi-scale features. We evaluate SSF-Net on a comprehensive PDE benchmark spanning multiple task types and assess its long-horizon stability. Compared with attention-based and convolution-based baselines, SSF-Net achieves lower prediction error with fewer parameters while retaining near-linear asymptotic complexity in the number of spatial tokens.

</details>


### [130] [Composable Causality: A Toolkit for Systematic Time-Series Causal Discovery and Treatment-Effect Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08918)
*Muhammad Hasan Ferdous,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可组合数据生成工具库DTCD，用配置独立组合拓扑、机制、噪声、观测掩码与干预策略，生成带真值同期/滞后邻接矩阵的多变量时间序列，并以缓存随机实现的方式生成配对反事实孪生样本，支持受控单因素实验与个体处理效应数值精确评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实多变量时间序列缺乏经校验的因果真值，故时序因果发现与处理效应估计研究依赖合成数据；但现有工具要么是发布后不可修改的固定数据集，要么是"脚本式"生成器——每个脚本固化一种机制、混杂与缺失性的组合，二者都无法在固定其他属性（如噪声分布、缺失机制、稀疏度）时仅改变单一属性做受控实验；反事实评估同样存在缺口：现有模拟器多为按论文定制、机制硬编码，或仅产生观测数据。

Method: 构建DTCD数据生成库：将拓扑、机制、噪声、观测掩码、干预策略设计为相互独立、可仅通过配置组合的组件；通过缓存事实性rollout中的随机实现，并在干预性rollout中重新注入，生成配对反事实孪生样本；提供含81种配置的八个因果发现算法参考基准，以及含18种配置的四个处理效应估计器基准；对varsortability进行度量和标准化消除。

Result: 配对反事实孪生可将个体处理效应恢复到数值精度；基准实验揭示聚合指标所掩盖的失效模式：(1) 标准因果发现方法无法处理缺失值，无论缺失机制是否为信息性；(2) 存在边际方差捷径（varsortability），经标准化后可度量并去除。

Conclusion: DTCD以"可组合配置"替代固定数据集与硬编码脚本，为时序因果发现与反事实/处理效应评估提供可做单因素受控实验、带因果真值且支持个体效应精确评估的基准平台，并暴露了现有方法在缺失数据与varsortability上的系统性缺陷。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Real-world multivariate time series rarely come with verified ground-truth causal structure, so research on time-series causal discovery and treatment-effect estimation depends on synthetic data. Existing tools are either fixed datasets that cannot be modified after release or script-based generators in which each script fixes one combination of mechanism, confounding, and missingness. Neither supports controlled experiments that vary a single property, such as sparsity, noise distribution, or missingness mechanism, while holding the others fixed. Counterfactual evaluation has the same gap, since existing simulators are built per paper with hardcoded mechanisms or produce observational data only. We introduce DTCD (Datasets and Toolkit for Causal Discovery), a data generation library that produces multivariate time series with ground-truth contemporaneous and lagged adjacency matrices. Topology, mechanism, noise, observational mask, and intervention policy are independent components that compose through configuration alone. Paired counterfactual twins, produced by caching the stochastic realization during the factual rollout and re-injecting it during the interventional rollout, recover individual treatment effects to numerical precision. A reference benchmark of eight discovery algorithms on eighty-one configurations and four treatment-effect estimators on eighteen configurations exposes failure modes that aggregate metrics hide. These include the inability of standard discovery methods to handle missing values, regardless of whether the missingness mechanism is informative, and a marginal-variance shortcut (varsortability) that we measure and remove by standardization. Code and data: https://github.com/hferdous/Composable_Causality

</details>


### [131] [ResQAdam: Residual-Preserving Adaptive Optimization for Memory-Efficient Quantization Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08924)
*Liqun Ma,Shengkun Tang,Liangyu Wang,Lin Zhao,Wei Luo,Xinjun Niu,Spandan Tiwari,Ashish Sirasao,Kun Zhang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ResQAdam：单个 BF16 动量 + 主子空间 r×r 坐标上的 Adam 更新 + 残差归一化符号更新，在2bit QAT 中相比 AdamW 降低 71%–74% 优化器状态内存，性能接近 AdamW，吞吐损失<2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAT 是获得准确低比特 LLM 最可靠的方法，但 AdamW 需为每个权重矩阵维护两个稠密 FP32 状态，内存开销大。现有低秩省内存优化器（GaLore、LDAdam）在 QAT 中显著退化：QAT 动量有相当部分落在 top-r 奇异子空间之外，而投影类方法每步都丢弃该部分。

Method: 关键观察：残差部分即使只保留符号仍然有用。据此设计 ResQAdam：仅维护单个 BF16 动量；对主子空间的 r×r 坐标施加 Adam 更新；再叠加由残差计算得到的归一化符号更新。

Result: 在两种从头 QAT 方法与三种 QAT 微调流程、权重量化至 2bit 的设置下评估：优化器状态内存较 AdamW 减少 71%–74%，多数设置下性能接近 AdamW，训练吞吐下降不到 2%。

Conclusion: 残差符号信息对 QAT 动量不可忽略，保留其符号即可弥补投影丢弃带来的损失；ResQAdam 以极小吞吐代价实现大幅内存节省，是 QAT 场景下兼顾内存与精度的优化器方案。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) is the most reliable way to obtain accurate low-bit large language models (LLMs), but training with AdamW keeps two dense FP32 states for every weight matrix. We find that low-rank memory-efficient optimizers such as GaLore and LDAdam degrade substantially in QAT. A considerable part of the QAT momentum lies outside its top-r singular subspace, and projection-based methods discard this part at every step. We further observe that this residual remains useful when only its sign is kept. Based on these observations, we propose ResQAdam. It keeps a single BF16 momentum, applies Adam only to the r x r coordinates of the principal subspace, and adds a normalized sign update computed from the residual. We evaluate ResQAdam with two from-scratch QAT methods and three QAT fine-tuning pipelines, with weights quantized down to 2 bits. It reduces optimizer-state memory by 71% to 74% relative to AdamW, stays close to AdamW in most settings, and lowers training throughput by less than 2%.

</details>


### [132] [Ordered Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08936)
*Dongkyu Derek Cho,Pan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPO（Ordered Policy Optimization），按重要性比排序并为极端比值分配自适应样本级裁剪半径，替代PPO的固定裁剪范围，在MuJoCo和Atari上取得优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO用统一固定裁剪区间处理所有样本的重要性比，无法区分统计上有害的极端比值与幅度较大但仍可接受的策略变化；作者认为可靠更新需要控制极端比值的分布，而非仅检查单个比值是否落在固定区间内。

Method: 在每个minibatch中对重要性比排序，选出对更新影响可能最大的极端比值，依据预设的"尾部轮廓"（tail profile，即对选中极端比值设定的目标值）推导出自适应的样本级裁剪半径，取代固定半径；目标函数仍为一阶形式，仅需minibatch排序与修改后的策略损失。

Result: 在MuJoCo与Atari基准上，该简单改动相对既有基线取得较强性能。

Conclusion: 将裁剪半径从固定值改为基于排序与尾部轮廓的自适应样本级半径，是一种简单有效、计算代价低的策略优化改进，能更精细地控制极端重要性比。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) stabilizes policy updates by clipping the importance ratio between the new policy and the data-collecting policy. The standard rule uses one fixed clipping range for all samples. While this choice offers simplicity and effectiveness, it fails to distinguish statistically harmful extreme ratio values from large, yet tolerable changes. We study how to adapt the clipping radius to the reliability of the induced importance ratio distribution. Our case study demonstrates that reliable updates require controlling these extreme ratios, rather than merely verifying that individual ratios fall within a fixed interval. Motivated by this observation, we propose Ordered Policy Optimization (\textsc{OPO}). In each minibatch, \textsc{OPO} sorts the importance ratios, selects the extreme ratios that potentially have the largest effect on the update, and assigns them adaptive sample-wise radii instead of using the fixed clipping radius. These radii are derived from a prescribed tail profile, which specifies desired target values for the selected extreme ratios. The resulting objective remains first-order and requires only minibatch sorting and a modified policy loss. Experiments conducted on MuJoCo and Atari benchmarks demonstrate that this simple modification achieves strong performance relative to established baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Surrogate Quality Score: A Novel Composite Performance Metric for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08937)
*Maaz Ahmad,Iftekhar A. Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代理模型质量分数（SQS），通过预测精度与模型复杂度的归一化加权乘积构成统一指标，并以用户参数控制精度—复杂度权衡，用于系统化模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程决策日益依赖高保真机器学习模型，传统仅以未训练点预测精度选模型不足；复杂度、可解释性、可扩展性、物理一致性等属性视应用背景可能同等重要，缺乏同时权衡精度与复杂度的系统化、客观选择准则。

Method: 提出复合性能指标SQS：分别对预测精度与模型复杂度度量做归一化，再以加权乘积形式启发式整合为单一得分；SQS含用户指定参数，可显式调节精度—复杂度权衡，将人的判断与偏好纳入选择过程；文中对SQS的构造形式进行讨论与合理性论证。

Result: 通过一个示例演示了SQS的实用性，说明其能在单一分数中同时反映精度与复杂度并支持可调的权衡。

Conclusion: SQS将预测精度与模型复杂度结合，为基于双重属性的模型选择提供了系统且客观的方法，适用于需在精度与复杂度间平衡的代理模型选择场景。

Abstract: The growing reliance on high-fidelity, state-of-the-art machine learning based models for engineering decision-making necessitates robust and systematic approaches for model selection. While high predictive accuracy at untrained points is a necessary trait in the final model, other attributes such as model complexity, forecastability, explainability, scalability, and physics-awareness may be equally important or relevant criteria depending on application context and user requirements. In this work, we propose a novel composite performance metric, Surrogate Quality Score (SQS) for model selection based on predictive accuracy and model complexity simultaneously. SQS heuristically integrates these two attributes through a weighted product of normalized measures, yielding a unified score to reflect overall model quality. A user-specified parameter in SQS enables explicit control over the accuracy-complexity trade-off, thereby incorporating human judgement and preference in the model selection process. We extensively discuss and justify the formulation of SQS and demonstrate its utility through an illustrative example. By combining predictive accuracy and model complexity, SQS offers a systematic and objective approach for model selection based on both attributes.

</details>


### [134] [Accuracy Is Not Value: Choosing Weak Supervisors by What the Student Inherits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08959)
*Romano Li,Xunfan Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"学生相对价值"框架：弱监督者的价值取决于其误差经过学生学习算子后的结果，而非原始误差；该价值虽不可从无标签数据辨识，但缺失部分很小（目标的两个有限维投影），并据此给出加权、教师训练规则与干净标签修正的充分必要刻画及匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上评估弱监督者（weak supervisors）以其自身误差大小为准，但在知识蒸馏/教师-学生设定中，真正重要的是学生从监督者身上学到了什么。原始误差与学生学习算子的几何不匹配，导致按原始误差加权可能严重次优，需要建立以学生风险为基准的监督者价值度量。

Method: 针对平方损失与全体监督者共享的线性学习器：将监督者误差经学生学习算子映射，定义学生相对价值；分析原始误差内积与可行学生风险之间的关系；用无标签数据加目标的两个有限维投影刻画可辨识性缺口；引入已知教师训练规则或以学生相对形式表述的标签模型条件进一步缩小缺口；用干净标签在未解析方向几何决定的代价下闭合剩余差距，并给出最坏情形匹配下界；在高斯设计下分析精确教师结构优于普通stacking、以及弱监督加干净标签优于仅用干净标签的条件；推广到拟合岭回归学生与有限无标签数据。

Result: 证明原始误差内积可与学生风险的任意可行值兼容，按原始误差加权在相对超额风险上可任意劣于最优加权；学生相对价值无法由无标签数据辨识，但缺口仅由一个有限维投影决定；干净标签闭合缺口的代价由未解析方向几何而非学生表示维数决定，且有匹配最坏情形下界；高斯设计下给出精确教师结构优于stacking、弱监督+干净标签优于纯干净标签的量化条件；结论延伸至拟合岭学生与有限无标签样本。

Conclusion: 监督者评估应以学生学习算子后的误差为准；该学生相对价值虽不可完全辨识，但缺失信息量小且可被干净标签以几何代价弥补，为弱监督加权、教师设计与标签获取提供了理论边界与可操作的权衡准则。

Abstract: Weak supervisors are usually judged by their own errors, but what matters is what a student learns from them. For squared loss and any linear learner shared by all supervisors, we show that a supervisor's value is determined by its errors after they pass through the student's learning operator. Consequently, the inner products of the supervisors' raw errors can be compatible with every feasible value of the student's risk, and weighting supervisors by them can be arbitrarily worse than the best weighting in relative excess risk. This student-relative value is not identifiable from unlabeled data, but what is missing is small: one finite-dimensional projection of the target decides whether training on a weak aggregate helps, another decides how to weight the supervisors. Known teacher training rules, or a label-model condition stated in student-relative form, shrink it further. Clean labels close the remaining gap at a cost set by the geometry of the unresolved directions rather than by the dimension of the student's representation, with matching worst-case lower bounds. In Gaussian designs we also quantify when exact teacher structure beats ordinary stacking and when weak supervisors, combined with clean labels, beat every procedure that uses the clean labels alone. The guarantees extend to fitted ridge students and finite unlabeled data.

</details>


### [135] [Energy-Based Reasoners: Your Recurrent Reasoner Should Be an Energy-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08977)
*William Xingxu Chen,Alexi Gladstone,Zeyu Michael Li,Heng Ji,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Energy-Based Reasoners (EBRs)：在潜空间推理状态上学习能量函数，将循环更新表述为该能量上的梯度下降，能量值给出停止信号，并配合探索式训练塑造能量景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环（looped）模型通过反复迭代同一循环块扩展有效深度、实现潜空间多步推理，但缺乏显式的“进展度量”，难以判断何时停止循环、如何改进当前状态。

Method: 学习隐推理状态上的能量函数，把循环更新视为对该能量的梯度下降：能量负责打分，梯度引导精修并提供停止依据；另引入基于探索的训练，发现额外模式并在更广状态集上更好地塑造能量景观。

Result: 在四个算法推理基准上与已有循环推理模型持平或更优；Sudoku-Extreme 上达 99.9% 准确率，推理算力比次优模型少 345 倍且训练算力相当；所有多解基准上取得不同有效解覆盖率的 SOTA，同时单样本准确率接近 100%。

Conclusion: 能量模型是通向强推理性能的有前景路径。

Abstract: Looping is a powerful inductive bias for reasoning. By repeatedly iterating a recurrent block, looped models can scale their effective depth and enable multi-step reasoning in latent space. However, existing looped models are not inherently guided by an explicit measure of progress, making it difficult to determine when to stop looping and how to improve upon the current state. To enable this, we introduce Energy-Based Reasoners (EBRs), which learn an energy function over latent reasoning states and formulate looped updates as gradient descent on this energy. The energy scores latent states, while its gradient guides refinement and provides a signal for stopping. To train EBRs effectively, we further introduce exploration-based training to discover additional modes and to better shape the energy landscape over a broader set of states. Across four algorithmic reasoning benchmarks, EBRs match or outperform prior recurrent reasoning models, achieving 99.9% accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme while using 345$\times$ less inference compute than the next-best model and maintaining comparable training compute. On all multi-solution benchmarks, EBRs achieve state-of-the-art coverage of distinct valid solutions while maintaining near-100% single-sample accuracy. These results suggest that energy-based models are a promising approach for strong reasoning performance.

</details>


### [136] [Action-Centered Causal Bandits for Budget-Constrained Promotion Targeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08990)
*Yifan Lin,Kaixu Yang,Di Mo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DR-CATE-CBwK：一种以动作中心化因果Bandit直接学习处理对比，在预算约束下结合离线双稳健学习、在线动作中心估计与原始-对偶控制，实现路径wise成本可行并给出Õ(Kd√T)遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台需在全局预算下为每个用户从多种昂贵干预中选择以最大化增量效果。该问题可建模为带异质处理效应的预算约束上下文Bandit。虽然与动作无关的基线在对比中会抵消，但显式建模非线性基线会增大统计复杂度，因此希望绕开基线建模、直接学习处理对比。

Method: 1) 构建动作中心化（action-centered）因果Bandit，直接学处理对比而非奖励；2) 在线更新不依赖逆倾向加权（IPW），且在基线模型误设下仍有效；3) 离线双稳健（DR）学习器热启动CATE估计；4) 在线动作中心估计器进行序贯对比学习；5) 原始-对偶控制器+可支付动作规则，路径wise强制有效成本预算；6) 理论分析对比流体LP（覆盖保证动作规则）；7) 实践变体采用Thompson限制策略。

Result: 在平稳i.i.d.、线性CATE假设下证明高概率遗憾界 Õ(Kd√T)（K为定价动作数，d为上下文维度，T为时域）。理论上证明动作中心估计矩在任何可预测基线估计下无偏，而分臂奖励回归在动作依赖记录与共享基线误设下会产生渐近对比偏差。在半合成职位市场基准（真实日志特征+模拟结果）上，实用Thompson限制变体在运行区间内取得最低遗憾，比最强奖励型基线降低约20%，优于原始奖励上下文Bandit、BwK基线及静态离线策略，且从未超出有效成本预算。

Conclusion: 直接以动作中心化方式学习处理对比可在无需IPW、容忍基线误设的条件下实现预算可行的增量决策，兼具理论遗憾保证与实证优势，说明奖励型建模在此类预算约束干预选择问题中存在系统性偏差风险。

Abstract: Web platforms select among costly interventions for each user to maximize incremental outcomes under a global budget. We formulate this problem as a budget-constrained contextual bandit with heterogeneous treatment effects. Although an action-independent outcome baseline cancels from treatment comparisons, modeling a nonlinear baseline can increase statistical complexity.
  We propose DR-CATE-CBwK, an action-centered causal bandit that directly learns treatment contrasts. Its online update remains valid under baseline-model misspecification without inverse-propensity weighting. It combines an offline doubly robust learner to warm-start treatment-effect estimates, an online action-centered estimator for sequential contrast learning, and a primal-dual controller with an affordable-action rule to enforce the effective-cost budget pathwise.
  For linear conditional average treatment effects (CATEs) in a stationary i.i.d. setting, we prove a high-probability (\widetilde{O}(Kd\sqrt{T})) regret bound, where K is the number of priced actions, d the context dimension, and T the horizon. We benchmark against the fluid linear program (LP) under the coverage-guaranteed action rule. We further show that the action-centered estimating moment remains unbiased under any predictable baseline estimate, whereas a specified separate-arm reward regression can exhibit asymptotic contrast bias under action-dependent logging and shared-baseline misspecification.
  On a semi-synthetic job-marketplace benchmark with real logged features and simulated outcomes, our practical Thompson-restricted variant achieves the lowest regret among evaluated budget-feasible methods in the operating regime. It reduces regret by approximately 20% versus the strongest reward-based baseline, outperforming raw-reward contextual bandits, BwK baselines, and a static offline policy, while never exceeding the effective-cost budget.

</details>


### [137] [Enforcing Symmetries on Pre-trained ML Models for Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08992)
*Wenjie Zhou,Valentino F. Foit,David W. Hogg,Soledad Villar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究事后对称化（组平均/TTA）在非紧群上的推广：紧群情形用Haar平均（Reynolds算子）有风险不增保证，非紧群因Haar测度不可归一化，改用Weyl酉技巧、数据依赖正交投影与frame averaging三种替代，并在单位重标度群与Lorentz群上做理论与实验分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后组平均无需改架构或重训练即可对预测器施加对称性，紧群下有标准Reynolds算子与风险不增保证；但真实物理系统中常见非紧对称群（如缩放、Lorentz群），Haar测度不可归一化，原有框架失效，需寻找可定义的替代方案并评估其实际收益。

Method: 先在物理仿真模型上做数值实验验证紧群组平均的有效性；随后针对非紧群提出三种替代：(1) Weyl酉技巧，需复化空间并扩展预测器；(2) 数据依赖的正交投影，在不变/等变函数空间上求解非平凡优化；(3) frame averaging，将每点映射到一组轨道代表元。在单位重标度群与Lorentz群上做理论刻画与实证。

Result: 非紧情形下，Weyl技巧与frame averaging相对基线并无一致性提升，仅在测试数据分布外（OOD）时带来数量级改进，Weyl技巧另在低数据区间有效；正交投影在分布内与分布外均可提升。此外，直接在不变特征上训练的模型表现持平或优于对无约束模型做投影。

Conclusion: 事后对称化框架可推广至非紧群，但其效果不一致地优于直接训练对称模型；实践中直接训练不变/等变模型（或基于不变特征训练）更为可靠，事后投影主要作为OOD鲁棒性手段。

Abstract: Post-hoc group averaging (or test-time augmentation) is a simple way to impose symmetry on a trained predictor without changing its architecture or retraining it. For finite and compact groups, averaging over a normalized Haar measure on the group gives the Reynolds operator: a canonical projection onto invariant or equivariant predictors with known non-increasing risk guarantees under certain assumptions. This paper provides numerical experiments on physics emulation models that demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in this setting. We then extend this framework to non-compact group symmetries. For non-compact groups, the Haar measure cannot be normalized, so group averaging cannot be defined in the same way. We replace it with three alternatives: the Weyl unitary trick (which requires complexifying the space and extending the predictor), a data-dependent orthogonal projection (which involves a non-trivial optimization on the space of invariant or equivariant functions), and a frame averaging approach (which involves mapping each point to a set of orbit representatives). We illustrate the constructions theoretically and empirically on the unit-rescaling group and the Lorentz group. Our results show that in the non-compact case, the Weyl trick and frame averaging do not provide a consistent improvement over the baseline, except where the test data is out-of-distribution (an orders-of-magnitude improvement in this case) and, for the Weyl trick, in low-data regimes. The orthogonal projection can provide improvements in-distribution and out-of-distribution. We also observe that models trained directly on invariant features matched or beat the projection of an unconstrained one. We conclude that post-hoc symmetrization extends to non-compact groups, but it does not consistently outperform training a symmetric model.

</details>


### [138] [When2What: A Timing-Conditioned Framework for Long-Horizon Forecasting of Continuous Cardiovascular Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08993)
*Chaoqi Zhang,Shujie Yan,Zhao Zhang,Yanhua Sun,Yu Wang,Li Li,Lingzhong Meng,Xiaoyi Chen,Haixu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 When2What 时序条件化框架（Predict–Commit–Generate 三阶段），先预测心搏时序并"提交"为高斯脉冲，再条件生成波形，解决准周期生理波形长程预测中 MSE 训练导致峰值扁平化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG/ABP/PPG 等准周期波形长程预测需同时回答"何时发生心搏"（时序）与"心搏形态如何"（形态）。但以点式 MSE 训练的序列预测器会对不确定的心搏相位取平均，将尖锐 R 峰与脉搏上升支抹平为无特征基线。作者通过术中 ECG 分析发现明显的时间不对称性：由历史预测心搏时刻的能力因心率变异性在数秒内迅速衰减，而按真实心搏时刻对齐的局部形态却能在数分钟内保持稳定。

Method: When2What 为时序条件化三阶段框架：(1) Predict：基于 120 s 历史预测密集 60 s 时序图；(2) Commit：选取离散心搏事件并锐化为单位高度高斯脉冲；(3) Generate：以已提交事件与观测历史共同条件化神经波形生成器。

Result: 在术中 ECG 上显著优于现有时序与生理信号预测器：近期波形事件技能分 0.644，生成波形中复现了 95.62% 的预测心搏，而直接长程预测器坍缩为平坦基线；并可零样本迁移到外部医院与房颤队列，并有效扩展到 ABP、PPG 脉搏波形。

Conclusion: 消融与合成实验表明，显式时序条件化是准周期长程波形预测的有效范式：将高不确定的时序与高稳定的形态解耦，分别建模再合成。

Abstract: Long-horizon forecasting of quasi-periodic cardiovascular waveforms, such as the electrocardiogram (ECG), arterial blood pressure (ABP), and photoplethysmogram (PPG), requires predicting both when future cardiac events will occur (beat timing) and what the local structural pattern looks like around each event (beat morphology). However, standard sequence forecasters trained with pointwise mean squared error (MSE) average across uncertain future heartbeat phases, flattening sharp R peaks and pulse upstrokes into featureless baselines. Our analysis of intraoperative ECG reveals a clear temporal asymmetry: beat-time predictability from history decays within seconds due to stochastic heart-rate variability, whereas local beat morphology aligned at true beat times remains stable over several minutes. Guided by this insight, we introduce When2What, a timing-conditioned framework comprising three stages: Predict, Commit, and Generate. Given a 120-s history, When2What predicts a dense 60-s timing map, selects and sharpens discrete beat events into unit-height Gaussian impulses, and conditions a neural waveform generator on both these committed events and the observed history. Across intraoperative ECG, When2What substantially outperforms existing time-series and physiological forecasters, achieving a near-term waveform event skill of 0.644 and reproducing 95.62% of its predicted heartbeats in the generated waveform where direct long-horizon forecasters collapse into flat baselines. Furthermore, When2What transfers zero-shot to external hospital and atrial fibrillation cohorts, extends effectively to ABP and PPG pulse waveforms, and is supported by ablations and synthetic analyses that establish explicit timing conditioning as an effective paradigm for quasi-periodic long-horizon waveform forecasting.

</details>


### [139] [Calibrating your confidence requires thinking of all the ways you could be wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08997)
*Marco Miani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种退化高斯后验：其协方差为与Fisher信息矩阵零空间对齐的投影矩阵，并给出高效的采样与预测置信度计算算法，在大模型上实现SOTA的置信度校准与OOD检测，同时从信息几何与PAC-Bayes角度提供理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度网络缺乏量化自身不确定性的机制，置信度校准差；增大模型与数据只能缓解而非解决这一结构性缺陷。贝叶斯方法虽提供原则性的不确定性框架，但计算开销大，难以兼顾可扩展性与质量，阻碍其广泛应用。
