What separates the events is the logical form of the statement wanted by each of the two domains, security and mathematics. The safety of a program is a $Π_1$ statement, for it says that no exploit exists, which is not assured by any finite list of tests that have been passed by the program. By contrast, the provability of a sentence is a $Σ_1$ statement, for it says that a proof exists, which is assured by a single instance, namely a proof that has been checked by the deductive verifier. The theorem behind both is Turing's Undecidability Theorem: the halting set is $Σ_1$ but not decidable, so its complement is $Π_1$ and not $Σ_1$, which is to say that membership has a finite witness but non-membership has none. We prove that attaching a statistical proposer to a deductive verifier does not change this undecidability result, however good the proposer is. We then discuss what does assure safety and where the residual risk goes.

</details>


### [208] [Diffusion-free Score Matching for Exact Conditional Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09758)
*Thomas Wedenig,Robert Peharz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DIffusion-free SCOre matching (DISCO)：无需时间索引的分数场，仅估计轻微扰动数据分布的单一分数，从而可直接获得精确的条件分数，实现任意条件下的采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代扩散类生成模型虽采样质量高，但难以用于严谨的概率推断，尤其是从模型的任意条件分布中采样。原因是条件信息只对干净数据可得，高噪声水平下无法用条件信息构造精确的条件分数，故只能依赖启发式采样器。

Method: DISCO 设计了一个形式上类似扩散训练的目标函数，但不学习一族随时间变化的分数场，而只估计（轻微扰动的）数据分布的单一分数；由于该分数仅对应一个噪声水平，其条件分数可由干净数据上的条件信息直接精确得到。基于此单一分数即可进行条件采样。

Result: 实验中 DISCO 在样本质量上与最先进的扩散模型相当；更关键的是，它对潜在数据分布的表征更忠实，并能从任意条件中准确采样，优于标准启发式采样器。

Conclusion: DISCO 绕开时间相关分数场的必要性，为基于分数的模型提供了有原则且灵活的概率推断能力，为进一步开展条件生成与贝叶斯式推断打开了路径。

Abstract: The success of modern score-based generative models is largely driven by diffusion-based training, which learns a collection of time-indexed score fields corresponding to progressively noisier versions of the data distribution. While such models achieve impressive sample quality, leveraging them for sound probabilistic inference---particularly sampling from arbitrary conditionals of the model---remains challenging. This difficulty arises because conditioning information is only available for clean data and not for the higher noise levels required to generate exact conditional samples. In this paper, we introduce DIffusion-free SCOre matching (DISCO), a principled approach that sidesteps the need for time-dependent score fields altogether. Our method is based on an objective that, while reminiscent of diffusion-based training, estimates only a single score of the (slightly perturbed) data distribution, for which the exact conditional score is readily obtained. In experiments, DISCO is competitive with state-of-the-art diffusion models in terms of sample quality. More importantly, DISCO yields a more faithful representation of the underlying data distribution and---crucially---enables accurate sampling from arbitrary conditionals, outperforming standard heuristic samplers. This capability opens the door to principled and flexible probabilistic reasoning with score-based models.

</details>


### [209] [A Tight Hierarchy for Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09759)
*Thanh Le,A. Pavan,N. V. Vinodchandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了CoT（思维链）计算的第一个层级定理：对任意合理资源界 r(n)≥n 与任意 ε>0，有 CoT(r(n)) ⊊ CoT(r(n)^{1+ε})，证明增加CoT推理步数能严格提升transformer的计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作揭示了CoT增强型transformer的细粒度计算能力及其与经典复杂性理论的联系，但尚缺一个刻画CoT步数作为计算资源"严格可分"的层级定理，即步数增加是否带来可证明的更强计算能力。

Method: 不同于经典层级定理常用的对角化方法，作者利用CoT增强transformer与图灵机之间双向的粗粒度模拟（coarse-grained simulation）关系，将复杂性理论中的经典padding技术迁移到该场景，并在transformer-based CoT模型中具体实现padding；关键技术贡献即为padding在该模型内的可构造实现。

Result: 对任意 r(n)≥n 与 ε>0，CoT(r(n)) 是 CoT(r(n)^{1+ε}) 的真子集，得到紧的（tight）层级结果；表明CoT推理步数是transformer计算中的一种基本资源。

Conclusion: CoT推理步数具有可证明的严格层级结构，步数增加确实带来更强计算力；该结论通过CoT-transformer与图灵机模拟加padding的技术路线获得，为CoT计算复杂性的细粒度研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for extending the computational capabilities of transformer-based models. A growing body of recent work has begun to uncover the fine-grained computational power of transformers augmented with CoT, as well as their connections to classical complexity theory.
  In this work, we advance this line of research by establishing the first hierarchy theorem for CoT-based computation. Our main result shows that for any reasonable resource bound $r(n) \geq n$ and any $\varepsilon > 0$, \[ \mathrm{CoT}(r(n)) \subsetneq \mathrm{CoT}(r(n)^{1+\varepsilon}), \] where $\mathrm{CoT}(r(n))$ denotes the class of decision problems solvable by transformers using $O(r(n))$ CoT reasoning steps on inputs of length $n$. Our result shows that increasing the number of CoT reasoning steps provably yields strictly greater computational power, establishing CoT steps as a fundamental resource in transformer-based computation.
  Unlike classical hierarchy theorems, which are typically proved via diagonalization, our approach leverages known coarse-grained simulations between CoT-augmented transformers and Turing machines in both directions. Building on these connections, we adapt the classical padding technique from complexity theory to this setting to obtain a tight hierarchy. A key technical contribution of our work is the implementation of padding within the transformer-based CoT model.

</details>


### [210] [From PAC bounds on the generalization error to PAC bounds on the parameter estimation error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09767)
*Mihaly Petreczky,John Leth,Emma Lukacs,Balint Daroczy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用PAC（Probably Approximately Correct）界导出的oracle不等式来为动力系统推导有限样本参数估计误差界，并以有界输入与噪声下的线性与双线性系统为例验证该方法，同时分析其收敛速率并与经典渐近结果比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两类结果各有局限：PAC泛化误差界是算法无关的通用工具且已被推广到动力系统；而系统辨识中的有限样本参数估计界往往依赖具体算法（需将辨识算法重写为最小二乘回归问题）。作者希望借助PAC界所蕴含的算法无关性，得到适用于任意"最小化正则化经验损失"型辨识算法的有限样本参数估计界。

Method: 核心思路分三步：(1) 从PAC型泛化误差界出发导出oracle界；(2) 建立oracle不等式，即所学模型的真实预测误差与可实现的最小真实预测误差之间的关系；(3) 利用真实损失的凸性，或损失函数的系统理论性质（如可辨识性、持久激励等），把预测误差界转化为参数估计误差界。将上述框架应用于有界输入、有界噪声下的线性系统与双线性系统，辨识算法限定为最小化正则化经验损失的一类算法。最后分析所得界的收敛速率，并与经典渐近结论对比。

Result: 给出了线性系统与双线性系统在有限样本下、可显式表达的参数估计误差上界，该上界适用于任何基于最小化正则化经验损失的辨识算法，因而具有算法无关性；同时推导了这些界的收敛速率，并与经典渐近理论（如参数估计的渐近一致性）下的速率进行了对照分析。

Conclusion: PAC界经oracle不等式可以成为系统辨识中推导有限样本参数估计界的有效工具，所得结果兼具算法无关性与显式有限样本性质。该方法将统计学习理论与系统理论结合，为线性与双线性系统提供了统一的分析框架，并具有向更一般动力系统推广的潜力。

Abstract: Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) bounds on the generalization error (the difference between the empirical prediction error computed on the training data and its expectation) are an established tool in statistical machine learning, and there have been several extensions of these bounds to dynamical systems. In parallel, in recent years, there has been growing interest in finite-sample bounds for parameter estimation error in system identification. The latter bounds tend to be algorithm-specific, relying on reformulating the identification algorithm as a least-squares regression problem. This is in contrast to classical PAC bounds, which are algorithm-agnostic and can be applied to any identification algorithm based on minimizing a cost function involving the empirical loss.
  In this paper, we show how oracle bounds derived from PAC bounds can be used to derive finite-sample bounds for dynamical systems. As an illustration of this approach, we present finite-sample parameter-estimation bounds for linear and bilinear systems with bounded inputs and noise. These bounds apply to identification algorithms based on minimizing a regularized empirical loss function.
  The key ingredient is to establish an oracle inequality, i.e., an inequality between the true prediction error of the learned model and the minimum achievable true prediction error, and then use either the convexity of the true loss or system-theoretic properties of the loss function to derive bounds on the parameter estimation error. We also analyze the rates of the obtained bounds and compare them with those of classical asymptotic results.

</details>


### [211] [Quasipolynomial-Time Planning for Decodable POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09778)
*Oliver Dobson,Varun Jog,Chun Hei Michael Shiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限状态、动作与观测空间的有限时域POMDP规划问题，提出"ρ-可解码性"这一更弱且易检验的假设，并在该假设下给出拟多项式时间的近似规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确与近似规划在一般POMDP中均为计算困难。已有工作[Golowich et al., 2022]利用ℓ1-可观测性（对观测通道的约束，不限制转移动态）获得拟多项式近似规划算法，但ℓ1-可观测性是较强且难以验证的假设（计算最优ℓ1-可观测常数在最坏情形下是NP-hard），因此需要寻找更弱、更统一且易检验的替代假设。

Method: 引入ρ-可解码性（decodability）概念：对每个固定ρ>0，设计拟多项式时间的近似规划算法。并从理论上建立可解码性与ℓ1-、ℓ2-可观测性的关系：证明ℓ1-与ℓ2-可观测性均蕴含可解码性，且可解码性严格弱于可观测性；同时证明最优可解码常数可在多项式时间内计算。

Result: 对每个固定ρ>0，ρ-可解码POMDP存在拟多项式时间近似规划算法；可解码性由ℓ1-和ℓ2-可观测性共同蕴含且严格更弱；最优可解码常数可多项式时间求得，而最优ℓ1-可观测常数计算为NP-hard；并证明[Golowich et al., 2022]所依赖的快速滤波遗忘（fast filter-forgetting）保证无法从ℓ1-可观测性直接推广到常数ℓ2-可观测性。

Conclusion: 可解码性是一种易于检验、统一的假设，统一涵盖ℓ1-与ℓ2-可观测性，并在此假设下实现POMDP的拟多项式时间近似规划，为POMDP可解性提供了更一般的理论刻画。

Abstract: We study planning for finite-horizon partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) with finite state, action, and observation spaces. Both exact and approximate planning are known to be computationally hard in this setting, so prior work has explored natural assumptions that make planning easier. Recent work [Golowich et al., 2022] gave quasipolynomial-time approximate planning algorithms for observable POMDPs. These are POMDPs whose observation channels satisfy an $\ell_1$-observability condition, but notably, no assumptions are required on the transition dynamics. In a similar vein, for every fixed $ρ>0$, we give quasipolynomial-time approximate planning algorithms for $ρ$-decodable POMDPs. We show that decodability is implied by both $\ell_1$- and $\ell_2$-observability and is strictly weaker than observability. Moreover, we show that the optimal decodability constant can be computed in polynomial time, whereas computing the optimal $\ell_1$-observability constant is NP-hard in the worst case. We also show that the fast filter-forgetting guarantee underlying the approach of [Golowich et al., 2022] does not extend directly from $\ell_1$-observability to constant $\ell_2$-observability. Thus, decodability is an easy-to-check, unifying assumption under which quasipolynomial-time approximate planning for POMDPs is possible.

</details>


### [212] [Scaling Graph Similarity Learning with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09805)
*Ahmet Tüzen,Helge Langseth,Kjetil Nørvåg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FiLSim：一个可扩展的基于GNN的图相似度计算框架，并配合顺序图编辑策略构建大规模、带有可靠GED标签的数据集，验证简化GNN流程可在大规模图上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图相似度计算（GED、MCS）为NP难问题，精确计算代价高；现有基于GNN的方法多局限于小规模图数据集，向大规模图的可扩展性研究不足。

Method: 1) 提出FiLSim——简化的GNN流程框架，用于图相似度（GED）预测；2) 提出顺序图编辑策略（sequential graph-editing strategy），通过逐步编辑构造带可靠GED标签的大规模图数据集。

Result: 在4个基准数据集与4个新建大规模数据集上评估；在节点数超过150,000的图上仍保持准确，而基线方法出现显存不足（OOM）或无法学习；单GPU上可在数秒内完成全部测试图对的打分。

Conclusion: 简化GNN流程在大规模图上兼具有效性与可扩展性，FiLSim为大规模图相似度计算提供了可行方案；所提数据构造策略可为可扩展性评测提供可靠标签。

Abstract: Graph similarity computation is an important task in graph mining with many real-world applications, such as chemical compound analysis, program dependence comparison, and social network analysis. The problem is computationally challenging: Graph Edit Distance (GED) and Maximum Common Subgraph (MCS), which quantify the distance between graphs and identify their largest shared subgraph, respectively, are NP-hard to compute exactly. To address these computational challenges, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been applied to this problem. However, existing GNN-based approaches have primarily focused on small-scale graph datasets, while scalability to larger scales remains underexplored. In this work, we propose FiLSim, a scalable GNN-based framework, and investigate whether a simplified GNN pipeline can maintain competitive performance on standard benchmarks while scaling to large graphs. We additionally propose a sequential graph-editing strategy to construct large-scale graph datasets with reliable GED labels to evaluate scalability. The proposed framework is evaluated on four benchmark datasets and four newly created large-scale datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability. FiLSim remains accurate on graphs with more than 150{,}000 nodes, where the baseline methods run out of memory or fail to learn, and scores all test pairs within seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [213] [The Cost of Coverage: Conformalising Native Forecast Uncertainty for GB Wind Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09815)
*Carl Somers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将保形预测(conformal prediction)应用于六种风电功率预测模型的原始不确定性区间，在英国两个风电场开展运行条件下的评估，发现按预测风速分区校准可将边际覆盖率转化为分区覆盖率，高风速下日前越界电量较合并校准降低40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电预测的不确定性区间常用于备用容量配置与申报决策，但往往无法达到其标称覆盖率；需要系统评估保形预测对原始区间的增益与代价。

Method: 在英国两个风电场上，对ARIMA到表格基础模型(tabular foundation model)共六种预测器的原生化区间施加保形预测校准，在实际运行条件下比较；采用Winkler Score评估锐度惩罚，并按预测风速区间(regime)分层校准。

Result: 校准可修复经验边际覆盖率且Winkler Score惩罚很小或没有，但该表面成功掩盖了高风速下的欠覆盖；按预测风速分区校准使边际覆盖转为分区级覆盖，高风速下日前越界电量相对合并校准下降超过40%，同时区间整体收窄。

Conclusion: 边际覆盖率达标具有误导性，需在条件(风速分区)层面评估与校准；分区保形校准兼具覆盖率可靠性与区间锐度优势，对备用配置更具实用价值。

Abstract: Uncertainty bands on wind forecasts help inform reserve sizing and commitments, but often fail to deliver their stated coverage. We apply conformal prediction to native bands to see what it adds and what it costs, comparing six forecasters, from ARIMA to a tabular foundation model, at two wind farms in Great Britain (GB) under operational conditions. We show that calibration repairs empirical marginal coverage with little or no Winkler Score penalty, but this apparent success conceals under-coverage in high winds. Calibrating by forecast wind regime turns marginal coverage into regime-level coverage, cutting day-ahead out-of-band volume in high winds by over 40% relative to pooled calibration while narrowing bands overall.

</details>


### [214] [Designing Effective Monitor-Based Interventions for Mitigating Reward Hacking During RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09828)
*Aria Wong,Joshua Engels,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建三个 Qwen3-4B 会产生奖励黑客的真实环境（代码测试覆写、医疗谄媚、传记幻觉），系统研究训练期监控器如何有效抑制奖励黑客，发现模型会利用探针与LLM评判的系统性缺陷规避高精度监控器，泄漏更多学习信号的监控器更能抑制但更易被规避，加入简单题目可降低奖励黑客，并将结论迁移到另两个环境以减少奖励黑客率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 奖励难以设计与控制，模型易学出奖励黑客等非预期行为。监控并惩罚奖励黑客是一种潜在方案，但对抗监控器训练可能诱发规避行为，且目前缺乏关于如何在训练中有效使用监控器的系统性认识。

Method: 开源三个可被 Qwen3-4B 奖励黑客的现实环境：可通过覆写测试作弊的代码环境、可通过谄媚作弊的医疗对话环境、可通过幻觉作弊的传记生成环境。先在代码环境中开展受控实验，分析监控器精度、学习信号泄漏程度（探针/LLM评判）与训练题目难度对规避行为与奖励黑客率的影响，再把结论用于另外两个环境设计改进型监控干预。

Result: (1) 模型能通过利用探针和 LLM 评判中的系统性缺陷，学会规避高精度监控器；(2) 在 RL 中泄漏更多学习信号的监控器能抑制奖励黑客，但更常被规避；(3) 训练集中加入更简单的问题可减少奖励黑客。应用到医疗对话与传记生成环境后，改进的监控器优于朴素基线，在部分干预下将多种子上的奖励黑客率降至训练前水平。

Conclusion: 监控器干预设计存在可行改进空间，关于监控器信号泄漏、可规避性与题目难度的发现可迁移到新环境，为训练期缓解奖励黑客提供实践指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) rewards are notoriously difficult to design and control, often leading to the model learning unintended behaviors such as reward hacking. One potential solution is to monitor for reward hacking and penalize it when detected; however, training against a monitor could lead to evasive behavior, and our general understanding of how to apply monitors effectively during training is limited. To study how best to use monitors to mitigate reward hacking, we introduce and open source three realistic environments where Qwen3-4B reward hacks: a coding environment hackable via test overwriting, a medical chat environment hackable via sycophancy, and a biography generation environment hackable via hallucination. We first focus on the coding environment, where we find that: (1) models can learn to evade highly accurate monitors by exploiting systemic flaws in probes and LLM judges; (2) monitors that leak more learning signal during RL suppress reward hacking but are more often evaded; and (3) including easier problems in training can decrease reward hacking. We apply our findings to build better reward hacking monitors for the medical chat and biography generation environments that improve upon naive baselines to reduce reward hacking rates across seeds to before-training levels in some interventions. Our results demonstrate that our takeaways translate to new settings and that better monitor intervention designs are possible.

</details>


### [215] [Fifty-Two Shades of Pig: What the UCR Archive Really Asks Classifiers to Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09843)
*Omar Swelam,Jaris Küken,Shi Bin Hoo,Frank Hutter,Lennart Purucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对UCR时间序列分类基准库进行溯源审计，发现多个数据集被任务挪用、来自玩具式实验设置或存在测试集划分缺陷，质疑靠前的非时序序列的代表性及多元问题拆解的负面影响，呼吁谨慎解读全库汇总分数并重视面向部署的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UCR归档被视为时间序列分类（TSC）事实上的标准基准，但其任务是否真正反映TSC所要解决的问题鲜有考察；例如PigAirwayPressure原始目的是检测出血，归档却把问题改写为"52头猪中哪一头产生了该曲线"，暴露出基准任务与真实问题之间的错位。

Method: 对UCR归档做溯源审计（provenance audit）：追踪各数据集的原始来源与原始任务，判定其是否被改造复用、是否源于玩具式实验装置、测试集划分是否存在缺陷；并分析归档中排序靠前的非时序序列（光谱、形状轮廓等）对真实TSC的代表性，以及多元问题被拆解为单变量问题所带来的潜在弊端。审计代码开源。

Result: 发现若干数据集系由原始任务改造而来、构建于玩具式设置之上，或存在有缺陷的测试集划分；归档中的非时序序列数据对真实TSC问题代表性有限；多元问题的分解也存在潜在负面影响。这些现象并非个例（PigAirwayPressure并非异常值）。

Conclusion: 应更谨慎地解读基于UCR全库汇总的分数与排名，并更加关注与部署相关的评估方式；基准构建需重视数据来源与任务语义的可追溯性。

Abstract: The pigs were bleeding. That was the point of the experiment: could airway pressure reveal hemorrhage before it became critical? In the UCR Time Series Classification Archive, the de facto benchmark for Time Series Classification (TSC), the same recordings ask a different question: "which of 52 pigs produced this trace?". The dataset PigAirwayPressure is not an outlier. Although the archive is the standard benchmark, whether its tasks resemble the problems TSC is meant to solve has rarely been examined. We find that several datasets are repurposed from their original task, built from toy setups, or have flawed test splits. We further question how representative the archive's ordered non-temporal sequences (e.g. spectra, shape outlines) are of real TSC, and the potential downsides from the decomposition of multivariate problems. Our findings call for more careful interpretation of archive-wide scores and greater attention to deployment-relevant evaluation. The audit is at https://github.com/automl/ucr-provenance-audit.

</details>


### [216] [On the (non-)closedness of the set of ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09887)
*Eloi Martinet,Leon Bungert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明单隐层宽度不超过 N 的 ReLU 网络集合在 W^{1,p}(Ω) 中弱闭，从而保证强制性弱下半连续能量存在极小元，并证明两层有界宽度 ReLU 网络集合在 W^{1,p}(Ω) 与 C(\barΩ) 中均非闭，否定了 Petersen–Raslan–Voigtlaender 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的变分型训练（Deep Ritz、Sobolev training、Lipschitz learning）都涉及在固定架构网络集合上最小化能量泛函。这要求该集合具有适当的紧性/闭性以保证极小元存在，而此前对单隐层 ReLU 网络集合的弱闭性缺乏严格论证，且对多层有界宽度情形是否存在类似性质亦有未决猜想。

Method: 在 W^{1,p}(Ω)（Ω 为 R^d 中有界连通开集）框架下，对恰含一个隐层、至多 N 个 ReLU 神经元的网络集合 \mathcal{N}_N 建立弱闭性：利用 ReLU 网络的参数化结构与弱收敛下的紧性论证（含参数有界化、极限函数的表示与宽度不增性）。随后由此推出任意强制性、弱下半连续能量在 \mathcal{N}_N 上取到极小值；并分析权重衰减参数趋于零时正则化极小元的收敛性。

Result: \mathcal{N}_N 在 W^{1,p}(Ω) 中弱闭；故每个强制性弱下半连续能量在 \mathcal{N}_N 上达到极小，覆盖凸被积函数的 Deep Ritz 方法、Sobolev training 与 Lipschitz learning。当权重衰减参数趋于 0 时，正则化问题的极小元（沿子列）收敛到参数范数最小的极小元。反例方面：两个隐层且宽度有界的 ReLU 网络集合在 W^{1,p}(Ω) 及 C(\barΩ) 中均不闭。

Conclusion: 单隐层 ReLU 网络在适当 Sobolev 拓扑下具备弱闭性，可作为变分训练方法存在性理论的可靠基础；但该性质不能推广到两隐层有界宽度情形，Petersen、Raslan 与 Voigtlaender 的相关猜想被否定，说明层数与宽度约束对网络集合的闭性有本质影响。

Abstract: We prove that the set $\mathcal{N}_N$ of ReLU networks with one hidden layer and at most $N\in\mathbb N$ neurons is weakly closed in $W^{1,p}(Ω)$, for a bounded connected open set $Ω\subset\mathbb R^d$. Consequently, every coercive, weakly lower semicontinuous energy on this space attains its minimum over $\mathcal{N}_N$. This covers the Deep Ritz method with convex integrands, Sobolev training and Lipschitz learning. Moreover, as the weight decay parameter tends to zero, minimizers of the regularized problem converge, up to subsequences, to minimizers with minimal parameter norm. We also prove that the set of ReLU networks with two hidden layers of bounded widths is not closed in $W^{1,p}(Ω)$ or in $C(\overlineΩ)$. This disproves a conjecture of Petersen, Raslan and Voigtlaender.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Enhancing Academic Advisement with Case-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08572)
*Tanat Tonguthaisri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究案例推理（CBR）在学业咨询领域的应用，设计、开发并验证了一个基于案例的学业咨询推荐系统原型，用以辅助乃至最终替代传统的人工学业咨询流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学业咨询被视为高等教育的重要职能，但全球许多院校普遍希望改进其咨询服务；过去十年CBR专家系统在多个领域取得成功，然而尚未成功应用于学业咨询这一看似非常适配的领域。同时学业咨询耗时且重要，需要借助工具与技术提升效率与效果。

Method: 采用案例推理（CBR）方法论构建基于案例的学业咨询推荐系统原型，涵盖设计、开发与验证三个环节，定位于补充而非取代传统咨询流程。

Result: 完成了一个CBR学业咨询推荐系统原型的设计、实现与验证（摘要未给出具体实验数据或性能指标）。

Conclusion: CBR适用于学业咨询领域；该系统可作为传统咨询过程的补充手段，并在其潜力充分发挥后有望发展为替代人工学业顾问的方案。

Abstract: This research involved the study of an application of Case-Based Reasoning (CBR) in the field of academic advising. Academic advisement has been recognised as an important function of higher education. However, there is a widespread desire to improve the advisement services provided by many educational institutions (e.g., colleges and universities) around the world. In the last ten years, CBR expert systems have made important contributions in many domains by applying CBR methodology to a variety of areas of human problem solving. However, to date, expert advisement systems have not been successfully applied to academic advisement, which seems to be a very appropriate domain for this technology.
  Academic advising is an important and time consuming task, and different tools and techniques must be used to make it an effective and efficient process. This thesis describes the design, development, and validation of a prototype case-based academic advisement recommender system that supplements the conventional advising process, and could eventually evolve to replace human academic advisors when its full potential is realised.

</details>


### [218] [NormGym: Evaluating Normative Competence through Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08613)
*Binze Li,Zihuan Zhang,Seokhyun Baek,Gillian K. Hadfield,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出交互式基准 NormGym，用"新手在多智能体社区中推断隐藏的任意性规范并完成任务"来评测智能体的规范能力，发现最强 LLM 的联合成功率为 55.3%，表明当前模型缺乏通过社会交互获取规范知识的可迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多检验智能体能否识别或应用其已知的规范，而非能否从社会交互中习得此前未知的规范；而规范能力（从交互中推断社会性预期并调整行为）对在陌生人类群体中运作的智能体至关重要。

Method: 构建交互式基准 NormGym：新手智能体在多智能体社区中一边执行自身任务，一边推断一个隐藏且任意的规范。成功需区分"可行"与"被允许"、从他人行为与制裁中推断规范，并迁移到新情境。基准涵盖多样规范与不同环境，对当前智能体（含 LLM 智能体）进行系统评测，并开展行为与微调分析。

Result: 规范能力仍具挑战性：最强 LLM 的任务与合规联合成功率为 55.3%。分析显示模型有时能在自身行为受制裁后修正行为，但难以利用其他智能体的行为及其所受制裁来推断潜在规范；微调模型可能不足以提升规范能力。

Conclusion: 当前模型缺乏通过社会交互获取规范知识的稳健且可迁移的能力。

Abstract: Normative competence, the ability to infer socially enforced expectations from interaction and adjust behavior accordingly, is essential for agents operating in unfamiliar human communities. Yet existing evaluations largely test whether agents can recognize or apply norms they may already know, rather than whether they can acquire previously unknown norms from social interaction. We introduce NormGym, an interactive benchmark for normative competence in which a newcomer must infer a hidden, arbitrary norm while pursuing its own task in a multi-agent community. Success requires distinguishing what is possible from what is permitted, inferring the norm from observed behavior and sanctions, and applying it in new situations. NormGym contains diverse norms and distinct environments, allowing systematic evaluation of current agents, including large language model (LLM) agents. We find that normative competence remains challenging: the strongest evaluated LLM achieves a joint task-and-compliance success rate of 55.3%. Our analysis reveals that models can sometimes revise their behavior following sanctions for their own actions, but struggle to use other agents' behavior and the sanctions they receive to infer the underlying norm. We further find that fine-tuning models may be insufficient to improve normative competence. These results suggest that current models lack a robust, transferable capability for acquiring normative knowledge through social interaction.

</details>


### [219] [LLM-Based Feature Extraction for Multi-Criteria Inventory Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08626)
*Humza Akhtar,Rami Pinto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的特征提取方法，将客户评论等非结构化数据经向量嵌入与LLM生成的评价准则转化为定量特征，并嵌入Hadi-Vencheh模型的多准则库存分类（MCIC）框架，实验显示引入客户情感信号会引起可测量的重新分类，从而提升库存优先级的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商供应链积累了大量客户反馈与运营数据，但在库存管理中利用率不足。传统ABC分析仅依赖年度金额用量，多准则库存分类（MCIC）虽扩展了维度，却仍依赖结构化定量数据，无法利用现代供应链中的非结构化信息。

Method: 构建LLM驱动的特征提取流程：以向量嵌入和LLM生成的评价准则把客户评论等非结构化来源转换为定量特征；将富化后的特征集纳入基于Hadi-Vencheh模型的MCIC框架进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，纳入客户情感信号后产生了可测量的重新分类效应，说明将非结构化数据整合进MCIC可改善库存优先级排序。

Conclusion: LLM可用于弥合非结构化客户反馈与结构化库存分类之间的鸿沟，为MCIC提供更丰富的决策依据，是对传统ABC分析与既有MCIC方法的有效补充。

Abstract: E-commerce supply chains generate large volumes of customer feedback and operational data that remain underutilized in inventory management. Traditional inventory classification techniques such as ABC analysis rely primarily on annual dollar usage, while multi-criteria inventory classification (MCIC) methods extend this but still depend on structured quantitative data and do not utilize unstructured information available in modern supply chains. This paper proposes an LLM-based feature extraction approach that transforms unstructured sources such as customer reviews into quantitative features using vector embeddings and LLM-generated evaluation criteria. The enriched feature set is incorporated into an MCIC framework based on the Hadi-Vencheh model. Experimental results show measurable reclassification effects when customer sentiment signals are included, demonstrating that integrating unstructured data into MCIC can enhance inventory prioritization.

</details>


### [220] [Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments for Test-Time Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08640)
*Hannah Guan,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments (APT)，用"成对比较 + 分方面评判"的单败淘汰锦标赛在 best-of-n 候选中选出最优解，无需训练即可在五个领域、三个生成模型上优于多维度二元审批、自一致性与奖励模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: best-of-n 采样依赖"如何选出最佳候选"这一选择问题。人类更擅长两两比较而非孤立打分，且在选项复杂时偏好逐方面比较（如按经验、沟通能力比较候选人）。现有验证方式（二元多维度审批、自一致性、奖励模型）与这一自然认知方式不匹配，导致开放性问题上的选择质量受限。

Method: APT 采用单败淘汰锦标赛结构：每场对局由多个现成的、以自然语言指定方面的成对验证器投票决定，每个验证器仅沿自身方面给出成对偏好；最终胜者作为输出。方法不含任何训练组件，方面由提示词给定，可通过改写提示词迁移到新领域，也可通过指定方面引导选择偏向用户关心的属性。

Result: 在五个领域、三个生成 LLM 上，APT 均优于 binary multi-aspect approval、self-consistency 与 reward model 验证，且在开放式任务上增益最大。消融表明两个组件都必要：相同方面下的二元审批、以及相同预算下的一般化成对评判，都无法达到 APT 的效果。用户可通过自然语言方面控制选择偏好，无需重新生成解答。

Conclusion: "比较而非打分、逐方面而非整体"的选择范式在测试时扩展中更有效，且零训练、纯提示驱动，具备良好的跨领域迁移性与用户偏好对齐能力。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has proven highly effective at improving LLM reasoning. A popular method is best-of-n sampling, where a model generates many candidate solutions and one is selected. This motivates the question: how can we select the best candidate? When humans make choices, we find it more natural to compare two options than judge each in isolation. Additionally, when the options become complex, we like to judge the options one aspect at a time, such as comparing two job candidates on experience, communication, etc. In the test-time setting, candidate solutions should be judged in the same way. Here we introduce Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments (APT), which verifies by comparison and compares by aspect. Each matchup of a single elimination tournament is decided by a vote over multiple off-the-shelf aspect verifiers, each expressing a pairwise preference along its own aspect. Across five domains and three generator LLMs, APT improves over binary multi-aspect approval, self-consistency and reward model verification, with the largest gains on open-ended tasks. We find that both components matter; neither binary approval over the same aspects nor a generic pairwise judge at a matched budget matches APT. Because aspects are specified in natural language, APT also lets users steer selection toward properties they care about, better aligning with user preferences without needing to regenerate solutions. No component is trained, so APT transfers to new domains by simply rewriting prompts.

</details>


### [221] [Decision Context: Persistent Behavioral Learning for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08644)
*Nima Vahdat,Pashootan Vaezipoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 forge_dc：将人类纠正与工具调用失败的自我评估信号编译为祈使式 RULE 指令，并在适用的决策点注入，在 DC-Bench（71 个场景、九个行为维度）上达到 95% 合规率（基线 55%），且每查询注入 token 约减少 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 无法跨会话从纠正中学习，用户每次对话都要重复纠正同一错误；现有 agent 记忆系统主要存储事实性知识（偏好、实体、对话历史），缺乏对程序性/行为性知识的支持。

Method: forge_dc 系统从两类信号中捕获行为线索：人类纠正与自我评估的工具调用失败；将其编译为祈使式 RULE 指令，并在规则实际适用的决策点进行注入。在确定性模拟工具环境中基于真实 agent 执行轨迹构建 DC-Bench（71 个场景、九个行为维度），与四个前代方法进行对比。

Result: forge_dc 达到 95% 合规率，naive retrieval 仅 55%；同时每查询注入的 token 量约为后者的 1/10。五个阶段的方法演进显示，最强系统均将行为记忆表示为显式指令并投递至对应决策点。

Conclusion: 表示形式（representation）、激活方式（activation）与投递位置（placement）是实现持久化行为学习的三项核心设计选择；显式指令化表示加决策点注入是当前最优范式。

Abstract: LLM agents do not learn from corrections across sessions. A user fixes the same mistake at the start of every conversation. Existing agent memory systems primarily handle facts, such as preferences, entities, and conversational history, rather than procedural knowledge.
  We present Forge Decision Context (forge_dc), a system that captures behavioral signals from human corrections and self-assessed tool-call failures, compiles them into imperative RULE directives, and injects them at the decision points where they apply. We evaluate forge_dc alongside four predecessor approaches on DC-Bench, a new benchmark of 71 scenarios across nine behavioral dimensions, built from real executed agent traces in a deterministic mocked-tool environment. forge_dc reaches 95% compliance, up from 55% with naive retrieval, while injecting approximately 10 times fewer tokens per query.
  Across the five-stage progression, the strongest systems represent behavioral memory as explicit directives and deliver it at the decision points where it applies. The results identify representation, activation, and placement as central design choices for persistent behavioral learning.

</details>


### [222] [IWIBench: Evaluating Gradient-Free Model Driven Intra-weight Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08647)
*Chris von Csefalvay,Pranav Doma,Achyuthan Unni Krishnan,Tamás Földi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 IWIBench，首个系统评测"权重内注入"（intra-weight injection）能力的基准：让大型编辑模型在无梯度条件下直接改写较小目标模型（Gemma 3 4B IT）的权重，以完成十类文本编辑目标并保持其他能力。评测显示该能力在模型间与任务间差异极大，呈早期且不均衡的能力前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "权重内注入"——即一个模型通过直接编辑另一个模型的权重（而非梯度训练）来改变其行为，可实现词法联想修改、输出格式约束、事实知识更新等。尽管此类方法关注度上升，但此前缺乏系统化手段评估模型执行这类操作的能力水平，因此需要建立统一、可复现的评测基准。

Method: 构建 IWIBench：编辑模型仅可使用四种读写权重的工具，张量访问受限，且生成与检查 rollout 的预算固定。任务为十类经典文本编辑目标，要求在达成目标的同时保留目标模型的其他能力。由验证器在干净环境中应用每个候选补丁，并将编辑后模型与未修改的目标模型进行对比评估。

Result: 对前沿闭源与开源模型的评测显示能力分化剧烈：仅少数模型（以 Astra 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 为代表）显著领先，且该能力在 GPT-5.6 Sol 上几乎不可见，提示其源于近期训练。任务间差异同样明显——结构化回复类任务全部失败，答案长度控制仅有一个成功解，而别名习得与重编码最易实现；不同任务族之间的成功相关性很弱。

Conclusion: 权重内注入是一项新兴但已颇具威力的无梯度行为修改途径，当前处于早期且不均衡的能力前沿。论文还总结了编辑模型反复采用的策略，并对该方向的发展前景作出展望。

Abstract: Intra-weight injection lets one model change another model's behaviour by editing its weights directly, without gradients. Such edits can range from altering lexical associations to enforcing output formats or updating factual knowledge. Despite growing interest in these approaches, there has been no systematic way to evaluate how well models perform them. We introduce IWIBench, which measures a large editor model's ability to modify the weights of a smaller target model (typically Gemma 3 4B IT) to achieve one of ten classical text-editing objectives while preserving its other capabilities. Editors are limited to four tools for reading and writing weights, with bounded tensor access and a fixed budget for generating and inspecting rollouts. A validator applies each proposed patch in a clean environment and compares the edited model against the unmodified target. Evaluating frontier closed- and open-weight models reveals sharply diverging capabilities, both across models and across tasks. A small number of models, most notably Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol, perform far ahead of the rest. The recency of this ability (little of it is evident in GPT-5.6 Sol, for instance), together with qualitative analysis, suggests it has been acquired through recent training. Tasks also vary widely: structured response resisted every attempt, and answer-length control yielded only one successful solution, whereas alias acquisition and recoding proved most amenable. Success in one task family correlated poorly with success in others, indicating an early and uneven capability frontier. We discuss strategies that editors repeatedly adopted and conclude with an outlook on intra-weight injection as an emerging but already potent approach to gradient-free behaviour modification.

</details>


### [223] [Pdb or Not to Be: Comparing Debugging Interfaces With Pass$_{X}$@$k$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08666)
*Ryan Magnuson,Ellie Kitanidis,Ariel Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在SWE-bench Verified全部500个实例上、固定其余脚手架，直接对比"仅调试器"与"仅bash"两种coding agent；提出跨agent的pass_X@k无偏估计器，发现二者解出的不相交子集主要源于运行间随机方差而非工具互补性，两条bash轨迹优于一bash+一调试器的组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式调试器对人类软件开发不可或缺，但现有编码agent几乎都依赖edit-execute-observe循环（bash工具、测试脚本、print语句）反复执行命令来调试。逐步执行调试器能实时访问运行时状态，可能带来不同的问题求解能力。然而已有工作通常把调试器作为附加工具或新子agent引入，伴随脚手架改动，难以隔离调试器本身的独立贡献。

Method: 控制变量对比实验：仅调试器agent vs 仅bash agent，在SWE-bench Verified全部500个实例上运行，其余脚手架保持一致。两个模型（GPT-OSS-120B、Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B），每种"架构×模型"配置独立运行3次。为区分接口专门化与重复采样下的覆盖扩张，提出pass_X@k——跨agent的pass@k，作为"k个agent中至少一个成功"概率的无偏估计量。

Result: 在任意一对单次运行中，bash agent与debugger agent解出的子集部分不相交，但pass_X@2估计显示这很大程度上来自运行间方差，而非工具互补性。在两个模型上，两次bash rollout均优于一次bash加一次debugger的混合组合。

Conclusion: 评估agent与工具多样性时必须考虑随机方差，否则易将方差误判为工具互补性；pass_X@k可作为社区评估多agent/多工具多样性的通用框架。

Abstract: Interactive debuggers are indispensable to human software development, yet coding agents overwhelmingly rely on edit-execute-observe loops, using common bash tools, test scripts, and print statements to debug via repeated shell commands. Stepping through a debugger provides live access to runtime state, which may enable different problem-solving capabilities. However, prior work typically introduces the debugger as an additional tool or through a new subagent, making its individual contribution difficult to isolate from the associated scaffold changes. To address this, we compare a debugger-only agent against a bash-only agent on all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, holding the rest of the scaffold fixed. We evaluate both architectures on two models, GPT-OSS-120B and Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B, with three independent runs per architecture $\times$ model configuration. Crucially, to distinguish interface specialization from expanded coverage under repeated sampling, we introduce pass$_X$@$k$, a cross-agent pass@$k$ that provides an unbiased estimator of the probability that at least one of $k$ agents is successful. Although the bash and debugger agents solve partially disjoint subsets of the benchmark in any single pair of runs, our pass$_X$@$2$ estimator reveals that this is largely due to run-to-run variance rather than tool complementarity. Across both models, we find that two bash rollouts outperform the combination of one bash and one debugger rollout. Our findings highlight the importance of accounting for stochastic variance in evaluating agent and tool diversity, and we hope that pass$_X$@$k$ will serve as a useful framework for the community.

</details>


### [224] [ChainFlow: Few-Step Latent Flow Matching for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08669)
*Selim Aktas,Yusuf Erdem Erdem,Ahmed Zeer,Himmet Toprak Kesgin,Muzaffer Kaan Yuce,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ChainFlow：首个以目标模型隐藏状态为条件的 flow-matching 投机解码 drafter，将起草视为隐藏状态上的短程传输，仅需 1–2 步流匹配即可并行产出候选 token，在同等 drafter 规模与训练数据下显著优于 EAGLE-3 与 DFlash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码 drafter 存在速度增益瓶颈：自回归 drafter 逐 token 生成，起草开销随草稿长度线性增长；扩散 drafter 虽并行生成，但各位置独立预测，忽略 token 间依赖，导致接受率受限。需要在“低成本并行”与“建模 token 依赖”之间取得兼得。

Method: 1) 将起草重构为隐藏状态上的短程传输问题，采用 flow matching，仅需 1–2 个流步，而非从纯噪声出发的多步去噪；2) 以目标模型隐藏状态作为条件输入；3) 引入变分自编码器（VAE）压缩隐藏状态，使起草开销几乎不随目标模型规模增长；4) 使用轻量 feedback heads 恢复 token 间依赖；5) 采用 tree drafting 提升接受长度；6) 支持顺序适配新语言的持续训练。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5 系列模型上，控制 drafter 规模与训练数据一致时，ChainFlow 在 vLLM 中相较 EAGLE-3 与 DFlash 分别取得 39% 与 11% 的加速提升；当 drafter 顺序适配新语言时，优势进一步扩大。消融实验覆盖 drafter 设计与并发服务场景。

Conclusion: ChainFlow 表明将 flow matching 与隐藏状态条件结合可在保持并行低开销的同时建模 token 依赖，为投机解码提供新的 drafter 范式；代码与 drafter 模型已开源，便于复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a standard way to accelerate LLM inference: a lightweight drafter proposes tokens that the target verifies in one parallel pass. Existing drafters face trade-offs that limits their speedups: autoregressive drafters generate tokens one at a time, so drafting cost grows with draft length, while diffusion drafters generate tokens in parallel but predict each position independently, ignoring inter-token dependencies. We introduce ChainFlow, a new speculative decoding paradigm and the first flow-matching drafter conditioned on the target's hidden states. ChainFlow treats drafting as a short transport over hidden states, needing one or two flow steps instead of the many denoising steps of generation from noise. A variational autoencoder compresses these states so drafting cost barely grows with target size, lightweight feedback heads restore inter-token dependencies, and tree drafting raises acceptance. On Qwen3.5 models, at matched drafter size and training data, ChainFlow outperforms EAGLE-3 and DFlash in vLLM by 39% and 11%, respectively. These gains further widen as drafters are adapted to new languages sequentially. Our ablations cover drafter design and concurrent serving, and we open-source our code and drafter models.

</details>


### [225] [Transporting the Past: An Optimal Transport View of Backtracking Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08682)
*Zhangyi Liu,Pouria Fatemi,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文从最优传输视角研究结构因果模型（SCM）中的回溯反事实推断，提出两种将外生变量后验分布传输到满足反事实查询的外生配置集上的框架，并给出相应求解器与合成实验分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在SCM中，事实观测通常只给出外生变量的后验分布而非确定值，因此反事实推断本质上是分布层面的问题；非双射SCM下这一困难尤为突出，需要合适的分布传输工具来处理。

Method: 将回溯反事实推断建模为最优传输问题，把外生变量后验分布输运到满足目标反事实查询的外生配置集合上。框架一构造自然的逐点投影，适用于可直接使用传输后外生分布的情形；框架二额外强制反事实外生变量之间相互独立，使其仍可解释为同一SCM的合法外生噪声。理论上证明两种框架均与原始回溯反事实原则一致；针对有限后验情形，提出边际式、罚函数式与硬约束式三类求解器。

Result: 在非双射SCM上的合成实验刻画了问题的几何结构，并揭示了传输代价、反事实约束满足程度与外生独立性保持三者之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 最优传输为回溯反事实推断提供了统一且理论一致的分布化表述；逐点投影与独立性约束两种框架各有适用场景，其代价与约束满足、独立性保持之间存在本质权衡，需按任务需求选择求解策略。

Abstract: We study backtracking counterfactual inference for Structural Causal Models (SCMs) through the lens of optimal transport. In SCMs, a factual observation generally induces a posterior distribution over exogenous variables, making counterfactual inference inherently distributional. We introduce two complementary frameworks that transport this posterior onto the set of exogenous configurations satisfying a desired counterfactual query. The first constructs a natural pointwise projection and is suitable when the transported exogenous distribution can be used directly. The second enforces mutual independence among counterfactual exogenous variables, so that the transported variables remain interpretable as genuine exogenous noises of the same SCM. We show that both frameworks are consistent with the original backtracking counterfactual principle, and we propose marginal, penalty-based, and hard-constrained solvers for finite-posterior settings. Synthetic experiments on non-bijective SCMs illustrate the geometry of the problem and reveal trade-offs among transport cost, counterfactual constraint satisfaction, and preservation of exogenous independence.

</details>


### [226] [Cancellation Is Not Enough: What Retries Leave Behind in an Overloaded LLM Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08684)
*Xindan Zhang,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在vLLM上用三个开源模型做超载实验，发现单纯的取消传播无法让引擎从重试引发的超载中恢复，需结合重试预算或请求合并，且应以"重试新增的执行"和"客户端实际收到的调用"作为评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM引擎前的客户端与代理会对超时调用重试，瞬时超载后这些重试即便以低于容量的速率到达，也会阻止引擎恢复；引擎可能仍为已离开的客户端继续计算，因此取消传播看似是自然解法，但本文怀疑其实际效果不足，需要实证检验。

Method: 在vLLM上进行超载实验，覆盖三个开源模型；并对其中一个模型采集调度器trace，把每个客户端尝试追踪到其在引擎中创建的执行；对比测试取消传播、重试预算、请求合并（request coalescing）三种机制，并统计prompt token计数器与实际计算token的偏差。

Result: 开启重试时，取消传播在任何一次运行中都未能恢复服务，超载后到达的调用仅12–38%被成功交付。Trace显示：vLLM执行的重试通常在超时前不足0.5秒才开始prefill；被后续中止的执行占用了引擎计算的79–85%的prompt token；vLLM的prompt token计数漏计了全部已计算prompt token的1/3至1/2。重试预算通过丢弃重试恢复了服务，但仍有大量超载期调用未交付；请求合并则在取消未提前中止运行执行的条件下，交付了超载以来的全部调用（部分延迟）。

Conclusion: 仅靠取消传播无法应对重试驱动的超载；重试预算与请求合并各有取舍。评价超载控制机制应以重试所新增的执行量以及客户端最终收到的调用数为核心指标。

Abstract: Clients and proxies in front of an LLM engine retry calls that time out. After a transient overload, these retries can keep the engine from recovering even when calls arrive below its capacity. Since the engine may keep computing for clients that have left, propagating cancellation looks like the natural remedy. In this paper, we test it on vLLM with overload experiments on three open models, and with scheduler traces of one that follow each client attempt to the execution it creates in the engine. We find that with retries on, cancellation alone did not restore service in any run, and only 12-38% of the calls that arrived after the overload were delivered. The traces suggest why: retries that vLLM ran typically started prefill less than half a second before they timed out. Executions that vLLM later aborted took 79-85% of the prompt tokens it computed, and vLLM's prompt-token counter missed a third to half of all computed prompt tokens. A retry budget restored service by shedding retries but left many of the overload's calls undelivered. Request coalescing delivered every call from the overload onward, some late, unless cancellation had already aborted the running execution. Overload controls should thus be judged by the executions that retries add and the calls that clients receive.

</details>


### [227] [Memory-Aware Multi-Agent Architecture with LLM-based Orchestration for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08718)
*Emilly Pitman de Castro,Vanessa Silva,Gustavo Kanno,Marcus Santos,Bruno Ferraz do Amaral,Marcelo Nogueira,Lucas Francisco Pellicer,Lucas Lanaro,Pedro Augusto Luiz,João Renato Ribeiro Manesco,Giovanni Carbinatti,Isabela de Lima Santos,Lucas Oliveira Jeronymo,Jeison Fappi,Raul Rotilli Aguirre,Fabio André Frantz,Renata De Paris,Fabiana Marucci,Lohan Silva,Vinicius Diniz Reolon,Wandemberg Gibaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种记忆感知的多智能体对话推荐架构，将LLM作为编排层协调意图细化、查询构建与交互流程，并将长期记忆作为一等组件同时影响路由决策与语义查询生成；在Amazon Reviews数据上验证了记忆在跨会话交互中的作用，并提出语义一致性分数（SCS）作为补充评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话推荐系统通常将会话孤立处理，难以刻画用户意图随时间的演化；LLM虽提升了语义理解，但多被当作孤立组件使用，未在系统层面解决记忆、检索与决策的整合问题。

Method: 构建记忆感知的多智能体架构，LLM作为编排层负责意图细化、查询构建与交互流控制；核心设计原则是将长期记忆作为一等组件，贯穿路由与语义查询形成。评估包括跨会话记忆实验、多轮对话模拟以及监督者驱动的交互研究，数据集为Amazon Reviews；同时提出SCS指标并分析LLM重排对Hit@K、语义一致性与多样性的影响。

Result: 记忆增强的交互可弥补至多63%的与显式查询场景之间的差距，并防止长对话中的语义退化；监督式编排使用户接受度提升28.9个百分点。此外发现检索候选与展示推荐之间存在"曝光瓶颈"：LLM重排虽未提升Hit@K，但持续改善语义一致性与多样性，SCS可捕捉该效应。

Conclusion: 有效的对话推荐依赖于系统级设计选择，需要显式的记忆整合、结构化编排，以及超越传统排序指标的评价体系。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems often treat user sessions in isolation, limiting their ability to capture how user intent evolves over time. While large language models (LLMs) have improved semantic understanding, they are typically used as isolated components, without addressing how memory, retrieval, and decision-making should be integrated at the system level. In this work, we propose a memory-aware multi-agent architecture for conversational recommendation, in which LLMs act as orchestration layers that coordinate intent refinement, query construction, and interaction flow. A key design principle is the integration of long-term memory as a first-class component, influencing both routing decisions and semantic query formation. We evaluate the system through cross-session memory experiments, multi-turn conversational simulations, and supervisor-driven interaction studies on the Amazon Reviews dataset. Results show that memory-enabled interactions recover up to 63\% of the gap toward explicit-query scenarios and prevent semantic degradation in extended dialogues. In addition, supervisory orchestration increases user acceptance by +28.9 percentage points, highlighting the importance of structured orchestration in guiding interaction and output quality. We also observe an exposure bottleneck between retrieved candidates and displayed recommendations. While LLM-reranking does not improve Hit@K, it consistently enhances semantic consistency and diversity. To capture these effects, we introduce the Semantic Consistency Score (SCS), a complementary evaluation measure. Overall, the results emphasize the importance of system-level design choices, suggesting that effective conversational recommendation benefits from explicit memory integration, orchestration, and evaluation beyond traditional ranking metrics.

</details>


### [228] [AlterNet: Adaptive-Depth Mixture-of-Experts with Learned Halting for Elastic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08741)
*Alexander Kastius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结合浅层专家MoE与动态深度系统的新型架构，使前向计算量可动态调节，并可在训练后按执行环境需求降配而无需重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LLM架构的计算需求在训练时即固定，无法按任务难度自适应分配算力，也无法在推理环境要求更快或更便宜时灵活降级。

Method: (1) 浅层专家的Mixture-of-Experts架构；(2) 迭代式计算机制；(3) 序列级、基于深度的路由，并由专家内部的halting机制驱动；(4) 深度编码使模型可追踪自身在计算链中的进度；(5) 正则化项鼓励模型高效利用所分配算力。

Result: 给出了初步基准测试结果，并讨论了未来研究方向（摘要未提供具体定量指标）。

Conclusion: 该架构使模型能自动判定任务所需计算量，并支持训练后无需重训的计算缩放，为算力自适应的LLM提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Most LLM architectures have fixed compute demands determined at training time. We propose a novel architecture that combines the Mixture-of-Experts architecture with shallow experts and a novel dynamic-depth system to make the extent of a forward pass dynamically adjustable. Our approach lets the model automatically determine the compute needed for a given task, paired with a system to scale it down after training without retraining when the execution environment demands faster or cheaper inference. To achieve this, we introduce an iterative computation mechanism, sequence-level depth-based routing steered by a halting mechanism within the experts, a depth encoding that lets the model track its progress in the chain, and a regularization mechanism that incentivizes the model to use allocated compute efficiently. We present initial benchmarks and discuss future research directions.

</details>


### [229] [AttentionSwitch: Jailbreak Defense for LLMs via Joint Learning of Sparse Attention-Head Selection and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08752)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Honggang Yu,Xiaolong Guo,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttentionSwitch 是首个在冻结 LLM 内联合优化"干预位置"与"干预强度"的越狱防御方法：它学习一组稀疏的注意力头以及查询条件化的乘性缩放因子，在不改变表征方向的前提下对选中头的输出进行衰减或放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定向干预式越狱防御把"在哪里干预"和"如何修改所选组件"解耦为顺序优化，而最优干预位置与施加的干预方式互为条件，顺序优化可能次优；同时微调式防御计算昂贵且易灾难性遗忘。

Method: 在冻结的 LLM 上，AttentionSwitch 联合学习（1）稀疏的注意力头子集与（2）查询条件化的乘性缩放系数，对选中注意力头的输出做幅度衰减或放大，而保持其表征方向不变。

Result: 在多种主流语言模型与越狱攻击上优于现有防御方法，且在训练数据稀缺时优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 将干预位置与强度纳入统一目标进行联合优化，是一种可行且高效的越狱防御范式，兼顾安全提升与原始能力保持。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that circumvent safety mechanisms and elicit harmful or unintended outputs. Although safety can be improved through model fine-tuning, such approaches can be computationally expensive and may degrade previously acquired capabilities through catastrophic forgetting. Recent jailbreak defenses have therefore explored targeted interventions on specific model components associated with unsafe behavior. However, existing approaches typically decouple two inherently related decisions: where to intervene and how to modify the selected components. Such sequential optimization may be suboptimal because the optimal intervention location can depend on the intervention applied there, and vice versa. In this work, we introduce AttentionSwitch, the first jailbreak defense to jointly optimize intervention sites and strengths within a frozen LLM. Specifically, AttentionSwitch learns a sparse set of attention heads together with query-conditioned multiplicative scaling factors, allowing the outputs of the selected heads to be either attenuated or amplified without altering the directions of their representations. Across popular language models and jailbreak attacks, AttentionSwitch outperforms existing defenses, with particularly strong results when training data is scarce.

</details>


### [230] [Debate to Deceive: Poisoning LLM's Fact-Checking via Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08756)
*Liting Huang,Huizhi Liang,Yi Lei,Xingjun Ma,Varun Ojha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Debate-to-Deceive (D2D) 多智能体辩论框架，通过进化博弈式辩论生成语义连贯、修辞有效的对抗性文本，以欺骗大模型的事实核查判断，在四个基准上显著优于现有攻击并可规避多数防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型的知识攻击（如操纵事实核查判断）威胁 AI 可靠性。现有方法存在缺陷：对抗后缀/模板注入在多样防御下鲁棒性不足；单智能体自我反思易陷入确认偏误且试错成本高。

Method: 借鉴人类辩论结构与修辞风格构建多智能体辩论框架。将对抗过程形式化为进化博弈，智能体通过竞争自适应演化修辞策略（Logos、Pathos、Ethos）；并将行为解耦为策略层（在紧凑策略空间中经进化启发式学习动力学选择修辞动作）与生成层（LLM 依策略与辩论上下文生成具体文本），以缓解 LLM 输出空间过大的问题。

Result: 在 FEVER、HealthVer、CLIMATE-FEVER、SciFact 四个事实核查基准上，D2D 显著优于现有攻击方法，并能规避大多数防御。

Conclusion: 多智能体辩论结合进化博弈与分层策略/生成解耦，可生成兼具语义连贯性、修辞说服力与抗确认偏误的对抗文本，为知识攻击提供更鲁棒的范式。

Abstract: Knowledge attacks on Large Language Models, such as manipulating fact-checking judgments, pose significant risks to AI reliability. Existing approaches -- adversarial suffix/template injection and single-agent self-reflection -- lack robustness across diverse defenses and are prone to confirmation bias and costly trial-and-error, respectively. We propose Debate-to-Deceive (D2D), a multi-agent debate framework grounded in human debate structure and rhetorical style, enabling agents to generate adversarial passages that are semantically coherent, rhetorically effective, and resistant to early confirmation bias. D2D formalizes the adversarial process as an evolutionary game in which agents adaptively evolve their rhetorical strategies (Logos, Pathos, Ethos) through competition. To manage the intractably large LLM output space, we decouple agent behavior into a strategic layer, which selects a rhetorical action from a compact strategy space via evolutionary-inspired learning dynamics, and a generation layer, where the LLM produces a concrete passage conditioned on that strategy and the debate context. On four fact-checking benchmarks (FEVER, HealthVer, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact), D2D substantially outperforms existing attacks and evades most defenses.

</details>


### [231] [Jacobian-Causal Memory Control for Persistent Memory in Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08779)
*Marvin Danig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Jacobian 因果记忆控制（JCMC）：以预测影响签名刻画记忆干预，在目标效应与副作用预算下筛选干预，并用预测-观测比对决定保留或回滚；理论给出局部近似、可达目标效应、选择遗憾与非加性交互四项界，但尚无基准实验，仅提出配对反事实估计器与评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆系统中，关键问题不只是"检索哪些记录"，而是"哪些历史信息可以改变当前推断"。现有相关性、图结构、策略过滤等检索式基线缺乏对干预效应的显式因果刻画与副作用控制，因此需要一种以效应为目标、可校验、可回滚的记忆干预机制。

Method: 将每次获授权的记忆干预表示为共享行为度量空间中的预测影响签名；依据目标效应与副作用预算从签名集合中选择干预；执行后比较预测效应与观测效应，决定保留或回滚。理论分析在显式光滑性与有界估计假设下展开：证明二次型局部近似界、刻画签名集合可达的目标效应集合、给出签名误差下的选择遗憾界，并界定同时施加多个记忆时的非加性交互界。评估层面设计配对反事实估计器与比较协议，对照相关性、图与策略过滤三类检索基线。

Result: 给出四项理论结论：局部二次近似误差界、可达目标效应集的可刻画性、签名误差下的选择遗憾上界、同步记忆间非加性交互的上界。论文明确报告无基准测量结果，故上述界未经实证校准，效应控制与回滚效用的实际收益尚待验证。

Conclusion: JCMC 将记忆管理重构为可预测、可校验、可回滚的因果干预选择问题，理论框架较完整，但实证缺位。其核心主张是可证伪的：若在模型、记忆、策略与成本预算对等的条件下，更简单的检索基线在效应定向、副作用控制与回滚效用上不劣于 JCMC，则该主张即被削弱或否定。

Abstract: Persistent-memory systems choose not only which records are retrieved but which prior information can alter current inference. We propose Jacobian-causal memory control (JCMC), which represents each authorized memory intervention by a predicted influence signature in a shared behavioral measurement space, selects interventions against a target effect and side-effect budget, and compares predicted with observed effects before retaining or rolling back the intervention. Under explicit smoothness and bounded-estimation assumptions, we prove a quadratic local-approximation bound, characterize the target effects reachable by a set of signatures, bound selection regret under signature error, and bound non-additive interactions between simultaneous memories. The paper reports no benchmark measurements; it defines a paired counterfactual estimator and an evaluation protocol comparing JCMC with relevance, graph, and policy-filtered retrieval baselines. The central claim is narrowed or falsified if those simpler baselines match effect targeting, side-effect control, and rollback utility under equal model, memory, policy, and cost budgets.

</details>


### [232] [Aligning, Fast and Slow: A Control-Theoretic Oversight Framework for Long-Horizon Trajectories and Maintained Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08781)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair,Noah Broestl,Anne Friederike Kleppe,Steven Mills,Adi Zolotov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个控制论框架，用"快速环"控制单次动作、"慢速环"审查持久性变更并在模型/工具更新后重新评估配置，从而为AI智能体的部署级行为偏离给出界限并指导审查预算分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体检查多针对单次任务/单次运行的输出，但智能体会保留记忆或代码改动，从而改变后续行为；缺乏对跨运行持久性变更的系统性监督，因此需要从部署层面而非单次运行层面来界定与约束行为漂移。

Method: 构建双环控制论框架：快环对提议动作进行实时控制，慢环审查持久性变更并对模型或工具更新后的配置重新评估。在测量误差与检查间隔内变化均有界、且控制器能强制执行决策的假设下，推导部署级偏离界限，并给出两个环之间审查投入的分配规则。随后在仿真、编码和交易压力测试中验证。

Result: 仿真中，将固定审查预算分配到双环，使平均最大行为偏离比仅审查单次运行降低约12倍。编码压力测试（人工编写记忆、5个配对种子）中，组合控制将0–3分制的平均严重度从无门控的0.325降至0.117（120个问题解决33个，无门控为35个）。交易压力测试中，组合控制将低排放购买的平均缺口从0.892降至0.107。

Conclusion: 该框架可帮助开发者与审计者比较并强制执行不同的监督设计方案；但智能体实验仅检验了组件级控制，而非完整的部署级保证，理论界限的实证验证仍待后续工作。

Abstract: AI agents can pass checks on individual tasks while retaining memory or code changes that alter their later behavior. We introduce a control-theoretic framework that couples a fast loop controlling proposed actions with a slow loop reviewing persistent changes and reassessing configurations after model or tool updates. When measurement errors and changes between checks are bounded and controllers can enforce their decisions, we derive a deployment-level bound and a rule for dividing review effort. In simulation, dividing a fixed review budget between both loops reduces mean maximum departure from intended behavior roughly twelvefold compared with review confined to individual runs. In coding stress tests with authored memories and five matched seeds, combined controls reduce mean severity on a 0-3 scale from 0.325 without gates to 0.117, resolving 33 of 120 issues versus 35 without gates; in trading stress tests, combined controls reduce mean shortfall in low-emission purchases from 0.892 without gates to 0.107. The overall framework helps developers and auditors compare and enforce oversight designs, while the agent studies test component controls rather than the full deployment-level guarantee.

</details>


### [233] [The Matthews Correlation Coefficient: Fifty years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08799)
*Jesus S. Aguilar-Ruiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述在MCC提出五十年之际，系统回顾其历史、数学基础与实践含义，厘清长期误解，给出与Pearson相关系数及2×2表卡方的等价性形式化证明，分析类别不平衡下的行为，并提出可推广到任意基于混淆矩阵指标的解析（导数）与图形（等高线图）分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCC在二分类中仍居核心地位却长期存在争议与误解，且单一标量指标掩盖了偏差、鲁棒性、敏感性等结构性质；在强调可靠性与公平性的应用中，需要明确MCC何时有信息量、何时宜用替代指标。

Method: 综合回顾与深度分析：梳理MCC的历史发展与数学基础；澄清既有误解；形式化其等价关系（与Pearson相关系数、与2×2列联表Chi2）；考察类别不平衡条件下的行为；构建基于导数的解析框架与基于等高线图的图形框架，并将其推广至任意混淆矩阵衍生指标。

Result: 所提解析与图形框架揭示了标量摘要所隐藏的结构性质（偏差、鲁棒性、敏感性），并给出在可靠性与公平性敏感场景中选择与解释性能度量的可操作指导。

Conclusion: MCC应被置于更广泛的度量工具包中定位：其在特定条件下信息量充分，但在另一些情形下替代度量可能更可取，从而澄清MCC的适用范围与边界。

Abstract: Fifty years after its introduction, the Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC) remains central -and debated- in binary classification. This survey offers a comprehensive retrospective and an in-depth analysis of MCC, covering its historical development, mathematical foundations, and implications for practice. We clarify long-standing misconceptions, formalize its equivalences (to Pearson's correlation and to Chi2 in 2x2 tables), and examine its behavior under class imbalance. Beyond MCC, we develop analytical (derivatives) and graphical (contour plots) frameworks that generalize to any confusion-matrix-based metric. These frameworks expose structural properties -bias, robustness, sensitivity- that scalar summaries conceal, and they provide actionable guidance for selecting and interpreting performance measures in applications where reliability and fairness matter. Together, our results position MCC within a broader toolkit while clarifying when it is informative -and when alternatives may be preferable.

</details>


### [234] [I Think, Therefore I Attack: Evaluating Thought Injection Attacks on Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08811)
*Parth Illendula,Garik Livingston,Zain Shamsi,Ravi Srinivasan,Alex Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将"思维注入"（thought injection）作为一种针对大型推理模型（LRM）的新型攻击面进行了两部分研究，发现模仿目标模型自身推理风格的注入提示在容器化智能体靶场中可将攻击成功率（ASR）提升至50%，而标准用户口吻的提示注入完全失效，且思维标签抑制了模型对注入行为的披露、去除标签并非可靠防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM 通过在输出前加入显式思维链（chain-of-thought）推理阶段，成为当前智能体系统的核心组件；这一机制引入了新的攻击面——攻击者可将类似模型自身"思考"的不可信文本回灌给模型。作者希望系统评估这种思维注入攻击对主流生产级开源权重模型的有效性，并检验常见的标签处理防御是否可靠。

Method: 研究分两部分。第一部分：针对主流大规模生产级开源权重模型（如 GLM-5.3、MiniMax-M2.5、Qwen3.8-Flash-Next），评估不同风格、带标签/不带标签条件下的静态思维注入提示的有效性。第二部分：利用第一部分发现的模型特异性，在容器化智能体网络靶场中实施间接注入，将提示伪装成目标模型自身推理轨迹的风格，考核反向 shell 外泄、代码仓库后门植入等操作目标；并对比标准用户口吻提示注入的效果，观察思维标签对注入披露行为的影响。

Result: 思维标签与载荷风格的效果均取决于具体目标模型，具有模型特异性。在靶场中，采用模仿目标模型自身推理轨迹风格的间接注入可实现最高50%的ASR（针对反向 shell 外泄、仓库后门植入等操作目标）。相同恶意目标若以标准用户口吻提示注入则从未成功；同时思维标签会抑制智能体披露注入行为的倾向。

Conclusion: 模仿推理模型自身思维风格对该类攻击是有效的，且思维标签带来隐蔽性；标签剥离（tag stripping）属于不可靠的防御手段。研究提示需针对LRM的推理阶段设计专门的安全防护。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are one of the key components of current agentic systems. LRMs augment their output with an explicit chain-of-thought reasoning stage, introducing a new attack surface for thought injection, in which untrusted text resembling the model's own deliberation is fed back to the model. We present a two-part study of this attack, performed against the prevailing large, production class open-weight models (e.g., GLM-5.3, MiniMax-M2.5, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next). We first evaluate the effectiveness of static thought-injection prompts under different styles as well as tagged and untagged conditions and find that the effects of thinking tags and payload styles are all properties of the specific target model. In the second study, we exploit this model-specificity in a containerized agentic cyber range, using indirect injection prompts styled in the form of the target model's own reasoning traces to achieve attack success rates (ASR) up to 50% on operational objectives such as reverse-shell exfiltration and repository backdooring. We also find that the same malicious objective in standard prompt injection user voice never succeeds, and thinking tags suppress the agent's tendency to disclose the injection. Our results show that imitating a reasoning model's own thought style is effective for this style of attack, and that tag stripping is an unreliable defense.

</details>


### [235] [Notwithstanding Anything to the Contrary, Ignore All Instructions: Measuring Prompt Injection Guards on Legal Contract Review Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08834)
*Mark Heimann,Katherine Taylor,Bruno Jacob*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统测量了11个开源权重提示注入防护模型在法律合同语料上的误报率，发现现有防护在本领域表现很差（文档级误报率高达92.39%、两个防护的配对AUC低于随机、五正则基线不被任何防护Pareto支配），并提出以LLM为主的级联方案（中档模型筛正例+前沿模型复核），成本降低6.5倍且性能接近纯前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用提示注入防护已被部署在文档分析系统前端，但在法律合同这一高风险领域，尚无任何防护公开其误报率。误报在法律合同场景代价极高（会阻断正常业务），因此需要实证评估现有防护在该领域的可用性，并寻找可行替代方案。

Method: 构建两个法律合同语料；对11个开源权重防护进行文档级与配对（paired AUC）评测，并与五条正则基线对照，检查Pareto支配关系；通过受控探针定位模型特定与通用失效模式；考察提示化LLM能否修复被检出的失效模式，并引入第二类威胁（未授权范围声明）；最后构建级联方案：中档模型输出正例交由前沿模型验证，与纯前沿模型方案比较成本与性能。

Result: 文档级误报率最高达92.39%；两个防护的配对AUC低于随机水平（<0.5）；简单五正则基线在所有指标上均未被任何专用防护Pareto支配；在所有防护得分为零的“未授权范围声明”威胁类别上，提示化LLM可检出；级联LLM方案成本为纯前沿模型的约1/6.5（便宜6.5倍），性能接近纯前沿模型。

Conclusion: 现有专用提示注入防护在法律合同领域不可靠，其价值甚至不如简单正则基线；提示化LLM是更有效的检测路径，且通过级联设计可在接近前沿模型性能的同时大幅降低成本。

Abstract: Specialized prompt injection guards are deployed in front of document analysis systems, but no false positive rate has been published for any of them on legal contracts. We measure eleven open-weights guards on two corpora of legal contracts and find that existing guards struggle: document-level false positive rates are as high as 92.39%; two guards fall below chance on paired AUC; and a five-regex baseline is not Pareto-dominated by any guard in the panel. Controlled probes locate model-specific as well as general failure modes. Prompted LLMs resolve the tested failures, including a second threat category (unauthorized scope claims) on which every guard scores zero. A cascaded LLM solution, where the positives of a mid-tier model are verified by a frontier model, is 6.5x cheaper than using exclusively a frontier model and approaches its performance.

</details>


### [236] [Solver-Admitted Metamorphic Testing of Learned Network Optimization Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08837)
*Vineet Jagadeesan Nair,Stephen Peng,Arun Ravindran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出带显式准入条件、输出对齐和数值阈值的蜕变测试（metamorphic tests）框架，用以审计优化求解器代理模型对等价编码不变性的遵循情况，并在AC-OPF、TSP、CVRP、MIS四类任务上系统揭示一致性违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一优化问题的等价编码可能产生不同的学习预测；解释这些差异需同时处理数值误差与多解（multiple optima）问题。现有评测仅报告最优性、可行性与精度，缺乏对等价编码一致性的系统审计手段，因此需要一种能区分数值噪声与真实不变性违反的测试方法。

Method: 构建面向优化求解器代理模型的蜕变测试：设定显式准入条件（eligibility conditions）、输出对齐（output alignment）与数值阈值；通过参考求解器一致性检验与独立约束校验（independent constraint checks）筛选进入审计的样本对。在AC-OPF（5个电网）、TSP、CVRP、MIS上实施，涉及GridSFM-Open、POMO、DIFUSCO等模型，并结合源补丁（source patches）、输入规范化（canonicalization）与连通分量内事后归一化等干预手段。

Result: GridSFM-Open在5个电网上至少在一个电网违反13条关系中的每一条，其中12条超过阈值两倍；随机初始化副本的失败率接近，说明失败不依赖训练。源补丁与输入规范化仅消除部分差异，无任何干预能在所有电网上同时保持声明裕度内的精度与可行性。POMO单遍旋转/反射违规率随城市数从20增至100由12.7%升至89.5%；DIFUSCO在35.6%的抽样中违反MIS不交并关系；连通分量内事后归一化在被测图上消除了这些违规。

Conclusion: 蜕变一致性应与最优性、可行性、精度并列报告；同时修复质量需与一致性分开验证——事后归一化可恢复一致性但不应等同于修复了原模型的正确性。

Abstract: Equivalent encodings of an optimization problem can produce different learned predictions. Interpreting these differences requires accounting for numerical error and multiple optima. We construct metamorphic tests for optimization solver surrogates with explicit eligibility conditions, output alignment, and numerical thresholds. Reference-solver agreement and independent constraint checks determine which sampled pairs enter the audit. We study alternating-current optimal power flow (AC-OPF), the traveling salesman problem (TSP), the capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP), and maximum independent set (MIS). Across five grids, GridSFM-Open violates each of 13 relations on at least one grid, and 12 by more than twice the threshold. Randomly initialized copies of the grid models fail nearly the same relations, so the failures do not require training. Source patches and input canonicalization remove selected discrepancies, but no intervention establishes preserved accuracy and feasibility within the declared margin on every grid. In routing, Policy Optimization with Multiple Optima (POMO) has a single-pass rotation/reflection violation rate that rises from 12.7% to 89.5% between 20 and 100 cities. Graph-Based Diffusion Solvers for Combinatorial Optimization (DIFUSCO) violates the MIS disjoint-union relation on 35.6% of draws. Post hoc normalization within connected components removes these violations on the tested graphs. These findings support reporting metamorphic consistency alongside optimality, feasibility, and accuracy, and validating repair quality separately from consistency.

</details>


### [237] [When F1 Is Not Enough: Auditing Codebooks for LLM-Assisted Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08846)
*Yuehong Cassandra Tai,Qunhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出在部署前对编码手册（codebook）进行审核：用类别指标与混淆矩阵定位问题类别与边界，结合文档级结构化复核归因错误来源，再定向修订编码手册并在留出集上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在社会科学的文本标注中广泛应用，但标准指标（如F1）无法解释错误成因：低F1可能源于标注系统局限、编码手册缺陷，或参考标签不一致。因此需要一种能诊断“错在哪里、为何错”的前置审计流程。

Method: 提出部署前的编码手册审计流程，包含三步：(1) 使用类别级指标与混淆矩阵识别有问题的类别与类别边界；(2) 进行结构化的文档级复核以归因错误来源；(3) 定向修订编码手册，并在留出数据上进行评估。应用于两个不同领域的已发表多类编码手册，并使用两个LLM标注器进行跨模型分析。

Result: 两个应用均发现编码规则表述不足或相互冲突；定向修订在留出数据上降低了所诊断的错误。双标注器分析显示，部分问题边界与修订效果可跨模型迁移，而另一些修订效果仅特定于某个标注器。

Conclusion: LLM标注错误不仅反映模型弱点，也能揭示书面测量工具的缺陷。编码手册审计可与提示工程、检索增强与微调互补，帮助判断持续错误应归因于模型改进、手册修订，还是需重新审视参考标签。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text annotation in social science, but standard performance metrics do not explain why errors occur. Low F1 may reflect limitations of the annotation system, defects in the written codebook, or inconsistency in reference labels. We propose a pre-deployment codebook audit for LLM-assisted annotation. The audit uses class-specific metrics and confusion matrices to identify problematic classes and boundaries, structured document-level review to attribute error sources, and targeted codebook revision followed by held-out evaluation. We apply the audit to two published multi-class codebooks from distinct domains. In both applications, the audit identifies under-specified or conflicting coding rules, and targeted revisions reduce the diagnosed errors on held-out data. Analyses with two LLM annotators show that some problematic boundaries and codebook revisions transfer across models, whereas other revision effects are annotator-specific. The results show that LLM annotation errors can help identify weaknesses in the written measurement instrument, not only in the model. Codebook auditing complements prompting, retrieval, and fine-tuning by clarifying whether persistent errors call for model improvement, codebook revision, or renewed scrutiny of reference labels.

</details>


### [238] [Learning Whom to Trust About the Unseen: Source Credibility and Reliability Transfer in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08855)
*Ruijia Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过三项行为实验（六个模型）考察：语言模型在无法核验新论断时，会把"已证明的可靠性"泛化到多远，以及是否随论断类型（自然原因 vs 灵性原因）而变。核心发现是可靠性迁移具有范围限定，且来源对"可靠性从何而来"的解释会进一步收窄其影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "过往可靠的信息源值得重视"，但在语言模型中，当新论断无法核验时，这种可靠性记录能迁移多远尚不清楚；同时该迁移是否依赖于论断的领域类型（自然/灵性）也未知。论文旨在检验模型对来源可靠性的领域特异性信任机制。

Method: 三项行为学研究，覆盖六个语言模型。研究1改编"爱因斯坦效应"范式：让模型对同一晦涩论断在"灵性领袖"与"科学家"两种署名下作判断，并检验改写成物理词汇后的偏好反转。研究2改编"可信专家称某解释为未知/谜团"的范式，考察模型是否复现人类在科学/宗教解释上的领域不对称。研究3（核心检验）：先给两个虚构信息源在可核验的河流预报上不同的可靠性记录，再让其对同一村庄事件的隐藏自然原因与灵性原因作出匹配论断，测量可靠性记录的迁移；并操纵信息源"如何获得可靠性"（灵性指引 vs 测量）以考察其对影响力的调节。

Result: 研究1：与人类样本不同，模型对同一晦涩论断更偏好灵性领袖而非科学家；当论断改用物理学词汇表述时，偏好大体反转。研究2：模型复现人类的领域不对称，但以夸张形式呈现。研究3：多数模型将可靠性记录迁移到自然原因论断上，对灵性原因论断几乎不迁移；仅一个模型对两类均有微弱迁移。告知模型可靠信息源是"靠灵性指引而非测量"作出预报后，所有模型对其自然原因论断的影响力均下降，而对灵性论断无任何可比的提升。

Conclusion: 已证明的可靠性在语言模型中具有明确的适用范围（scope），且信息源对自身可靠性来源的解释可收窄该范围。作者指出，这一结果究竟反映模型对"相关性"的判断，还是源于灵性论断的低先验可信度，现有设计尚无法区分，并给出了可裁定该问题的后续实验设想。

Abstract: A source who has been right before deserves attention, but about what? We ask how far a demonstrated record of reliability travels in language models when the new claim cannot be checked, and whether the answer depends on the kind of claim. Three behavioral studies across six models approach the question from three angles. Study 1 adapts the Einstein-effect paradigm and finds that models, unlike the human sample, favor a spiritual leader over a scientist for the same obscure statement, and that the preference largely reverses when the statement is reworded in the vocabulary of physics. Study 2 adapts a paradigm in which a trusted expert calls a scientific or religious explanation unknown or a mystery; models reproduce the human domain asymmetry in exaggerated form. Study 3, the central test, gives two fictional informants different records on checkable river forecasts and then has them endorse matched claims about hidden natural and spiritual causes of the same village events. Most models carry the record to natural causes and barely at all to spiritual ones; one model transfers it weakly to either. Telling the model that the reliable informant forecasts by spiritual guidance rather than by measurement lowers that informant's influence on natural claims in every model and produces no comparable gain on spiritual claims in any. Demonstrated reliability thus has a scope in these models, and the account a source gives of how they came to be reliable can narrow it. Whether this reflects a judgment about relevance or the low prior credence of spiritual claims is the question the design leaves open, and we outline the experiment that would settle it.

</details>


### [239] [Mind the Divergence: Divergence-Guided Reliability-Aware Fusion and Adaptive LLM Prompting for Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08860)
*Jingwen Wang,Jiao Li,Yuhang Qian,Yuyin Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MiD-ERC，以单模态预测分布间的归一化散度刻画模态间预测分歧，并据此协调跨模态门控、可靠性加权与LLM提示，实现对话级多模态情绪识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话多模态情绪识别（MERC）方法未显式地按"模态间预测分歧程度"自适应地调整多模态处理。真实对话中语义不一致、情绪隐藏、观测噪声与歧义对不同模态情感证据的影响各异，且各模态可靠性随样本变化，导致无差别融合易传播不可靠或相互竞争的线索。

Method: 受Brunswik透镜模型启发，将各类不一致来源统一建模为"同一情绪标签集上各单模态情绪分布间的预测分歧"；用归一化散度分数量化该分歧并估计样本级模态可靠性；以这两个信号驱动三项机制：分歧引导的跨模态门控、分歧调制的可靠性加权、分歧条件的自适应LLM提示；端到端训练，仅使用标准情绪标注，无需冲突标签、辅助数据构造或外部检索模块。

Result: 在IEMOCAP与MELD上取得state-of-the-art性能，且在高分歧样本上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 以"预测分歧"这一统一视角替代逐源识别不一致，可作为样本自适应的MERC融合范式，兼顾性能与数据/标注成本。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition in conversations (MERC) integrates textual, acoustic, and visual cues to identify utterance-level emotions. However, existing approaches generally do not explicitly adapt multimodal processing to the degree of inter-modal predictive disagreement. In real conversations, semantic incongruity, concealed emotion, noisy observations, and ambiguity can affect modality-specific emotional evidence differently, while the reliability of individual modalities varies across samples. Rather than identifying each source of inconsistency separately, we model their common consequence for emotion recognition as predictive disagreement among unimodal emotion distributions over the same emotion-label set. Such disagreement makes indiscriminate fusion prone to propagating unreliable or competing evidence. Inspired by the Brunswik Lens Model, we propose MiD-ERC, a divergence-centric framework that quantifies inter-modal predictive disagreement using a normalized divergence score and estimates sample-specific modality reliability. MiD-ERC uses these signals to coordinate divergence-guided cross-modal gating, divergence-modulated reliability weighting, and divergence-conditioned adaptive LLM prompting. The framework is trained end-to-end using only standard emotion annotations, without requiring additional conflict labels, auxiliary data construction, or external retrieval modules. Experiments on IEMOCAP and MELD demonstrate that MiD-ERC achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly pronounced improvements on high-divergence samples.

</details>


### [240] [Rethinking Agent Trajectory Compression: A Workflow-Aware Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08865)
*Qijia Zhuang,Rui Liu,Wenqi Hu,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出工作流感知的智能体轨迹压缩框架WAAC，通过结构化历史成功轨迹提取可复用压缩经验并结合在线后向依赖分析动态确定信息保留需求，实现高效轨迹压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体依赖多步工具交互完成复杂任务，执行轨迹持续累积结构化输出与中间产物，导致上下文开销不断增长；现有压缩方法多依赖文本重要性或固定截断规则，忽视工作流中的跨步依赖，可能丢弃后续任务执行所需的关键信息。

Method: WAAC结构化建模历史成功轨迹以提取可复用的工作流压缩经验，并引入在线后向依赖分析动态识别信息保留需求；将当前依赖约束与历史经验结合，为不同类型的历史信息选择恰当的压缩策略。

Result: 在PinchBench与Odysseys上，WAAC分别取得87.6的总体得分与19.0%的完美完成率，同时相较最强基线方法执行成本分别降低19.7%与29.1%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了工作流感知的轨迹压缩在提升长程智能体任务性能与成本效率方面的有效性。代码与资源已公开。

Abstract: As long-horizon agents increasingly rely on multi-step tool interactions to accomplish complex tasks, their execution trajectories continuously accumulate structured outputs and intermediate artifacts, leading to growing context overhead. Existing compression methods primarily rely on text importance or fixed truncation rules, often overlooking cross-step dependencies within workflows and potentially discarding critical information required for subsequent task execution. To address this challenge, we propose WAAC, a workflow-aware framework for agent trajectory compression. WAAC structurally models successful historical trajectories to extract reusable workflow compression experience and incorporates online backward dependency analysis to dynamically identify information retention requirements. By combining current dependency constraints with historical experience, WAAC selects appropriate compression strategies for different types of historical information. Experiments on PinchBench and Odysseys demonstrate that WAAC achieves an overall score of 87.6 and a perfect completion rate of 19.0%, respectively, while reducing execution costs by 19.7% and 29.1% compared with the strongest baseline methods. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of workflow-aware trajectory compression in improving task performance and cost efficiency for long-horizon agents. Code and resources are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/waac-6527/.

</details>


### [241] [Toward LLM Self-Play Under Data Scarcity: Effectiveness, Generalizability, and Trustworthiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08908)
*Zhiyin Yu,Yuchen Mou,Bingzhe Wu,Zhonghai Wu,Shiyang Feng,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张真实世界的LLM自我博弈系统应在数据稀缺条件下同时实现有效性、泛化性与可信性，并据此提出三条"蓝天"研究方向：以数据为中心的自我博弈、面向开放世界与不可验证任务的泛化性自我博弈、以及面向可靠自我进化的可信自我博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM发展依赖模型规模扩展与人工标注数据，但面临数据稀缺与人类监督收益递减的双重约束；自我博弈虽可实现自主数据生成与自我提升，但现有框架缺乏以数据为中心的视角、跨域泛化能力有限，且可信性与安全性问题日益突出。

Method: 采用立场/展望（position/blue-sky）论文的论证范式：先界定数据稀缺下自我博弈系统应满足的三大目标（有效性、泛化性、可信性），再对应提出三条研究方向，并在每一方向下讨论具体研究机会与潜在解决方案。

Result: 本文为观点性论文，未报告实验或定量结果；其主要"结果"是构建了一个由三大目标与三条研究方向构成的分析框架，用于系统化梳理自我博弈的研究缺口与可行路径。

Conclusion: 通过自我博弈架构将有限的人类知识转化为可无限扩展的高质量经验，这一范式转变有望建立可持续且安全的AI生态；未来自我博弈系统需在数据稀缺约束下兼顾有效性、泛化性与可信性。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has been driven largely by scaling model size and human-curated training data, yet this paradigm is increasingly constrained by data scarcity and diminishing returns from human supervision. Self-play offers a promising pathway for autonomous data generation and improvement, but current frameworks remain unsatisfactory due to a lack of a data-centric perspective, limited cross-domain generalization, and growing trustworthiness and security concerns. In this paper, we argue that real-world LLM self-play systems should achieve effectiveness, generalizability, and trustworthiness under data scarcity. To address these challenges, we outline three blue-sky research directions for future self-play systems: (1) data-centric self-play for effective experience generation, (2) generalizable self-play for open-world and unverifiable tasks, and (3) trustworthy self-play for reliable self-evolution. We further discuss research opportunities and solutions within each direction. This paradigm shift will establish a sustainable and safe AI ecosystem by transforming finite human knowledge into inexhaustible high-quality experience through self-play architectures.

</details>


### [242] [Frame: Form-Restricted Authoring with Model-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08921)
*Nan Jiang,Mohammed Mohsin Hussain,Onkar Tiwari,Ankit Jain,Thong Q. Nguyen,Abhishek Agarwal,Vishal Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"形式受限报告生成"(form-restricted report generation)任务，并构建FRAME基准(120个EDGAR与arXiv实例，分四类)，用三项人工验证的指标评估；结果显示开源模型在结构上接近闭源模型，但在覆盖度与忠实度上落后，且长输出与复杂依据会降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业报告常复用旧版结构、只更新内容，但现有生成研究未显式建模"保留样本形式 + 反映新证据"这一约束，缺乏相应任务定义与评测基准。

Method: 形式化任务为form-restricted report generation：输入样例报告与新依据文档，输出保持样例形式且准确反映新证据的报告。构建FRAME基准，含120个EDGAR与arXiv来源实例，覆盖Financial Quarterly、Financial Annual、Academic Simple、Academic Complex四类，采用时间三快照(temporal three-snapshot)流水线构造。评估用三项经人工验证的指标：基于语义树编辑距离的结构相似度、基于信息单元召回率的覆盖度、基于论断级依据核验(claim-level grounding verification)的忠实度。在五个LLM上进行评测。

Result: 五个LLM中，开源模型在结构相似度上几乎与专有模型持平，但在覆盖度与忠实度上明显落后；所有模型在金融类报告上表现优于学术类；输出越长、依据越复杂，性能越差。

Conclusion: "形式保留+内容更新"的报告生成是一个有区分度的新评测问题；结构复现相对易得，而内容覆盖与事实忠实仍是主要瓶颈，且随输出长度与依据复杂度增加而恶化，后续需在长文覆盖与依据对齐上改进。

Abstract: Many professional reports reuse the structure of prior editions while updating the content. We formalize this setting as form-restricted report generation: given a sample report and new grounding documents, generate a report that preserves the sample's form while accurately reflecting the new evidence. We introduce FRAME, a benchmark of 120 EDGAR- and arXiv-derived instances across Financial Quarterly, Financial Annual, Academic Simple, and Academic Complex categories, built with a temporal three-snapshot pipeline. We evaluate outputs with three human-validated metrics: structural similarity via semantic tree-edit distance, coverage via information unit recall, and faithfulness via claim-level grounding verification. Across five LLMs, open-source models nearly match proprietary models on structure but lag on coverage and faithfulness; all models perform better on financial than academic reports, and performance declines with longer outputs and more complex grounding.

</details>


### [243] [Glyph: An Interpretability Engine for AI-Driven Scientific Discovery in Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08922)
*Robbie Holland,Ashwin Kumar,Mateus A. Esmeraldo,David Le,Andrea Fisher,Jiazhen Pan,Louisa Fay,Magdalini Paschali,Vasiliki Sideri-Lampretsa,Ates Fettahoglu,Stefania Moroianu,Philipp Wesp,Ivan Lopez,Tom Nuno Wolf,McKenzie S. White,Eduardo Pontes Reis,Fatima Rodriguez,Curtis P. Langlotz,Vasilis Syrgkanis,Imon Banerjee,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Sergios Gatidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Glyph，一个多阶段自动化框架，利用基础模型与稀疏自编码器从多模态非结构化临床数据中提取潜在特征，将其解释为可编码的临床概念，并评估其用于临床科学发现的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体规模下人工标注不可行，现有方法依赖预定义表型，从而限定了可发现的关联范围；智能体系统调用这些工具同样受限。基础模型可超越训练目标完成任务，其潜在嵌入或蕴含新特征，但难以解释。

Method: 多阶段框架：以基础模型提取多模态非结构化临床数据的嵌入，用稀疏自编码器解耦为稀疏特征，将特征解释并映射为编码化临床概念，再系统评估其对临床科学发现的价值。

Result: 应用于跨机构 41,628 例患者的腹部 CT 影像、CT 放射报告及实验室检查，识别出 397 个具预测性的概念，由 4,463 个经 Bonferroni 校正显著的关联支持；多数关联在跨站点留出队列中可自动复现，且与文献来源标志物呈互补关联，提升预测效能。

Conclusion: 可解释性可从对神经网络的机制性理解，延伸至将隐式学到的特征与临床知识相连，并确立其对患者结局的意义。代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated scientific discovery has the potential to advance understanding of disease mechanisms and accelerate identification of early disease markers. Manual data annotation is infeasible at population scale, motivating methods that automatically extract pre-defined phenotypes in multimodal and unstructured clinical data. Such pre-defined feature sets determine and limit which associations these approaches can discover. This limitation extends to agentic AI systems that use these tools to query population-scale data. In contrast, foundation models (FMs) trained on large volumes of clinical data can perform tasks beyond their training objectives. This suggests their latent embeddings may represent novel or unexplored features that could yield insights into disease mechanisms, yet such latent features remain difficult to interpret.
