To address this challenge, we introduce Glyph, an automated multistage framework that extracts features from multimodal and unstructured clinical data using FMs and sparse autoencoders, interprets them as coded clinical concepts, and evaluates their value for clinical scientific discovery. We applied Glyph to abdominal CT volumes, CT radiology reports and laboratory measurements from 41,628 patients across different institutions. We identified 397 predictive concepts supported by 4,463 Bonferroni-significant associations. Moreover, a majority of discovered associations were automatically reproducible in held-out cohorts across different sites. Glyph concepts provided complementary associations to literature-derived markers, leading to gains in predictive power. We envision that interpretability can extend beyond a mechanistic understanding of neural networks to connecting implicitly learned features with clinical knowledge and establishing their significance for patient outcomes. We release Glyph at https://github.com/StanfordMIMI/Glyph.

</details>


### [244] [Flex-RSI: Benchmarking Recursive Self-Improvement of Frontier Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08946)
*Jingdong Zhang,Jionghao Wang,Zhengming Yu,Yancheng Cai,Wei Chow,Chris Choy,Zhiyang Dou,Hang Hua,Jiachen Li,Zhixuan Liu,Chen Wang,Songlin Wei,Siyuan Yang,Yu Yuan,Xiaohang Zhan,Yucheng Zhang,Shuhong Zheng,Xin Li,Lingjie Liu,Wojciech Matusik,Zhengzhong Tu,Wenping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Flex-RSI 基准，在固定预算下考察智能体对"工具链（harness）"本身的递归自我改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常固定智能体的 harness 只评估其产出的解，或聚焦大规模模型训练；缺少对"智能体能否利用任务反馈改进自身工作方式（而非仅改进答案）"的系统评测。

Method: 构建 Flex-RSI 基准：每轮中执行器改进任务解，仅当在留出验证集上得分更高才保留；改进器随后修订 harness（含执行器与改进器自身）。所有运行均从同一通用 harness 出发，最终解在隐藏测试集上评分。任务覆盖感知、推理、机器人动作、3D 重建、视觉创作与基础设施；评测闭源与开放权重模型。

Result: 自我改进在智能体自行构建解时收益最大；仅记录成功/失败的反馈会限制开放权重模型表现；开放权重模型在需通过物理稳定性或精确正确性严格检验的输出上落后最多；配对实验显示修订 harness 本身对增益有贡献。

Conclusion: 智能体的自我改进关键在于对 harness 的递归编辑能力，且反馈粒度与严格校验是当前开放权重模型的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Frontier language models are increasingly deployed as agents, and how well an agent performs depends not only on its model but also on its harness: the prompts, tools, memory, and control loop through which the model acts. When an agent is applied to a new problem, a natural question is whether it can use the feedback of the task to improve the way it works, and not merely its answer. Most existing benchmarks keep the agent's harness fixed and score the solution it produces, or target large-scale model training. We introduce Flex-RSI, a benchmark for the recursive self-improvement of agents on vision and robotics tasks under a fixed budget. In each round, an executor improves the task solution, which is kept only if it scores higher on held-out validation data, and an improver then revises the harness, including the executor and itself. Every run starts from the same general-purpose harness, and the final solution is scored on hidden test data. Evaluating closed-source and open-weight models on tasks spanning perception, reasoning, robot action, 3D reconstruction, visual creation, and infrastructure, we find that self-improvement helps most when the agent builds its own solution, that feedback recording only success or failure holds back open-weight models, and that open-weight models fall furthest behind where outputs must pass strict checks of physical stability or exact correctness. Paired runs further show that editing the harness itself contributes to the gains.

</details>


### [245] [PhysioWave-T: Token-Efficient Wavelet Foundation Models for Biosignals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08969)
*Yanlong Chen,Simone Benatti,Luca Benini,Cuntai Guan,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PhysioWave-T，一个小波域token高效生理信号基础模型族（EEG/ECG/sEMG），通过动态尺度折叠将序列长度从(J+1)CS压缩到CS，并以可靠性感知隐变量融合（RALF）整合多模态编码器，在EEG、sEMG、ECG多项基准上取得领先或SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理信号（EEG/ECG/sEMG）存在多导联、多采样率、多蒙太奇差异，小波域建模通常需展开J+1个子带导致token序列过长、Transformer延迟与显存开销大；同时单一模态模型难以覆盖不同佩戴配置，且现有多模态融合多为加权logit或拼接，缺乏可靠性自适应。

Method: 1) 动态尺度折叠：在token化前重组合J+1个小波子带，将序列长度由(J+1)CS降为CS。2) 路由专用前端+可学习通道名嵌入，使每个模态单个Transformer适配多种蒙太奇与采样率；未见配置通过轻量适配器复用骨干。3) 分别预训练EEG（30M参数，>15M窗口，19–128通道）、ECG（30M）、sEMG（12M）编码器。4) RALF：以样本自适应权重融合独立预训练编码器的summary token。

Result: EEG四条路由上，尺度折叠相比尺度展开降低Transformer延迟4.0–7.2倍、峰值显存6.8–12.7倍且无精度损失；EEG编码器在9项指标中8项优于BENDR、BIOT、LaBraM、EEGPT，并在可穿戴癫痫检测上以更少参数超过LUNA-L。SeizeIT2上RALF将11次误报/小时下的事件敏感度由61.4%提升至74.4%，窗口AUPRC由0.321升至0.399，优于加权logit与拼接融合。sEMG达SOTA，PTB-XL Super与Chapman-Shaoxing取得最高macro-F1。

Conclusion: 尺度折叠在token化前压缩小波子带可大幅降低计算与显存成本而不损精度；单骨干+路由前端与通道名嵌入有效提升跨蒙太奇/采样率泛化；RALF为多模态生理信号提供优于朴素融合的可靠集成方案，PhysioWave-T构成统一、高效、可扩展的生理信号基础模型族。

Abstract: We present PhysioWave-T, a token-efficient wavelet foundation model family for EEG, ECG, and sEMG. Dynamic scale folding recombines J+1 wavelet subbands prior to tokenization, compressing sequence length from (J+1)CS to CS tokens for J levels, C channels, and S temporal patches. Route-specific front ends and learned channel-name embeddings allow one Transformer per modality to handle diverse montages and sampling rates, while unseen setups reuse the backbone via lightweight adapters. We pre-train a 30M-parameter EEG model on over 15M windows (19--128 channels), alongside ECG (30M) and sEMG (12M) encoders. Across four EEG routes, scale folding cuts Transformer latency by 4.0--7.2x and peak memory by 6.8--12.7x over scale expansion without accuracy loss. Our EEG encoder leads 8 of 9 metrics against BENDR, BIOT, LaBraM, and EEGPT, and surpasses LUNA-L on wearable seizure detection with fewer parameters. Reliability-aware latent fusion (RALF) further integrates summary tokens from independently pre-trained encoders via sample-adaptive weights. On SeizeIT2, RALF raises event sensitivity at 11 false alarms/h from 61.4% to 74.4% and window AUPRC from 0.321 to 0.399 over the best unimodal baseline, outperforming weighted-logit and concatenation fusion across all variants. PhysioWave-T also achieves state-of-the-art sEMG accuracies and top macro-F1 on PTB-XL Super and Chapman-Shaoxing. Code and models: https://github.com/ForeverBlue816/PhysioWave.

</details>


### [246] [Rethinking Optimization Methods for Hierarchical Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08991)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对HTNL（层次任务数值合取特征学习）的四类优化方法（MM、VQF、ADMM、WGL）进行统一诊断与比较，提出ADMM-Bisect改进，并通过合成与真实任务实验验证VQF在AUC与支持集紧凑性上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有事件预测依赖可解释的合取特征（关键词×地点×时间线索联合出现）。HTNL通过结构化稀疏目标选出数值合取特征（NCFs），但其优化问题求解困难，优化设计直接决定实际性能，因此需要系统厘清不同优化族的差异与优劣。

Method: 统一分析四种HTNL优化族：原始majorization-minimization（MM）、变分二次型（VQF）、ADMM与加权组lasso（WGL）。识别出三个关键因素：(1) 在匹配的变分更新下VQF与MM代数等价，均优化平方惩罚形式；(2) ADMM通过共识分裂优化非平方目标，将其截断FISTA近端求解器替换为标量二分法更新（ADMM-Bisect），以更准确处理近阈值块；(3) WGL同样针对非平方目标，且各方法都面临活跃集准入分数快速衰减的问题。

Result: 在两个合成数据集与11个任务（civil-unrest与流感监测）上，VQF取得最高平均AUC，且支持集最紧凑、不发生坍缩；ADMM-Bisect降低了实测运行时间；经尺度校准的WGL避免了原始版本的空支持集失败。合成实验进一步揭示近端精度、剪枝与搜索预算如何影响候选恢复，尤其对较长合取更为明显。

Conclusion: HTNL优化行为由三项因素共同决定：等价性与惩罚形式、近端求解精度、活跃集准入机制。实践中推荐VQF作为精度与支持集质量的首选，ADMM-Bisect用于效率提升，WGL需尺度校准后方可用；代码将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Rare-event forecasting often depends on interpretable conjunctions: a single keyword, location, or temporal cue may be weak, while their joint occurrence can identify a meaningful precursor. Hierarchical-Task Numerical conjunctive feature Learning (HTNL) selects Numerical Conjunctive Features (NCFs) through a structured sparsity objective. However, the resulting optimization problem is challenging, making optimization design critical to its practical performance. We give a unified diagnosis of four HTNL optimization families: original majorization-minimization (MM), variational quadratic form (VQF), alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), and weighted group lasso (WGL). Our analysis distinguishes three key factors that jointly determine HTNL optimization behavior. First, VQF and MM are algebraically equivalent under matched variational updates and optimize a squared-penalty formulation. Second, ADMM optimizes the unsquared objective through consensus splitting; we replace its truncated FISTA proximal solver with a scalar bisection update (ADMM-Bisect) for more accurate treatment of near-threshold blocks. Third, WGL targets the same unsquared objective, while all methods face rapidly decaying active-set admission scores. Across two synthetic datasets and 11 civil-unrest and flu-surveillance tasks, VQF achieves the highest mean AUC with the most compact non-collapsing supports. ADMM-Bisect reduces measured runtime, while calibrated WGL avoids the empty-support failures of its original scale. Synthetic results further show how proximal accuracy, pruning, and search budget affect candidate recovery, especially for longer conjunctions. Code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [247] [SIMBAD: Spatio-Temporal Traffic Forecasting Robust to Aperiodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09015)
*Daniel Y. Lee,Seungwon Shin,Seunghoon Han,Sungsu Lim,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SIMBAD，通过自适应调节周期信号与动态空间建模两项机制，提升时空图神经网络对非周期（突发事件引起）交通波动的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的交通预测对城市规划至关重要，但突发事件导致的不规则模式使问题依然困难。现有 STGNN 擅长建模周期性数据，却难以应对这类非周期波动，泛化能力不足。

Method: SIMBAD 包含两个关键机制：(1) 对周期信号进行自适应调节，优先关注近期信号；(2) 动态空间建模，依据上下文关系调整相连节点之间的影响强度。上述机制建立在简单的相似度度量之上，以适配非周期模式。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上优于现有方法，在预测未见过的、不规则的交通事件方面表现更佳。

Conclusion: 以轻量相似度度量为核心的自适应周期调节与动态空间建模，可有效增强 STGNN 对非周期交通事件的泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate traffic prediction is essential for urban planning but remains challenging due to the irregular patterns caused by unexpected events. While Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (STGNNs) effectively model periodic data, they often struggle with these aperiodic fluctuations. To address this, we propose SIMBAD, which improves generalization irregular patterns via two key mechanisms: 1) adaptive regulation of periodic signals to prioritize recent signals, and 2) dynamic spatial modeling that adjusts influence between connected nodes based on contextual relations. Built upon simple similarity metrics to adapt to aperiodic patterns, SIMBAD outperforms existing methods on real-world benchmark datasets, demonstrating superior performance in predicting unseen and irregular traffic events.

</details>


### [248] [Scout Before You Route: Predicting Task Success and Token Count from a Small LLM Prefill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09028)
*Aristides Milios,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究"按查询预测成本"的LLM路由器：用单个小型冻结模型（scout）的prefill激活同时预测候选模型的成功概率与输出长度，在推理模型候选上以中位长度定价为基线可节省9–33%（匹配准确率下），成本显著低于ZeroRouter等已有估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由器以固定成本对模型定价，假设每个查询的输出token数相同；但推理模型的输出长度在不同查询间相差达一个数量级，导致固定定价与实际开销严重失配。文章要回答三个问题：何时值得做逐查询成本预测、成本由什么因素驱动、预测能多便宜地实现。

Method: 利用一个小型冻结LLM（scout）的prefill激活，同时以探针预测每个候选模型的成功概率与输出长度；将预测成本与完美成本知识、中位长度定价对齐比较，并复现/改造ZeroRouter与MixLLM风格预测器作为对照；进一步分析scout规模、标注量（20个标注问题）与跨基准迁移性。

Result: 在九个数据集中的八个上，完美逐查询成本知识可节省29–61%开销，而非推理候选节省明显更少；这八个数据集上预测成本在中位长度定价基础上节省9–33%（匹配准确率），达到完美知识的最高69%。在多数数据集上显著便宜于复现的估计器（ZeroRouter、MixLLM式预测器）。输出长度主要由问题本身决定；数学/代码数据集中难题更长，仅用预测成功概率即可近似定价，而知识类与混合格式数据集中二者不相关，长度探针明显更优。探针仅需20个标注问题即保留大部分收益，并在7个新基准中的4个上无需标注即可迁移。在九个基准混合上比各自中位长度定价再省22%。scout约2B时收益饱和，超过4B得不偿失。

Conclusion: 逐查询成本预测对推理模型路由具有实质价值，且可由极小scout的低成本探针实现；是否需要预测成本取决于任务类型（数学/代码中成功概率可代理成本，知识/混合格式则需专门的长度探针），并存在明确的最优scout规模（约2B）。

Abstract: Routers typically choose a model per query by trading predicted correctness against cost. Most price each model at a fixed cost, as if every query used the same number of output tokens, but the output length of reasoning models varies by an order of magnitude across queries. When is per-query cost worth predicting? What factors drive cost? How cheaply can predictions be made? We predict both the success probability and the output length of candidate models from the prefill activations of a single small frozen LLM, the scout. With reasoning candidates, perfect per-query cost knowledge would save 29-61% of spending on eight of nine datasets, and much less with non-reasoning candidates on the same problems. On those eight datasets, our predicted costs save 9-33% at matched accuracy relative to median-length pricing, up to 69% of what perfect knowledge would save. They are significantly cheaper than each estimator we reimplemented or adapted from published work, including ZeroRouter and a MixLLM-style predictor, on a majority of datasets. Output length depends mostly on the problem. On most math and code datasets, harder problems are longer, so predicted success alone prices cost nearly as well. On knowledge and mixed-format datasets they are not, and predicted success falls well behind a probe trained on length. The probe keeps most of its saving with 20 labelled problems and transfers without labels to four of seven new benchmarks. On a mixture of all nine benchmarks, it saves 22% beyond pricing each benchmark at its own median length. Savings stop growing at about a 2B scout, so above 4B a larger scout costs more than it adds.

</details>


### [249] [CARGO: Causal Attribution and Responsibility-Guided Preventive Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09139)
*Jingjia Teng,Yang Li,Wenjie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CARGO——一个面向混合交通协同汇入的多智能体强化学习框架，通过因果归因与责任引导优化提升成功率并降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通中匝道协同汇入需在多智能体间进行可靠的贡献归因与风险责任分配，以支撑合作奖励塑形与预防性安全优化，现有MARL方法缺乏显式因果归因与责任量化机制。

Method: 构建共享潜在上下文后验与动作条件响应模型以估计有向行为影响，并将后验不确定性传播到交互边选择；采用互补归因机制将带符号影响与边际团队效用耦合用于合作奖励塑形，并基于相对安全参考策略的正向超额尾部风险分配责任；使用条件扩散生成未来响应以进行动作相关风险评估；责任引导的预防性优化在策略信任域内按局部容量校准各智能体降险需求，含预留安全裕度、自适应需求缩放与联合可行性验证。

Result: 理论分析在明确假设下建立风险单调性与环境回报非递减的局部充分条件；highway-env实验中，三种交通密度下均有验证，图恢复与归因对照实验支持框架有效性；高密度交通下成功率较最优基线提升66.04%，碰撞率降低56.23%。

Conclusion: CARGO将因果归因与责任引导结合到多智能体策略优化中，兼顾合作效率与安全约束，具备理论保证与实验验证，可作为混合交通协同驾驶的安全强化学习方案。

Abstract: This study presents Causal Attribution and Responsibility-Guided Optimization (CARGO), a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for cooperative on-ramp merging in mixed traffic. A shared latent-context posterior and an action-conditioned response model estimate directed behavioral influence, with posterior uncertainty propagated into interaction-edge selection. Complementary attribution mechanisms couple signed influence with marginal team utility for cooperative reward shaping and assign responsibility from positive excess tail risk relative to a safe reference policy. Conditional diffusion generates future responses for action-dependent risk estimation. Responsibility-guided preventive optimization calibrates agent-specific risk-reduction requirements to local capacity within a policy trust region, using reserved safety margins, adaptive requirement scaling, and joint feasibility verification. Theoretical analysis establishes risk monotonicity and sufficient local conditions for non-decreasing environmental return under explicit assumptions. Experiments in highway-env across three traffic densities, together with controlled graph-recovery and attribution studies, support the effectiveness of the framework. Under high-density traffic, CARGO increases the success rate by 66.04\% and reduces the crash rate by 56.23\% relative to the best evaluated baseline for each metric.

</details>


### [250] [BMMS: Policy-Conditioned Measurement of Behavioral Compliance in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09147)
*Anthony Rhodes,Gadi Singer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出行为错位度量套件（BMMS），以政策语料为输入、输出合规画像，包含CAS、CRM、BIV、BDI四个指标，并用"归因性准入检验"筛选指标；在1,003条义务上验证了抽取保真度与跨裁判稳健性，并揭示GPT-4o对其自身发布的Model Spec硬约束失败率达70.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型行为评测的事前固定维度（幻觉、谄媚、毒性、过度拒答）无法回答受监管行业操作者的核心问题：系统是否满足某一特定规则手册的要求。因此需要一种以政策为条件、可面向任意已发布政策与任意自然语言应答系统进行度量的错位评测框架。

Method: 提出BMMS套件：以政策语料为参数，返回覆盖该语料的合规画像。核心指标CAS为对约束系统的义务的严重度加权达成度；CRM为在分级对抗压力下仍存活的达成度份额；同时规定并发布BIV与BDI。维度纳入须通过公开的准入检验，其约束性判据为归因性——若固定被测对象而仅更换裁判即导致数值变动，则该量测的是工具而非对象。

Result: 在来自五类登记册、十一份文件的1,003条义务上：准入判据对供应商规格的盲测人类判断达94.4%精确率，对分析性指南为50.0%；抽取保真度94.6%；更换两家其他供应商的裁判重打分后，目标排序保持不变，但单一目标的绝对分数跨度可达0.22。准入检验还剔除了套件自身曾报告的一个量。应用于两份最详细公开行为规范时，扣除文档难度后自规范优势未能显著偏离零，但绝对自合规性差异明显：GPT-4o在其Model Spec所约束的硬性要求上失败率70.7%，两个Claude模型分别为0.0%与9.1%。

Conclusion: 政策条件化的合规度量可行且必要；归因性准入检验能有效筛除"测仪器而非测对象"的伪指标，且指标在跨裁判下具备排序稳健性但绝对分数不稳定。相对比较（自规范优势）证据不足，而绝对自合规水平存在显著差异，说明行为规范的对齐不能由"擅长自身规范"来推定。

Abstract: Behavioral evaluation of language models fixes its dimensions in advance: hallucination, sycophancy, toxicity, over-refusal. An operator in a regulated industry needs a different question answered: does a system do what a particular rulebook requires? We introduce the Behavioral Misalignment Metric Suite (BMMS), which measures policy-conditioned misalignment by taking a policy corpus as an argument and returning a compliance profile over it, for any published policy and any system that answers in natural language. The Compliance Attainment Score (CAS) measures severity-weighted attainment over the obligations that bind the system, and the Compliance Robustness Margin (CRM) the share of that attainment surviving graded adversarial pressure; Behavioral Invariance (BIV) and the Behavioral Disposition Index (BDI) are specified and released with the suite. Dimensions enter only by passing a published admission test whose binding criterion is attribution: a quantity that moves when the judge changes, holding the target fixed, is measuring the instrument. Applying it excluded one of the quantities the suite itself reported. Across 1,003 obligations from eleven documents in five registers, the admission criterion holds 94.4% precision against blinded human judgment on vendor specifications and 50.0% on analytical guidance, extraction fidelity is 94.6%, and re-scoring under judges from two other providers leaves target ordering invariant while the absolute score of a single target spans up to 0.22. We apply it to the two most detailed published behavioral specifications. Own-specification advantage is not resolved from zero once document difficulty is subtracted, but absolute self-compliance is another matter: GPT-4o fails 70.7% of the hard constraints binding it in the Model Spec published by OpenAI, where the two Claude models fail 0.0% and 9.1% of theirs.

</details>


### [251] [Learning When to Think: Dual-Reference Offline Optimization for Adaptive VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09154)
*Hongbin Lin,Sizhe Zou,Juangui Xu,Xinyue Xu,Zhuoli Ouyang,Stanislav Kolchin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双参考 BOLT（DR-BOLT），在离线奖励加权训练中区分"期望的快/慢混合参考分布"与"采样分布"并校正二者失配，使 VLM 能自适应地在简单输入上直接作答、在需要时逐步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强制所有输入走长推理链会导致过度思考与不必要的推理开销；而快答与慢推理轨迹往往来自不同的行为策略，单参考离线目标会对其中某一模式加权失当，因此需要显式建模快/慢混合目标分布。

Method: 提出 DR-BOLT：将期望的快/慢混合参考分布与采样分布区分开，在奖励加权训练中做源级（source-wise）校正，近似精确目标；并以快/慢分区的竞争关系解释最优目标中的路由行为，该近似在回答格式可良好区分时成立。实验采用配对 Qwen3-VL 比较，使用相同候选池以隔离目标函数与慢教师质量的影响。

Result: 在 ScienceQA 及三个留出迁移基准上，DR-BOLT 相比所评估的离线与在线基线改善了分数—长度权衡；跨基准慢推理比例为 24.1%，端到端训练相比复现的 GRPO 加速 2.90 倍。

Conclusion: 结果支持在所研究的双参考设定下实现自适应推理；更广泛的稳健性仍有待验证。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reasoning traces improve vision-language models on complex multimodal tasks, but forcing every input to follow a long reasoning process leads to overthinking and unnecessary inference cost. We study adaptive fast/slow thinking for VLMs, where one model answers directly for simple inputs and reasons step by step when needed. Fast answers and slow traces often come from distinct behavior policies, making a single-reference offline objective misweight one mode. We propose DUAL-REFERENCE BOLT (DR-BOLT), which distinguishes the desired fast/slow mixture reference from the sampling distribution and corrects their mismatch in reward-weighted training. Fast/slow partition competition explains routing in the optimal target; an efficient source-wise correction approximates the exact objective when response formats are well separated. Matched Qwen3-VL comparisons use identical candidate banks to isolate the objective from slow-teacher quality. DR-BOLT improves score-length trade-offs over the evaluated offline and online baselines on ScienceQA and three held-out transfer benchmarks, with a 24.1% cross-benchmark slow ratio and a 2.90-fold end-to-end training speedup over reproduced GRPO. These results support adaptive reasoning in the studied two-reference setting, while broader robustness remains open. Our code is available at https://github.com/HongbinLin3589/DR-BOLT.

</details>


### [252] [Evaluation of a Multi-Link Machine Learning Approach for Rainfall Detection Using Satellite Microwave Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09156)
*Mostafa Ftouni,Margherita Spalla,Matteo Colli,Andrea Randazzo,Christian Gianoglio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用卫星微波链路（SML）机会性传感网络结合随机森林（RF）与卷积神经网络（CNN）进行降雨检测，结果表明融合多链路信息的聚合数据显著优于单链路方法，且RF在稳定性与可靠性上优于CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化影响加剧，对可靠的实时降雨监测系统需求上升；传统雨量计与气象雷达在覆盖与实时性上存在局限，需探索低成本、可扩展的替代监测手段。

Method: 基于卫星微波链路的机会性传感网络，对聚合后的SML数据进行处理，并纳入相邻链路的信息；分别训练随机森林（RF）与卷积神经网络（CNN）两类机器学习分类器用于湿/干期判别。

Result: 利用多链路信息相比单链路方法显著提升湿期与干期的检测效果；RF相比CNN提供更稳定、更可靠的权衡，整体提升降雨检测性能并减少误分类，在恶劣天气条件下表现尤为突出。研究覆盖2021至2024年间最多32个降雨日。

Conclusion: 多链路聚合的SML数据结合机器学习可有效提升降雨检测性能，RF模型因稳定性与可靠性更优而更具应用潜力；但实验样本规模有限（最多32个降雨日），结论的泛化性仍需在更长时段与更多区域上进一步验证。

Abstract: The impacts of climate change have increased the need for reliable real-time rainfall monitoring systems. This study investigates rainfall detection using a network of opportunistic sensors based on satellite microwave links (SMLs) combined with machine learning (ML) classifiers. Two models, a random forest (RF) and a convolutional neural network (CNN), were trained on aggregated SML data, including information from neighboring links. The results demonstrate that leveraging information from multiple links substantially enhances the detection of both wet and dry periods compared to single-link approaches. Moreover, the RF model consistently provides a more stable and reliable trade-off than CNN, improving rainfall detection and reducing misclassifications overall, with particularly strong performance during harsh weather conditions. The study spanned up to 32 rainy days between 2021 and 2024.

</details>


### [253] [TailDART: Compute-Efficient Foundation Models for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09176)
*Chiang Qin Zhi,Ranjan Satapathy,Erik Cambria,Joyjit Chattoraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TailDART：面向量化金融的轻量级（268万参数）时序基础模型，通过分组查询注意力降低KV缓存带宽开销，实现最高48.1%推理加速，并在多资产、多时间尺度基准上以紧凑规模达到与大型基线相当的预测精度，且在高波动区间误差鲁棒性更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在高信息密度物理系统中零样本迁移能力强，但直接用于量化金融受限于低信噪比、非平稳性以及自回归解码延迟过高；需在表征学习能力与机构级运行约束（低延迟、高吞吐）之间取得平衡。

Method: 构建密集型轻量基础模型TailDART（2.68M参数）；在轻量骨干网络中引入分组查询注意力（GQA），以削减Key-Value缓存的内存带宽开销；面向金融时序预测进行架构与推理路径优化，支持流式低延迟执行。

Result: 相较主流SOTA TSFMs实现最高48.1%的推理加速；跨不同时间分辨率的多资产基准显示，紧凑模型在预测保真度上与显著更大的基线相当，并在高波动市况下表现出更优的误差韧性。

Conclusion: 在金融场景中，"小模型+高效注意力"可替代过度参数化TSFM，兼顾预测精度与运营可行性；GQA驱动的KV缓存优化是降低自回归解码延迟的有效路径，紧凑模型在高噪声、非平稳市场环境中具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Although time-series foundation models (TSFMs) achieve strong zero-shot transferability across high-information-density physical systems, applying overparameterized TSFMs to quantitative finance is fundamentally constrained by low signal-to-noise ratios, non-stationarity, and prohibitive autoregressive decoding latency. To reconcile representation learning with institutional operational constraints, this study presents TailDART, a dense 2.68 million-parameter foundation model engineered for financial time-series forecasting. By integrating grouped-query attention into a lightweight backbone, the architecture drastically reduces Key-Value cache memory bandwidth overhead, achieving major inference acceleration (up to 48.1%) over prevailing state-of-the-art TSFMs to enable low-latency, high-throughput streaming execution. Furthermore, multi-asset benchmarks across varying temporal resolutions show that a compact model can remain competitive for market forecasting: TailDART matches the predictive fidelity of substantially larger baselines while demonstrating superior error resilience in high-volatility regimes.

</details>


### [254] [Analyzing Code Provenance in LLMs for Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09177)
*Subhankar Maity,Faiza Belbachir,Omar Boucelma,Guilherme Medeiros Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将 Min-K% 概率方法扩展为"合成非成员代码生成"版本，用于 Python 代码的成员推断与代码溯源（code provenance），以区分学生自写、AI 生成与外部来源代码，并在 Stack Overflow 数据与高校学生作业上验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在编程教育中日益普及，能瞬间生成完整可运行代码，削弱了以学生提交代码作为学习与投入度指标的可靠性。因此需要代码溯源能力，以追踪代码来源、区分学生自写与 AI 生成/外部拷贝成分，从而支撑 AI 辅助编程教育的有效评估。

Method: 在 Min-K%（最低 k% token 平均对数概率）成员推断方法基础上，引入"合成非成员代码生成"以增强非成员参考分布，并将其适配到 Python 代码上的成员推断与溯源任务。评估覆盖免费/开源模型（GPT-2、DistilGPT-2、DialoGPT）与先进商用模型（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5、Mistral、DeepSeek、LLaMA 3.3）；先在 Stack Overflow 数据上验证方法可行性，再应用于高校学生作业。

Result: 免费/开源模型相比商用模型可实现更灵敏的代码记忆检测；通过系统比较各 LLM，刻画了检测准确率与隐私保护之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将溯源检测与过程可见性结合，为 AI 辅助编程教育的评估迈出第一步；同时揭示了不同模型在记忆检测灵敏度上的差异及其隐私代价。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) play an increasing role in programming education, challenging traditional assessment methods as they can generate fully functional code instantly. This disrupts the reliability of student submissions as indicators of learning and effort. To address this issue, code provenance is essential for tracing the origins of code and distinguishing student-authored, AI-generated, and externally sourced components. We extend the Min-K% probability approach by incorporating synthetic non-member code generation and apply it to Python code for membership inference and provenance tracking. Evaluations cover free LLMs such as GPT-2, DistilGPT-2, and DialoGPT, as well as state-of-the-art commercial models including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5, Mistral, DeepSeek, and LLaMA 3.3. Before evaluating our approach on higher-education student assignments, we validate its feasibility using Stack Overflow data. The results show that free models enable more sensitive code memorization detection than commercial models. By systematically comparing these LLMs, we assess trade-offs between detection accuracy and privacy protection, taking an initial step toward effective assessment of AI-assisted programming education through combined provenance detection and process visibility.

</details>


### [255] [Large Language Model Consensus and Corpus-Adaptive Topic Modeling for Classifying and Mapping Patent Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09194)
*Sercan Ozcan,Enes Ozgozler,C. Okan Sakar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该设计导向研究提出一个可审计的LLM辅助专利分析流水线，将新颖性/创新类型分类、基于PatentSBERTa嵌入的聚类主题发现与LLM主题标注串联，并在30,964件公共部门相关专利上验证了可行性与可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利分析日益依赖Transformer表示与LLM，但概念分类、主题形成与解释三个环节之间的衔接缺乏对模型角色与各阶段保留证据的明确设计，导致结果难以审计与修订。

Method: 三个LLM分别对每件专利做新颖性（radical/incremental）与创新类型（product/process/service）分类，需两票支持，incremental为缺省新颖性标签；以PatentSBERTa嵌入聚类发现主题，参数区间随语料规模缩放；仅在c-TF-IDF表示主题时使用专利专用停用词表；第二个嵌入模型用于主题间相似度、层次细化与可视化；最后由LLM依据排序关键词与代表性专利为每个主题命名。数据为Lens.org检索的30,964件公共部门相关专利。

Result: 在591至29,215件专利的五个类别语料上共生成74个类别特定主题实例，文档覆盖率宏平均98.1%，主题多样性0.900–1.000，UNKNOWN分类输出不足1%。

Conclusion: 示范验证了流水线的可行性与可追溯性，因保留每次模型投票与主题标签证据而支持审计与修订；下一步为专家编码验证标签、与其他流水线基准对比，可作为技术情报中LLM辅助专利分析的可复用基础。

Abstract: Patent analytics increasingly relies on transformer representations and large language models (LLMs), yet linking conceptual classification, topic formation and interpretation requires explicit decisions about model roles and the evidence retained at each stage. In this design-oriented study, we propose and demonstrate a patent analytics pipeline that links these tasks and keeps each decision open to audit. Three LLMs separately classify each patent by novelty (radical or incremental) and innovation type (product, process, service). Radical and innovation-type labels each require two supporting votes, with Incremental used as the fallback novelty label. Topics are discovered by clustering PatentSBERTa embeddings, with parameter ranges scaled to corpus size. A patent-specific stopword list is applied only when topics are represented with class-based term frequency--inverse document frequency (c-TF-IDF), and a second embedding model supports inter-topic similarity, hierarchical refinement and visualization. An LLM then labels each topic from its ranked keywords and representative patents. We demonstrate the pipeline on 30,964 public-sector-related patents retrieved from Lens.org. Across five category corpora ranging from 591 to 29,215 patents, the pipeline produced 74 category-specific topic instances with a macro-average document coverage of 98.1% and topic diversity of 0.900--1.000, and fewer than 1% of classification outputs returned UNKNOWN. Because every model vote and the evidence behind each topic label are retained, analysts can audit and revise the outputs. The demonstration establishes the feasibility and traceability of the pipeline; testing its labels against expert coding and benchmarking it against alternative pipelines are identified as the next steps. The pipeline offers a reusable basis for LLM-assisted patent analysis in technology intelligence.

</details>


### [256] [Safe Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Option Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09197)
*Gian Luca Ascari,Isidoro Tamassia,Ying Jiao,Luc De Raedt,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出概率选项屏蔽（POS），将概率逻辑屏蔽（PLS）从单步层面扩展到选项层面，用无模型critic估计整个option安全完成的概率，从而在无需完整环境模型的前提下恢复长时程安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统屏蔽依赖完整环境模型才能提供长期安全保证；PLS虽摆脱了模型要求，但只做一步或数步的短时安全判断，缺乏长时程保障。

Method: POS在option层面扩展PLS：从一步PLS信号中以无模型方式学习一个critic，用于估计在不确定性下整个option能够安全完成的概率。

Result: 在LogiCity环境的安全导航任务上，POS实现显著更安全的训练，同时任务性能与标准步级PLS及其他安全强化学习基线相当。

Conclusion: 通过将屏蔽提升到option层级，POS在不重新引入完整环境模型需求的情况下兼顾了长时程安全性与任务表现。

Abstract: Shielding is a safe reinforcement learning technique that provides safety guarantees while learning, by blocking unsafe actions. Traditional shielding relies on a complete model of the environment to provide long-term guarantees. Probabilistic logic shielding (PLS) lifts the requirement of having such model, but only considers safety in the short term, typically looking only one or few steps ahead. We propose Probabilistic Option Shielding (POS), which extends PLS to the option level, recovering long-horizon safety without reintroducing the need for a complete environment model. POS learns a model-free critic directly from one-step PLS signals, to estimate the probability that an entire option will complete safely under uncertainty. We evaluate POS on safe navigation tasks in the LogiCity environment, and show that it achieves considerably safer training while matching the task performance of standard step-level PLS and other safety related reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [257] [Fused-Softmax for efficient quantized attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09225)
*Tianxing Wu,Juuso Korhonen,Jesus Carabano Bravo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Fused-Softmax Attention：无需训练，通过重写并近似softmax，把其主导运算折叠进注意力GEMM，将向量单元负载迁移到矩阵核；在AMD CDNA 4上实现MXFP8/6/4核，达约3.0/4.0/4.6 PFLOP/s，相比BF16注意力加速2.1×/2.8×/3.2×，额外误差低于GEMM操作数量化误差下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU低精度矩阵吞吐增长远快于向量运算吞吐，softmax成为注意力流水线瓶颈，使注意力无法获得GEMM的可达性能。

Method: 训练无关算法：重构并近似softmax，将其主导运算折叠进注意力GEMM，从而把工作从向量单元转移到矩阵核；针对AMD CDNA 4架构实现MXFP8、MXFP6、MXFP4内核。

Result: 在AMD Instinct MI355X上分别达约3.0、4.0、4.6 PFLOP/s，相对BF16注意力加速2.1×、2.8×、3.2×；Fused-Softmax引入的额外误差低于GEMM操作数量化误差下限；Wan2.2-T2V-A14B上混合MXFP6/4调度端到端加速最高2.05×且保持视觉质量；内核已进入AMD开源AITER库，支撑MLPerf Inference v6.1 Wan2.2提交，比同硬件上一轮结果快1.7×。

Conclusion: 重新设计softmax以利用矩阵流水线，可使注意力受益于低精度矩阵吞吐的持续增长。

Abstract: Low-precision matrix throughput on modern GPUs is growing far faster than vector-arithmetic throughput. Attention therefore cannot realize the available GEMM performance because softmax has become the pipeline bottleneck. We introduce Fused-Softmax Attention, a training-free algorithm that reformulates and approximates softmax to fold its dominant operations into the attention GEMMs, moving work from vector units to matrix cores. Implemented for the AMD CDNA 4 architecture, our MXFP8, MXFP6 and MXFP4 kernels reach approximately 3.0, 4.0 and 4.6 PFLOP/s on an AMD Instinct MI355X GPU, yielding speedups of $2.1\times$, $2.8\times$ and $3.2\times$ over the BF16 attention. Importantly, the additional error introduced by Fused-Softmax remains below the error floor set by quantizing the GEMM operands. On Wan2.2-T2V-A14B, a hybrid MXFP6/4 schedule delivers up to $2.05\times$ end-to-end speedup while preserving the visual quality of the outputs. The kernels ship in AMD's open-source AITER library, and they powered AMD's MLPerf Inference v6.1 Wan2.2 submission, which ran $1.7\times$ faster than AMD's previous-round result on the same hardware. Our results show that redesigning softmax to utilize the matrix pipeline enables attention to capitalize on continued growth in low-precision matrix throughput.

</details>


### [258] [RAG-Enhanced LLMs for Interactive Explainability in Clinical Decision Support Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09232)
*Lorenzo Mannocci,Francesca Naretto,Lucia Passaro,Anna Monreale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种临床决策支持系统（CDSS），将预测模型、XAI与检索增强生成（RAG）模块结合，把技术性解释转写为可交互、可溯源的自然语言解释，并以初步实验验证该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在高风险领域（医疗、金融、司法）的决策应用日益广泛，对可解释AI的需求上升；但现有XAI解释形式对领域专家不友好、实用性受限。LLM虽能以自然语言搭桥，却带来幻觉输出与来源不可追溯的风险。

Method: 构建CDSS架构：标准预测模型 + XAI技术 + 检索增强生成（RAG）模块作为交互增强器。RAG将解释锚定在经核验的医学知识上，把技术输出重写为类人表述，并支持临床医生主动查询，形成人在回路（human-in-the-loop）的交互环境。

Result: 初步实验对所提方法进行了验证，表明该流程（预测—解释—检索增强重述—交互查询）可运行并具备可行性；摘要未给出量化指标或对比基线结果。

Conclusion: 用RAG约束LLM解释可缓解幻觉与溯源缺失问题，交互式人在回路设计有助于领域专家探查模型决策逻辑、结合语境理解解释并建立对AI辅助诊断的信任；后续需补充系统性评估与临床验证。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in high-risks decision-making systems across domains such as healthcare, finance, and the judicial system. As reliance on predictive models grows, so does the demand for Explainable AI (XAI) to ensure transparency, trust, and interpretability. However, existing XAI approaches often fail to present explanations in a format accessible to domain experts, limiting their practical utility. Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as a bridge between complex models and end-users, offering natural language explanations. Yet, their use introduces new risks, including hallucinated outputs and lack of source traceability. To address these challenges, we propose a Clinical Decision Support System (CDSS) that not only combines standard predictive models with XAI techniques but also incorporates a Retrieval-Augmented Generation module as an interactive enhancer. This module grounds explanations in verified medical knowledge, reformulates technical outputs into a human-like format, and enables users-such as clinicians-to actively query the system. By fostering an interactive, human-in-the-loop environment, our approach empowers domain experts to explore model decisions, contextualize explanations, and build trust in AI-assisted diagnostics. The study concludes with preliminary experiments validating the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [259] [MM-DyGraph: A Dataset, Benchmark, and Model for Multimodal Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09240)
*Zongyuan Wu,Pengjie Wang,Lingshan Chen,Tianhang Wan,Yuan Meng,Xin Wang,Ling Feng,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个多模态动态图基准 MM-DyGraph，并发现朴素特征拼接存在"目标级不一致"问题，据此提出目标感知时空多模态融合框架 TST-MF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中动态图节点与边常伴随文本、图像等多模态信号，但显式研究多模态动态图学习的基准与方法稀缺，阻碍了对多模态信息在下游任务中作用的理解与利用。

Method: 构建 MM-DyGraph 基准：多领域真实数据集、细粒度时序图演化、丰富多模态属性，配套三项评估任务；对代表性动态图方法进行基准评测。基于"朴素拼接导致不同预测目标受益/受损不一致"的观察，提出 TST-MF（Target-aware Spatial-Temporal Multimodal Fusion），将多模态融合同时以预测目标与时空关系为条件，实现上下文自适应的跨模态交互，生成目标特异性表示。

Result: 基准实验揭示模态信息量随预测目标与时间动态变化；TST-MF 在多数据集、多任务上表现稳健且有竞争力，在多模态设置下取得明显增益。

Conclusion: 多模态动态图需要目标感知的融合机制而非简单特征拼接；MM-DyGraph 为后续研究提供了统一评测平台，TST-MF 验证了目标条件化时空融合的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal dynamic graphs are pervasive in real-world applications, where nodes and edges are accompanied by multimodal signals, including text and images. Despite their practical importance, benchmarks and methods that explicitly study multimodal learning in dynamic graphs remain scarce, hindering the full understanding and effective utilization of multimodal information for downstream tasks. To address this gap, we introduce MM-DyGraph, to the best of our knowledge, the first benchmark for multimodal dynamic graph learning, consisting of diverse real-world datasets with fine-grained temporal graph evolution and rich multimodal attributes. MM-DyGraph provides three evaluation tasks to comprehensively assess model performance in dynamic settings. We benchmark representative state-of-the-art dynamic graph methods and reveal that naive feature concatenation yields target-level inconsistencies: multimodal inputs benefit certain prediction targets while harming others. This observation indicates that the modality informativeness depends on the specific prediction target over time. Building on these observations, we propose a Target-aware Spatial-Temporal Multimodal Fusion (TST-MF) framework, which jointly conditions multimodal fusion on the prediction target and spatial-temporal relations, enabling context-adaptive cross-modal interplay for target-specific representations. Extensive experiments over diverse datasets and tasks show that TST-MF delivers robust and competitive performance, achieving clear gains in multimodal settings.

</details>


### [260] [Zero-Query Transferable Pretend Benign Attacks with Amortized Online Generation for Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09256)
*Hongwei Lin,Xun Huang,Chenglu Wen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出受害者无关的可迁移"伪装良性"(TrPB/RT-TrPB)攻击，可从异质代理模型离线构建并复用扰动字典，快速绕过共识防御；同时提出STTrust用时空一致性与身份级信任检测此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知中收益最大的消息往往最难被本地观测验证，原始Pretend Benign(PB)利用这种验证不对称性，但依赖受害者模型的架构、参数与梯度，难以迁移到未见过的受害者。可迁移攻击需捕获跨协同检测模型共享的性质，而非单一网络特性。此外，针对伪装成良性协作的PB类攻击，缺乏有效防御。

Method: 1) 构建受害者无关攻击：由异质集成代理模型生成，并将攻击能力编码为可复用字典以降低单条消息生成成本。2) TrPB：将PB的区域选择准则与多目标扰动策略跨异质代理迁移，形成离线攻击。3) RT-TrPB：将可迁移区域与扰动模式分别编码为区域预测器和可复用扰动字典，摊薄逐消息攻击生成开销。4) STTrust防御：基于空间与时间一致性验证接收消息，并引入身份级信任。

Result: 在常用协同感知基准上，RT-TrPB以接近100%的成功率绕过基于共识的防御ROBOSAC与FLD，生成单条扰动耗时低于56 ms。STTrust检测出超过90%的PB类攻击，并将感知性能恢复至接近无攻击水平。

Conclusion: 通过捕获跨模型共享特性并将攻击模式字典化，可实现高效、可迁移的伪装良性攻击，暴露共识防御的脆弱性；同时时空一致性结合身份信任能有效检测并缓解该类攻击，为协同感知安全提供攻防双向参考。

Abstract: Cooperative perception enables an ego agent to acquire information from collaborators beyond its own sensors' reach. Yet, the most beneficial messages are often the hardest to verify from local observations. Pretend Benign (PB) exploits this asymmetry by targeting regions with weak local verifiability and applying multi-objective perturbations, making harmful feature changes appear to be benign assistance. However, PB is victim-dependent: selecting regions and optimizing perturbations require the victim model's architecture, parameters, and gradients, limiting transfer to unseen victims. A transferable attack must capture properties shared across cooperative detection models rather than those specific to one network. We address this by building a victim-agnostic attack from a heterogeneous ensemble of surrogate models and encoding its capabilities in a reusable dictionary to reduce per-message generation cost. Specifically, Transferable Pretend Benign (TrPB) transfers PB's region selection criterion and multi-objective perturbation strategy across heterogeneous surrogates to construct an offline attack. We further propose RT-TrPB, which encodes transferable regions and perturbation patterns into a region predictor and reusable perturbation dictionary, amortizing per-message attack generation. Experiments on widely used cooperative perception benchmarks show that RT-TrPB bypasses the consensus-based defenses ROBOSAC and FLD with success rates approaching 100% and generates each perturbation in under 56 ms. To counter PB-style attacks masquerading as benign collaboration, we also propose STTrust, which verifies received messages using spatial and temporal consistency and incorporates identity-level trust. STTrust detects over 90% of PB-style attacks and restores perception performance to near the attack-free level.

</details>


### [261] [Forecasting cumulative intraoperative hypotension burden: patient-disjoint evaluation of quantile boosting and alert policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09259)
*Yunfan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在VitalDB动脉MAP数据上建立"负荷预测"基准，用条件分位数预测未来15分钟MAP低于65 mmHg的累积亏欠深度，并明确区分预测技能与警报策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低血压预测多为二分类（是否发生），无法刻画未来低压的深度与持续时间；而负荷预测虽可支持分级预警，但更高的预测分数并不必然转化为更有用的警报，因此需要区分"预测性能"与"警报性能"。

Method: 采用VitalDB公开有创动脉MAP，降采样为10秒中位数且不做前向填充；按受试者不重叠划分为训练(70,402)、验证(14,270)与测试(15,575)窗口。对比气候学基线、校准持续性模型、仅用最近MAP的boosting、直接与残差分位数boosting（含/不含额外MAP历史特征）。以患者加权pinball损失和受试者bootstrap区间评估预测，验证集选择警报阈值后在测试集进行哈希留痕的一次性评估。

Result: 测试集90分位数上，预设的"残差vs持续性"比较损失为2.256 vs 2.900 mmHg·min（差值-0.644，95%区间-0.814至-0.487）；直接预测为2.196，直接预测加额外MAP特征为2.193。事件级警报结果对警报规则与操作阈值敏感。

Conclusion: 该回顾性基准将负荷预测技能与警报策略性能分离；结果支持继续研究直接负荷估计，但未证实患者获益、前瞻可靠性或跨院可迁移性。

Abstract: Background: Binary hypotension forecasts do not describe the depth and duration of future low pressure. Burden forecasts may support graded early warning, but better forecast scores need not yield more useful alerts.
  Objective: To evaluate conditional quantiles of the next 15-minute mean arterial pressure (MAP) deficit below 65 mmHg and distinguish forecasting performance from episode-warning performance.
  Methods: Public VitalDB numeric arterial MAP was reduced to 10-second medians without forward filling. Subject-disjoint training, validation and test splits included 70,402, 14,270 and 15,575 labeled windows. We compared climatology, calibrated persistence, last-MAP-only boosting, and direct and residual quantile boosting, with and without additional MAP-history features. Patient-weighted pinball loss and subject-bootstrap intervals evaluated forecasting. Validation selected alert thresholds before a hash-recorded test evaluation.
  Results: In the test split, the prespecified residual-versus-persistence comparison at the 90th percentile yielded losses of 2.256 and 2.900 mmHg-min, respectively (difference -0.644; 95% interval -0.814 to -0.487). Direct prediction achieved 2.196, and direct prediction with additional MAP features achieved 2.193. Episode-warning results were sensitive to the alert rule and operating threshold.
  Conclusions: This retrospective benchmark separates burden-forecast skill from warning-policy performance. Findings support further study of direct burden estimation; they do not establish patient benefit, prospective reliability or transportability beyond the source hospital.

</details>


### [262] [Model-based learning for DoA estimation and array calibration via continuous sparse recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09263)
*Baptiste Chatelier,José Miguel Mateos-Ramos,Vincent Corlay,Matthieu Crussière,Christian Häger,Henk Wymeersch,Nir Shlezinger,Luc Le Magoarou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于SOMP的联合阵列校准与DoA估计方法，用投影梯度下降同时校正增益-相位与阵元位置误差并精化连续字典下的DoA估计，无需标签样本即可超越传统稀疏恢复与子空间类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC中DoA估计精度与下游通信性能会被硬件损伤（增益-相位误差、阵元位置偏差）削弱，而既有高精度DoA估计研究普遍忽视这类硬件损伤，缺少有效的补偿手段。

Method: 在基于模型的机器学习范式下，将同时正交匹配追踪（SOMP）与投影梯度下降（PGD）结合：用PGD对受严重增益-相位及阵元位置损伤的天线阵列进行校准，目标可为监督式或非监督式；同时把SOMP扩展到连续字典，并通过PGD精化步提升DoA估计精度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法能高效补偿硬件损伤，且在无标注样本条件下依然有效；DoA估计性能优于传统稀疏恢复与子空间类基线方法。

Conclusion: 将模型驱动先验与PGD优化结合，可在一个框架内实现阵列自校准与高精度DoA估计，降低对标注数据的依赖，为ISAC中的实际硬件非理想场景提供可行方案。

Abstract: Direction of arrival (DoA) estimation, a classical signal processing problem, has experienced renewed interest with the advent of integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). While decades of research have yielded highly accurate DoA estimators, hardware impairments have frequently been neglected, despite degrading both DoA estimation accuracy and downstream communication performance in ISAC systems. This work introduces a joint array calibration and DoA estimation method based on the simultaneous orthogonal matching pursuit (SOMP) algorithm, following the model-based machine learning paradigm. The proposed method enables the calibration of antenna arrays subject to severe gain-phase and antenna location impairments through projected gradient descent (PGD), with either supervised or unsupervised objectives. Additionally, DoA estimation is enhanced by extending SOMP to continuous dictionaries via PGD refinement steps. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method enables efficient hardware impairment compensation, even without labeled samples, while outperforming conventional sparse recovery and subspace-based DoA estimation baselines.

</details>


### [263] [Nudging Decision Models: Do Digital Nudges Steer Fast, Generation-Free AI Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09264)
*Sascha Kaltenpoth,Haya Halimeh,Kevin Bösch,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将人类购物实验适配到三种非生成式决策模型（Jev、Decision-1、d1），首次证明这类“系统1模型”易受数字助推影响，且对自动型助推的反应强于反思型助推，与推理型LLM智能体的已有结果相反，提示助推效应依赖智能体类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在商业与消费任务中日益自动化，但速度慢、成本高；决策模型（不生成文本、直接在选项间选择）更快更便宜，开发者以其为“系统1模型”并援引双加工理论。作者据此提出两个问题：这类模型能否被线上数字助推引导？按理论预期，自动型助推是否比反思型助推对其影响更大？

Method: 改编此前用于人类和LLM智能体的在线购物实验，迁移到三个决策模型（Jev、Decision-1、d1）；每个模型运行600次，考察默认选项助推（预设默认）与社会影响信息助推两种处理，并与控制组对照，比较自动型与反思型助推的效应强度。

Result: 各模型在97.5–99.9%的选择中采纳预设默认项；在75–96%的选择中选中带社会影响信息的产品，而对照组为47–49%。三个模型均对自动型助推反应更强，这与推理能力较强的LLM智能体已报道的结果相反。

Conclusion: 首次提供证据表明非生成式决策模型在“系统1”模式下易受数字助推影响，说明助推效应具有智能体类型特异性，不能简单从LLM智能体或人类结果外推至决策模型。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly automate business and consumer tasks but are slow and expensive. Decision models, a new class that chooses among options without generating text, are far faster and less costly. Developers call them "System One models," referencing dual process theory. We ask whether such models can be steered by digital nudges online and whether, as the theory predicts, automatic nudges affect them more than reflective ones. We adapted an online shopping experiment, previously run with humans and LLM agents, to three decision models (Jev, Decision-1, d1). Across 600 runs per model, they chose a preselected default in 97.5-99.9% and a product with social influence information in 75-96% of choices, versus 47-49% in the control. All responded more strongly to the automatic nudge, the opposite of what has been reported for reasoning-capable LLM agents. We provide first evidence that non-generative decision models are susceptible to digital nudges in a System 1 pattern, implying agent-type-specific nudge effects.

</details>


### [264] [Zero-Shot Weight-Space Programming Across a Difficulty-Graded Ladder of Two-Player Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09271)
*Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"权重空间编程"的难度阶梯实验：让大模型零样本手工设定前馈网络全部权重（无梯度下降、无训练数据）来实现博弈程序，用四档难度（Nim、井字棋、两种尺寸的四子棋、象棋残局）×每档两个结构无关游戏共十个网络检验可行性与失败机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多依赖梯度下降与训练数据，"由显式计算规格手工写定每个权重"这一范式在大模型上能走多远缺乏系统证据；同时需要把"难度水平"与"具体游戏"两个混淆因素分开。

Method: 设计四档难度阶梯，覆盖四个难度维度；每档另建一个难度匹配但结构无关的游戏作为复制体（replicate），共10个网络并独立验证。用独立预言机（oracle）检验正确性；在象棋残局档将权值固定、仅加浅层搜索做消融，以区分"架构上限"与"工程量"。

Result: 6个网络获得完整、非统计意义上的正确性证明；象棋残局档在"正确性便宜"处精确（合法走法、某网络的一步杀检测器），在"正确性昂贵"处崩塌：一步战术84.1–100%，完整对抗对局降至0.57–9.13%；复制体在所需规划更短、安全检查改为精确而非启发式后，崩塌程度减轻一个数量级。标准尺寸档证实剩余差距是架构天花板，且该崩塌在两个结构无关游戏中复现。

Conclusion: 权重空间编程可零样本实现对简单/局部化计算的精确编程，但难以扩展到需要多步组合规划与对抗性搜索的任务；瓶颈在于架构而非投入。作者发布10个网络、预言机、构造成本测量及冷提示协议，供未来跨模型比较。

Abstract: Weight-space programming sets every weight of a feed-forward network by hand, from an explicit specification of the desired computation, with no gradient descent and no training data anywhere in the construction. We ask how far a large language model can carry this practice, zero-shot, across games of deliberately increasing difficulty. We design a four-rung ladder (Nim, tic-tac-toe, two sizes of Connect Four, and a chess endgame) spanning four axes of difficulty, and build a second, independent network at each rung, using a structurally unrelated game at matched difficulty, to separate what a rung's finding says about the difficulty level from what it says about the one game used to measure it. Ten networks are built and independently verified. Six admit a complete, non-statistical proof of correctness against an independent oracle. The chess-endgame rung is exact where correctness is cheap, move legality and, in one network, an exact one-ply mate detector, and collapses where it is expensive, composing a multi-move mating plan, from 84.1-100% success at the one-ply tactics it was built to get right down to 0.57-9.13% in full adversarial play; its replicate shows this collapse is an order of magnitude less severe under a shorter required plan and exact rather than heuristic safety checks. The standard-sized rung confirms, by holding hand-set evaluation weights fixed and adding only shallow search, that the remaining gap is an architectural ceiling rather than a matter of engineering effort, and this exact collapse reproduces across two structurally unrelated games. We release all ten networks, their independent oracles, construction-cost measurements, and a cold-prompt protocol for a future cross-model comparison

</details>


### [265] [PHISHREV+: An ASP-Based Post-Hoc Defeasible Machine Learning Framework for Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09273)
*Mainak Sen,Kumar Sanka Ray,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出PHISHREV+，将钓鱼网站检测器的高精度预测视为可废止信念，通过基于ASP的非单调推理层在事后形式化修正初始预测，在四个分类器上一致降低假阳性且不增加假阴性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习分类器在基准数据集上精度虽高，但训练完成后决策边界固定，无法在不重训练的情况下纳入新的情境证据；同时现有钓鱼检测方法缺乏实例级可解释性，无法说明某次预测为何成立或被推翻。

Method: 提出"事后可废止机器学习"框架PHISHREV+，分两阶段：机器学习阶段在87特征基准数据集上训练SVM、KNN、DT、RF四个分类器；事后非单调推理阶段，将初始预测作为可废止信念，用从训练集探索性数据分析中导出、并经卡方检验与Mann-Whitney U检验验证的四条可废止规则，在ASP推理层形式化修正初始预测；并用XASP生成形式化推导图实现实例级解释；另做七种规则配置的消融实验。

Result: 四个分类器假阳性均一致下降：SVM 41→39、KNN 47→46、DT 62→61、RF 52→51；假阴性保持不变（完全对等），且不增加额外开销；消融实验表明四条规则的层次化组合是实现该最优平衡所必需的。

Conclusion: 将分类器预测视为可废止信念、由ASP非单调推理层进行事后修正，是一种不改动原分类器即可提升钓鱼检测可靠性并赋予实例级形式化可解释性的有效途径。

Abstract: Phishing website detection remains a critical cybersecurity challenge. Although machine learning classifiers achieve high accuracy on benchmark datasets, they suffer from a fundamental limitation: once trained, their decision boundaries are fixed and cannot incorporate new contextual evidence without retraining the model. This work presents \textbf{PHISHREV+}, a hybrid framework that instantiates the notion of \textit{post-hoc defeasible machine learning}. This framework does not replace the classifier. It treats the initial classifier predictions as defeasible beliefs and revises them through an Answer Set Programming based non-monotonic reasoning(NMR) layer. The framework operates in two phases. In the machine learning phase, four classifiers (SVM, KNN, DT and RF) are trained on the 87 feature benchmark dataset. In the post-hoc NMR phase, four defeasible rules derived from exploratory data analysis of the training set, and validated through Chi-square and Mann-Whitney U tests, formally revise initial predictions. The proposed framework achieves consistent false positive reduction across all our classifiers SVM (41$\rightarrow$39), KNN (47$\rightarrow$46), DT (62$\rightarrow$61), and RF (52$\rightarrow$51) while maintaining complete false negative parity with zero increase overhead. A comprehensive ablation studies across seven rule configurations confirms that the hierarchical combination of all four defeasible rules is necessary to achieve this optimal balance. Instance level explainability is provided through eXplainable ASP (XASP), which generates formal derivation graphs showing which defeasible rules fired and why the initial belief was retracted, a capability absent from all existing phishing detection approaches.

</details>


### [266] [An Empirical Study of Issues in GPU Kernel Libraries for LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09280)
*Yanjie Gao,Jiayi Bi,Haoxiang Lin,Ling Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个针对LLM系统GPU内核库缺陷的综合性实证研究：手工分析7个代表性库的300条高质量issue，刻画症状、根因、修复与测试调试实践，发现60.33%问题源于编译器/构建系统与运行时环境，内核特化带来隐式正确性约束，现有测试调试手段难以覆盖低精度数值与硬件特定失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练与推理日益依赖专用GPU内核库（实现attention、GEMM、量化、低精度算子，并管理张量形状、内存布局、代码生成与硬件分发）。该层缺陷会静默污染输出、挂起GPU、导致编译失败、引入部署不兼容或性能下降，损害开发者生产力并浪费昂贵GPU资源；然而此前缺乏对该层缺陷的系统性认识。

Method: 首个面向LLM系统GPU内核库的综合实证研究。人工分析7个代表性库（FlashInfer、FlashAttention、Triton、CUTLASS、DeepGEMM、xFormers、bitsandbytes）的300条高质量GitHub issue及其讨论与可用修复，对症状、根因、修复方式、测试与调试实践进行分类刻画。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 60.33%的采样issue来源于"编译器与构建系统"或"运行时环境"维度，说明验证须覆盖部署与执行栈而非仅内核代码；(2) 内核特化对张量形状、元数据布局、内存别名与硬件资源施加隐式正确性约束，违反约束即使对受支持的算子也会产生错误输出或启动失败，凸显显式契约校验与边界用例测试的必要性；(3) 现有测试与调试实践往往不足以识别低精度数值问题和硬件特定执行失败。

Conclusion: 为支持未来LLM GPU内核开发，论文提出了相应研究方向与工具化展望，强调需在部署执行栈层面加强验证、引入显式契约校验与边界测试，并改进低精度数值与硬件特定失败的测试调试支持。

Abstract: Behind the expanding use of large language models (LLMs) across application domains lies a growing dependence on specialized GPU kernel libraries. These libraries underpin the correctness and efficiency of training and inference across diverse GPU platforms, implementing attention, GEMM, quantization, and low-precision operators while managing tensor shapes, memory layouts, code generation, and hardware-specific dispatch. Problems at this layer can silently corrupt outputs, hang GPUs, prevent compilation, introduce deployment incompatibilities, or degrade performance, thereby reducing developer productivity and wasting expensive GPU resources.
  We present the first comprehensive empirical study of issues in GPU kernel libraries for LLM systems. We manually analyzed 300 high-quality GitHub issue reports, their discussions, and available fixes from seven representative libraries: FlashInfer, FlashAttention, Triton, CUTLASS, DeepGEMM, xFormers, and bitsandbytes. The analysis characterizes symptoms, root causes, fixes, and testing and debugging practices. Our main findings are: (1) 60.33% of the sampled issues originate in the Compiler and Build System or Runtime Environment dimensions, so validation must cover the deployment and execution stack, not just kernel code. (2) Kernel specialization imposes implicit correctness constraints on tensor shapes, metadata layouts, memory aliasing, and hardware resources. Violations can produce incorrect outputs or launch failures even for supported operations, highlighting the need for explicit contract validation and boundary-case testing. (3) Current testing and debugging practices are often insufficient for identifying low-precision numerical problems and hardware-specific execution failures. We outline research opportunities and tooling directions to support future LLM GPU kernel development.

</details>


### [267] [Ontology-Driven Control of Conversational Agents with a Use-Case for Job Interview Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09282)
*Barbara Gendron,Gaël Guibon,Mathieu D'aquin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于本体的对话策略设计方法，通过形式化定义并自动微调LLM，使其在多个并发对话维度上遵循所需策略，并以求职面试练习场景验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域特定场景下需要将LLM适配为具备特定特征（语气、风格等单轮特征，或整体对话策略）的对话代理，但现有适配方式多为临时性、不可复用且不透明的开发。

Method: 提出基于本体的对话策略设计方法：依据文本话语的期望特征与可测量特征，用本体形式化、透明地定义对话策略及其多个并发维度；再自动微调LLM以提升其对本体所定义对话特征的遵循度。

Result: 以求职面试练习用例表明，该方法可控制LLM遵循本体定义的对话策略；基于形式化概念训练LLM能有效提升其与所需特征的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 本体引导的对话控制可在形式化、透明定义的前提下同时兼顾多个并发对话方面，实现可复用的对话策略控制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely used as conversational agents in both domain-specific and open-domain situations. In domain-specific context, it is often necessary to adapt those LLMs to obtain conversations with desired characteristics, whether those apply at the level of each response (e.g. for tone or style) or more globally (i.e. at the level of a conversational strategy). Such adaptations are, however, generally realized in an ad hoc way, through specific, non-reusable, and non-transparent developments. In this paper, we propose an ontology-based approach to design conversational strategies that rely on the desired and measured characteristics of textual utterances in the conversation. Through a use case related to job interview practice, we show how this approach can be used to control LLMs to follow the strategy defined in the ontology. To this end, we automatically fine-tune the LLM to improve its compliance with ontologically-defined conversation characteristics. Our results show that training the LLM from formally defined concepts effectively enhances alignment with the requested characteristics. Hence, this work demonstrates how ontology-guided conversation control can be achieved while taking into account multiple, concurrent conversation aspects that are formally and transparently defined.

</details>


### [268] [AutoRefine: Auto-Calibrating Code Judges from Workflow Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09291)
*Shervin Shahidi,James Poon,Hang Xie,Kenneth O. Xu,Robin Håkanson,Vikram Nair,Jens Pillgram-Larsen,Nachiappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRefine：用开发流程中"免费但含噪"的结果标签，通过带弃权机制的信息型教师模型迭代重写LLM评审提示词，实现可部署评审器的自动校准与再校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评审器被广泛用作无测试预言（no test oracle）代码变更的质量门，但评审提示必须针对任务校准，并随被评系统演进而再校准。人工标注成本高且易过时；开发工作流天然产生结果标签（测试通过/失败、合并/放弃），但噪声大。

Method: 提出AutoRefine循环：反复重写可部署评审器的提示词以复现结果标签。核心是"信息型教师"——除评审器输入外还能看到真实结果，对保留的每个标签给出解释，当结果无法从评审器可见信息中解释时弃权。弃权既剔除噪声标签，也标记"评审器输入不足"的变更。

Result: 1) 在有执行预言的留出集上，校准将机会校正一致性（Scott's π）由0.36提升至0.60。2) 在部署代理的结果标签上随机翻转25%制造噪声时，教师弃权翻转fail标签的比率是真实标签的两倍以上，将整体标签噪声从25%降至20%。3) 用AutoRefine再校准该代理评审器，在当前工作流17个独立留出diff上与合并/放弃结果的一致性由π=0.16升至0.51；但在旧工作流16个diff上旧评审器仍更优，说明系统演进后需再校准。

Conclusion: 基于结果标签的教师-评审器协同校准可在无人工标注下显著提升评审可靠性；弃权机制兼具噪声过滤与"输入不足"诊断功能，提示团队何时应改进评审器输入而非提示词；且校准具有时效性，需随系统演化再校准。

Abstract: LLM judges increasingly serve as quality gates for code changes that have no test oracle, but a judge prompt must be calibrated for its task and recalibrated as the system it judges evolves, while hand labels for the calibration are resource intensive and go stale. Development workflows emit outcome labels for free (tests pass or fail, changes land or are abandoned), but these labels are noisy. We present AutoRefine, a loop that rewrites a deployable judge's prompt to reproduce outcome labels, with an informed teacher that sees the judge's input plus the outcome, explains each label it keeps, and abstains when the outcome cannot be explained from what the judge sees. On a held-out dataset with an execution oracle, calibration raised chance-corrected agreement (Scott's $π$) from 0.36 to 0.60. On a deployed agent's outcome labels, with 25\% flipped at random to introduce noise, the teacher set aside flipped \textsc{fail} labels at more than twice the rate of genuine ones, cutting overall label noise from 25\% to 20\%. Recalibrating that agent's judge using AutoRefine raised its agreement with merge/abandon outcomes on 17 independently held-out diffs from the current workflow ($π$ 0.16 to 0.51); on 16 diffs from older workflows, the previous judge stayed ahead, illustrating the need for recalibration as the agent evolves. By abstaining, the teacher not only removes noisy labels but also flags changes whose outcome the judge's input cannot explain, telling a team when a judge needs better inputs rather than a better prompt.

</details>


### [269] [Towards an Autonomous AI Steward for Large-Scale Physics Experiments: Integrating Causal Diagnostics, RAG, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09298)
*Oliver Noll,Nicholas Tan Jerome,Alexander Lehnen,Frank Simon,Frank Maas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Helmholtz Co-Pilot"混合多智能体系统，作为大型粒子物理实验的自主实验管家，通过异步因果诊断引擎持续监控实时数据流并定位级联报警根因，再交由基于DAG的ReAct推理层生成带覆盖率保证的建议，并在KATRIN实验中开展影子测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的粒子物理实验面临两大运维难题：机器数据流非平稳、难以监控；专家隐性知识难以保留与传承。现有手段缺乏对级联报警根因追溯与跨工况可靠预测的能力，也缺乏对AI建议的可信度约束。

Method: 系统为混合MAS架构：1）异步因果诊断引擎独立于MAS推理核心运行，持续监控DAQ、慢控制等实时硬件流，执行动态因果发现以追溯级联报警根因，并采用自适应模型选择保证工况漂移下的预测可靠性；2）检测到异常后向MAS传递结构化诊断报告；3）推理层由高吞吐C++内核与Python边缘架构组成，编排特化ReAct工作者的有向无环图；4）集成RAG作为机构记忆，并用保形预测为建议提供数学覆盖保证；5）软件强制的Human-in-the-Loop护栏。

Result: 该扩展摘要给出架构、HitL护栏集成方案，并报告在KATRIN实验中进行影子测试（ongoing shadow-testing）的进展，尚未披露定量性能结果。

Conclusion: 提出一种将因果诊断、自适应建模、多智能体推理与保形预测相结合的自主实验管家范式，可作为长周期大科学装置的知识留存与运维辅助框架；后续需通过KATRIN影子测试验证其实际有效性。

Abstract: Long-term particle physics experiments face critical operational challenges: monitoring non-stationary machine data streams and preserving tacit expert knowledge. We present the "Helmholtz Co-Pilot," a hybrid Multi-Agent System (MAS) acting as an autonomous experiment steward. The system can be triggered interactively by human operators or autonomously by an asynchronous causal diagnostics engine that runs independently of the MAS reasoning core. Continuously monitoring live hardware streams such as data acquisition and slow control, this engine performs dynamic causal discovery to trace cascading alarms back to their root causes and applies adaptive model selection to keep forecasts reliable across shifting machine states; upon detecting an anomaly, it hands a structured diagnostic report to the MAS. The reasoning layer itself relies on a high-throughput C++ core and a Python edge architecture orchestrating a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of specialized ReAct workers. It integrates a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) backbone for institutional memory and leverages conformal prediction to bound its evidence-based recommendations with mathematical coverage guarantees. This extended abstract outlines the architecture, the integration of software-enforced Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) guardrails, and ongoing shadow-testing within the KATRIN experiment.

</details>


### [270] [ABHBench: Evaluating Moral Decision-Making of Foundation Models from an Agentic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09326)
*Chaoran Li,Yukun Li,Zeyuan Zhao,Shao Zhang,Xihuai Wang,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个基于《底特律：变人》的多模态第一人称道德智能体基准 ABHBench，衡量基础模型作为决策主体的道德行为，发现情境化智能体设置会显著改变模型道德表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德评测基准多让模型以第三人称文本小故事评判人类行为，未考察模型自身作为行动主体时的道德决策，与基础模型被部署为自主智能体的现实需求脱节。

Method: 构建 ABHBench 多模态基准：以交互叙事游戏《底特律：变人》的分支剧情为基础，为模型提供视觉观测、对话、记忆与动作选择，使其在第一人称人机困境中行动。借鉴阿西莫夫机器人三定律，设计 Harmlessness（无害性）、Obedience（服从性）、Self-preservation（自保性）三项核心张力指标，以及 Autonomy（自主性）、Stability（稳定性）、Certainty（确定性）三项决策模式指标，评测 12 个基础模型，并与纯文本抽象版本对照。

Result: 75% 的模型在纯文本抽象任务与匹配的多模态 DBH 场景之间出现显著行为偏移；具备推理增强的模型在一致性与亲人类行为上并未呈现单调提升。

Conclusion: 道德行为高度依赖情境化智能体语境，仅依赖第三人称判断的评测不足，需在第一人称、多模态的具身决策场景中评估模型道德行为。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed as agentic systems that perceive context and act autonomously, raising the need to evaluate their moral behavior as active decision-makers. Existing benchmarks, however, largely ask models to judge human actions from third-person textual vignettes, leaving untested how models behave when they themselves become the acting agent. We introduce ABHBench, a multimodal benchmark built from Detroit: Become Human, an interactive narrative game, to evaluate how foundation models make moral decisions in first-person human-android dilemmas. ABHBench places foundation models in branching narrative scenarios with visual observations, dialogue, memory, and action choices. Inspired by Asimov's Three Laws of Robotics, our framework measures Harmlessness, Obedience, and Self-preservation to capture the core tensions in human-AI relations, along with Autonomy, Stability, and Certainty to assess decision-making patterns. Across 12 foundation models, we find that moral behavior changes substantially under situated agentic context: 75% of models shift significantly between a text-only abstraction and the matched multimodal DBH scenario, and reasoning-enhanced models show no monotonic gains in consistency or human-oriented behavior. These results highlight the necessity of evaluating moral behavior in situated agentic contexts rather than relying on third-person judgment alone.

</details>


### [271] [AUTEUR: Acoustic Plan Learning for Red-Teaming Text-to-Audio Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09351)
*Jiashu Zhang,Jiaming He,Zheming Zhang,Kaizheng Wang,Bing Li,Zhiyuan Zha,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"声学计划学习"（acoustic plan learning）框架，将文本到音频（TTA）模型的红队测试描述表示为结构化声学计划（前景声、上下文、人声属性），通过监督微调加音频反馈强化学习训练计划生成器，实现可复用、可修订的对抗性测试提示生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化红队测试文本到音频模型需要既能通过文本筛查、又能诱发出有害声学内容的测试提示。核心难点在于把目标模型生成的声音与两方面联系起来：(1) 可复用的测试用例生成；(2) 提示修订/迭代优化。

Method: 以结构化"声学计划"表示测试描述，显式区分前景声音、上下文与人声属性。计划生成器先做监督微调（SFT），再用音频（波形）反馈进行强化学习，期间目标音频模型保持冻结。推理阶段同一模型先生成初始计划，再依据反馈修订上下文与人声属性，同时保留前景不变。

Result: 在四个目标系统上攻击成功率分别为 TangoFlux 79.4%、Kling 95.6%；生成的测试提示可跨开源与闭源模型迁移，表明存在共通的声学脆弱性。

Conclusion: 结构化声学计划是连接波形反馈与提示生成/修订的有效中介，使红队测试既可自动化又具可迁移性，揭示了 TTA 模型间共享的声学脆弱面。

Abstract: Automated red-teaming of text-to-audio models requires test prompts that pass textual screening and expose harmful acoustic content. The central challenge is to connect the sounds produced by the target model to both reusable test generation and prompt revision. Our key idea is to represent test descriptions as structured acoustic plans that organize foreground sounds, context, and vocal attributes. To this end, we propose acoustic plan learning, a framework that learns and refines these plans using waveform feedback. We train the plan generator through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with audio feedback while keeping the target audio model fixed. At inference time, the same trained model generates initial plans and uses feedback to revise their context and vocal attributes while preserving the foreground. Experiments across four target systems show attack success rates of 79.4% on TangoFlux and 95.6% on Kling. The resulting test prompts also transfer across open-source and closed-source models, indicating shared acoustic vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [272] [Challenges, Opportunities, and Misconceptions in Neurosymbolic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09356)
*Giuseppe Marra,Pierre Genevès,Meghyn Bienvenu,Thomas Schiex,Sylvie Thiébaux,Silviu Maniu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文是一篇立场性综述，探讨在神经模型日益强大背景下神经符号AI的价值空间，聚焦语义对齐与符号推理计算开销两大挑战，并澄清可微性、独立性假设与可扩展性方面的常见误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经模型能力不断增强，但其在效率、安全性与可解释性方面仍存不足；作者希望厘清神经符号AI在何时、以何种方式仍能带来实质增益，并纠正领域内对神经符号方法的固有偏见。

Method: 非穷尽式综述与观点综合：基于2026年CNRS研讨会"NPR"工作组的讨论，梳理应对语义对齐与符号推理/学习计算成本的代表性方法论，并对可微性、独立性假设、可扩展性三类误解进行辨析。

Result: 提出两大核心挑战（神经—符号组件的语义对齐；符号推理与学习的计算成本）作为分析框架，并给出对应代表性方法路径，同时指出若干通行看法在概念上并不成立。

Conclusion: 神经符号AI应被视为与强神经模型互补而非替代的路线，其研究议程应集中于语义对齐机制与符号推理的效率化，并需以更精确的假设而非笼统质疑来评价其可行性。

Abstract: We examine opportunities for neurosymbolic AI alongside increasingly powerful neural models, particularly with regards to efficiency, safety and interpretability. We identify two major challenges: ensuring semantic alignment between neural and symbolic components, and managing the computational cost of symbolic inference and learning. We discuss representative methodological approaches to these challenges and address several common misconceptions concerning differentiability, independence assumptions, and scalability. This paper synthesizes perspectives developed through discussions in the Neurosymbolic Programming and Reasoning (NPR) working group at the 2026 CNRS workshop Neurosymbolic Methods and Their Applications in Data Science, rather than providing an exhaustive survey.

</details>


### [273] [SafeOrchestrate: Runtime Checked Intent-Driven Orchestration for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09364)
*Muhammad Ashfaq,Ahmed R. Sadik,Ewan Warburton,Pyry Kotilainen,Niko Mäkitalo,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Safe Orchestrate：面向CPS的自然语言编排参考架构，通过“意图→原子目标→可运行时检查规约→设备能力接地→SMT护栏校验”的流程，提升LLM驱动工具调用的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CPS由异构传感器、执行器、控制器与服务组成，且在运行时而非设计时组合。用自然语言编排此类系统很有吸引力，但不受约束的LLM工具调用存在风险：动作必须满足设备能力、运行时状态与安全约束。

Method: 提出Safe Orchestrate参考架构：给定NL意图，先分解为原子目标，为每个目标生成可运行时检查的规约，将规约接地到设备affordance，并在执行前依据运行时状态与安全约束校验每个候选动作。在WoT实例化中，从NL意图生成LTL风格规约，通过MCP接地的TD affordance，并用SMT后端护栏检查候选动作的可满足性。

Result: 在模拟智能家居、温室与工业自动化任务上，与“LLM直连MCP”基线对比：Safe Orchestrate在所有任务上保持100%必需动作准确率，除一项外MCP ES均为100%（该项为92%）；基线在多设备、事件触发与顺序任务上显著退化，MCP ES降至13%。

Conclusion: 将LLM规约生成与角色感知接地、运行时约束检查相结合，可在无需增强TD标注、无需规划领域专业知识的前提下，提升开放CPS编排的可靠性。

Abstract: Modern cyber physical systems (CPS) integrate heterogeneous sensors, actuators, controllers, and services, composed at runtime rather than at design time. Orchestrating such systems through natural language (NL) is attractive, but unconstrained large language model (LLM) tool use is risky because actions must respect device capabilities, runtime state, and safety constraints. We present Safe Orchestrate, a reference architecture for runtime checked, intent driven CPS orchestration. Given an NL intent, it derives atomic goals, generates a runtime checkable specification for each, grounds the specifications to device affordances, and verifies every candidate action against runtime state and safety constraints before execution. In a Web of Things (WoT) instantiation, the architecture produces Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) style specifications from the NL intent, grounds them to Thing Description (TD) affordances exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and checks candidate actions against satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) backed guardrails. We evaluate this instantiation on simulated smart home, greenhouse, and industrial automation tasks against a direct LLM to MCP baseline. Safe Orchestrate maintains 100% required action accuracy on every task and 100% MCP Execution Similarity (MCP ES) on all but one, where MCP ES is 92%. By contrast, the baseline degrades on multi device, event triggered, and ordered tasks, with MCP ES falling to 13%. These results suggest that combining LLM based specification generation with role aware grounding and runtime constraint checking improves reliability for open CPS orchestration without enriched TD annotations or planning domain expertise.

</details>


### [274] [Enabling Privacy-Preserving Natural Language Access to Industrial Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09372)
*Carlos Martín-Abellán,Mark Kröll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OpenMeter：一个模式无关（schema-agnostic）的多智能体系统（MAS），将自然语言查询翻译为可执行代码并生成人类可读洞察，可在本地受限硬件上部署，以满足能源基础设施的数据隐私要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网与能源管理人员依赖智能电表数据做异常检测、性能监控与决策，但从大规模异构遥测数据库中提取洞察需要高级 SQL 与数据工程能力，非技术人员存在瓶颈；同时关键能源基础设施的严格隐私法规与安全约束通常禁止将敏感用户数据通过云端大模型处理。

Method: 构建 schema-agnostic 的多智能体系统 OpenMeter，将自然语言查询端到端转换为可执行代码与自然语言洞察；在多种场景下开展消融实验（multi-scenario ablation），对比完整多智能体编排、最强单智能体配置以及选择性启用各组件的方案，并分别在资源受限本地硬件与前沿规模（frontier scale）模型上评测，指标为执行准确率 EX-R。

Result: 在资源受限的本地硬件上，完整多智能体编排相对最强单智能体配置取得最大观测精度提升（EX-R +6.1 pp）；在前沿规模下，各组件仍有价值，但需选择性启用。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排的收益依赖于部署条件：全编排的成本仅在隐私要求强制本地执行时才明确合理；前沿规模模型下应采用选择性组件启用的折中策略。OpenMeter 降低了非专家人员与复杂遥测数据库交互的门槛。

Abstract: Energy management officers and grid operators increasingly rely on smart meter data for anomaly detection, performance monitoring, and strategic decision-making. However, extracting actionable insights from large-scale, heterogeneous telemetry databases requires advanced SQL and data engineering expertise, creating a critical bottleneck for non-technical personnel. Furthermore, the strict data privacy regulations and security constraints inherent to critical energy infrastructure often prohibit the routing of sensitive consumer data through cloud-based Large Language Models (LLMs). To address these challenges, we present OpenMeter, a schema-agnostic Multi-Agent System (MAS) designed to translate natural language queries into executable code and human-readable insights. This architecture lowers the technical barrier for day-to-day utility operations by empowering non-expert personnel to interact with complex databases through intuitive natural language dialogue, transforming raw telemetry into a highly valuable tool for routine grid workflows and strategic decisions. Multi-scenario ablation studies over this environment demonstrate that full multi-agent orchestration shows its largest observed accuracy gains on resource-constrained local hardware (+6.1 pp EX-R over the strongest single-agent configuration); at frontier scale, individual components retain value but should be enabled selectively, indicating that the cost of full orchestration is justified specifically where privacy demands local execution.

</details>


### [275] [KineCouple: Substrate-conditioned residual readout for enzyme kinetic prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09377)
*Chun Kang,Yunbo Zhang,Fanhu Zeng,Jian Liu,Siwei Deng,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KineCouple，用底物作为query、残基作为key/value的交叉注意力实现底物条件化的残基聚合，以改进酶动力学参数(kcat、Km)预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有酶动力学参数预测方法通常在将蛋白特征与底物特征融合前先对残基级特征做池化，这种与底物无关的池化无法在聚合时根据底物调整残基权重，可能稀释与特定酶-底物对相关的信息；而残基对催化的贡献本身可能依赖底物。

Method: KineCouple以底物为query、残基级特征为key和value进行交叉注意力，使底物在聚合前引导残基选择；保留全局蛋白表示，并加入门控校正项（条件化读出与均匀读出的差值）。ProtT5与MolT5保持冻结，kcat与Km分别用独立模型在log尺度上预测。

Result: 在CataPro基准的折外池化预测上，相对复现的CataPro：kcat的RMSE由1.333降至1.330，PCC由0.492升至0.496；Km的RMSE由0.998降至0.997，PCC由0.633升至0.634；两项任务的Spearman相关也均有提升。

Conclusion: 底物条件化的交叉注意力聚合可在不微调预训练编码器的前提下带来一致的性能改进，但提升幅度较小，接近噪声水平，实际价值有待更大规模验证。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of enzyme kinetic parameters can support enzyme engineering and metabolic modeling by complementing experimental characterization. Existing prediction methods often pool residue-level protein features before combining them with substrate features. Such substrate-independent pooling cannot adapt residue weighting to the substrate during aggregation, potentially diluting information relevant to a specific enzyme-substrate pair. Since the contributions of individual residues to catalysis can depend on the substrate, substrate-conditioned aggregation offers a way to emphasize residue features relevant to the prediction. We introduce KineCouple, which uses substrate queries and residue-level keys and values in cross-attention, allowing the substrate to guide residue selection before aggregation. The model preserves the global protein representation and adds a gated correction defined as the difference between the conditioned readout and a uniform readout. ProtT5 and MolT5 remain frozen, and separate models predict kcat and Km on a logarithmic scale. In pooled out-of-fold predictions on the CataPro benchmarks, KineCouple reduces kcat RMSE from 1.333 to 1.330 and increases PCC from 0.492 to 0.496 relative to reproduced CataPro. For Km, RMSE decreases from 0.998 to 0.997 and PCC increases from 0.633 to 0.634. Spearman correlation also improves for both tasks.

</details>


### [276] [Exploring Weight-Space Programming: Capabilities, Generalization, and Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09382)
*Junru Hu,Cheng Tang,Wei Li,Hongze Zhu,Chun-Mei Feng,Qi Zhang,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地实证研究了Weight-Space Programming（WSP）——让推理智能体直接检视与修改神经网络参数的方法，在7个基准、2个模型规模（Qwen3.5-0.8B / 9B）、16条编辑轨迹上的能力边界、跨任务泛化性与实际局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSP作为梯度适配的替代方案，允许智能体直接编辑权重，但其真实能力、跨任务泛化性和实际局限尚缺乏充分理解，作者因此开展系统性调查以厘清其可行性与边界。

Method: 在7个基准（含MATH-500、GPQA Diamond、IFEval、SciBench、SciCode等）与两个模型规模（Qwen3.5-0.8B、Qwen3.5-9B）上设计16条权重编辑轨迹，进行受控的权重空间干预实验；在完整留出集（held-out）上评估效果，并测试由数学任务编辑所得检查点在其他任务上的零样本迁移表现。

Result: (1) 高度局部化的干预即可带来显著增益：MATH-500提升29.45、GPQA Diamond提升4.42、IFEval提升36.72、SciBench提升17.11个百分点；仅修改1,023个参数即取得29.45点提升。(2) 跨任务泛化有潜力但不均衡：数学编辑后的检查点使GPQA Diamond、IFEval、SciBench分别提升3.03、12.18、20.10点，而SciCode性能不变。(3) 编辑有效但困难且不稳定：不同轨迹结果差异大，16条轨迹中有6条因候选改进无法可靠验证而保留原始权重。

Conclusion: WSP在高度局部化的模型适配方面具有潜力，但在干预发现、可靠性验证与跨任务迁移方面仍存在重要挑战；其结果提示需进一步研究编辑策略的稳健性与可验证性。

Abstract: Weight-Space Programming (WSP) enables reasoning agents to directly inspect and modify neural network parameters, offering an alternative to gradient-based model adaptation. However, its capabilities, cross-task generalization, and practical limitations remain insufficiently understood. We systematically investigate WSP through 16 editing trajectories across seven benchmarks and two model scales (Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-9B). Our findings reveal three key insights. First, WSP can achieve substantial performance gains through highly localized interventions. On full held-out evaluations, accuracy improves by up to 29.45, 4.42, 36.72, and 17.11 percentage points on MATH-500, GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench, respectively. Notably, modifying only 1,023 parameters yields a 29.45-point improvement. Second, WSP exhibits promising but uneven cross-task generalization. A Math-edited checkpoint improves GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench by 3.03, 12.18, and 20.10 points, respectively, without further editing, while leaving SciCode performance unchanged. Third, effective editing remains challenging and inconsistent. Outcomes vary substantially across trajectories, and six of the 16 trajectories retain the original weights when candidate improvements cannot be reliably validated. Together, these results highlight WSP's potential for highly localized model adaptation while revealing important challenges in intervention discovery, reliability, and cross-task transfer.
  Homepage: https://wsp-scope.github.io/ GitHub: https://github.com/WSP-Scope/WSP-Scope-Project

</details>


### [277] [Against All Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09385)
*David Lopez-Paz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文是一篇立场论文，主张以"跨环境变换的不变性"替代"单一分布下的概率"作为语言模型陈述真伪的判据，并讨论其对OOD泛化、推理期检验与持续学习的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然是大语言模型的主导训练目标，但似然既非真理的必要条件也非充分条件：罕见事件每天都在发生，而高频重复的陈述也可能为假。因此需要为"真"寻找独立于概率的判据。

Method: 概念性论证与文献综合。援引哲学中将客观性归于不变性而非概率的传统（Nozick的客观事实观、Wigner的不变性原理、Poincaré与Cassirer的结构实在论），并提出判据：陈述的真伪应由其在环境相关变换下的不变性决定，而非其在单一分布下的概率；进而与机器学习中的分布外泛化与不变性学习文献对接，勾勒在推理期用语言模型检验不变性的思路。

Result: 论文为立场性工作，未报告实验数据；其"结果"在于建立"似然—不变性"的替代框架：给出不变性判据的哲学依据、与不变性学习/OOD泛化的理论对应，并指出该判据在推理时可操作化的路径，以及对持续学习的含义（如以不变性而非似然作为保留与更新知识的准则）。

Conclusion: 似然不等于真理，应以跨环境变换的不变性作为判断陈述真实性的核心标准；这一视角可统一解释OOD泛化与不变性学习的价值，并为推理期验证和持续学习提供新的原则性方向。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood is the dominant training principle for large language models, yet likelihood is neither necessary nor sufficient for truth. Unlikely events happen every day, and frequently repeated statements can be false. Drawing on a philosophical tradition that ties objectivity to invariance rather than probability (from Nozick's account of objective facts and Wigner's principles of invariance to the structural realism of Poincaré and Cassirer), we argue that the truth of a statement should be judged not by its probability under a single distribution, but by its invariance across relevant transformations of the environment. We connect this view to machine learning literature on out-of-distribution generalization and invariant learning, sketch how invariance could be tested at inference time with language models, and discuss implications for continual learning.

</details>


### [278] [Travel Cost Calibration for Multi-Agent Target Assignment and Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09396)
*Yu Kumagai,Ryohei Banno,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式TAPF方法，利用快速次优MAPF求解器反馈校正的旅行代价估计来改进目标分配，使分配依据智能体实际付出代价而非单智能体最短路代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAPF（目标分配与路径规划）中，常规做法是先以"智能体—目标"单智能体最短路距离之和最小化做分配，再解MAPF。但最短路距离只是忽略智能体间干扰的代理指标，而真实代价取决于所有智能体的分配与路由，因此基于代理的分配难以得到好的最终解。

Method: 提出迭代式TAPF框架：以快速、次优的MAPF求解器给出的实际反馈来校准各"智能体—目标"对的旅行代价估计，并在迭代中据此更新目标分配，从而把智能体间的交互影响纳入分配决策；分配与路径求解交替进行直至收敛或达到迭代上限。

Result: 实验表明：在小规模实例上，所得解接近最优；在较大规模实例上，解质量优于现有TAPF规划器。
