However, studying human-in-the-loop robot learning is difficult: real-world experiments are slow and expensive, so most algorithm development relies on simulated human models. We show that common human modeling assumptions do not match how real people intervene in practice. Through analyzing intervention data from human participants, we find that humans tend to intervene when agent progress stalls, and that their corrective actions are often suboptimal. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a progress-based model of human intervention that achieves higher rank correlation with real-user algorithm rankings, enabling realistic algorithm benchmarking and development in simulation. Building on these insights, we propose Q-OIL, a method that adds optimistic bonuses to intervention transitions in reinforcement learning and softly guides exploration toward human corrections. We find that decoupled critics are critical for stability because they prevent the bonuses from backing up to pre-intervention states. As a result, Q-OIL learns more efficiently than competitive baselines, achieving 10-50% higher success in simulation and on real robots, and improving pretrained VLAs on two bimanual manipulation tasks. As we ground both evaluation and algorithms in realistic human behavior, this work provides a practical path toward scalable robot learning from interventions.

</details>


### [313] [Meituan-Robotics-0: A Vision-Language-Action Foundation Model for Desktop Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08577)
*Meituan Robotics Team,Caicheng Wang,Chen Zhao,Chengpeng Jin,Duo Xu,Fengjiao Chen,Guanglu Wan,Heqing Yang,Jia Wang,Jiacong Zhou,Jie Hu,Jiyuan Ru,Lingfeng Zhang,Linjiang Huang,Manyuan Zhang,Ningchu Liu,Shijie Zhou,Si Liu,Shuo Li,Tianyi Zhang,Xi Zhang,Xin Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Yanbin Zeng,Yang Yang,Yang Yi,Yanli Tan,Yifu Xu,Yimeng Jia,Yuchen Tang,Yuchen Xie,Yuxiang Zheng,Zerwei Chng,Zilong Huang,Zipeng Ye,Ziyuan Qin,Ziqin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MR0 is a vision-language-action foundation model and embodied-agent system for stationary-base desktop manipulation, trained with 147M vision-language samples and 15,060 hours of robot trajectories across 12 embodiments via a three-stage recipe.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior VLA models lack a systematic recipe for aligning large-scale multimodal pretraining with robot behavior, and struggle with heterogeneous embodiments, variable inference latency, and interactive correction data without expensive value learning/advantage labeling. The paper also seeks to solve persistent task-state tracking and conditional task resumption in agentic deployment.

Method: Three-stage training: (1) discrete action-token pre-training aligning multimodal representations with behavior; (2) flow-matching mid-training transferring priors to continuous cross-embodiment control; (3) real-world post-training policy adaptation. Introduces Rollback, pairing replaced policy segments with human corrections to supply positive/negative supervision for CFG-DAgger without value learning or advantage labeling. Adds a unified end-effector interface for heterogeneous embodiments, asynchronous action-chunk merging for smooth execution under variable latency, and an event-driven agent maintaining persistent task events with cross-task retrieval and conditional resumption.

Result: 93.6% success on RoboTwin 2.0; 5th place in the RoboDojo VLA track. Under matched HG-DAgger post-training, higher average success rate and progress score than π0.5 across three real-robot tasks. On Ethernet insertion, CFG-DAgger with Rollback data reaches 70% success and 0.90 progress score. Qualitative agent deployments show cross-task event retrieval and recovery from temporarily unsatisfied conditions.

Conclusion: MR0 validates that a staged discrete-to-continuous, cross-embodiment training pipeline combined with Rollback-based interactive correction and event-driven agent design yields strong desktop manipulation performance and practical resumption/recovery capabilities.

Abstract: We present Meituan-Robotics-0 (MR0), a vision-language-action foundation model and embodied-agent system for stationary-base desktop manipulation. MR0 combines 147 million vision-language samples with 15,060 hours of curated robot trajectories spanning 12 embodiments. A three-stage training recipe aligns multimodal representations with robot behavior through discrete action-token pre-training, transfers these priors to continuous cross-embodiment control through flow-matching mid-training, and adapts the policy through real-world post-training. We introduce Rollback, which pairs replaced policy segments with human corrections to provide positive and negative supervision for CFG-DAgger without value learning or advantage labeling. A unified end-effector interface supports heterogeneous embodiments, while asynchronous action-chunk merging maintains smooth execution under variable inference latency. An event-driven agent maintains persistent task events and reassesses plans when progress or feasibility changes, enabling cross-task retrieval and conditional task resumption. MR0 achieves 93.6% success on RoboTwin 2.0 and ranks fifth in the RoboDojo VLA track. Under matched HG-DAgger post-training, MR0 achieves higher average success rate and progress score than $π_{0.5}$ across three real-robot tasks. On Ethernet insertion, CFG-DAgger trained with Rollback data reaches 70% success and a progress score of 0.90. Qualitative agent deployments demonstrate cross-task event retrieval and recovery from temporarily unsatisfied task conditions.

</details>


### [314] [CS-Pi: Constraint-Satisfying Flow Policy Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08654)
*Soumojit Bhattacharya,Oliver Kroemer,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CS-Pi采样器，将投影与拒绝结合，在flow-matching流式采样的加噪过程中反复"抽取-投影-重噪"，使推理期新增约束下的动作同时具备安全性与策略可信性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现场部署的flow-matching策略常需满足演示中未出现的新约束（如避障），重训练不现实，只能推理期施加。梯度引导在策略不确定（左右均可绕行）时会把干净动作估计为两侧均值，直接穿过障碍；纯拒绝在安全动作稀少时等待不可行；纯投影高效但结果未必可信。

Method: 核心洞察是flow-matching采样天然支持投影与拒绝的融合。提出CS-Pi（draw-project-renoise）：每次采样先投影到最近的安全动作，按投影位移量相应地回填噪声，再用未经修改的策略重新去噪，循环直至得到安全样本；只需替换投影步骤即可扩展到连续代价约束。

Result: 在2D诊断任务、7自由度货架抓取放置、朝向偏好任务及零样本Franka实机部署上验证，CS-Pi在货架任务达81%安全成功率、实机达55%，而纯投影分别为55%和40%，同时保持动作可信性。

Conclusion: 通过在采样的中间噪声状态上投影而非在干净动作估计上投影，并按位移缩放重噪，CS-Pi兼顾了投影的效率与拒绝的可信性，为推理期约束满足提供了无需重训练、无需修改策略的通用方案。

Abstract: A flow-matching policy deployed in the field must often satisfy a constraint that was absent from its demonstrations, such as avoiding an obstacle it has never seen. Retraining for every new constraint is impractical, so the constraint must be enforced at inference time. Enforcing it must not discard what the policy learned: an action the policy would rarely sample may avoid the obstacle yet fail the task. We therefore want actions that are both safe, satisfying the constraint, and plausible, remaining likely under the policy. Gradient guidance, the most common approach, steers noisy states, so it must evaluate its cost on the policy's running estimate of the clean action; when the policy is uncertain between passing left or right of an obstacle, this estimate is their average and can pass straight through it. We instead build on rejection and projection. Rejection redraws actions until one is safe: every output is plausible, but the wait is intractable when safe actions are rare. Projection moves an action to the nearest safe one: it is efficient, but the result need not be plausible. Our key insight is that flow-matching sampling lets us combine the two, keeping projection's efficiency and rejection's plausibility. CS-Pi, our draw-project-renoise sampler, projects each draw to the nearest safe action, adds noise back scaled to how far the projection moved it, and re-runs the unmodified policy until a draw is safe. Replacing only the projection step extends CS-Pi to continuous costs. Across a 2D diagnostic, a 7-DoF shelf pick-and-place, an orientation preference, and a zero-shot Franka deployment, CS-Pi reaches 81% safe success on the shelf and 55% on hardware, against 55% and 40% for projection, while staying plausible.

</details>


### [315] [ForPC: Force-Aware Whole-Body Control for Precise Humanoid Cart Pushing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08664)
*ZhenYu Zou,Xing Liu,ShiChuang Wang,YunLong Dong,LiNan Deng,Feng Hua,Jun Wan,Cheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForPC——一种力感知全身控制框架，用两阶段强化学习实现人形机器人双臂推车：先训练运动先验提供平衡支持，再由无外力传感器推断的力/状态隐变量驱动残差策略协调手臂、腰与腿，仿真中速度RMSE较基线降低68.2%、横向RMSE降低74.2%，并零微调迁移到Unitree G1，以0.5 m/s推动载重30 kg的推车。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业、物流与医疗场景中的重物搬运需要人形机器人直接操作现有轮式推车，以避免改造工作环境；但双臂抓握形成力耦合闭环，驱动推车的接触力同时扰动平衡，且负载变化会改变耦合动力学，使推车任务难以稳定控制。

Method: 两阶段强化学习：第一阶段在多样手部负载与上半身姿态下训练运动先验（locomotion prior）以提供平衡支撑；第二阶段从机载观测（无外部力传感器）推断力隐变量与状态隐变量，输入残差策略，输出有界的手臂、腰部、腿部调整量及基座速度指令；多目标奖励惩罚双臂内力对顶、抓握滑移与执行器饱和，从而在无人类示范条件下按需施加接触力。

Result: sim-to-sim实验中，推车速度RMSE相对仅运动基线（Loco-only）最多降低68.2%，多弯道路线横向跟踪RMSE最多降低74.2%；仅用仿真训练的策略零样本迁移至实体Unitree G1，无需真实数据训练或微调，在无负载特定调整下以0.5 m/s推动载重达30 kg的推车。

Conclusion: 力感知全身控制可在力耦合与负载变化条件下实现稳定的人形推车作业，并具备仿真到实机的零样本迁移能力，表明人形机器人有望在日常生活场景中替代人完成高效物料搬运。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are expected to take over strenuous material handling in manufacturing, logistics, and healthcare, where operating existing wheeled carts would let them move heavy loads without modifying the workplace. However, humanoid cart pushing remains challenging because the bimanual grasp closes a force-coupled loop in which the contact wrenches that drive the cart also disturb the robot's balance, and payload changes alter the coupled dynamics. We propose ForPC, a force-aware whole-body control framework that regulates cart velocity through two-stage reinforcement learning. A locomotion prior is first trained under varied hand loads and upper-body postures to provide balance support. A force latent and a state latent are then inferred from onboard observations, without external force sensors, and fed to a residual policy that coordinates bounded arm, waist, and leg adjustments with base-velocity commands. Multi-objective rewards penalize bimanual internal opposition, grasp slip, and actuator saturation, encouraging the policy to apply contact force on demand without human demonstrations. Sim-to-sim experiments show that ForPC reduces cart-velocity RMSE by up to 68.2% relative to a locomotion-only baseline (Loco-only) and cross-track RMSE by up to 74.2% on the multi-bend route. Trained solely in simulation, the policy also transfers to a physical Unitree G1 without real-world training or fine-tuning and, with no payload-specific adjustment, pushes a cart carrying up to 30 kg at 0.5 m/s. These results suggest that humanoid robots could eventually replace humans for efficient material handling in daily life.

</details>


### [316] [National and State-Level Approaches to Automated Vehicle Certification: A Comparative Framework for State Operational Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08681)
*Sk Nahia Ahsan,Md Abdullah Al Hasan,Danjue Chen,George List*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 美国L4-5级自动驾驶部署缺乏统一联邦认证框架，各州监管差异显著。本文梳理州级法规，提出三种州认证模型（基线合规、行政自认证加持续监管、牌照许可授权），比较国际实践，并为北卡罗来纳州提供监管策略建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国AV部署扩张但缺少联邦统一认证制度，州级授权与监管差异大，导致跨州一致性不足；北卡罗来纳州等地区亟需基于实证的监管路径选择依据。

Method: 文献与政策文本分析：检索公开的州法规、行政法规、机构指南与联邦政策文件；选取10个具有代表性的州，采用涵盖运行范围、治理与监督、联邦互动、实施效果的结构化框架进行比较；并以欧盟、中国、日本、澳大利亚的认证实践作对照。

Result: 识别出三种州级认证模型，监管介入程度依次递增：基线法律合规、行政自认证配合持续监督、部署前需正式机构审查的许可授权；北卡州目前属基线合规模型。国际框架趋向场景化测试、基于ODD的批准、安全案例文档、网络安全保障与全生命周期监管，但直接移植至美国存在局限。

Conclusion: 提出三模型类型学以系统比较州级AV认证路径，并将州实践与国际框架整合，为评估各辖区在监管介入度、治理复杂度与机构职责上的差异提供一致基准，可支撑北卡州未来监管策略设计。

Abstract: Automated vehicle (AV) deployment in the United States is expanding without a unified federal certification framework, leading to substantial variation in how states authorize and oversee AV testing and deployment. This paper examines how U.S. states regulate Society of Automotive Engineers (SAE) Level 4-5 automated driving systems (ADS), identifies common certification approaches, compares them with international practices, and evaluates the implications for North Carolina's future regulatory strategy. Publicly available state statutes, administrative regulations, agency guidance, and federal policy documents were reviewed. Ten states, selected to capture the range of regulatory approaches, were analyzed using a structured framework covering operational scope, governance and oversight, federal interaction, and implementation outcomes. Certification practices in the European Union, China, Japan, and Australia were reviewed for comparative context. Three state certification models were identified, reflecting increasing regulatory involvement: baseline legal compliance, administrative self-certification with ongoing oversight, and permit-based authorization requiring formal agency review before deployment. North Carolina currently aligns with the baseline compliance model. International frameworks are converging toward scenario-based testing, operational-design-domain-specific approval, safety-case documentation, cybersecurity assurance, and lifecycle oversight, although direct transferability to the U.S. is limited. The paper develops a three-model typology for comparing state AV certification approaches and integrates state practices with international frameworks, providing a consistent basis for evaluating differences in regulatory involvement, governance complexity, and institutional responsibilities across jurisdictions.

</details>


### [317] [VICTR: Value-Informed In-Context Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08698)
*Luca Macesanu,Tianluo Zhang,Aditya Patil,Archit Sharma,Kevin Angers,Christopher M. Clark,Chen Feng,Juexiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VICTR 框架，利用基于参考示范视频的进度估计器（IC-VFE）检索与当前执行阶段对齐的示范片段，为 VLA 模型提供上下文，从而在无需微调的情况下实现未见任务的上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的后训练方法使预训练 VLA 具备上下文学习能力，但检索效果依赖所选取的上下文是否与当前执行阶段相关，而单纯视觉相似度难以刻画任务阶段相关性。

Method: 构建 In-Context Value Function Estimator（IC-VFE），以参考示范视频、语言指令和当前观测为输入估计任务相对进度；由于进度估计以参考示范为条件，可对训练时未见任务实现当前状态与示范执行的对齐。随后依据估计进度选取对应执行阶段的示范片段，输入经上下文动作生成后训练的 π0.5 策略。

Result: 在未见过的域外真实世界操作任务上，成功率由微调 π0.5 基线的 6.2% 提升至 23.8%，任务进度率由 28.9% 提升至 55.5%（提升超过一倍）。

Conclusion: 以进度（而非纯视觉相似度）为检索依据可显著增强 VLA 的上下文学习泛化能力，在域外真实操作任务上同时提升成功率与执行进度。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot foundation models have highlighted the potential of in-context learning (ICL) for adapting robot behavior from demonstrations without further fine-tuning. Previous work has used retrieval-based post-training to equip pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models with the capability for ICL by teaching them to condition action predictions on retrieved visually-relevant demonstration chunks. However, the effectiveness of retrieval hinges on selecting context relevant to the current execution stage, which visual similarity alone may not capture. We introduce VICTR, a framework enabling in-context learning in VLAs via progress-informed context retrieval built around an In-Context Value Function Estimator (IC-VFE). IC-VFE conditions on a reference demonstration video, a language instruction and the current observation to estimate task-relative progress. By conditioning progress estimation on a reference demonstration, IC-VFE can align the current state with a demonstrated execution even for tasks unseen during training. VICTR uses the estimated progress to select demonstration chunks at corresponding stages of execution and supplies them to a $π_{0.5}$ policy post-trained for in-context action generation. On unseen out-of-domain real-world manipulation tasks, VICTR raises the success rate from 6.2% for a fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ baseline to 23.8% and more than doubles the task progress rate, from 28.9% to 55.5%. Project page with code, data and videos: https://ai4ce.github.io/VICTR/

</details>


### [318] [It Takes Two to Tangram: Multi-Agent Object Centric Reinforcement Learning for Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08814)
*Paul Knaußt,Kay Pompetzki,Nico Bohlinger,Niklas Funk,Cristiana de Farias,Rolf Findeisen,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以物体为中心的强化学习框架，将每个零件视为一个智能体，用图神经网络编码零件、目标位姿及空间关系，并通过每体PPO与中心化图评论家学习共享策略，实现平面七巧板装配的并行运动与时序协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何装配需要在紧凑间隙下避免碰撞与死锁，要求精细的时空协调；传统方法难以在单一反馈策略中同时学习运动控制与时间调度。

Method: 多智能体强化学习：以零件为智能体，GNN表示零件、目标位姿与空间关系，输出各部件平移与角速度；采用每体近端策略优化（per-agent PPO）配中心化图评论家；通过程序化生成目标构型与随机初始布局进行训练评估；以平面Tangram装配为试验平台；消融关系结构与多智能体优化，并与采样规划对比。

Result: 仿真中实现并行装配；策略可迁移至真实机器人进行顺序执行；消融实验验证关系结构与多智能体优化的作用，并与基于采样的规划方法进行比较。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的表述将学习到的装配协调与底层物理控制解耦，使仿真中学习的并行协调策略能有效迁移到真实机器人的顺序执行，为多零件几何装配提供可行范式。

Abstract: Geometric assembly requires fine spatiotemporal coordination to place parts without causing collisions or deadlocks under tight clearances. To address this, we present an object-centric reinforcement learning framework that treats each part as an agent and learns a shared policy for continuous assembly control. A graph neural network represents the pieces, their target poses, and their spatial relationships, and predicts translational and angular velocities for each component. Per-agent proximal policy optimization with a centralized graph critic directly learns motion and temporal coordination within a single feedback policy. We study this formulation through planar Tangram assembly, where pieces must slide and rotate into a given configuration while remaining on the assembly surface. Procedural generation of target configurations and randomized initial arrangements support training and evaluation across diverse assembly problems. Our experiments isolate the roles of relational structure and multi-agent optimization, and compare learned control with sampling-based planning. We demonstrate concurrent assembly in simulation and transfer the policy to sequential execution on a real robot, as our object-centric formulation decouples learned assembly coordination from low-level physical control. Project page: https://tangrambench.org/

</details>


### [319] [ActPrune: Action-Grounded Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08827)
*Sungwon Moon,Minji Kim,Hyemin Boo,Daeun Song,Jiyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、在视觉编码器之前做决策的Token剪枝方法，利用上一步预测的动作块作为几何先验，对第三人称视图保留末端轨迹相关patch，对手腕视图剪除运动补偿后不变的patch，实现最高1.61倍加速与59.5% FLOPs降低且成功率几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型的推理开销限制了闭环控制中的响应速度。已有视觉Token剪枝方法依赖编码后的视觉嵌入或注意力来估计冗余，必须等视觉编码器处理完整个观测后才生效，剪枝收益被前置编码开销摊薄，无法降低编码器本身的计算量。

Method: 免训练、剪枝决策前置于视觉编码器的Token剪枝：以上一控制步输出的动作块（在当前观测编码前已可得）作为几何先验，它刻画相邻观测间末端执行器与相机的运动。再按视角非对称处理——固定第三人称视角沿投影的末端轨迹保留patch并保留语义锚点；手腕视角在补偿动作引起的相机运动后剪除保持不变的patch。

Result: 在LIBERO与VLABench两个仿真环境及四项真实机器人任务上，取得最高1.61×加速、59.5% FLOPs缩减，成功率下降可忽略。

Conclusion: 将剪枝决策从编码后前移至编码前的动作几何先验驱动，可有效降低VLA闭环推理成本，且无需额外训练、跨仿真与真机任务泛化良好，为动作条件化的视觉计算分配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models perform versatile manipulation tasks from language instructions, but their inference cost limits how fast a robot can respond in closed-loop control. Visual token pruning reduces this cost; however, prior methods estimate token redundancy from encoded visual embeddings or attention, only after the visual encoder has processed the entire observation. We propose \ours, a training-free token pruning method that moves the pruning decision ahead of the vision encoder by grounding it in a geometric prior derived from the robot's own action. The action chunk predicted at the previous control step is already available before the current observation is encoded, and it specifies the end-effector and camera motion between consecutive observations. The same action affects the two standard camera views asymmetrically: in the fixed third-person view, \ours retains patches along the projected end-effector trajectory together with semantic anchors, while in the wrist view, it prunes patches that remain unchanged after compensating for the action-induced camera motion. Extensive experiments on two simulation environments (LIBERO and VLABench) and four real-robot tasks demonstrate that \ours achieves up to 1.61 $\times$ speedup and 59.5\% FLOPs reduction with negligible degradation in success rates.

</details>


### [320] [STRIDE: Learning Humanoid Locomotion in Minutes with Acceleration-Based Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08857)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 STRIDE 方法，仅依赖机载 IMU 数据、以跟踪指令加速度剖面为奖励，结合 off-policy 算法 XQC、高增益关节位置控制与左右对称增强，在真实 Unitree G1 与 Booster T1 人形机器人上从数分钟样本中学会行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在真实人形机器人上学习运动可规避 sim-to-real gap，但主流 RL 运动奖励追踪基座速度，而基座速度无法在无外部位姿捕捉系统或良好标定状态估计器的情况下机载测量。

Method: 用"跟踪指令加速度剖面"替代基座速度追踪作为奖励，仅需机载 IMU；采用 off-policy RL 算法 XQC；将关节位置控制器设为高增益，并结合左右对称数据增强。

Result: 在仿真中，STRIDE 在约 14 分钟样本内使 Unitree G1 与 Booster T1 学会行走；相比不含高增益与对称增强的版本，训练期跌倒减少 81%，而无这两项组件时学习需超过 1 小时。

Conclusion: 以加速度剖面奖励配合 XQC、高增益控制与对称增强，可实现无需外部状态估计的高样本效率真实人形机器人运动学习。

Abstract: Learning locomotion directly on a humanoid robot is a promising direction to avoid the sim-to-real gap. However, locomotion rewards in Reinforcement Learning (RL) typically track the base velocity, which cannot be measured onboard without an external motion capture system or well-calibrated state estimator. This is why we introduce \method{}, which learns humanoid locomotion from minutes of samples with a reward that only requires the onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU). Instead of tracking the base velocity, STRIDE rewards the robot for following a commanded acceleration profile. To learn efficiently from this reward, we use the off-policy RL algorithm XQC and combine a high gain setting for the joint position controllers with left-right symmetry augmentation. In simulation, STRIDE learns to walk on the Unitree G1 and Booster T1 humanoids within 14 minutes of samples. Compared to learning without these two components, it reduces the falls during training by 81%, while without them the robot learns only in over an hour.

</details>


### [321] [What Limits mmWave Human-State Sensing for Robot Perception?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08863)
*Shuntian Zheng,Guangming Wang,Hongyang He,Shuai He,Yu Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出统一散射场建模与Cramér-Rao型联合定位误差下界框架，将帧融合、多视角、回波选择与位姿估计等方法视为对该误差下界的"匹配探针"，在三个数据集与实机部署中验证各策略对误差的影响方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人（助残、搜救）需在烟雾、黑暗与隐私敏感场景中可靠感知人体状态，摄像头与LiDAR受限，毫米波雷达可替代；但适配机器人尺寸、功耗、成本的紧凑阵列孔径受限，数米处联合定位误差达分米级，危及安全裕度与跟踪精度。既有流水线逐项考察帧融合、多视角、回波选择或更强估计器，未将其效应关联到统一的测量极限，也未区分"来自额外信息的增益"与"来自估计器补全的增益"。

Method: 将人体与环境建模为单一时变散射场，推导联合定位的Cramér-Rao型误差下界，其自变量包括带宽、孔径、信噪比、融合帧数、相位相干性与保留回波的可见性；随后把现有帧融合、多视角、回波选择与位姿估计方法作为该下界的匹配探针进行对照实验，而非相互竞争的流水线。

Result: 在三个数据集与一次实机部署上，各策略使联合定位误差按预测方向变化，例如引入第二个视角将平均每关节位置误差从约97 mm降至65 mm；更强估计器主要通过对测量无法分辨的关节进行补全来降低整体误差。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人社区在部署可靠毫米波人体感知时，判断应优先投入观测设计还是估计器容量提供了有理论依据的决策基础。

Abstract: Assistive robots and search-and-rescue platforms require reliable human state sensing in smoke, darkness, and privacy-sensitive environments, where cameras and LiDAR may be unreliable Millimeter-wave radar offers an alternative that works without ambient light, penetrates smoke, and does not capture visual appearance. However, the compact arrays that fit robot size, power, and cost budgets have limited apertures, producing joint-localization errors on the order of decimeters at a few meters, large enough to compromise the safety margins and tracking accuracy a robot depends on. Existing pipelines reduce pose error through frame fusion, multiple viewpoints, return selection, or stronger estimators, but examine one technique at a time, without relating its effect to a shared measurement limit or separating gains from added information from gains from estimator completion. We address this gap with a framework that models the human and environment as one time-varying scattering field and states a Cramer-Rao type error floor for joint localization as a function of bandwidth, aperture, signal-to-noise ratio, fused frame count, phase coherence, and visibility of retained returns. We then use existing frame fusion, multi viewpoint, return selection, and pose estimation methods as matched probes of this floor rather than as competing pipelines. Under the evaluated conditions, experiments on three datasets and a live deployment show these strategies move joint localization error in the predicted directions, for example lowering mean per-joint position error from about 97 to 65 mm with a second viewpoint, while a stronger estimator mainly lowers overall error by completing joints the measurement cannot resolve. These results give the robotics community a principled basis for deciding whether to prioritize observation design or estimator capacity when deploying reliable mmWave human sensing.

</details>


### [322] [Lodos-v2: An Efficient Primal Formulation of the Exact Frictional Contact Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08868)
*Onur Beker,Andreas René Geist,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lodos-v2 是面向大规模并行刚体接触动力学的高吞吐 GPU 仿真框架，通过"外层近端最小化 + 级联凸松弛"求解精确摩擦接触问题，并将接触组重参数化推广到锥互补问题的原始形式，从而可与关节限位、焊接等全功能模拟器约束整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前身 Lodos-v1 存在两处缺陷：(1) 仅求解接触动力学的标准凸松弛，继承抬离(lift-off)、法向人工柔度及切向蠕变(creep)等物理伪影；(2) 其接触组重参数化方案对每个碰撞对仅用单一因子缩放弹簧-阻尼力，无法与其他约束（关节限位、焊接等）联合求解，难以构成完整模拟器。论文旨在同时解决这两点，并保持大量精细网格碰撞场景下的高吞吐能力。

Method: 贡献一：提出高效算法求解精确摩擦接触问题——在外层近端最小化循环中，以原始形式(primal form)顺序求解一串级联的凸松弛问题。贡献二：将 Lodos-v1 的接触组重参数化从原方案扩展到基于锥互补的接触动力学原始表述，使其可与全功能模拟器的其他约束集成。整体实现于 GPU 并行架构。

Result: 实验表明，上述两项贡献相对既有成熟的刚体仿真框架带来了吞吐量提升(throughput improvements)；代码开源。

Conclusion: Lodos-v2 以近端级联松弛求解精确摩擦接触，并解决重参数化与通用约束的兼容性，在保持高吞吐的同时消除 v1 的物理伪影，可用于大量精细网格碰撞的刚体仿真。摘要未给出具体加速倍率与精度指标，需参阅正文。

Abstract: Lodos-v2 is a GPU-based simulation framework for massively parallel rigid-body dynamics with contact. It is specifically designed to achieve high throughput in scenes involving collisions among large numbers of detailed meshes. Its predecessor, Lodos-v1, had two main limitations. First, it solved the standard convex relaxation of cone-complementarity-based contact dynamics, thereby inheriting well-known physical artifacts, including lift-off and artificial compliance in the normal direction, as well as creep in the tangential direction. Second, its contact-group reparameterization scheme, which rescales spring-damper forces using a single factor per collision pair, could not be integrated with other constraints required by a full-fledged simulator, such as joint limits and welds. Lodos-v2 addresses both limitations through two main contributions. First, it introduces an efficient algorithm for solving the exact frictional contact problem by sequentially solving a cascade of convex relaxations in primal form within an outer proximal minimization loop. Second, it extends the contact-group reparameterization scheme of Lodos-v1 to the primal formulation of cone-complementarity-based contact dynamics, enabling its integration with the other constraints of a full-fledged simulator. Experiments demonstrate that these contributions result in throughput improvements over established rigid-body simulation frameworks. The source code is available at https://github.com/martius-lab/lodos.

</details>


### [323] [Lodos: A Lightweight Simulator Designed for Contact-Rich Interactions with Detailed Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08872)
*Onur Beker,Andreas René Geist,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从零设计的刚体合仿真框架 Lodos，含新颖的 broadphase/narrowphase 碰撞检测与接触动力学例程，在物理可信度上与成熟仿真器持平，而在精细 mesh-mesh 碰撞场景下吞吐量提升约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行刚体仿真在强化学习、随机最优控制与随机平滑轨迹优化中至关重要；接触密集场景的可扩展性依赖高效碰撞几何表示，通常需用球、胶囊、圆柱、盒等基元粗近似，若精度不足则须用 mesh-mesh 碰撞，导致吞吐量大幅下降。

Method: 从零构建仿真框架，重新设计宽相（broadphase）与窄相（narrowphase）碰撞检测例程，并配套实现后续接触动力学计算，专门针对 mesh-mesh 碰撞做优化。

Result: 实验验证：物理保真度与现有成熟仿真器相当，在所专长的精细 mesh-mesh 碰撞设置下吞吐量约高一个数量级。代码开源于 https://github.com/martius-lab/lodos。

Conclusion: mesh-mesh 碰撞的吞吐瓶颈可通过整体重构碰撞检测与接触动力学流水线有效缓解，为接触密集的并行刚体仿真提供高效替代方案。

Abstract: In recent years, massively parallelized rigid-body simulation has played a central role in robotics research, enabling rapid progress in areas such as reinforcement learning, stochastic optimal control, and randomized smoothing-based trajectory optimization. Particularly in contact-rich settings, the scalability of such simulations in large part depends on well-optimized representations of collision geometry, often requiring a coarse approximation of the underlying surfaces using primitives such as spheres, capsules, cylinders, and boxes. Whenever the complexity of the surfaces or the precision demands of the application render such approximations insufficient, it is instead necessary to employ mesh-mesh collisions, which impose a significant reduction in throughput. This paper proposes a simulation framework designed from scratch to address this bottleneck, with novel routines for broadphase and narrowphase collision detection as well as the subsequent contact dynamics computation. As experimentally validated, the framework matches existing well-established simulators in terms of physical faithfulness, and can achieve approximately an order of magnitude higher throughput in the detailed mesh-mesh collision setting that it specializes in. The source code is available at https://github.com/martius-lab/lodos.

</details>


### [324] [Localizing and Editing the Goal Representation of a Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08895)
*Sudhanshu Srivastava,Faisal Aqlan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究OpenVLA是否遵循"不要做X"式禁止性指令，以及目标在模型内部的表示位置；发现否定指令对行为几乎无效（79%的否定回合仍执行被禁任务），并定位到目标信息在层8（指令token处）可被转向、层16之后需对每个动作token打补丁才能转向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作者常用"不要执行X"这类禁止语句为机器人设定安全约束，但尚不清楚开放词汇视觉-语言-动作模型（如OpenVLA）是否真正理解并遵从否定，也不清楚其执行的目标在何处表征。该问题直接关系到安全约束能否通过指令实现。

Method: 利用LIBERO-Goal的10个任务共享同一场景，可标注每个rollout实际执行的目标。结合闭环rollout、激活补丁（activation patching）、线性探针、激活转向（activation steering）与运行时监控器进行因果与表征分析；测试指令token与最后prompt token在不同层的因果作用、固定转向向量的效果、逐动作token打补丁的效果，以及层32目标读出与外部探针的合规检测能力。

Result: 否定对OpenVLA执行哪一任务无可检测影响：否定rollout中79%仍执行被禁任务，普通任务完成率78%。目标探针在"not X"与"X"下读出相似。首个动作在层12前主要依赖指令token，层16起依赖最后prompt token。层8指令token处的固定转向向量使18次rollout中12次改变目标；层12–20的固定向量与前缀补丁对158次rollout均无效果；层16起仅对每个解码动作token全部打补丁才有效（层24下18次中15次）。rollout中指令改变时，层32目标读出检测不合规优于失败检测器，图像+指令特征的外部探针性能相当。

Conclusion: OpenVLA基本不遵从指令中的否定，因此禁止性安全约束应在指令之外（外部）强制执行，并对目标读出进行对照规范的运行时监控。若需通过编辑目标实现行为改变，应在层12之前作用于指令token，或对每个动作token逐一致补丁。

Abstract: Operators can state safety constraints for robots as prohibitions such as "do not put the bowl on the stove". We study whether OpenVLA follows such prohibitions and where it represents the goal it executes. The 10 tasks of LIBEROGoal share one scene, so we can label the goal that each rollout executes. We combine closed-loop rollouts with activation patching, linear probes, activation steering and runtime monitors. On LIBERO-Goal, negation had no detectable effect on which task OpenVLA executed. The model executed the forbidden task in 79% of negated rollouts and completed 78% of plain tasks. A goal probe gives similar read-outs under "not X" and "X". Activation patching shows that the first action depends mostly on the instruction tokens before layer 12 and on the last prompt token from layer 16 onward. A fixed steering vector at the instruction tokens at layer 8 redirected 12 of 18 rollouts. Fixed vectors and prefill patches at layers 12 to 20 redirected none of 158. From layer 16, only patches at every decoded action token redirected the robot (15 of 18 rollouts at layer 24). When the instruction changes during a rollout, the layer-32 goal read-out detects non-compliance better than failure detectors, and an external probe on image and instruction features performs comparably. These results support enforcing prohibitions outside the instruction and monitoring goal read-outs against the specification. Goal edits in OpenVLA should act before layer 12 or patch every action token.

</details>


### [325] [FATHOM: World-Model-Guided Diffusion Action-Trajectory Selection for Sparse-Marker AUV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08898)
*Xiaorui Wang,Zeyu Sha,Mingyang Yang,David Chen Wang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FATHOM：世界模型引导的扩散动作轨迹选择框架，用于稀疏光标记AUV相对跟踪的部分观测控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作式AUV相对跟踪在非线性动力学与水下视觉退化下困难；主动光标记提升外观鲁棒性但观测刻意稀疏，动态运动中易被遮挡、反射和检测噪声破坏，导致部分观测控制问题。

Method: 多模态任务监督的循环状态空间模型，融合结构化标记观测、惯性测量、动作历史与时序动态，构建预测性跟踪信念；扩散动作先验生成多条多推进器专家式轨迹；经学习到的潜动力学预测各候选的未来跟踪后果；在显式滚动时域目标下选择最优候选而非直接执行，并基于机载观测持续更新决策。

Result: 离线分析验证了预测信念、多步世界模型预测、扩散轨迹生成与预测式选择的有效性；真实AUV实验（含强位姿基线对比与组件消融）在标称与挑战性运动中、自然发生的部分标记可见性下实现鲁棒自主跟踪，且在无机动捕捉反馈条件下保持实时机载运行。

Conclusion: 将扩散轨迹生成与世界模型预测选择相结合，可有效应对稀疏标记部分观测下的AUV相对跟踪，并在真实平台上实现实时鲁棒运行。

Abstract: Reliable relative tracking is fundamental to cooperative autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs), yet remains challenging under nonlinear vehicle dynamics and degraded underwater visual conditions. Active light markers improve robustness to appearance variation, but provide deliberately sparse observations that can become incomplete or corrupted by occlusion, reflections, and detection noise during dynamic motion. To address the resulting partial-observation control problem, this paper proposes FATHOM, a world-model-guided diffusion action-trajectory selection framework for sparse-marker AUV tracking. FATHOM first integrates structured marker observations, inertial measurements, action history, and temporal dynamics into a predictive tracking belief using a multimodal task-supervised recurrent state-space model. A diffusion action prior then generates multiple expert-like multi-thruster trajectories, whose future tracking consequences are predicted through the learned latent dynamics. Rather than directly executing a generated trajectory, FATHOM selects the most favorable candidate under an explicit receding-horizon objective and continually updates the decision from onboard observations. Extensive offline analyses verify the learned predictive belief, multi-step world-model prediction, diffusion trajectory generation, and predictive selection. More importantly, comprehensive real-AUV experiments, including strong pose-based comparisons and component ablations, demonstrate robust autonomous tracking across nominal and challenging motions with naturally occurring partial marker visibility, while maintaining real-time onboard operation without motion-capture feedback.

</details>


### [326] [Polarization calibration of fisheye cameras using skylight in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08911)
*Shuo Wang,Shiqi Che,Dianze Dong,Wenxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种联合标定鱼眼镜头与偏振分析器的方法，用八参数统一建模入射角相关的镜头透射及分析器增益与角度偏置，仅需手持户外短序列即可完成标定，大入射角区AoLP误差显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天空偏振可为机器人导航提供方向线索，但鱼眼镜头效应与分析器不理想会随视场角退化偏振测量精度，需要一种无需专用偏振标定设备和预设天空偏振模型的实用标定手段。

Method: 在已知几何标定基础上，利用视觉特征估计相机帧间旋转；基于跨帧方向偏振光一致性进行偏振标定；将入射角相关的镜头透射与分析器增益、角度偏置统一为八参数模型，用自然天空下的短手持户外序列联合求解。

Result: 实拍户外序列上加权跨帧AoLP平均绝对误差在全入射角范围内低于0.5°，80°–90°入射角区域误差降低73.4%；无需重新拟合即可泛化到独立序列，整体MAE为0.466°；四足机器人初步太阳方位实验显示与可见太阳中心对齐更好。

Conclusion: 联合镜头-分析器标定可有效补偿鱼眼与分析器误差，提升大入射角AoLP精度并具良好泛化性，为相对航向估计及未来偏振-惯性导航提供支持，标定软件将在论文录用后发布。

Abstract: Skylight polarization provides directional cues for robotic navigation, but fisheye lens effects and analyzer imperfections can degrade polarization measurements across the field of view. We present a joint lens-analyzer calibration method that explicitly models incidence-dependent lens transmission together with analyzer gains and angular offsets in a unified eight-parameter formulation. Given known geometric calibration, rotations between camera frames can be estimated using visual features and cross-frame directional polarized light consistency can be used for polarization calibration. Therefore, our calibration approach requires only a short handheld outdoor sequence under natural skylight, without dedicated polarization calibration equipment or a predefined sky polarization model. On real-world outdoor sequences, the proposed method achieves a weighted cross-frame angle-of-linear-polarization (AoLP) mean absolute error below 0.5° across all incidence angles, with a 73.4% reduction in the 80°-90° incidence angle region, and generalizes to an independent sequence with an overall mean absolute error of 0.466° without refitting. Preliminary solar-azimuth experiments using a camera mounted on a quadruped robot further show improved alignment with the visible solar center, supporting the potential of calibrated AoLP measurements for relative-heading estimation and future polarization--inertial navigation. The calibration software tool will be released upon acceptance of this paper.

</details>


### [327] [HomeLoop: A Simulation Framework and Benchmark for Robot-IoT Coordination in Everyday Domestic Assistance and Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08914)
*Ramy ElMallah,Marc Bishara,Hyegang Kim,Yuxi Zhang,Chongyu Zhu,Chi-Guhn Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HomeLoop——基于Isaac Sim的家庭具身仿真框架与基准，集成机器人本体、联网IoT设备、可穿戴设备、模拟住户与访客、故障传感器及随时间演化的危险情境；包含5大类20项任务，同一警报可对应不同成因，并用时序逻辑运行时监测器对结果与过程动作打分（动作有预设成本）。评测6个VLM（开源与闭源），最佳模型家族均衡得分61.9%，且普遍存在"过度反应"：含禁止性干预的失败回合数超过遗漏必要干预的两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM驱动的机器人有望成为家庭助理，但家庭中已存在摄像头、IoT设备与住户可穿戴设备；机器人须与既有智能基础设施协同，并在警报原因高度歧义的场景（烟警报可能源于将起火的锅、已着火的锅、住户烹饪或传感器故障）下做出差异化响应。现有基准缺乏这种多源、歧义、动态危险与过程成本约束的评测环境。

Method: 构建基于Isaac Sim的仿真框架，融合机器人本体、网络化设备与可穿戴设备、模拟住户/访客、故障传感器及随时间演化的危险；设计5个任务族共20项任务，使同一警报具有不同成因；采用运行时监测器以时序逻辑对最终结果与轨迹中的动作进行校验，并为每个动作设定预设成本；对6个开源/闭源VLM进行系统性评测。

Result: 最佳模型家族均衡得分为61.9%；跨模型普遍呈现过度反应模式——含禁止性干预的失败回合与遗漏必要干预的失败回合之比超过2:1，模型常未查明警报原因即关闭炉灶。框架可扩展发布：2100个可自动多样化布置的户型、5种机器人本体、20类联网设备。

Conclusion: HomeLoop为机器人-IoT协同、长期记忆、主动式辅助及隐私-安全权衡研究提供可扩展基础平台；当前VLM在歧义家庭场景中的因果排查与干预克制明显不足，过度反应是主要失败模式。

Abstract: Recent progress in embodied robotics is bringing robots closer to deployment in homes, as vision-language models (VLMs) are demonstrating significant advances in reasoning, making VLM-driven robots promising home assistant agents. These agents will often be introduced into homes that already have cameras, Internet of Things (IoT) devices, and residents with wearables. To fulfill their potential, they need to integrate with existing smart infrastructure and reason through ambiguous scenarios: a smoke alarm may come from a pan about to ignite, a pan already on fire, a resident cooking or a faulty sensor, and each calls for a different response. To that end, we introduce HomeLoop, a simulation framework and benchmark built on Isaac Sim that combines robot bodies, networked devices and wearables, simulated residents and visitors, faulty sensors, and hazards that evolve over time. Our benchmark is composed of 20 tasks across five families grounded in real-world household hazards and needs, and designed so that the same alert can have different causes. A runtime monitor scores each episode with temporal-logic checks on the final outcome and the actions along the way, and every action carries a predefined cost. We evaluate six open-weight and proprietary VLMs. The best achieves a family-balanced score of 61.9%, and across models we see a pattern of over-reaction: failed episodes involving prohibited interventions outnumber those involving missed required interventions by more than two to one, with models often shutting off the stove before investigating the alarm's cause. HomeLoop is an extensible foundation intended for use in research on robot-IoT coordination, long-term memory, proactive assistance, and privacy-safety trade-offs. HomeLoop will be released with 2,100 home layouts that can be automatically furnished in diverse configurations, five robot bodies and 20 networked device types.

</details>


### [328] [PRISM: Primitive Routing via In-context Skill Mixing for Retentive VLA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08917)
*Jiachen Tao,Gengyu Zhang,Haoxuan Wang,Bing Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM 将可复用技能建模为参数级低秩"原语"，通过技能专家适配器库 + 逐chunk路由器实现长程组合任务，在RoboCasa365上原子技能56.43%、组合任务20.00%，优于π0.5-LoRA两阶段微调（17.86%/17.50%），并在AgileX PIPER实机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需同时具备技能保持与长程组合能力：既要保留已学技能，又要在行为随阶段变化的组合任务中按轨迹调用相应技能。但标准做法把所有新任务塞进同一次共享更新，既缺少可复用技能的参数级载体，也缺少沿轨迹调度技能的路由机制。

Method: 关键观察：缺失的结构是"隐式"而非"不存在"——各技能的低秩更新落在稳健可分的低维子空间，单个组合任务LoRA约一半Frobenius能量集中于该子空间，随机旋转对照接近零。据此提出PRISM：每个技能族以专用低秩action-expert适配器存于原语库；原语附加技能专属低秩残差；推理时由逐chunk、VLM条件化的路由器读取前缀表示以跟踪任务进度进行组合；路由器由确定性、任务条件化、动作导出的oracle生成逐chunk标签监督。组合训练中更新视觉编码器、语言LoRA、技能残差、路由器及共享action投影与time MLP，所有原语冻结，从而按构造保留基座技能适配器。

Result: RoboCasa365：PRISM原子技能成功率56.43%，长程组合任务20.00%；标准π0.5-LoRA两阶段微调为17.86%与17.50%。另在AgileX PIPER真机平台完成验证。

Conclusion: 通过在参数空间显式构造技能原语并配合进度感知路由器，可在冻结原语的前提下实现技能保持与长程组合的兼顾，显著优于共享更新的单模型微调范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise a single language-conditioned policy adaptable to many manipulation behaviors, yet deployment additionally requires skill retention and long-horizon composition: a policy must retain previously acquired skills and reuse them inside composite tasks whose behavior changes across phases. The standard recipe absorbs every new task into one shared update, which provides neither a parameter-level locus for reusable skills nor a mechanism for invoking each skill along the trajectory. We observe that this missing structure is latent rather than absent: per-skill low-rank updates of a pretrained VLA occupy a robustly distinguishable, low-dimensional subspace, and a single composite-task LoRA concentrates roughly half of its Frobenius energy in this subspace, against a near-zero matched random-rotation control. Building on this geometry, we introduce PRISM, which represents reusable skills as parameter-level primitives: each skill family is stored as a dedicated low-rank action-expert adapter in a primitive bank, primitives are augmented with skill-specific low-rank residuals and composed at inference by a per-chunk VLM-conditioned router that reads the prefix representation to track task progress, and the router is supervised by per-chunk labels from a deterministic, task-conditioned, action-derived oracle. During composite training, the vision encoder, the language LoRA, the skill residuals, the router, and the shared action projections and time MLP are updated; every primitive is held frozen, so the base per-skill action-expert adapters are preserved by construction. On RoboCasa365, PRISM achieves 56.43% atomic-skill success and reaches 20.00% on long-horizon composite tasks, while a standard $π_{0.5}$-LoRA two-stage fine-tune reaches 17.86% atomic and 17.50% composite success. We additionally validate PRISM on an AgileX PIPER real-robot setup.

</details>


### [329] [HOC2R: Hand-Object Contact Trajectories for Human-to-Robot Motion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08920)
*Hongkun Yu,Francisco M. López,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HOC2R，一种融合冻结DINOv3与SAM 2.1特征及学习头的手-物接触轨迹提取器，可从人类视频中抽取机器人可用的接触轨迹，作为伪标注方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频富含物体操作演示，但不直接提供机器人可跟随的运动线索，需要一种能从人类演示中提取机器人可用接触轨迹的伪标注方法。

Method: HOC2R结合冻结的DINOv3与SAM 2.1特征，并配合学习头实现手-物关联与接触定位；支持两种模式：计数感知自动化（在未裁剪视频中检测、计数并定位可见接触）与固定双接触定位（面向双指夹爪、已知接触帧）。

Result: 30任务点标注基准上，计数感知模式将接触计数准确率由58.1%提升至71.4%，平均定位距离由19.81px降至10.46px（对比适配的VRB2基线）；7个未见RGB-D rollout上，五任务固定双检查点将平均定位距离由67.67px降至27.02px；6条机器人评测序列中，30任务固定双检查点产出4条可行轨迹、3个任务成功，各适配基线仅2条可行轨迹、1个任务成功。单手场景具竞争力，对手部颜色偏移鲁棒，多手场景表现最强。

Conclusion: HOC2R可作为从人类演示中提取机器人可用接触轨迹的实用伪标注方法。

Abstract: Human videos contain abundant object-manipulation demonstrations, but they do not directly provide motion cues that a robot can follow. We present HOC2R, a Hand-Object Contact trajectory extractor for Robot motion that combines frozen DINOv3 and SAM 2.1 features with learned heads for hand-object association and contact localization. HOC2R supports two modes: count-aware automation that detects, counts, and localizes visible contacts in untrimmed videos, and fixed-two localization for two-finger grippers when contact frames are known. On a 30-task point-annotated benchmark, count-aware automation improves contact-count accuracy from 58.1% to 71.4% and reduces mean localization distance from 19.81 to 10.46 pixels relative to an adapted VRB2 baseline. On seven unseen RGB-D rollouts, a five-task fixed-two checkpoint reduces mean localization distance from 67.67 to 27.02 pixels. Across six robot evaluation sequences, the 30-task fixed-two checkpoint produces four feasible trajectories and succeeds on three tasks, compared with two feasible trajectories and one successful task for each adapted baseline. HOC2R is competitive in the evaluated single-hand scenes, robust to the hand-color shift, and strongest in the evaluated multi-hand scenes. These results support HOC2R as a practical pseudo-labeling method for extracting robot-usable contact trajectories from human demonstrations.

</details>


### [330] [Wellness Robots and the Path to Full Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08930)
*Mohammad H. Mahoor,Matthew Hessler,Lombe Chileshe,Ola Alsaadi,Eshrat Emamian,Kerstin S. Haring,Richard M. Voyles*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"自主健康机器人"这一新型社会辅助系统类别，并构建六等级自主性分类体系CRAS，以Ryan机器人为案例，报告其纵向临床证据与通向L5的发展路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会辅助机器人缺乏针对"持续评估并响应七维健康（ICAA维度）"能力的统一定义与自主性分级标准，导致该类系统难以被规范描述、比较与监管；作者希望借鉴汽车自动化的成熟分级范式，为健康机器人建立可比较的自主性标尺。

Method: 1）界定"自主健康机器人"为覆盖ICAA七维健康的连续评估—响应型社会辅助系统；2）提出Care Robot Autonomy Scale（CRAS）：参照SAE J3016的六等级（L0–L5）分类法，沿四个健康相关维度评估；3）以丹佛大学研发、DreamFace Technologies商业化的Ryan机器人为案例；4）采用NIH/NIA资助的纵向研究（N=18，887小时）评估认知（SLUMS）与抑郁（PHQ-9）结局，并检验新颖性效应。

Result: 纵向证据显示认知功能显著改善（SLUMS +2.61，p=0.015）；抑郁症状呈临床意义的方向性下降（PHQ-9 −2.50，p=0.073，未达显著性）；未观察到新颖性效应。按CRAS评级，Ryan当前处于L1–L2，部分维度已显现L3能力。

Conclusion: CRAS为健康机器人提供了可对标汽车自动化的自主性分级框架，Ryan的实证数据支持其临床价值。作者规划三阶段路线图，目标于2030年代初实现L5全自主，并呼吁以分级标准推动该领域的规范化评估与落地。

Abstract: This paper introduces the autonomous wellness robot as a distinct category of socially assistive system, defined by its capacity to continuously assess and respond across the seven ICAA dimensions of wellness (see Figure 2). We propose the Care Robot Autonomy Scale (CRAS): a six-level taxonomy (L0-L5) modeled on the automotive automation standard SAE J3016 and evaluated across four wellness-aligned dimensions. We present Ryan, developed initially at the University of Denver and commercialized by DreamFace Technologies, as a case study. NIH/NIA-funded longitudinal evidence (N = 18; 887 hours) shows statistically significant cognitive improvement (SLUMS +2.61, p = 0.015) and a clinically meaningful directional reduction in depressive symptoms (PHQ-9 -2.50, p = 0.073), with no novelty effect. We position Ryan today at CRAS Level 1-2, with emerging Level 3 capabilities in some dimensions, and outline a three-phase roadmap to L5 (full autonomy) by the early 2030s.

</details>


### [331] [RevoSim: Scalable Multimodal Tactile Simulation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08939)
*Ke Zhang,Lixin Xu,Ziyi Wang,Xiyue Dong,Jie Tan,Lianghu Shao,Xianpeng Wang,Chuanyu Li,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RevoSim：面向BrainCo Revo3灵巧手的可扩展高保真多模态触觉仿真器，联合建模285个压阻式触觉单元与5个视触觉指尖，可同步生成压力、深度、RGB与标记位移数据，单张RTX 5090上支持最多4096个并行环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态触觉仿真（压阻阵列+视触觉）在真实灵巧手上的高保真、可扩展建模仍缺失，制约了纯仿真训练与sim-to-real迁移。

Method: 构建Revo3手的多模态触觉仿真器：对285个压阻taxel与5个视触觉指尖联合建模；以深度条件驱动的标记位移渲染机制生成同步的压力、深度、RGB及marker-displacement观测；基于GPU并行实现大规模环境实例化，并在仿真中训练手内操作与抓取策略。

Result: 在单张RTX 5090上，深度条件标记位移配置可支持至多4096个并行环境；实现四类触觉观测的同步高保真输出，并成功纯仿真训练手内操作与抓取策略。

Conclusion: RevoSim为多模态触觉驱动的灵巧操作提供了可扩展仿真基础，为后续sim-to-real迁移奠定平台，但真实迁移效果尚待验证。

Abstract: We present RevoSim, a scalable and high-fidelity multimodal tactile simulator for the BrainCo Revo3 hand that jointly models 285 piezoresistive taxels and five visuotactile fingertips. RevoSim efficiently generates synchronized pressure, depth, RGB, and marker-displacement observations; on a single RTX 5090, its depth-conditioned marker-displacement configuration supports up to 4,096 parallel environments. We train in-hand manipulation and grasping policies entirely in simulation, establishing a foundation for future sim-to-real transfer. Project page: https://braincotech.github.io/RevoSim-web/.

</details>


### [332] [VLA Handover: Failure-Prediction-Based Switching from On-Robot to Server VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08987)
*Takayuki Nishio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"VLA handover"框架：机器人本地运行小型VLA（S-VLA），仅当预测其可能失败时才切换到大服务器端VLA（L-VLA），在满足成功率要求下最小化远端请求开销，且不重训任何VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型VLA泛化好但在机器人上难以实时运行；小型VLA可实时运行但失败率高。借助B5G/6G远端推理可让机器人使用服务器上的大VLA，但每个动作块都查询服务器会限制单服务器可支持的机器人数。因此需要按需调用大模型。

Method: 将切换问题建模为在给定成功率约束下最小化请求代价的优化问题；由于该问题无法直接求解，提出一个启发式框架来实现切换，且无需对S-VLA或L-VLA进行重新训练。仿真中采用SmolVLA与π_{0.5}，在LIBERO四个套件中的三个上评估。

Result: 相比仅用S-VLA，成功率提升11–22个百分点；相比纯服务器端控制，请求数减少49–77%，成功率仅低2–4个百分点；且完成时间随网络时延增长更平缓。

Conclusion: 按需的"小模型本地+大模型远端"切换策略可在保证成功率的同时显著降低通信请求负担、提升单服务器可服务机器人数，并对网络时延更鲁棒，为实时部署大VLA提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize well but are difficult to run in real time on a robot, and small VLAs that run there fail more often. Remote inference over beyond-5G and 6G networks lets a robot use a large VLA on a server, but querying the server for every action chunk limits the number of robots per server. We propose VLA handover: the robot runs a small VLA (S-VLA) and switches to a large VLA (L-VLA) on a server only when the S-VLA is likely to fail. We formulate the switching as minimizing the request cost under a required success probability and, since this problem cannot be solved directly, propose a heuristic framework that retrains neither VLA. In simulation with SmolVLA and $π_{0.5}$ on three of the four LIBERO suites, VLA handover raised the success rate by 11-22 percentage points over the S-VLA alone and sent 49-77% fewer requests than server-only control at a 2-4 percentage-point lower success rate, and its completion time grew less with the network latency.

</details>


### [333] [FASTFUNNELS: Agile Multi Robot Navigation in Narrow Spaces via Adaptive Dynamic Funnels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09005)
*Vignesh Rajagopal,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FastFunnels：高层用强化学习实时生成可形变的“水滴状”椭圆软约束斑块，低层用分布式MPC控制Ackermann车辆保持在其中并避碰，实现多机器人高速穿越狭窄空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱狭窄空间中多机器人高速导航需兼顾安全与敏捷，但现有方法常以牺牲一方换取另一方（瓶颈处减速避碰 vs 高速下无法及时转向），对类车系统尤为突出。传统编队控制依赖刚性预设队形与解析切换（如先成方形、再变线性、再回方形），其转换依赖初始条件、环境与车辆动力学，高速下难以维持，不实用。

Method: 受水滴随流经空间连续自适应变形的启发，采用分层框架：高层由强化学习策略生成动态的团块状椭圆斑块，实时挤压、拉伸与调整朝向以绕障并穿越狭窄通道；低层用分布式模型预测控制（DMPC）使各机器人保持在该斑块内（斑块作为软约束/引导函数），同时保证相互避碰。

Result: 在F1TENTH仿真赛车上测试14个场景、N=1至6、每场景20次试验平均：FastFunnels达到89%完成率，速度7.73 m/s；所有基线要么无法完成赛道，要么仅在0.07–1.79 m/s极低速下完成（约40%完成率）。

Conclusion: 以可形变软约束替代刚性队形，可在不减速前提下兼顾安全与敏捷，显著优于现有编队/导航基线在高速紧约束场景中的表现。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of high-speed multi-robot navigation in cluttered spaces with tight spatial constraints. A key challenge is balancing safety with agility, as existing methods often trade one for the other (slowing down near a bottleneck to avoid collisions versus failing to swerve in time at high speeds), especially for car-like systems. Our approach belongs to the family of formation control methods, in which Ackermann-steering agents coordinate while navigating tight spaces. However, current formation control techniques rely on rigid, pre-defined shapes and transitions that depend on initial conditions, the environment, and vehicle dynamics, and are impractical to maintain at high speeds. For example, agents passing through a small gap must first form a square, transform into a line using analytical equations, and then return to a square. In contrast, our approach is inspired by a droplet of water that continuously adapts its shape to the space it flows through without slowing down. We propose a hierarchical algorithm in which, at the high level, a reinforcement learning policy produces a dynamic, blob-like elliptical patch that morphs its shape in real time, squeezing, stretching, and orienting itself around obstacles and through tight spaces. At the low level, the robots are controlled via distributed model predictive control (DMPC) to stay inside the patch, which acts as a soft forcing function, while avoiding collisions with each other. We evaluate our approach, FastFunnels, on simulated F1TENTH racing cars across 14 scenarios with N=1 to N=6 robots, averaged over 20 trials each. FastFunnels achieves an 89% completion rate at 7.73 m/s, while all baselines either fail to complete the track or succeed only at very low speeds (0.07-1.79 m/s, about 40% completion rate).

</details>


### [334] [A Controlled Study of Models, Skills, Feedback, and Curriculum in Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09030)
*Kaiyuan Chen,Xinyue Zhu,Shuangyu Xie,Uksang Yoo,Thi Thu Phuong Luong,Marcus Gualtieri,Christian Juette,Liu Ren,Spencer Huang,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过覆盖9个操作任务、631种实验配置、2.8万次编码智能体 rollout 的受控仿真研究，系统考察智能体机器人学的自学习闭环，指出瓶颈在于"信息解读"而非"信息获取"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体机器人学让编码智能体基于仿真与真实物理交互实现并迭代改进机器人策略，但自学习闭环的实际瓶颈尚不清晰，因此需要拆解模型能力、技能先验、反馈结构与课程设计等影响因素。

Method: 开展受控仿真研究：在9个操作任务上，系统变化四个维度（模型能力、技能先验、反馈结构、课程），共631种实验配置、2.8万次编码智能体 rollout，对自学习闭环进行因子化剖析。

Result: 实验数据表明，瓶颈通常并非缺乏充足信息，而在于如何将信息转化为正确的策略更新；自学习闭环的成功取决于智能体的失败归因能力、对任务相关空间与几何结构的推理能力，以及有选择地重构代码以改进策略的能力。

Conclusion: 成功的智能体机器人学自学习闭环应聚焦于归因、几何/空间推理与选择性代码重构三项核心能力，而非单纯增加信息或提升模型规模。

Abstract: Agentic robotics asks coding agents to implement and iteratively improve robot policies from simulated and real-world physical interactions. We study the self-learning loop of agentic robotics through a controlled simulation study spanning nine manipulation tasks, 631 different experiment configurations, and 28K coding agent rollouts, systematically varying model capability, skill priors, feedback structure, and curriculum. The experiment data suggests that the bottleneck is often not the access to sufficient information, but interpreting it into the right policy update. Across these factors, a successful self-learning loop depends on how effectively agents attribute failures, reason task-relevant spatial and geometric structure, and selectively restructure code to improve policies.

</details>


### [335] [ToolDex: An Ego-Exo Human Demonstration Dataset for Creative Physical Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09036)
*Mohamed Elmoghany,Kehan Li,Ruoqu Chen,Tarun Chiruvolu,Shutong Zhang,Ryan Wang,Stephen Tian,Yaru Niu,Michelle Guo,Ding Zhao,Weiyu Liu,Mengdi Xu,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ToolDex：面向创造性物理工具使用的大规模自我中心-外中心人类演示数据集与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造性工具使用是通用具身智能的重要能力，需推理可供性、物理约束与操作策略；但现有基准多为仿真、交互简化或缺乏细粒度观测与标注，缺少真实世界中大规模、高保真、多样化的工具使用数据。

Method: 用同步的自我中心相机与8台外中心相机采集人类选择、改造、挪用物体作为工具的场景，提供多视角高保真观测及手、工具、目标物体与可变形交互的丰富标注；并构建覆盖三类核心能力的基准套件：物理工具使用推理、稀疏视角3D/4D重建、机器人策略学习。

Result: 形成大规模ego-exo演示数据集与配套基准，提供数据集可视化与基准评测结果（见项目主页），可作为研究人类工具使用行为与具身智能体自适应工具使用的基础平台。

Conclusion: ToolDex弥补真实世界创造性工具使用数据的空白，为工具推理、重建与策略学习提供统一评测基础，推动复杂真实环境中自适应工具使用的具身智能体研究。

Abstract: Creative tool use is a fundamental human capability and an important step toward general-purpose embodied intelligence. It requires agents to reason about object affordances, physical constraints, and manipulation strategies, especially when standard tools are unavailable. However, progress in this area is limited by the lack of real-world datasets that capture human tool-use behavior with sufficient scale, fidelity, and diversity. Existing benchmarks are often simulation-based, focus on simplified interactions, or lack detailed observations and annotations.
  We introduce ToolDex, a large-scale egocentric-exocentric (ego-exo) human demonstration dataset for creative physical tool use. ToolDex captures scenarios in which humans select, adapt, and repurpose available objects as tools. Using synchronized egocentric cameras and eight exocentric cameras, the dataset provides high-fidelity multi-view observations and rich annotations for hands, tools, target objects, and their deformable interactions. Beyond data collection, we establish a benchmark suite characterizing three core capabilities: physical tool-use reasoning, sparse-view 3D and 4D reconstruction, and robot policy learning. ToolDex provides a foundation for studying human tool-use behavior and developing embodied agents that can adaptively use tools in complex real-world environments.
  Dataset visualizations and benchmarking results are available at https://tooluse-dataset.github.io/.

</details>


### [336] [DobotTrack: Target-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Learning for Embodied Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09041)
*Shengjun Deng,Yinghan Zhang,Chengrui Wei,Liu Liang,Songyuan Liu,Pengkun Zheng,Yajun Zheng,Ying Zhang,Yuan Tian,Xi Li,Teng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DobotTrack：目标接地（target-grounded）的VLA框架，通过辅助接地监督与高效时序建模实现语言引导的具身人员跟踪，0.8B模型在EVT-Bench上平均成功率77.9%，超OmTrackVLA 16.9个百分点，并构建了四足机器人真实跟踪数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的具身人员跟踪要求移动智能体从指代描述中识别目标，并在自我中心观测下持续跟随。现有VLA方法在目标判别性外观/空间线索的保持与历史信息压缩效率上存在不足，且缺乏可自动生成的接地监督与真实世界数据。

Method: 系统对比五种辅助任务（人员分割、包围盒接地、视觉-空间分类VSC、未来DINO特征预测、未来帧预测），采用掩码与包围盒互补监督以增强目标轮廓勾勒与定位；提出Q-identity目标条件历史视觉压缩模块，利用目标接地预测在紧凑历史token中选择性保留身份判别性外观特征与空间线索；提出基于掩码标注的标注流水线，可自动派生包围盒与视觉-空间标签（水平位置、表观图像尺度）；构建四足机器人真实人员跟踪数据集（126条轨迹、596,323帧掩码标注）。

Result: EVT-Bench上三项任务平均成功率77.9%，较OmTrackVLA提升16.9个百分点；参数量仅0.8B；提供126 episodes、596,323 mask标注帧的真实数据集，掩码可自动导出包围盒与视觉-空间标签，免去单独人工标注。

Conclusion: 辅助接地监督（掩码+包围盒）与目标条件历史压缩相结合，能在小参数量下显著提升语言引导人员跟踪性能；框架与标注流水线共同支持从仿真与真实数据中学习目标接地的跟踪策略，代码与数据流水线已开源。

Abstract: Language-guided embodied person tracking requires a mobile agent to identify a target from a referring description and consistently follow the target under egocentric observations. We present DobotTrack, a target-grounded vision-language-action (VLA) framework that integrates auxiliary grounding supervision with efficient temporal modeling. We systematically investigate five auxiliary tasks---person segmentation, bounding-box grounding, visual-spatial classification (VSC), future DINO feature prediction, and future-frame prediction---and adopt complementary mask and bounding-box supervision to enhance target delineation and localization. We introduce Q-identity, a target-conditioned historical visual compression module that leverages target-grounding predictions to selectively preserve identity-discriminative appearance features and spatial cues in compact historical tokens. On EVT-Bench, our 0.8B-parameter model achieves an average success rate of 77.9%, exceeding OmTrackVLA by 16.9 percentage points across the three tasks. We further construct a real-world person-tracking dataset collected using a quadruped robot, comprising 126 episodes and 596,323 mask-annotated frames. The target masks enable automatic derivation of bounding boxes and visual-spatial labels encoding horizontal position and apparent image scale, providing additional grounding supervision without separate manual annotation of these labels. Together, our framework and annotation pipeline support learning target-grounded tracking policies from simulated and real-world data. Code is available at https://github.com/Dobot-Team/dobot_track.

</details>


### [337] [When to Trust the BIM: Identifiability-Aware LiDAR-Inertial--BIM Fusion under As-Built Deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09045)
*Yujie Zhang,Yuxuan Guo,Xinbao Sun,Chunlei Zhao,Langqi Miao,Xiaohui Jia,Jinyue Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将BIM与LiDAR惯性里程计配准中的"扫描-BIM不一致"归因问题形式化为三类故障识别问题，推导可归因性的几何判据，并提出可识别性感知方法：仅在可归因时给出归因，否则保留候选并延迟歧义方向上的BIM更新与位姿校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIM可作为机器人LIO定位的全局几何参考，但实际建成（as-built）与设计模型（as-designed）存在偏差，导致扫描与BIM的不一致可能源于位姿误差、墙面偏移（建成偏差或未建模遮挡物）或房间整体平移；在特定几何下这些解释会产生完全相同的观测，若无判别地归因将造成误判与错误的BIM更新/位姿校正。

Method: 1) 将归因问题形式化为三类故障（位姿误差、表面偏移、房间平移）上的故障识别问题；2) 推导归因在几何上何时可能的可识别性准则；3) 基于该准则设计可识别性感知方法：仅在故障可归因时输出唯一归因，否则保留候选集、在歧义方向上暂缓BIM更新与BIM位姿校正，待新参考到来后恢复。评估包括预注册的二维研究、实验室房间（含IFC模型）成对故障注入，以及SLABIM数据集三层楼实验，并以基于不确定性弃权的基线作对比。

Result: 在"可检测但不可归因"的案例上，方法条件错误率为0.83%，同回答率下不确定性弃权基线为31.67%；在5,600个可归因案例中，5,574次给出唯一归因且无错误归因。实验室168种条件中0次误目标归因、SLABIM 1,302种条件中0次误归因；实验室8个被预测为可归因的故障全部正确归因，而两个复现基线在所有设置下均产生偏离目标的错误结构性报警。

Conclusion: 归因不确定性具有可刻画的几何本质，显式建模可识别性并采用"存疑即弃权、延迟更新"的策略，可在不牺牲可归因案例覆盖度的前提下近乎消除错误归因，显著优于仅依赖不确定性阈值的弃权基线，为BIM辅助定位中的鲁棒偏差处理提供了原则性方案。

Abstract: Building information models (BIM) can provide a global geometric reference for robot localization with LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO). However, as-built deviations from the as-designed model mean that a disagreement between a scan and the BIM may arise from a pose error, an offset wall surface (an as-built deviation or an unmodeled object in front of the wall), or a shifted room, and in some geometries these explanations produce identical observations. In this paper, we formalize this attribution problem as fault identification over three fault classes and derive a geometric criterion for when attribution is possible. Building on it, we propose an identifiability-aware method that reports an attribution only when the fault is attributable; otherwise, it retains the candidates, defers BIM updates and BIM-based pose corrections in the ambiguous directions, and recovers once new references arrive. In the pre-registered two-dimensional study, on cases whose fault is detectable but not attributable, the method's conditional error rate was 0.83%, against 31.67% for a baseline with uncertainty-based abstention at the same answer rate. On the 5,600 attributable cases, the method reported a unique attribution 5,574 times, with no incorrect attributions. In paired fault injections in a laboratory room with its IFC model and on three floors of the SLABIM dataset, it raised no off-target attribution (0 of 168 conditions in the laboratory; 0 of 1,302 on SLABIM) and attributed all eight laboratory faults predicted to be attributable, whereas both reproduced baselines raised off-target structural flags in every setting.

</details>


### [338] [Robust Readout of Cross-Point Resistive Arrays via Time-Domain Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09059)
*Taro Tako,Hiroshi Kaminaga,Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种时域优化读出的方法，用于交叉点电阻阵列的鲁棒读出，通过拟合完整时域波形而非仅依赖频谱幅值来估计阻值，在16×16阵列仿真中平均误差降低20%，1000次试验中平均误差与标准差分别降低24.6%和77.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉点电阻阵列因减少布线复杂度而适合高密度触觉传感，但传统基于频率的读出方法在机器人传感环境中受长互连线、测量噪声与环境噪声影响，观测信号退化导致读出精度下降，因此需要更鲁棒的读出方法。

Method: 提出时域优化方法：在给定时间区间内，将时域观测模型拟合到实测波形，利用完整波形信息而非仅用频谱幅值来确定电阻值，从而提升噪声观测条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在16×16阵列仿真中，与基线方法相比平均读出误差降低20%；1000次试验验证显示，平均误差和标准差分别降低24.6%和77.6%；在模拟触觉传感器响应电阻分布的交叉点阵列仿真中亦验证了改进效果。

Conclusion: 利用完整时域波形的拟合策略可显著提升交叉点电阻阵列在噪声环境下的读出鲁棒性与稳定性，适用于高密度触觉传感应用。

Abstract: Cross-point resistive arrays are attractive for high-density tactile sensing because they reduce wiring complexity. However, conventional frequency-based readout methods can become inaccurate in robotic sensing environments, where long interconnects and measurement and environmental noise degrade the observed signals. We propose a time-domain optimization method for robust readout of cross-point resistive arrays. The method determines resistance values by fitting a time-domain observation model to the measured waveform over the interval, rather than relying solely on spectral amplitudes. By exploiting the full waveform, the proposed method improves robustness under noisy observation conditions. In simulations of a 16 x 16 array, the proposed method reduced the mean readout error by 20% compared with the baseline method. Furthermore, validation over 1,000 trials showed that the proposed method achieves more robust estimation than the conventional method, reducing the mean error and standard deviation by 24.6% and 77.6%, respectively. The improvement was also confirmed in cross-point array simulations with resistance distributions emulating tactile sensor responses.

</details>


### [339] [Safe Circumnavigation on Heterogeneous Orbits: A Range-Only HOCBF-QP Framework for Nonholonomic Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09112)
*Shubham Singh,Anand Singh,Anjali Tiwari,Lara Briñón-Arranz,Katsuki Fujisawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种安全关键控制框架，使多机器人以各自不同轨道半径环绕静止目标，同时保证目标、地理围栏与机器人间安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人环绕静止目标时，各机器人需保持自身站位距离、与相邻机器人均匀分布、并实时避碰；当各机器人因传感器或角色需求被分配不同轨道半径时，该任务尤为困难，现有方法难以保证安全。

Method: 每个机器人同时控制加速度与转向角速率，仅测量到目标的距离，且仅与环上两个邻居通信。设计有三点：利用模型的结构性质使单一控制律适用于任意半径的机器人；加速度精确驱动半径至目标值，转向角速率平衡与邻居的编队分布；采用高阶控制障碍函数二次规划（HOCBF-QP）同时约束两个输入上的目标、地理围栏与机器人间安全，在远离约束时不修改名义控制器，并在未测距变化率估计期间仍保持有效。

Result: 仿真验证了上述安全保证；与最接近的现有多轨道设计对比，后者即使具备全状态信息也会违反目标站位距离与机器人间安全裕度，而所提控制器不违反。

Conclusion: 所提框架以最小感知与通信需求实现了异构半径下的安全环绕编队控制，安全性与可行性优于现有全状态方法。

Abstract: Circumnavigating a stationary target with a team of robots requires each robot to hold its own standoff distance, to stay evenly spaced from its neighbours, and to avoid collisions with the target and with each other at every instant. The task becomes arduous when the robots are assigned different orbital radii, as their sensors or roles may demand. This paper presents a safety-critical control framework for this problem. Each robot commands both its acceleration and its turn-rate, measures only its distance to the target, and exchanges information only with its two ring neighbours. The design has three features. First, a structural property of the model lets one control law serve every robot regardless of its assigned radius. Second, the acceleration drives each radius exactly to its target, while the turn-rate balances the formation with the neighbours. Third, a higher-order control-barrier-function quadratic program enforces target, geofence, and inter-robot safety on both inputs at once; it leaves the nominal controller untouched away from the constraints and stays valid while the unmeasured range rate is still being estimated. Simulations confirm these guarantees. In a comparison, the closest existing multi-orbit design, even with full state information, violates both the target standoff and the inter-robot margin, whereas the proposed controller does not.

</details>


### [340] [Toward the Era of Agentic Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09124)
*Chao Liang,Ke Chen,Fengxiaoxiao Li,Shibo Huang,Yufeng Sun,Zhaokai Yin,Jianhuan Lai,Haozhe Feng,Chenghuan Jia,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A2-MEAS：以"代码"为扩展接口的具身多智能体框架，结合元大脑监督与修正编码器/适配器，实现高效控制与失败恢复，在BEHAVIOR 2026、RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人任务上大幅领先基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽已广泛用于指导机器人动作，但持续自主运行仍需要高效的控制机制与失败恢复能力；现有VLA策略在长程任务、部署期纠错与训练吞吐上存在短板。

Method: 提出A2-MEAS框架，以代码作为扩展接口：学习式修正代码融合纠正性语言、空间证据、执行历史与动作提议；MetaBrain提供训练监督与运行时指导；修正编码器与适配器在训练阶段将知识吸收进策略权重，部署时以冻结权重配合新生成代码调整行为。动作模型A2-WLA在VLA策略中引入运动感知历史与潜在世界模型，并进行动作预训练与任务特化。

Result: BEHAVIOR 2026公开50任务子集（在线修正前）Q-score 0.5755，为2025年冠军报告值0.2605的2.21倍；全部100任务达0.6411 Q-score与45.40%成功率。RoboTwin 2.0平均成功率93.98%，全系统95.05%。五类家庭场景12个真机任务平均成功率57.92%（pi0.5为26.67%）。手冲咖啡长程任务中，历史条件适配器将两个滤纸处理步骤的失败后完成率由14%→94%、8%→82%。训练基础设施吞吐427.1 samples/s，为pi0.5参考实现（同硬件）的4.03倍。

Conclusion: 以代码为扩展接口的简洁设计配合动作预训练与任务特化，可同时获得强性能、部署期自适应纠错与高训练效率；作者将开源系统、模型、训练脚本与基础设施以支持高效具身学习。需注意跨基准版本与配置的对比不完全同口径。

Abstract: Foundation models increasingly guide robot actions, yet sustained autonomy requires efficient control and failure recovery. We introduce A2-MEAS, an embodied multi-agent framework built around code as a scaling interface. Learned correction codes combine corrective language, spatial evidence, execution history, and action proposals. MetaBrain provides training supervision and runtime guidance. A correction encoder and adapter absorb this knowledge into policy weights during training, while fresh codes adapt behavior with frozen weights at deployment. The action model, A2-WLA, adds motion-aware history and a latent world model to a vision-language-action policy. Surprisingly, this simple design with action pretraining and task specialization reaches 0.5755 Q-score on the 50-task BEHAVIOR 2026 public subset before online correction, 2.21x the 2025 winner's reported 0.2605 across different benchmark editions and training configurations. On all 100 BEHAVIOR 2026 public tasks, A2-MEAS achieves 0.6411 Q-score and 45.40% task success. On RoboTwin 2.0, A2-WLA achieves 93.98% average success, rising to 95.05% with the complete system. Across 12 real-robot tasks in five household settings, A2-WLA achieves 57.92% average success versus 26.67% for pi0.5. In long-horizon pour-over coffee preparation, a history-conditioned adapter raises post-failure completion at two filter-handling steps from 14% to 94% and from 8% to 82%, compared with A2-WLA retries. Our training infrastructure delivers 427.1 samples per second, 4.03x the throughput of a pi0.5 reference implementation on matched hardware. We will release the system, models, training scripts, and infrastructure to support efficient embodied learning.

</details>


### [341] [Auditing Grasp Feasibility Predictors with Planner-Verified Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09130)
*Sajid Ansari,Antony Thomas,Girish Varma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"规划器验证的反事实（CF）测试"，揭示现有抓取可行性预测器在Task and Motion Planning基准上的高准确率并未证明其真正使用了运动规划器所检验的碰撞几何；八种预测器在CF-Pair AUROC上从0.92–0.996跌至随机水平（0.47–0.504）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抓取可行性预测器在基准上报告近乎完美的准确率，但高准确率并不能验证预测器是否利用了运动规划器实际检查的碰撞几何。缺乏一种直接检验预测器"是否真懂几何可行性"的评测方法。

Method: 设计规划器验证的反事实测试：固定抓取位姿，在接近走廊中插入实心障碍物，将该障碍物点加入模型输入，再由规划器重新推导标签，使标签从可行翻转为不可行。用CF-Pair AUROC衡量模型能否跟随标签翻转。实验覆盖点云、体素、深度、物体token等八类预测器，跨四种规划器、两种机械臂，并包含真实物体障碍实验；还对点云模型做表征层面的剖析。

Result: CF-Pair AUROC从自然场景高值（0.920–0.996）降至随机（0.470–0.504），翻转结论在四种规划器、两种机械臂与真实障碍物下稳定成立；不同模型失效的分辨率各异，从包围盒到12.5 cm体素不等。剖析发现障碍物要么使表征沿与是否阻挡抓取无关的方向移动，要么根本未被编码。一个基于走廊点云间隙（clearance）的读取模块在未修改基准上训练即达0.983 CF-Pair AUROC，但仍受限于训练时的障碍物摆放位置。

Conclusion: 现有抓取可行性预测器依赖的并非规划器所检验的碰撞几何，基准高准确率存在严重的评测偏差。间隙感知读取是有希望的方向，但位置不变（placement-invariant）的间隙表征仍是开放问题。作者公开了反事实标签、指标、数据表与重标注流程。

Abstract: Grasp-feasibility predictors report near-perfect accuracy on Task and Motion Planning benchmarks, but high accuracy on these benchmarks does not verify that a predictor uses the collision geometry that the motion planner checks. We propose a planner-verified counterfactual (CF) test: holding the grasp pose fixed, we insert a solid obstacle into the approach corridor, add its points to the model input, and let a planner re-derive the label, which flips from feasible to infeasible. Across eight predictors spanning point clouds, voxels, depth, and object tokens, CF-pair AUROC falls from high natural AUROC (0.920-0.996) to chance (0.470-0.504), and the flip holds across four planners, two arms, and a real-object obstacle. Predictors fail at different resolutions, from a bounding box to a 12.5 cm voxel. In the point-cloud models we dissect, the obstacle either moves the representation in a direction unrelated to whether it blocks the grasp or is not registered at all. A clearance-aware read of the corridor points, trained on the unmodified benchmark with no counterfactual scenes, reaches 0.983 CF-pair AUROC, but it remains bound to its trained obstacle placements; a placement-invariant clearance representation is still open. We release the counterfactual labels, metrics, datasheet, and relabeling pipeline at https://github.com/codewithsajid/graspfc-cf.

</details>


### [342] [TPS-A*: Path Planning for Any-Shape Vehicles with Certified Collision Checking in Trajectory Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09210)
*Jose Luis Blanco-Claraco,Shravan S. Rai,Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,José Luis Torres-Moreno,José Carlos Moreno-Úbeda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TPS-A*是一种基于轨迹参数空间认证碰撞检测的格规划器，用每个足迹预计算一次网格即可一次性返回整条候选轨迹的认证自由距离，使单节点碰撞代价与足迹复杂度和轨迹数量无关；在300个杂乱场景中均求得无碰撞解，中位耗时3ms，与hybrid A*相当且快约60倍于状态格规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局地面车辆规划通常用圆盘近似车辆，在安全性与保守性之间折衷；而精确足迹检查需对每条候选运动、每个位姿和每条足迹边逐一检测，计算开销大。

Method: 提出TPS-A*：格边为由参数化轨迹生成器产生的运动学可行轨迹；对每个足迹预计算一张网格，一次遍历局部障碍物即得到沿每条候选轨迹的认证自由距离；证明该网格对连续扫掠、可能非凸的足迹的自由距离永不高估，并给出无碰撞路径与相对格抽象的次优性有界保证；带/不带朝向的目标由Reeds-Shepp射击精确到达并认证为同一组生成器的轨迹。

Result: 独立精确测试未发现路径碰撞。300个杂乱场景、真实足迹与相同目标位姿下，全部查询求得无碰撞目标位姿，中位耗时3ms（AMD Ryzen 7 2700X），与hybrid A*相当（后者45%路径与障碍重叠），比另一唯一无碰撞的状态格规划器快近60倍；位置目标下比hybrid A*快89倍。在自动泊车与窄门场景中与hybrid A*持平，可对无凸模型能容纳的非凸叉形足迹完成停靠，并在真实机器人上穿越有人群的大学校园。

Conclusion: 通过轨迹参数空间的认证碰撞检测与足迹无关的预计算网格，TPS-A*在保证无碰撞与有界次优的同时实现毫秒级精确足迹规划，适用于非凸车辆足迹与真实环境部署。

Abstract: Global planners for ground vehicles usually approximate the vehicle by a disk, trading safety against conservativeness, while exact-footprint checking pays for every candidate motion, pose, and footprint edge. We present TPS-A*, a lattice planner whose edges are kinematically feasible trajectories from parameterized trajectory generators, built on certified collision checking in trajectory parameter space. A grid precomputed per footprint returns, in one pass over the local obstacles, a certified free distance along every candidate trajectory, so the per-node collision cost is independent of footprint complexity and trajectory count. We prove that this grid never overestimates the free distance of the continuously swept, possibly non-convex footprint, and state the planner's guarantees: collision-free paths and bounded suboptimality relative to its lattice abstraction. Goals with or without heading are reached exactly by Reeds-Shepp shots certified as trajectories of the same generators. An exact independent test finds no collision in its paths. On 300 cluttered worlds, with the true footprint and the same goal pose for all planners, it solves every query with a collision-free goal pose in a median of three milliseconds on an AMD Ryzen 7 2700X, on par with hybrid A*, 45\% of whose paths overlap obstacles, and nearly sixty times faster than a state-lattice planner, the only other collision-free one; with position goals, eighty-nine times faster than hybrid A*. It matches hybrid A* in automated parking and on narrow doorways, docks a non-convex fork footprint that no convex model admits, and drove a real robot across a university campus among people.

</details>


### [343] [SUGARv2: Learning Diverse Visual Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Skills from Synthesized Interaction References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09266)
*Tianshu Wu,Tianxiang Gui,Yue Chen,Tingxu Liu,Ziye Peng,Guangjie Yang,Xiangqi Kong,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUGARv2：通过交互参考合成与共享训练流水线，低成本、统一地学习多样化视觉人形机器人全身移动操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展人形机器人视觉移动操作技能时，随着任务与空间配置增多，人工成本急剧上升，主要体现在示范采集与任务特定训练设计（如奖励函数）两方面。

Method: 1) 参考合成：将可复用任务模板适配到不同空间配置，自动合成协调的机器人—物体运动参考；2) 共享训练流水线：以参考驱动的模仿作为跨任务统一学习目标；3) 先训练特权教师策略执行参考，再通过教师蒸馏与强化学习训练视觉策略，并采用多样物体实例与视觉域随机化提升视觉泛化与sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在五个全身移动操作任务上，仿真平均成功率达91.0%；在真实人形机器人上部署，验证了全身操作的sim-to-real迁移成功。

Conclusion: 该框架以低成本参考合成与统一训练目标替代示范采集和任务特定奖励设计，具备规模化学习多样化视觉人形移动操作技能的潜力。

Abstract: Scaling up visual humanoid loco-manipulation skill acquisition requires reducing the human effort needed as tasks and spatial configurations expand, particularly in demonstration collection and task-specific training design. We present SUGARv2, a unified framework for learning diverse visual humanoid loco-manipulation skills through interaction reference synthesis and a shared training pipeline. To obtain diverse references at low cost, we adapt reusable task templates to different spatial configurations, synthesizing coordinated robot and object motions. To reduce task-specific reward design and training effort, we use reference-based imitation as a common learning objective across tasks. We first train privileged teachers to execute these references, then train visual policies through teacher distillation and reinforcement learning with the same reference-based mimic rewards, using diverse object instances and visual domain randomization to support visual generalization and sim-to-real transfer. Across five humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, SUGARv2 achieves a mean success rate of 91.0% in simulation. Deployment on a real humanoid robot further demonstrates successful sim-to-real transfer for whole-body manipulation. These results demonstrate the framework's potential for learning diverse visual humanoid loco-manipulation skills.

</details>


### [344] [EGT: Execution-Grounded Traversability through Learned Closed-Loop Trajectory Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09270)
*Chinmay Mundane,Amith Manoharan,Arun Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Execution-Grounded Traversability (EGT)，定义为"车辆-控制器对在满足轨迹级跟踪与进度要求下执行候选路径的概率"，用潜空间flow matching预测闭环轨迹分布来估计该概率，显著降低安全路径误拒与危险路径误收，并提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统崎岖地形可通行性评估沿规划路径逐点计算roll、pitch或force-angle裕度，忽略了地形扰动与底层控制器修正对车辆实际运动的影响，导致指标不准确且过度保守。

Method: 将可通行性重新定义为EGT概率指标；以多分辨率地形特征、3D车辆姿态与轮地接触点先验为条件，采用潜空间flow matching (FM)模型预测闭环轨迹分布，从而估计EGT；并将EGT嵌入滚动时域采样规划器。

Result: 在未见地形上650次仿真试验中，EGT将安全路径误拒率降至2.2%（姿态裕度与force-angle基线为11.4%–39.5%），危险路径误收率7.7%；集成到receding-horizon采样规划器后，相比[1]和[2]导航失败率分别降低24–42与30–44个百分点，到达目标时间最高缩短20.5%与45%；并实现仿真训练模型到真实户外实验的zero-shot迁移。

Conclusion: 以闭环执行结果为导向的可通行性度量优于逐点几何/力裕度指标，兼顾安全性与非保守性，且具备仿真到现实的泛化能力。

Abstract: Rough terrain traversability is often estimated through point-wise roll, pitch, or force-angle margins along a planned path. However, these metrics are often inaccurate and overly conservative because they ignore how terrain disturbances and low-level controller corrections can affect the vehicle's motions. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Execution-Grounded Traversability (EGT), defined as the probability that a vehicle-controller pair can execute a candidate path while meeting trajectory-level tracking and progress requirements. We estimate EGT by predicting the distribution of closed-loop trajectories using a latent-space flow matching (FM) model conditioned on multi-resolution terrain features and 3D vehicle attitude and wheel-ground contact points prior. Across 650 simulated trials on unseen terrains, EGT reduces safe-path rejection to 2.2% (versus 11.4%-39.5% for pose-margin and force-angle baselines) and unsafe acceptance to 7.7%. Integrated into a receding-horizon sampling-based planner, EGT reduces navigation failure rates by 24-42 and 30-44 percentage points over [1] and [2], respectively, while cutting goal-reaching time by up to 20.5% and 45%. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot transfer of our simulation-trained model to real-world outdoor experiments.

</details>


### [345] [Task-Driven Closed-Loop Design Optimization of Passively Morphing Quadrotors for Contact-Mediated Narrow Gap Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09272)
*Etor Arza,Luca Girardi,Gabriel Maquignaz,Javier Paez,Stefan Leutenegger,Stefano Mintchev,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出闭环设计方法，联合优化四旋翼几何与被动折叠臂的扭矩-挠度响应，以仿真中接触式穿越缝隙的成功率作为反馈信号，生成可被动变形的紧凑耐撞机体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中作业的空中机器人常需穿过比自身标称宽度更窄的开口。现有被动变形无人机虽能缩小宽度，但多为人工设计，难以在折叠比、飞行效率与控制跟踪性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 构建闭环设计优化框架：以穿越任务成功率为反馈信号，由优化算法采样候选设计，并在基于接触的仿真中评估；同时联合优化四旋翼几何构型与两条被动折叠臂的扭矩-挠度响应。

Result: 原型机宽度由31.3 cm被动压缩至14.1 cm，以2 m/s参考速度穿越15 cm宽开口；可容忍最大30°的接近角误差，并能折叠通过40 cm长的受限通道；与刚性同构型相比，无接触飞行时位置RMSE仅增加2 mm，电功率增加不足1%。

Conclusion: 任务驱动的闭环设计优化能够生成在接触时紧凑、对接近误差具有容忍性、且在常规飞行中近似刚性的被动变形机体结构。

Abstract: Aerial robots operating in cluttered environments may encounter openings narrower than their nominal width. Existing passively morphing drones reduce their width upon traversal, but they typically involve human-made designs, which can struggle to find the right balance among folding ratio, flight efficiency, and control tracking performance.
