We introduce a closed-loop design method that jointly optimizes quadrotor geometry and the torque-deflection response of two passively folding arms driven by the success rate in simulation for contact-mediated gap traversal. An optimization algorithm samples candidate designs that are evaluated in a contact-based simulation of the traversal task, where the task success rate is used as a feedback signal to sample progressively better designs over time. The resulting prototype passively reduces its width from 31.3 to 14.1 cm and traverses a 15 cm wide opening at a 2 m/s reference speed. It also tolerates gap approach-angle errors of up to 30 degrees and folds through a 40 cm long confined passage. Compared to a rigid twin, the soft configuration adds only 2 mm of position RMSE and less than 1% electrical power during contact-free flight. These results show that task-driven closed-loop design optimization can produce passively morphing airframes that are compact under contact, tolerant to approach error, and effectively rigid during nominal flight.

</details>


### [346] [MDF++: Visuo-Tactile World Action Model with End-to-end Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09293)
*Zixuan Huang,Jinhao Yu,Zachary Charlick,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MDF++ 是一种视觉-触觉世界-动作模型，用扩散骨干端到端学习多模态观测的隐表示，并通过多任务学习策略利用跨模态相关性避免特征坍缩，在接触密集操作任务上优于基于冻结预训练表示的 WAM 最高 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）依赖以重建目标训练的冻结预训练编码器，其表示未必适合机器人控制；因此需要为 WAM 学习具有物理基础、与控制相关的表示。

Method: 提出 MDF++：视觉-触觉世界-动作模型，采用扩散骨干网络端到端学习多感官观测的隐表示；针对朴素联合训练易导致特征坍缩的问题，引入多任务学习策略，利用跨模态相关性学习任务相关表示。

Result: 在一系列接触密集操作任务（含真实世界的螺栓拧入与螺母装配）上评估，性能较基于冻结预训练表示的 WAM 提升最高约 50%；另提供项目网站与视频。

Conclusion: 端到端学习的物理基础多模态表示可有效替代冻结预训练编码器，为 WAM 提供更强的控制相关特征；多任务学习是缓解跨模态联合训练特征坍缩的有效手段。

Abstract: Recently, world-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a powerful framework for robot manipulation. By jointly modeling action generation and future observation prediction, WAMs can act and anticipate the outcomes of the actions. However, existing WAMs typically rely on frozen pretrained encoders trained with reconstruction, which may not produce representations well suited for robot control. In this paper, we tackle the problem of learning physicallygrounded, control-relevant representations for WAMs. We propose MDF++, a visuo-tactile world-action model that learns latent representations of multisensory observations end-to-end with a diffusion backbone. While naive joint training can lead to feature collapse, MDF++ introduces a multi-task learning strategy that leverages cross-modal correlations to learn task-relevant representations. We evaluate MDF++ on a suite of contact-rich manipulation tasks, including realworld bolt screwing and nut mating, and show that it outperforms WAMs built on frozen pretrained representations by up to 50%. Additional videos are available on our website: jointmdf.github.io.

</details>


### [347] [Horizon-Coupled Obstacle Guidance for Relative-Action Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09340)
*Guangyuan Wang,Haishan Xia,Zitao Zhang,Kejia Chen,Yuanchen Sun,Zhenguo Sun,Donghao Zhang,Yu Zhang,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种推理时障碍引导框架，用于预训练的"相对动作"扩散策略：将累积相对动作展开为工作空间轨迹，用基于SDF的障碍模型评估轨迹上的障碍交互，并在去噪过程中把每个障碍响应传播回所有贡献该位置的动作增量，从而生成完整、任务导向的绕行运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人部署需在无新示范、无重训练条件下适应未见障碍。现有策略级引导多在单个horizon步独立施加障碍修正，只能解决局部碰撞违反，难以协调出完整、任务导向的绕行动作序列；障碍响应如何在动作horizon上传播尚缺乏研究。

Method: 推理时障碍引导框架作用于预训练的相对动作扩散策略：将累积相对动作rollout为工作空间轨迹；以SDF障碍模型沿轨迹评估障碍交互；在去噪中将每个障碍响应传播至所有贡献该轨迹位置的动作增量（horizon耦合）。

Result: 四类操作任务、七个障碍约束场景的真机实验中，方法取得105/140（75.0%）无碰撞任务成功率，绝对动作逐点引导仅8/140（5.7%）。消融显示相对动作horizon耦合公式在两种障碍模型下均带来更大提升。

Conclusion: 障碍引导应在动作horizon上耦合传播而非逐点独立修正；相对动作表述与horizon耦合是关键增益来源，可在不重训练、不采集新示范的前提下实现无碰撞的任务导向避障。

Abstract: Real-world robot deployment requires adapting learned policies to unseen obstacles without collecting new demonstrations or retraining. Existing policy-level guidance often applies obstacle corrections independently at individual horizon steps, which may address local obstacle violations but fail to coordinate the action sequence into a complete, task-directed detour. How obstacle responses should be propagated across the action horizon to coordinate complete, task-directed avoidance motion remains underexplored. We introduce an inferencetime obstacle-guidance framework for pretrained relative-action diffusion policies. It rolls out cumulative relative actions into a workspace trajectory, evaluates obstacle interactions along the trajectory with an SDF-based obstacle model, and propagates each obstacle response to all contributing action increments during denoising. Real-robot experiments across seven obstacleconstrained scenarios spanning four manipulation tasks show that our method achieves 105/140 (75.0%) collision-free task successes, compared with 8/140 (5.7%) for absolute-action pointwise guidance. Ablations show that the relative-action horizoncoupled formulation provides the larger observed improvement across both obstacle models.

</details>


### [348] [Can LLMs Debug Robots by Editing Their Weights?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09376)
*Jusheng Zhang,Keze Wang,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 语言模型可仅编辑65行token嵌入实现对机器人策略词法指令接地的精确修复，但仅限词法层面而非通用能力获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将"权重空间编程"引入机器人基础策略，使大语言模型在不做梯度更新、不需新演示的条件下直接读取并编辑策略权重，修复其语言指令接地缺陷。

Method: GPT-6 Astra读取π_{0.5}策略权重，借助CPU工作区与带预算的仿真器反馈进行定向编辑（仅修改token嵌入行），并与随机方向编辑、完整词表替换等对照，在765条留出改写指令及13个额外机器人会话上评估。

Result: 三次独立会话均提升成功率，最佳从86.3%升至92.0%（48处修复、4处回退，配对精确p=1.31e-10）；含被编辑token的335条指令成功率由74.3%升至87.5%，其余430条无变化；随机方向编辑无效；完整替换增益相当但回退更多，与编辑器差异不显著；13个额外会话中12个候选被拒，唯一保留编辑无显著增益；另发现基准元数据使FastWAM成功率从70.0%降至48.6%，而所评VLA总体率不变。

Conclusion: 证据支持的是精准的词法修复，而非机器人缺失能力的通用获取；同时提示基准元数据可显著干扰评测结果。

Abstract: A language model can repair a robot policy's lexical instruction grounding by editing just 65 token-embedding rows. We bring weight-space programming to robot foundation policies: GPT-6 Astra reads weights and uses a CPU workspace and budgeted simulator feedback, without gradient updates or new demonstrations. Three independent sessions on $π_{0.5}$ all improve success on 765 held-out paraphrases; the best raises success from 86.3\% to 92.0\% (48 repairs, four regressions; exact paired $p=1.31\times10^{-10}$). Among the 335 instructions containing edited tokens, success rises from 74.3\% to 87.5\%; the other 430 episodes have zero outcome changes. Matched random-direction edits do not help. Full replacement using the editor's own lexicon explains much of the gain but produces more regressions; its direct difference from the editor is not significant at 0.05. Across 13 further robot sessions, the editor declines 12 candidates; the sole retained edit does not show a significant held-out gain. Separately, benchmark metadata lowers FastWAM success from 70.0\% to 48.6\%, while the evaluated VLA's aggregate rate is unchanged. The evidence supports precise lexical repair, not general acquisition of missing robot capabilities.

</details>


### [349] [Mechanical Interaction Sensing for Robotic Kiwifruit Handling with an Optoelectronic Soft Suction Cup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09468)
*Romina Adhami,Ebrahim Shahabi,Alessio Mondini,Samuel M. Youssef,Cosimo Della Santina,Gualtiero Volpe,Barbara Mazzolai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种双组分设计的猕猴桃专用光电软吸盘，利用光学信号定量估计末端执行器与果实的接触力，在未见果实上取得分组留出R²=0.968、MAE=0.138 N的高精度力估计，并探索性实现了中等精度的软/硬二分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取易损水果需要柔性接触并对末端执行器机械交互进行定量感知。然而现有软体夹持器多依赖外部力传感器，成本高、集成难；作者希望利用软吸盘内部的光电信号本身来反演接触力，从而实现"结构-感知一体化"，并针对猕猴桃这一特定果形与质地设计专用吸盘。

Method: 设计双组分（bicomponent）光电软吸盘并采集真实猕猴桃接触过程数据：在已知接触时段内同步记录光学响应与参考力。采用分组交叉验证（按果实分组、留一采集日验证）评估模型泛化性；对比多种浅层网络与仅用平均光学响应的单调映射；并作为次要探索分析，用压缩测试参考标签做软/硬二分类与三分类分级。

Result: 在排除于训练之外的果实上，最佳浅层网络取得分组折外合并R²=0.968±0.005（MAE=0.138 N），逐果留出R²中位数0.973（MAE=0.134 N）；仅用平均光学响应的单调映射即可达R²=0.925±0.005，说明光学响应中已蕴含大量力相关信息。留一采集日验证R²=0.955±0.031，表明跨采集会话稳健。软/硬二分类在分组留出果实验证下平衡准确率约0.705，三分类分级性能有限。

Conclusion: 光电软吸盘可在不额外增加力传感器的条件下实现对真实果实接触力的高精度、跨果实与跨会话稳健估计，且光信号与力之间存在近单调的强相关性；但基于同一信号的质地分级（尤其三分类）仍有局限，未来需改进特征与建模以支撑更细粒度品质评估。

Abstract: Robotic handling of delicate fruit benefits from compliant contact and quantitative sensing of the mechanical interaction at the end-effector. This study investigates an optoelectronic soft suction cup with a kiwifruit-specific bicomponent design for estimating interaction forces during contact with real fruit. During known-contact intervals, the optical signals accurately estimate interaction force on fruits excluded from model training: the best shallow network achieves grouped out-of-fold pooled R-squared = 0.968 +/- 0.005 (MAE = 0.138 N), with a median per-fruit held-out R-squared = 0.973 (MAE = 0.134 N). A simple monotonic mapping from the mean optical response alone achieves R-squared = 0.925 +/- 0.005, showing that substantial force-related information is already present in the optical response. Leave-one-collection-day-out validation remains strong (R-squared = 0.955 +/- 0.031), supporting robustness across acquisition sessions. As an exploratory secondary analysis, binary Soft/Firm classification from compression-test reference labels achieves moderate balanced accuracy (approximately 0.705) under grouped held-out-fruit validation, while finer three-class grading shows limited performance.

</details>


### [350] [CucumberVision at the Edge: Accuracy, Throughput, and Energy of RGB-D Cucumber Sizing on Jetson Orin Nano and Raspberry Pi 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09490)
*Manveen Kaur,Rajmeet Singh,Saeed Mozaffari,Majid Ahmadi,Shahpour Alirezaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在Jetson Orin Nano与Raspberry Pi 4上部署曲率感知黄瓜长度测量流水线，并与RTX 3050 Ti工作站对照，量化边缘部署的精度-吞吐-能耗代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D非接触水果尺寸测量在工作站硬件上已达亚厘米精度，但田间分级需在低功耗单板计算机上运行同一流水线，而这种迁移的代价鲜有报道。

Method: 将YOLO26n检测、提示式分割与中轴弧样条估计器组成的曲率感知黄瓜测长流水线部署于Jetson Orin Nano和Raspberry Pi 4；用50组相同温室采集（7个样本）回放做跨平台表征，采用样本级聚类自助法统计；并进行TensorRT FP16编译与四组消融（分割后端、INT8量化、能耗模式等）。

Result: 精度保持：工作站与Jetson在匹配精度下MAE均为0.58 cm（95% CI 0.46–0.69），配对差为零；TensorRT FP16使Jetson检测延迟降低2.57倍，形成72 ms/0.84 cm与2.74 s/0.64 cm两个工作点；分割后端决定精度（跨平台复现误差≤0.01 cm）而平台决定成本（差异达214倍）；检测头整图INT8量化因单张量混合像素坐标与置信度而结构性失效、无校准策略可避免，排除检测头后恢复并提速1.37倍；Jetson每果能耗42.2 J，其7 W模式比15 W模式多耗能73%；Pi配MobileSAM每果成本为Jetson配SAM的2.5倍，尽管功耗低47%，且持续负载下无热降频。

Conclusion: 结果给出含三个非支配配置的精度-吞吐-能耗包络，并给出一套配置选择规则；同时表明精度与成本决策可解耦。

Abstract: Non-contact fruit sizing from RGB-D imagery reaches sub-centimetre accuracy on workstation hardware, but in-field grading requires the same pipeline to run on a low-power single-board computer, and the cost of that transition is rarely reported. We deploy a curvature-aware cucumber length-measurement pipeline (YOLO26n detection, prompt-based segmentation and a medial arc spline estimator) on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano and a Raspberry Pi 4, and characterize both against an RTX 3050 Ti workstation by replaying 50 identical greenhouse captures of 7 specimens, with specimen-level cluster-bootstrap statistics. Accuracy is preserved: mean absolute error (MAE) is 0.58 cm (95% CI 0.46-0.69) on both the workstation and the Jetson at matched precision, with a paired difference of zero. TensorRT FP16 compilation cuts Jetson detection latency 2.57x, giving two operating points on one board: 72 ms per fruit at 0.84 cm, or 2.74 s at 0.64 cm. Four ablations map the design space. For prompted segmentation backends, measurement accuracy is fixed by the backend and reproduces across platforms to within 0.01 cm, while its cost is fixed by the platform and varies 214x, so the two choices are separable. Whole-graph INT8 quantization of the NMS-free detection head fails structurally, returning no detections, because one output tensor carries both pixel coordinates and confidences; we show that no calibration strategy can avoid this. Excluding the head restores function at 1.37x. Energy per fruit is 42.2 J on the Jetson, and its 7 W mode consumes 73% more than its 15 W mode. The Raspberry Pi with MobileSAM costs 2.5x more per fruit than the Jetson with SAM despite drawing 47% less power, and it runs without thermal throttling under sustained load. The results define an accuracy-throughput-energy envelope with three non-dominated configurations and give rules for selecting among them.

</details>


### [351] [Lightweight Path-Aware Frontier Re-ranking for LiDAR-Based UAV Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09561)
*Duc Manh Do,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Ngoc Minh Do,Mai Thanh Thai,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级路径感知前沿重排序方法，用时间受限RRT*估计候选前沿的真实可行路径代价以改进LiDAR无人机自主探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限飞行时间与机载算力约束下，传统基于欧氏距离的前沿选择在杂乱环境中无法反映真实无碰撞通行代价，导致前沿选择低效。

Method: 先用轻量效用函数保留top-N有前景的前沿候选，再用时间受限RRT*规划估计其可行路径代价并据此重排序；选中前沿所生成路径复用为导航路径，减少冗余全局规划。

Result: 在三个类仓库环境中，该方法相比传统前沿基线持续获得更短探索轨迹，行驶距离最多减少14.1%；两个环境中探索时间最多缩短9.5%，第三个环境中保持竞争力；地图细节与OctoMap基线相当。

Conclusion: 路径感知的前沿重排序可在保持建图质量的同时有效提升探索效率，实现效率与建图质量之间的良好权衡。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D exploration with UAVs requires efficient target selection under limited flight time and onboard computation. In conventional Euclidean-distance-based frontier pipelines, geometric distance may poorly represent the actual collision-free travel cost in cluttered environments, leading to inefficient frontier choices. This paper presents a lightweight path-aware frontier re-ranking method for LiDAR-based UAV exploration. The method first retains a small top-N set of promising frontier candidates using a lightweight utility function and then applies time-bounded RRT* planning to estimate their feasible path costs for re-ranking. The path generated for the selected frontier is further reused for navigation to reduce redundant global planning. Experiments in three warehouse-like environments show that the proposed method consistently produces shorter exploration trajectories than conventional frontier baselines, with traveled-distance reductions of up to 14.1%, while reducing exploration time by up to 9.5% in two environments and remaining competitive in the third. The method maintains map detail comparable to the conventional OctoMap-based baseline, demonstrating an effective trade-off between exploration efficiency and mapping quality.

</details>


### [352] [Extended Model-Based Learned Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09584)
*Sasanka Kuruppu Arachchige,Joni-Kristian Kämäräinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出体坐标系表示并引入体坐标系力矩动力学（由旋翼信号导出）改进学习式惯性里程计，提升四旋翼未见轨迹上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯惯性测量受传感器误差与时变偏置影响导致显著位姿漂移；现有无人机竞速中的学习式惯性里程计对训练时未见轨迹泛化能力有限。

Method: 采用体坐标系（body-frame）表示，并将体坐标系力矩动力学纳入学习流程；模型预测短时域相对位移，再与IMU测量在扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）中融合。

Result: 在未见轨迹上的四旋翼位姿估计优于此前最优的学习式惯性里程计方法；在四个公开四旋翼数据集的整条轨迹形状留出评估中，所有留出集速度误差均更低，且力矩输入由旋翼信号导出时信息量最大。

Conclusion: 体坐标系表示与力矩动力学输入有效提升学习式惯性里程计泛化性；代码、数据流水线与训练模型公开。

Abstract: Inertial odometry is a compelling approach to state estimation for agile quadrotor flight due to its affordability, low weight, and robustness to perceptual degradation. However, relying only on integrated inertial measurements is impractical, as sensor errors and time-varying biases lead to significant pose drift. Although recent advances have enabled inertial odometry for drone racing, existing approaches show limited generalization to trajectories not seen during training. This work improves generalization by adopting a body-frame representation and incorporating body-frame torque dynamics into the learning pipeline. The learned model predicts short-horizon relative displacements, which are fused with IMU measurements in an Extended Kalman Filter. Experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art learned inertial odometry approach for quadrotor pose estimation on unseen trajectories. An additional evaluation on four public quadrotor datasets, with whole trajectory shapes held out of training, confirms a lower velocity error on every held-out set and shows that the torque input is most informative when it is derived from the rotor signals. Code, data pipeline and trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [353] [DKWBC: Decoupling Motion Tracking and Balancing for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09605)
*Dingkun Wei,Zehong Shen,Huaijin Pi,Xueyang Wang,Xing Zhu,Hujun Bao,Yujun Shen,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DKWBC 提出一种动力学信息驱动的全身控制框架，将基于模型的显式跟踪（逆动力学前馈+关节PD反馈）与学习型平衡残差策略结合，用单一平衡策略实现跨数据集的运动跟踪与抗扰平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身控制需在重力、力矩极限、接触切换与外部扰动下跟踪多样参考运动并保持平衡。现有RL跟踪器把运动跟踪与平衡合为统一控制输出学习，但相似参考位姿的动态需求可能差异很大，导致策略须同时学习运动相关控制需求与平衡修正，增加学习难度并引起控制输出高频抖动。

Method: 针对浮基动力学中关节力矩由模型项与环境外力项共同贡献的特性，DKWBC 将控制解耦：用逆动力学前馈加关节PD反馈提供跟踪力矩，再由残差策略学习额外的关节力矩修正以实现平衡执行；显式计入运动相关力矩需求以简化策略学习任务。

Result: 仿真与真机实验表明，单一学习型平衡策略可跨多个运动数据集泛化，在地面支撑、附加负载及物理交互场景下均保持准确的运动跟踪与平衡。

Conclusion: 将模型驱动的跟踪控制与学习型平衡残差显式分离，是一种有效降低学习复杂度、提升泛化性与控制平滑性的全身控制范式。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body control requires a robot to track diverse reference motions while maintaining balance under gravity, torque limits, contact switching, and external disturbances. Existing reinforcement-learning-based trackers typically learn control outputs that jointly address motion tracking and balance. However, similar reference poses can correspond to substantially different dynamic demands, requiring a unified policy to learn motion-dependent control requirements alongside balance corrections. This can complicate learning and lead to high-frequency fluctuations in control outputs. We propose DKWBC, a dynamics-informed whole-body control framework that combines explicit model-based tracking with a learned balancing residual. Inspired by the model-based and environmental-wrench contributions to joint torques in floating-base dynamics, DKWBC uses inverse-dynamics feedforward and joint PD feedback to supply tracking torques, while a residual policy learns additional joint-torque corrections for balanced execution. By explicitly accounting for motion-dependent torque demands, this structure simplifies the policy's learning task. Experiments in simulation and on real hardware demonstrate that a single learned balancing policy generalizes across motion datasets while maintaining accurate motion tracking and balance across ground support, attached payloads, and physical interaction. Project page: https://robbyant-research.github.io/DKWBC/.

</details>


### [354] [LACART: Lookahead-Augmented Collision Avoidance and Reference Tracking Strategies for Safety-Critical Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09640)
*Pedro Lopes,Amit Shivam,Luis Tiago Paiva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LACART方法，将前瞻型控制障碍函数(CBF)、松弛型控制李雅普诺夫函数(CLF)与二次规划(QP)统一在一个优化层中，同时处理圆形障碍物、赛道边界与参考轨迹跟踪，用于自动驾驶赛车避撞，仿真中计算耗时低于对比方法并在1:43小车平台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车对碰撞避免的几何约束与计算实时性要求极高，需要在严格受限的算力下同时保证安全性与跟踪性能，并解决单一优化层内统一处理多类约束的需求。

Method: LACART框架：以基于前瞻点的CBF处理圆形障碍物与赛道边界约束，配合松弛CLF保证参考跟踪可行，通过QP对名义控制指令进行最小修正；并在1:43比例车辆平台上（相机视觉估计+无线执行）以约25 Hz控制频率实现验证，同时推导前瞻点间隙与车体间隙之间的关联条件。

Result: 与Huang等人的方法相比，完整赛道行驶轨迹相近，而单步中位计算时间为0.31 ms vs 0.33 ms，单次QP求解为0.023 ms vs 0.025 ms；小车平台实现约25 Hz控制回路，并记录了安全干预与恢复的演示。

Conclusion: 前瞻点CBF+松弛CLF+QP的统一优化结构可在低算力下实现实时避撞与跟踪，具备地面机器人避撞与实时安全监控的应用价值；硬件实验亦暴露了平台固有局限，为后续实物验证提出改进方向。

Abstract: Autonomous racing provides a demanding testbed for collision avoidance under tight geometric and computational constraints. Building on a recent conference contribution and a master's dissertation, LACART combines lookahead-based control barrier functions (CBFs), a relaxed control Lyapunov function (CLF), and quadratic programming (QP) to modify nominal vehicle commands for obstacle avoidance and reference tracking. The method handles circular obstacles and track boundaries in a common optimisation layer. Comparative simulation results against Huang \emph{et al.} \cite{r9} show similar full-track paths and lower median computation times: 0.31 versus 0.33 ms per step and 0.023 versus 0.025 ms per QP solve. The dissertation further demonstrates a 1:43-scale vehicle with camera-based estimation and wireless actuation at a reported 25 Hz control rate. We identify the conditions linking lookahead-point clearance to body clearance, illustrate hardware limitations, and propose a recorded demonstration of safety intervention and recovery. The contribution targets ground-robot collision avoidance and real-time safety monitoring.

</details>


### [355] [Goodhart in the Planner: Sampling-Based Control Exploits Rewards Learned from Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09670)
*Soovadeep Bakshi,Apurba Bose*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文量化了"用人类偏好学得的奖励模型配合更硬的搜索（MPPI/best-of-N）"对真实回报的破坏：在多个任务上，更强的规划反而使真实回报下降最多76%（真实标签下抽屉任务0/20成功），而留出标签准确率仍在0.75–0.97；并给出χ²正则MPPI在评分器误差尺度下的最坏情况最优性与安全校准修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在MPC或best-of-N中依赖从人类偏好学得的奖励模型选动作，但奖励模型仅是proxy。作者要回答：若按此proxy做更激进的搜索优化，会对真实目标造成多大损害、损害由什么量决定、以及能否给出可证明的保障与实用修复。

Method: 在HalfCheetah、Hopper（众包标签）与Meta-World drawer-open、plate-slide（合成标签+两位作者盲标）上，比较不同搜索强度（MPPI温度/样本数、best-of-N）与克隆策略的真实回报，同时报告留出标签准确率；用√KL度量规划器权重偏离提议分布的程度并与真实回报关联；理论上分析χ²正则MPPI，温度由评分器在提议上的误差散布设定，并推导回报按步分解及可认证增益的条件；提出用100对提议、以真实回报为oracle在训练未用初始状态上校准温度，并比较单人多比较校准、剔除人类无法区分的样本、引入第二标注者等方案。

Result: 更难搜索把真实回报最多降低76%；drawer-open人类标签下20次运行0成功，而克隆策略10/10成功，同时留出准确率保持0.75–0.97，说明proxy精度与真实回报脱钩；plate-slide上人类标签比合成标签更难被利用，window-open上搜索有益；真实回报大体随√KL（权重相对提议的偏移）变化；合成标签verifier的best-of-N甚至低于单个带噪动作；χ²正则MPPI在给定误差散布下最坏情况最优，散布超过评分器自身误差时无法认证正增益；oracle校准规则在五个独立集合的所有arm运行中均成功，在plate-slide与drawer-open上平均保留克隆策略86–94%回报，但在搜索有益处偏保守。

Conclusion: 偏好奖励模型上的激进优化存在系统性reward hacking，且以标签准确率评估会掩盖该问题；理论给出可认证增益的边界与按步回报分解，实用的oracle校准χ²正则MPPI是所测最安全方案但保守；从单人比较校准无法检出该标注者所导致的偏差，剔除难以区分的比较或引入第二标注者能限制损害。

Abstract: Robots increasingly choose actions by searching against a reward model learned from human preferences, in model-predictive control or best-of-$N$ selection; we measure what this does to the true objective. On HalfCheetah and Hopper with crowd-sourced labels, and on Meta-World drawer-open and plate-slide with synthetic labels and blind labels from the two authors, harder MPPI search cuts true return by up to 76%: with human labels the drawer opens in 0 of 20 runs against 10 of 10 for the cloned policy the planner starts from, while held-out label accuracy stays at 0.75-0.97. On plate-slide human labels are exploited less than synthetic ones, and on window-open search helps. True return roughly follows how far the planner's weights move from its proposals ($\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}}$) for both weighting rules we test, and best-of-$N$ with a verifier from synthetic labels falls below a single noisy action. We prove that $χ^2$-regularized MPPI at a temperature set by the scorer's error spread on the proposals is worst-case optimal, that no positive gain can be certified once that spread exceeds the scorer's own, and that an episode's return splits exactly into per-step terms, of which the guarantee covers the selection terms. Calibrated from 100 proposal pairs ranked by true return (an oracle) on start states the evaluation never uses, this rule is the safest fix we test: with each of five independent sets it succeeds in every arm run and keeps on average 86-94% of the cloned policy's return on plate-slide and drawer-open, though it is cautious where search helps. Calibration from a person's comparisons cannot detect a bias the scorer learned from that person; correcting for the pairs people cannot tell apart, or a second person, limits the damage.

</details>


### [356] [ODEAction: From Predicting Chunks to Evolving Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09672)
*Qishen Wang,Pengxiang Ding,Shifeng Bao,Mingyang Sun,Hengtao Li,Yi Gao,Baohua Yin,Yang Liu,Jiajun Fu,Jiacheng Liu,Changjing He,Peiyang Li,Runze Suo,Yan Wang,Xiaobo Wang,Yonghao Dang,Cheng Chi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ODEAction：用常微分方程（ODE）连续演化动作取代动作分块（action chunk）预测，消除动作块切换处的不连续性，性能与轨迹质量优于匹配的分块基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型以"动作块"方式预测动作，将连续机器人执行切分为独立预测的片段，块与块之间存在潜在的不连续性（跳变、抖动），影响执行平滑性与精细操作。

Method: 提出 chunk-free 动作生成范式"连续动作演化"：以视觉观测、语言指令和机器人本体感知为条件，在动作空间上学习一个动作转移场，其中转移向量刻画当前动作相对演化时间的演化速率。执行时以当前动作为初值条件，用 ODE 积分该学习到的场，递归生成后续动作，使动作间具有显式依赖关系。ODEAction 实现为动作头，不依赖特定的视觉-语言骨干架构。

Result: 在 LIBERO、EBench、RoboTwin 及真机实验中，相比匹配的动作分块基线，任务成功率与轨迹质量均提升，在精细操作任务上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 以 ODE 定义的连续动作演化可作为动作分块的有效替代方案，模块化（动作头形式）易集成，兼顾平滑性与精度，适合对轨迹连续性敏感的精细操作场景。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically generate actions in chunks, organizing continuous robot execution into separately predicted segments and thus introducing potential discontinuities at action-chunk transitions. We propose ODEAction, a chunk-free action generation paradigm that replaces action-chunk prediction with Continuous Action Evolution. Given visual observations, language instructions, and robot proprioceptive information, ODEAction learns an action transition field defined over action space, where a transition vector characterizes the evolution rate of the current action with respect to evolution time. During execution, the model takes the current action as its initial condition and integrates the learned field using an ordinary differential equation (ODE), recursively generating subsequent actions with explicit dependencies. ODEAction is implemented as an action head and does not depend on a specific vision-language backbone architecture. Experiments on LIBERO, EBench, RoboTwin, and real robots show that ODEAction improves both task performance and trajectory quality over matched action-chunking baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on fine manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [357] [Autonomous Particle Image Velocimetry Using Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09679)
*Chang Rajani,Luke Sebastien Marsden,Stephan Kunz,Claudio Mucignat,Ivan Lunati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将相机位姿规划建模为序贯决策问题，用强化学习训练6-DoF机械臂相机在水洞中自主定位、取景并高分辨重建水翼尾流，仿真训练后零样本迁移到真实装置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIV重建质量受限于目标流结构在图像中的分辨情况，而采集位置通常由操作者事先固定，缺乏自适应性，难以在未知位置水翼尾流上获得高分辨率与低统计误差。

Method: 将相机布置转化为序贯决策任务，训练RL智能体控制搭载相机的6-DoF机械臂，输出相机位姿；观测量仅为由时间分离图像对估计的速度场与不确定度，而非原始图像。智能体完全在光真实感水洞仿真中训练，尾流由不同翼型、位置、攻角的CFD仿真生成。

Result: 零样本迁移至真实水洞后，策略能可靠定位并取景随机摆放的水翼尾流；相比固定视角具有更高重建分辨率与更低流动统计误差，耗时约为全扫描的一半，且所需图像对显著减少。

Conclusion: 表明以PIV速度场与不确定度为观测的强化学习主动测量框架，可在仿真中训练并零样本部署于真实实验，实现高效、自适应的流场测量，优于固定相机与全扫描基线。

Abstract: Particle image velocimetry (PIV) reconstructs a flow field from the motion of tracer particles but the reconstruction quality is bounded by how well the flow structure of interest is resolved in the images. The acquisition position is typically fixed by the operator before the measurement. We cast camera placement as a sequential decision-making problem and train a reinforcement learning agent to control a 6-DoF manipulator equipped with a camera, enabling it to locate, frame and resolve a hydrofoil wake in a water tunnel. The agent commands the camera pose and observes the scene only through the velocity field and uncertainty estimated from time-separated image pairs. It is trained entirely in a photorealistic simulation of our water tunnel, with wakes generated by Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations of hydrofoils with varying profiles, positions and angles of attack. After zero-shot transfer to the real water tunnel, the policy reliably locates and frames the wakes of randomly placed hydrofoils. It resolves the wake at a higher resolution than a fixed camera view and with lower error in the measured flow statistics, in roughly half the time of a full scan and with far fewer image pairs.

</details>


### [358] [SURE:Spatiotemporal Uncertainty Representation for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09684)
*Jingye Zhang,Choo Sin Wai,Dake Zhong,Zhifang Liu,Yuhong Zhang,Siyao Xiao,Guorui Song,Guocun Wang,Dongbin Zhang,Peng Jiao,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SURE 是一个数据高效的 VLA 训练框架，通过显式建模动作空间的空间与时间不确定性（转置卷积概率动作解码器 + Soft-DTW 对齐监督），显著降低对演示数据量与训练步数的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略虽在端到端机器人控制中表现突出，但严重依赖大规模演示数据。在操作任务中，动作空间存在结构化的时空不确定性：多个邻近动作或轨迹都能成功完成任务，语义等价的演示往往以不同速度执行。标准逐点监督把每条演示动作当作精确目标，在不确定性下带来不必要的学习负担。

Method: 在 VLA 主干上增加轻量级转置卷积概率动作解码器，通过相邻动作 bin 间的局部相关性建模空间不确定性；同时引入 Soft-DTW 对齐监督，容忍预测动作块与演示动作块之间的局部时序变化。两个组件共同降低对演示者特定姿态与执行时序的敏感性。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实世界操作任务上评测；仅使用 10% 演示数据或 20% 训练步数，SURE 的操作成功率即可匹配或超过基线 VLA。

Conclusion: 将动作监督从点式精确回归转向对空间多模态与时间弹性对齐的显式建模，是提升 VLA 数据效率的有效途径，且以轻量组件实现、可即插即用。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have become a promising paradigm for end-to-end robot control, but their effectiveness still depends heavily on large volumes of demonstration data. In manipulation tasks, this dependence is compounded by structured spatiotemporal uncertainty in the action space: multiple nearby actions or trajectories may successfully solve the same task, while semantically equivalent demonstrations often unfold at different speeds. Standard pointwise supervision treats each demonstrated action as an exact target, imposing an unnecessary learning burden under such uncertainty. We present SURE, a data-efficient VLA training framework that explicitly models both spatial and temporal uncertainty. SURE augments a VLA backbone with a lightweight transposed-convolutional probabilistic action decoder that captures spatial uncertainty through local correlations among neighboring action bins. It further introduces Soft-DTW alignment supervision to accommodate local timing variations between predicted and demonstrated action chunks. Together, these two components reduce sensitivity to demonstrator-specific poses and execution timing, enabling more data-efficient action learning. We evaluate SURE on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks. Even with only 10% of the demonstration data or 20% of the training steps, SURE achieves manipulation success rates that match or surpass baseline VLAs.

</details>


### [359] [A wearable input/output interface learns personalized models of haptic perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09708)
*Wen Li,E. Magnus Adams,Zijie Xu,Jiawen Yao,Aybala Esmer,Xiaoyue Ni,Bozhi Tian,Pedro Lopes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发集成36通道电触觉阵列与电容触控传感的可穿戴触觉I/O设备，在同一身体参考坐标系内同步记录刺激与用户报告的感觉位置；发现电触觉与牵涉性热感觉图谱存在显著个体间差异但保留电极对特异的空间结构，据此提出人机回环（human-in-the-loop）校准框架与个性化感知模型，实现逆向渲染并在机器人遥操作、视觉-触觉替代、人际触觉通信与VR中演示个性化触觉渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴触觉接口向皮肤施加物理刺激，但用户感知位置差异大且偏离执行器实际位置，尤其在新兴触觉反馈技术中更明显；现有设备难以在同一坐标系中把刺激位置与主观感知位置对齐建模，缺乏面向个体的感知模型，导致通用化渲染策略效果受限。

Method: 构建可穿戴触觉输入/输出（I/O）设备，将36通道电触觉阵列与电容式触摸传感集成，使刺激投射与用户报告的感觉位置在同一身体参考坐标系中配准；在24名被试中采集电触觉与牵涉性热感觉（referred-thermal）感知图谱，分析个体间变异性及跨被试的电极对特异空间结构；采用前向预测策略的人机回环校准框架，为每个用户生成个性化感知模型，并基于该模型进行逆向渲染，按目标感知反推刺激模式。

Result: 两种模态的感知图谱存在明显个体间差异，但均在被试间保留了电极对特异的空间结构；触觉与热感觉在空间上存在对应关系，其强度因人而异。个性化模型支持的逆向渲染在触觉引导机器人遥操作、视觉到触觉的感觉替代、人际触觉通信及虚拟现实四类应用中均实现了定制化触觉渲染。

Conclusion: 该平台提供了一条通往自适应触觉接口的路径，可按每个用户的感知进行测量、建模与渲染，为个性化、可校准的可穿戴触觉反馈奠定基础。

Abstract: Wearable haptic interfaces deliver physical stimuli to the skin, yet perceived locations vary widely across users and diverge from actuator locations, particularly for emergent tactile-feedback techniques. We developed a wearable haptic input/output (I/O) device integrating a 36-channel electrotactile array with capacitive touch sensing, enabling stimulation and user-reported percept locations to be registered within the same body-referenced coordinate frame. In 24 participants, the device revealed electrotactile and referred-thermal perceptual maps with notable interparticipant variability. Despite this variability, both modalities retained electrode-pair-specific spatial structure across participants. Tactile and thermal percepts also showed spatial correspondence, whose strength varied across participants. These findings motivated our human-in-the-loop calibration framework, which adapts a forward-prediction strategy to individual users and generates personalized perceptual models. These models enabled inverse rendering by selecting stimulation patterns predicted to generate a target percept for a specific user. Using these models, we demonstrate personalized haptic rendering in haptic-guided robot teleoperation, visual-to-haptic sensory substitution, interpersonal haptic communication, and virtual reality (VR). Together, this platform provides a route toward adaptive haptic interfaces that measure, model, and render according to each user's perception.

</details>


### [360] [Autonomous Whole-body Loco-manipulation Using a Snake Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09777)
*Adarsh Salagame,Henry Noyes,Haroon Hublikar,Alireza Ramezani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出无需专用附肢或抓取器的蛇形机器人全身运动—操作（loco-manipulation）闭环方法：在Moreau-Jean时间步进框架下建立统一接触隐式动力学模型，结合几何规划器与比例控制器生成轨迹优化，使COBRA在侧向蜿蜒行进中自主将物体推至目标位置，并在硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛇形机器人具备超冗余形态，可同时作为推进机构与操作工具，但全身loco-manipulation极具挑战。已有轮式、足式与蛇形平台多通过专用附肢、身体分段或夹持器将运动与操作解耦，限制了全身接触能力的利用。

Method: 构建接触隐式动力学模型，在Moreau-Jean时间步进框架内统一刻画机器人与地面、机器人与物体、物体与地面三类交互；在完整阶动力学上建立轨迹优化，并由几何规划器与标称比例控制器引导，实现侧向蜿蜒运动中的自主推物。

Result: 在硬件上对规划器与控制器在不同构型下进行了验证；并给出简化轨迹优化的仿真结果，该优化可解析机器人与物体、物体与地面之间的接触。

Conclusion: 无需对运动与操作作功能区分、允许身体任意部位接触的闭环loco-manipulation对蛇形机器人是可行的；统一的接触隐式建模与规划控制框架为全身接触式操作提供了基础。

Abstract: Snake robots can leverage their hyper-redundant morphology for both locomotion and manipulation through distributed body contact but whole-body loco-manipulation is particularly challenging since the entire body serves simultaneously as propulsive mechanism and manipulation tool Prior work on wheeled legged and snake robot platforms has decoupled these functions through dedicated appendages body segmentation or grippers We present closed-loop loco-manipulation with our snake robot COBRA without this distinction such that any part of the body may make contact We develop a contact-implicit dynamic model unifying robot-ground robot-object and object-ground interactions within a Moreau-Jean time-stepping framework and formulate a trajectory optimization over the full-order dynamics guided by a geometric planner and nominal proportional controller to autonomously push an object to a goal location during sidewinding We validate the planner and controller on hardware across varied configurations and present simulation results for a simplified trajectory optimization that resolves robot-object and object-ground contacts

</details>


### [361] [Seeing What Matters: Benchmarking Task-Conditioned Actionable Spatial Belief for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09794)
*Yina Jian,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 "Seeing What Matters" 基准，用于评测任务条件下的"可操作空间信念"（task-conditioned actionable spatial belief）：衡量视图选择策略能否针对当前指令获取使其可执行的证据，而非仅描述场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准只评测模型能否描述场景，但操作任务需要的是"获取使当前指令可执行的特定证据"的能力。同一工作台上，对某任务有用的视角对另一任务可能毫无价值，因此场景级完备性不足以刻画操作所需的感知能力，需按任务条件化评测。

Method: 构建仿真桌面操作基准，涵盖咖啡工作站、抽屉/柜、微波炉/电器三个领域，含 860 个冻结评测用例；扩展到方法、预算与推理重复后超过 115,000 个评测回合，采用两种协议：闭环可执行性与同物体功能分布外（functional-OOD）泛化。每个用例将候选 RGB 视图集合与操作指令、共享脚本化技能及隐藏后置条件配对，直接度量视图选择策略是否获得指令所需的观测。另提供诊断性参考流水线：维护空间-功能信念状态，按缺失证据对视图排序。

Result: 主预算设置下参考流水线达 98.8% 闭环与 98.9% functional-OOD 成功率；消融功能组件或指令绑定组件后性能跌至近零。七个视觉语言模型在相同用例上仅 26-43%。以冻结的 RGB 派生分数替换参考流水线的覆盖元数据仍保持 98.5%/98.9%，而打乱视觉分数则崩溃至 15%/11%，表明结果依赖图像内容。

Conclusion: 操作所需的空间信念应按"是否获取当前指令所需证据"来评测，而非场景级完备性；现有 VLM 在此指标上存在显著差距。

Abstract: When should a robot look at the cup, and when at the portafilter? Spatial-reasoning benchmarks evaluate whether a model can describe a scene, but manipulation needs something more specific: the ability to acquire the particular evidence that makes the current command executable. A view that is informative for one task can be useless for another, even at the same workstation. We introduce Seeing What Matters, a benchmark for task-conditioned actionable spatial belief in simulated tabletop manipulation. Across three domains--coffee workstation, drawer/cabinet, and microwave/appliance--the benchmark provides 860 frozen evaluation cases; expanding across methods, budgets, and inference repeats yields more than 115,000 evaluator episodes under two protocols: closed-loop actionability and same-object functional out-of-distribution generalization. Each case pairs a set of candidate RGB views with a manipulation command, shared scripted skills, and hidden postconditions, so the benchmark directly measures whether a view-selection policy acquires the observations its command actually needs. We also provide a diagnostic reference pipeline that maintains a spatial-functional belief state and ranks views by missing evidence. At primary budget settings, this pipeline reaches 98.8% closed-loop and 98.9% functional-OOD success; ablating the functional or command-binding components collapses performance to near zero. Seven vision-language models, evaluated on the same cases, reach only 26-43%. Replacing the reference pipeline's coverage metadata with frozen RGB-derived scores preserves 98.5%/98.9% success; shuffling the visual scores collapses it to 15%/11%, confirming that the result depends on image content. These gaps suggest that spatial belief for manipulation should be evaluated not by scene-level completeness, but by whether it acquires the evidence the current command requires.

</details>


### [362] [Grasp-TAGS: Task-Aware Grasp Selection via Critic Distillation for Prehensile Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09864)
*Xun Tu,Minghao Zou,Karthik Desingh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Grasp-TAGS，一个面向目标条件大物体重排的任务感知抓取选择框架，通过将多抓取配置下训练的PPO策略critic蒸馏为轻量抓取效用网络，在接触前为候选抓取打分并选取最优抓取，成功率由最佳单一抓取策略的79.87%提升至89.84%（+9.97个百分点），并降低最终姿态误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作在长时域内的移动-操作协调是大物体重排的关键能力，但机器人-物体、物体-环境的交互动力学复杂，可靠执行困难。基于模型的方法依赖精确动力学模型；基于学习的方法虽降低该要求，但通常假定固定或预设的初始抓取。而几何上稳定的不同抓取会导致显著不同的下游操作表现，因此初始抓取选择本身应被显式优化。

Method: 面向目标条件的大物体重排，训练一个跨多种初始抓取配置的统一PPO强化学习策略；随后将学到的critic蒸馏为一个轻量抓取效用网络，该网络仅依据候选抓取配置与目标信息对候选抓取进行排序。推理阶段在尚未接触物体前，即选择预测下游效用最高的抓取交由下游策略执行任务。

Result: 相比固定单一抓取策略与随机抓取选择，Grasp-TAGS提升重排成功率：平均成功率89.84%，而表现最佳的单一抓取策略为79.87%，提升9.97个百分点；同时在所有评估物体上均降低了最终朝向误差。

Conclusion: 初始抓取配置对下游长时域重排性能有实质影响，将多抓取策略的价值函数蒸馏为抓取效用排序器，可在接触前实现任务感知的抓取选择，且无需精确动力学模型与人工固定的初始抓取假设。

Abstract: Loco-manipulation, which requires mobile manipulators to coordinate locomotion and manipulation over long horizons, is a key capability for large-object rearrangement in applications such as service robotics and warehouse automation. Despite recent progress, reliably executing these tasks remains challenging because performance depends on complex robot-object and object-environment interaction dynamics. Existing approaches address this challenge either through model-based methods, which often require accurate dynamics models, or through learning-based methods, which reduce this requirement but typically assume a fixed or predefined initial grasp. However, different geometrically stable grasps can lead to substantially different downstream manipulation performance. Therefore, we propose Grasp-TAGS, a task-aware grasp selection framework for goal-conditioned large-object rearrangement. We train a unified PPO-based reinforcement learning policy across multiple initial grasp configurations and distill the learned critic into a lightweight grasp utility network that ranks candidate grasps using only the candidate grasp configuration and goal information. At inference time, we select the grasp with the highest predicted downstream utility for the downstream policy to complete the task, before even making a contact. Experiments demonstrate that Grasp-TAGS improves rearrangement success over both fixed single-grasp policies and random grasp selection, achieving an average success rate of 89.84% compared with 79.87% for the best-performing single-grasp policies, an improvement of 9.97 percentage points. Grasp-TAGS also reduces final orientation errors across all evaluated objects. For more qualitative results, refer to the supplementary webpage: https://rpm-lab-umn.github.io/Grasp-TAGS-web-page/

</details>


### [363] [Locating Contact on Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots From Tendon Measurements Alone via Constrained Moving Horizon Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09877)
*Haitham El-Hussieny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出腱驱动连续体机器人的本体接触力在腱测量中存在至少七维零空间，属结构性不可观问题；当障碍几何已知时，非穿透与互补条件使接触力局部可辨识，并证明系统局部指数强可检测，据此提出实时约束移动时域估计器(MHE)，精度显著优于投影EKF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体机器人可穿越受限空间，但沿身体分布的接触在位于电机端的传感器上不可见，此前常被视为调参问题。作者意在说明这是传感映射本身的结构性局限，并寻求在已知障碍几何条件下恢复接触可辨识性的方法。

Method: 首先分析接触力到腱测量的映射，证明在接触支撑自由时其零空间维数至少为7，任何估计器均无法定位接触。随后引入已知障碍几何带来的非穿透与互补约束，证明含未知接触输入的系统的局部指数强可检测性；进而构造约束移动时域估计器，将约束编码为每个采样求解一次二次规划，并给出按模式划分的局部鲁棒稳定性结果。在双段机器人的有限元基准上，与带状态投影的EKF对比，并测试障碍位置毫米级误差、未建模壁面摩擦及40次随机模型失配（含未建模腱摩擦）。

Result: 在压靠并滑移于壁面的双段机器人有限元基准中，MHE接触定位误差0.9 mm、接触力估计误差0.22 N；调参后的投影EKF分别为65 mm与0.72 N。MHE可容忍毫米级障碍位置误差与未建模壁面摩擦，而投影EKF在此条件下失效；40次随机模型失配试验中MHE在力与位置两项指标上全部占优。无接触工况下MHE相对EKF无优势，印证其收益来源于接触约束。

Conclusion: 本体接触不可观测是腱驱动连续体机器人的结构性限制，而非调参可解问题；借助已知障碍几何与互补/非穿透条件可实现局部可辨识与强可检测，约束MHE在实时性、精度与鲁棒性上优于投影EKF，且优势确由接触约束带来。该报告为投稿IEEE Control Systems Letters之letter的扩展版，含完整证明与补充实验。

Abstract: Tendon-driven continuum robots can reach through constrained spaces, but contact along their bodies is invisible to the sensors at their motors. We show that this is a structural limitation rather than a tuning problem: the map from contact forces to tendon measurements has a null space of dimension at least seven, so no estimator that leaves the contact support free can locate contact. When the obstacle geometry is known, however, non-penetration and complementarity make the contact force locally identifiable, and we prove that the resulting system with unknown contact inputs is locally exponentially strongly detectable. We present a constrained moving horizon estimator (MHE) that encodes these conditions and runs in real time as one quadratic program per sample, and we give a local, mode-wise robust stability result for it. In a finite- element benchmark of a two-section robot pressing and sliding on a wall, the estimator located contact to 0.9 mm and estimated contact force to 0.22 N, compared with 65 mm and 0.72 N for a tuned extended Kalman filter (EKF) with state projection. The estimator tolerated millimeter- level errors in the obstacle position and unmodeled wall friction, under which the projected EKF failed. Under randomized model mismatch over 40 trials, including unmodeled tendon friction, it remained better in force and location in every trial. Without contact, the MHE offered no advantage over the EKF, which confirms that its benefit comes from the contact constraints. This report extends a letter submitted to IEEE Control Systems Letters with complete proofs and additional experiments.

</details>


### [364] [Scenario Orchestration for Closed-loop Policy Evaluation of Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09888)
*Frieda Rong,Zhaoyue Wang,Veer Kapadia,Vishaka Rajani,Aren A. Babikian,Xujie Si,Mahsa Golchoubian,Raquel Urtasun,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出场景编排器（scenario orchestrator），通过重定时、重路由和角色重分配等干预手段在闭环中自适应调整非自车交通参与者，以可靠地实现预设交互场景，从而更清晰地评估自动驾驶策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于场景的仿真对自动驾驶策略评估至关重要，但预先脚本化（固定参与者路线与速度）的场景在被测自车闭环响应后，可能无法保持其原本意图的交互；脚本场景无法适应意外的自车行为，而学习型交通参与者往往仅保持反应性，难以可靠地实现特定场景意图。因此需要一种能在闭环中维持场景意图的机制，并将"场景是否实现"与"策略是否失败"区分开来。

Method: 引入场景编排器，持续监测联合交通状态，并对非自车参与者实施重定时（re-timing）、重路由（re-routing）与角色重分配（role reassignment）三类干预。每一步求解一个带约束的优化问题，在保持预期交互的同时限制干预幅度并维持交通可行性。此外区分场景实现判定与策略失败判定：先判断预期交互是否发生，再评估自车策略的响应表现。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器上针对多类场景族和多种驾驶策略进行实验，编排方法较脚本化与学习型参与者基线更可靠地实现目标交互，尤其在初始条件和自车行为发生变化的条件下优势明显。

Conclusion: 闭环场景编排可作为实现可控、可复现策略评估的有效工具。

Abstract: Scenario-based simulation is central to evaluating autonomous-driving policies, but a scenario scripted before execution (i.e., with predetermined, fixed actor routes and speeds) may not preserve its intended interaction once the tested ego policy responds in closed loop. Scripted scenarios cannot adapt to unanticipated ego behavior, while learned traffic actors may remain reactive without reliably realizing a particular scenario intent. We introduce a scenario orchestrator that continuously monitors the joint traffic state and adapts non-ego actors through re-timing, re-routing, and role reassignment for creating a specific scenario intent. At each step, the orchestrator solves a constrained optimization problem that preserves the intended interaction while limiting interventions and maintaining traffic feasibility. We further distinguish scenario realization from policy failure, allowing a test to first be judged based on whether the intended interaction occurred and then by how the ego policy responded. Experiments in the CARLA simulator across multiple scenario families and driving policies show that orchestration realizes targeted interactions more reliably than scripted and learned actor-level baselines, particularly under variations in initial conditions and ego behavior. These results support closed-loop scenario orchestration as a tool for controlled and reproducible policy evaluation.

</details>


### [365] [On Agentic Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09892)
*Chen Chen,Omid Bagherieh,Naveen Nikamanth Appiah Balaji,Jesse T. Gonzalez,Negin Heravi,Fahad Islam,Xueting Li,Jean-Baptiste Regli,Kejia Ren,Petr Švec,David J. Yoon,Matthias Müller,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通用AI模型直接控制机械臂的"智能体式操作策略"(AMP)，零样本超越VLA/WAM专家策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作方法多为在演示数据上训练的专用策略(VLA、WAM)，泛化性与鲁棒性受限；希望验证通用AI模型直接控制机械臂能否更可靠、更通用。

Method: 提出AMP范式：由通用AI模型直接向机械臂下达指令并随任务进展自适应调整动作。构建AMP1参考框架，同一框架不加改动地运行于单臂、双臂与移动操作场景；不使用演示数据与任务特定训练。另引入跨回合携带的持久记忆表示，使智能体可从经验中学习。

Result: RoboLab-120零样本成功率85.6%（领先VLA 49.2%、WAM 42.9%）；108次真机试验覆盖12项任务、3个机器人实例，104次零样本成功，相对强VLA/WAM基线失败率降低约20倍与15倍。基础框架不含抓取、推、放置技能，智能体自行形成寻找隐蔽物体、以物为工具、标定相机等策略；加入记忆后，RoboDojo双臂任务失败率较零样本再降4.6倍。

Conclusion: AMP比专用操作策略更具泛化性与鲁棒性，有望成为机器人操作的有前景基础范式。

Abstract: Recent work on robot manipulation has focused on specialist policies trained on demonstrations. We show that generalist AI models can control manipulators more reliably than specialist policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs). We systematically analyze agentic manipulation policies (AMPs), in which a generalist AI model commands the manipulator directly, adapting its actions as the task unfolds. We present AMP1, a reference harness that runs unchanged across single-arm, bimanual, and mobile manipulation. AMP1 achieves state-of-the-art results on the RoboLab-120 and CaP-Gym benchmarks, with no demonstrations or task-specific training. On RoboLab-120, AMP1 reaches an 85.6\% success rate zero-shot, compared to 49.2\% and 42.9\% for leading VLA and WAM policies. The agent succeeds zero-shot in 104 of 108 real-world trials spanning twelve tasks and three robot instances, reducing failure rates by 20x and 15x relative to strong VLA and WAM baselines. The basic harness supplies no grasping, pushing, or placing skills; the agent develops its own strategies, such as searching for hidden objects, using objects as tools, and calibrating its cameras. With the addition of a persistent memory store, the agent can also learn from experience: an augmented harness with a memory representation that is carried across episodes lowers the failure rate on RoboDojo bimanual manipulation tasks by 4.6x relative to zero-shot deployment. Our analysis suggests that AMPs are substantially more general and robust than specialist manipulation policies, providing a promising foundation for robot manipulation.

</details>
